Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.18

Chapter 1701
After running for a while, the long-haired man looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. "No matter what kind of enemies you have, I will definitely kill them." He looked at the dungeon and smiled. "As for those fellows in the dungeon, don't let them out!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He did not have to release everyone. If Qin Luo was able to kill Mu Han, Mu Tian and Mu Jiu, there was indeed no need for him to release everyone from the dungeon.
"If you really have the confidence to kill those three, there's naturally no need to release them," Luo Zheng said with a nod.
The people in the dungeon were originally afraid of swearing their inner demons. After hearing Luo Zheng and Qin Luo's words, they all panicked. Luo Zheng was only releasing them because he had a favor to ask of them. If Luo Zheng could get through this crisis, they might not get another chance!
Thinking of this, the powerful existences in the dungeon panicked.
"Zhan Beihai! What do you mean! "
"Burning the bridge after crossing the river? What a waste of my teaching you!"
"That … You're Luo Zheng, right? I'm willing to swear by my inner demons that I, Old Man Extreme Evil, will listen to your orders for the rest of my life. If I disobey, I will die from my inner demons! "The old voice in the dungeon said anxiously.
Besides Old Man Extreme Evil, the other "Big Mouth" also said, "I'm willing too! I, Wu Yu, am willing to submit to Luo Zheng for the rest of my life! If I disobey, I will be burned by my inner demons and die from my inner demons! "
Hearing the two of them swear on their inner demons, Zhan Beihai's face revealed a trace of depression.
Many years ago, Zhan Beihai was captured by Gu Bei and brought to the Immortal Mansion. However, Gu Bei didn't lock him up in the dungeon. Compared to the three old monsters in the dungeon, Zhan Beihai wasn't qualified to be locked up in the dungeon.
However, Zhan Beihai, who was locked up outside, had also obtained a lot of benefits. At the same time, he had fabricated a lot of things and obtained a lot of benefits from "Big Mouth Monster" Wu Yu and Old Man Extreme Evil. If they were released at the same time, these two old fellows would inevitably cause trouble for him. That was why Zhan Beihai strongly advocated not to release these two fellows.
"You don't have to release them. I can help you kill those three True Gods. Even if they are disciples of Saints, they shouldn't be my match," Zhan Beihai advised.
"Stinking brat! I knew you were a liar! "
"When I come out, I will tear you apart alive!"
When the two people in the dungeon heard Zhan Beihai's words, they were immediately enraged and cursed loudly in the dungeon …
"Let them out. After all, they have already sworn by their inner demons," Luo Zheng said. Then, he paused and said, "I don't care what grudges there are between the three of you. Since you have sworn by your inner demons, you can't be rude in front of me. As for the future, it's up to you."
Luo Zheng proposed this plan and Zhan Beihai agreed for the time being. Compared to the two vicious fellows in the dungeon, Zhan Beihai was much more normal. If he really fell into the hands of these two fellows, he could imagine what would happen to him.
After the discussion, Luo Zheng asked Zhan Beihai to wait at the side while Luo Zheng stepped into the entrance of the dungeon.
From the entrance of the dungeon, there was a row of steps covered with moss that extended all the way down. The walls on both sides of the dungeon were also covered with purple moss. The moss seemed to be alive as it wriggled on the walls and steps.
Out of curiosity, Luo Zheng stopped and looked around. However, he instantly felt a suction force coming from under his feet. It actually sucked his feet firmly in place!
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned and was about to lift his legs to pull them out. He did not expect that he would not be able to get rid of the suction force. The moss actually began to wriggle crazily and grew rapidly. In an instant, his feet were wrapped in it!
"Huh!"
Shocked, Luo Zheng poured a huge amount of power into his feet. He used the power of the platform to pull his feet out!
"This moss is a plant unique to Takeshi Xuan Forest. It's good that you're strong enough to run fast. Hehe, otherwise, even an ordinary True God would die if they were caught in it." The old voice laughed.
The enchantment of the entire Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion isolated everything in the forest. However, there were always some small things that could get in. For example, this moss was one of them.
Although it was just an unremarkable layer of moss, it contained a terrifying devouring power. Moreover, this thing had a strong vitality. Even if it was removed completely, it would grow back in a few days.
Every once in a while, the few True Gods in the dungeon would clean up the moss in the dungeon. The Evil Old Man even used the Evil Thoughts Fire to burn the entire dungeon, but the moss continued to grow vigorously.
"Just a layer of moss is so magical. The Takeshi Xuan Forest you speak of … is it the huge forest outside?"
This was the first time Luo Zheng had heard the name of the forest. In fact, the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion was built in the Takeshi Xuan Forest.
Perhaps Luo Zheng had asked a taboo question, but the Evil Old Man did not answer Luo Zheng's question. He only said in a low voice, "Quickly come in and let us go. We will help you deal with a strong enemy. After that, we will never see you again!"
The Oath of the Heart Demon they had sworn was extremely harsh. Strictly speaking, it meant that they would obey Luo Zheng for all eternity.
These people were considered experts in the Realm of the Gods. How could they be willing to be ordered around by others? After helping Luo Zheng this time, they naturally had to go far away. It would be best if they could never see Luo Zheng again …
Seeing that the Evil Old Man was unwilling to answer, Luo Zheng did not press him. He quickly walked down the stairs and entered a narrow corridor. The dungeon was not big. There were only two cells on this floor. Above the cells, there were snow-white fences.
These fences were probably made of the same material as the Immortal Essence Rope. They had been erected here for countless years, but their surface still shone brightly. Luo Zheng also noticed that there were many dents on the fences. They were probably marks left behind by the people inside who wanted to destroy them.
Through the fences of the first cell, Luo Zheng saw an old man with a head full of silver hair sitting inside. This old man's eyebrows were upside-down, the corners of his eyes drooped down. His aquiline nose was extremely straight, and the sides of his mouth drooped down along the sides of his beard. His appearance was very strange. No matter who it was, he would leave a very deep impression. This face gave people a strong sense of malice. This had to be the Evil Old Man!
Luo Zheng sized up this old man, and the old man did the same to Luo Zheng. After the two of them looked at each other, the Evil Old Man laughed coldly. "A good disciple that Gu Bei took a liking to. I thought you had three heads and six arms!"
Luo Zheng did not bother to pay attention to him. He activated the Qian Token. A faint gray light shot out of it and shot toward the silver fences in front of him.
"Crack …"
The moment this light entered the fences, dense cracks began to appear all over them. As it collapsed, it disappeared before Luo Zheng's eyes … Previously, when he had released Zhan Beihai, Luo Zheng had felt that this material was very mysterious. Once it cracked, it disappeared without a trace, as if it could evaporate into thin air like water. However, it was this material that could imprison a True God!
"Ding ling ling …"
When the Evil Old Man saw that the fences had disappeared, he extended his hands in front of him. "There's more!" There were still two Immortal Essence Chains in his hands.
After Luo Zheng released the two chains that bound the Evil Old Man, he stretched his wrists, a joyful smile on his face. This feeling of regaining freedom was indeed exhilarating, but when it came from the Evil Old Man's face, it felt like a sinister smile …
Then, the Evil Old Man flipped over and disappeared before Luo Zheng's eyes. Luo Zheng only felt a gust of wind brush past him. When he turned to look, the Evil Old Man had already rushed out of the dungeon!
"There's still me, there's still me!"
An urgent voice came from the other dungeon.
This big-mouthed monster had been imprisoned here with the Evil Old Man for countless years. Seeing that the Evil Old Man had truly regained his freedom, he must be itching for it.
When Luo Zheng arrived in front of the big-mouthed monster's cell, he could not help but laugh. This big-mouthed monster was just like his name. He had a huge mouth with two sausages hanging from his lips. He looked very comical.

Chapter 1702
"Is it very funny?"
The big-mouthed monster's face instantly became ferocious.
He was not human, so his appearance was naturally strange. However, his pride was extremely strong, and he could not tolerate others mocking his appearance.
"I'm sorry." Luo Zheng also stopped smiling.
Although he had forced these people to swear an Oath of Inner Demons, these people were almighties in the Divine Territory after all. Luo Zheng also knew that he had been rude.
Seeing Luo Zheng's sincere apology, the big-mouthed monster was slightly stunned. He did not expect Luo Zheng to be so easy to talk to.
He had made such a serious Oath of Inner Demons, which meant that he was enslaved by Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng did not need to apologize to him.
For example, he and the Evil Old Man were considered fugitives in the Divine Territory. Many forces did not tolerate them. Naturally, the people they befriended were extremely vicious. There were many examples of them fighting because of a disagreement. There were many True Gods who fought with him because they mocked his appearance.
However, the Devouring Sky Technique that the big-mouthed monster cultivated was extremely powerful. Almost all the True Gods who fought with him were forcefully swallowed by him. The special thing about his Devouring Sky Technique was that after he swallowed a True God, it did not destroy his Dantian. Instead, it could integrate the Inner World in his Dantian into his Inner World. Hence, the more True Gods he swallowed, the more his Inner World would expand and his abilities would become stronger!
His current abilities might be slightly weaker than the Evil Old Man's, but his speed of improvement was definitely much faster than the Evil Old Man's. As long as he swallowed enough True Gods, he could even be promoted to a Complete Paragon or even a Sage … However, the number of True Gods he needed to swallow was an unimaginable number. After he had swallowed thousands of True Gods, he was captured by Gu Bei and brought here!
"Sigh, I'm used to it …" The big-mouthed monster waved his hand and felt bored. "Let me go quickly!"
If you treated someone with respect, they would always return the favor. It was the same for these villains.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. Then, he used the "Qian" token to help the big-mouthed monster break the bindings in the dungeon.
The big-mouthed monster, like the Evil Old Man, escaped from the dungeon like the wind and breathed in the free air …
Luo Zheng stood where he was and looked at the depths of the dungeon again.
In the backyard, other than the big dog, there was only Zhan Beihai, who was locked outside the dungeon. The Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster, Wu Yu, were locked up on this floor of the dungeon. However, Luo Zheng had heard another voice saying that Luo Zheng's request was too much. From beginning to end, this person had only said one sentence.
At the end of the dungeon, there was a passageway leading downwards.
The passage was already covered in moss. Probably because this person was unwilling to clean up the moss, the moss grew layer by layer and wrapped the passage tightly. It could only allow one person to walk sideways.
A hint of hesitation appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
The Great Yan Universe already had one True God, Star Tail. Thus, he only needed two True Gods to fight. Zhan Beihai, the Extreme Evil Old Man, or the Big Mouth Monster would be enough. There was no need for so many people.
Moreover, although these people were locked up in the dungeon, they seemed to be able to use some methods, such as removing the moss in the dungeon.
Just now, Luo Zheng had waited until the Extreme Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster had sworn the Heart Demon Oath before he entered the dungeon. If the people on the second floor of the dungeon were unwilling to swear the oath, he naturally wouldn't enter recklessly and risk his life.
Since this person was unwilling to speak, Luo Zheng wouldn't ask for a rebuff. He turned and prepared to leave.
However, just as he took a step, he heard the voice faintly, "Young man, I can't swear the Heart Demon Oath …"
Luo Zheng blinked his eyes and immediately turned around. He faced the second floor of the dungeon and said, "Senior, I don't have to force you to swear an oath of the Heart Demon. It's just that junior's strength is still weak and I have no other choice."
"Sigh …" The voice sighed faintly, "I know."
With Luo Zheng's abilities, he wouldn't be able to control the situation if he rashly let these people out. He would be courting death.
The voice on the second floor of the dungeon was silent for a while. Then, it said faintly, "Young man, I can't swear the Heart Demon Oath for some reason. However, there's another method that can set your mind at ease."
This person's abilities might be far superior to the Big Mouth Monster and the Extreme Evil Old Man, but he also yearned for freedom …
"What method?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Whoosh!"
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, a green light shot out from the second floor of the dungeon and landed steadily at Luo Zheng's feet. "This is an enslavement order. I can let you enslave me. What do you think?"
"Enslavement order?" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback when he saw the green card on the ground.
Compared to the control of the Heart Demon Oath, the control of this enslavement order was much stronger.
Although Luo Zheng made the three people outside the dungeon swear the Heart Demon Oath, it was only a restriction. Luo Zheng could order them to do things that didn't endanger their lives.
However, if he made the three of them go through fire and water and sacrifice their lives, they could also violate their oath. However, they would suffer a strong backlash from the Heart Demon Oath, and there was a high possibility that they would die. However, they would die anyway. Whether it was the Extreme Evil Old Man or Zhan Beihai, in a fit of anger, they would probably kill him first!
This enslavement order was a true enslavement. If Luo Zheng controlled the enslavement order, he would be the absolute master of this person. He could even change this person's emotions, erase their memories, and turn them into a completely puppet-like existence.
Not willing to swear the Heart Demon Oath, but willing to be enslaved — this made Luo Zheng even more suspicious. At the same time, he began to suspect this person's motives.
"If you don't believe the authenticity of this enslavement order, you can let the servant in Immortal Mansion verify it. That Afo has followed Gu Bei for many years. With his experience, he should be able to recognize this order!" the voice continued.
For the sake of caution, Luo Zheng picked up the green, lustrous token on the ground and walked out of the dungeon.
After leaving the dungeon, Luo Zheng presented the token to Afo.
When Afo saw the token, he was slightly taken aback. "This is the Imperial Enslavement Order, the highest level of enslavement order. Apart from Saints, you can enslave anyone … Did that person on the second floor of Immortal Mansion give it to you?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
"Heh, that's quite cunning." Afo smiled.
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
"There was a period of time when enslavement orders were all the rage in the divine realm. Later on, all living beings hated this thing to the extreme. They would rather die than be enslaved." Afo said indifferently. "That person cultivates the Dao of the Heart and Soul, so he can't swear the Heart Demon Oath. That would have a great impact on his Dao of the Gods. That's why he was willing to be enslaved by you."
The person on the second floor of the dungeon was also secretly observing Luo Zheng's character. He knew that Luo Zheng wasn't a despicable person, so he dared to give the enslavement order to Luo Zheng because he was eager to escape …
This was what Afo meant by "cunning".
After confirming this, Luo Zheng took the token back to the dungeon. Under the guidance of the person on the second floor of the dungeon, he injected a strand of his soul power into the token.
The moment his token connected with the token, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a cold feeling invade his mind. Then he felt the existence of a strange and powerful soul, and he could read any memory in this soul …
Luo Zheng had the Will of the World. Through the Will of the World, he could also read the lives of countless living beings. But that was from the perspective of a third party. What Luo Zheng could read now was any secret in this soul, and even the comprehension of various techniques and life. This soul didn't have any defenses against Luo Zheng. He could even erase the memories in it at will!
Luo Zheng was the owner of this soul now!
"Can you let me out now?" That person's faint voice sounded again.
Luo Zheng nodded. His body flashed, and he rushed down the narrow passage on the second floor. The moment he rushed down the passage, the moss on both sides seemed to have been waiting for him for a long time. It quickly squeezed towards Luo Zheng, completely wrapping him in it!
"Hmph!"
Luo Zheng snorted coldly and began to move forward.

Chapter 1703
Luo Zheng forcibly squeezed through this narrow passageway and entered the second floor of the dungeon.
But the scene in front of him made Luo Zheng extremely shocked!
Compared to the cramped space on the first floor of the dungeon, the second floor of the dungeon was much more spacious, with a depth of several tens of zhang.
Above and below the prison, there were eight bright silver discs on the walls. Hanging from these discs were eight Immortal Essence Ropes. These Immortal Essence Ropes extended towards the center, and in the end, they bound a person!
Those Immortal Essence Ropes were like a cocoon, tightly wrapping around that person. It was completely impossible to see that person's appearance.
But Luo Zheng saw countless thin and long strands of hair extending through the gaps of the Immortal Essence Ropes and falling to the ground …
"Could it be a woman?" Luo Zheng thought to himself.
Although he had enslaved this person through the Enslavement Order, he didn't pry into the memories of the other person's soul. Just now, when he heard the voice from the second floor of the dungeon, Luo Zheng didn't distinguish whether this person was a man or a woman.
From a gap in the Immortal Essence Ropes, a snow-white arm stretched out.
This arm was slender and pure white, as if carved from white jade. It had an extremely moving shape. Just now, the Enslavement Order should have been thrown by this hand.
What kind of cultivation did this person have to use so many Immortal Essence Ropes to bind him? Luo Zheng was very curious.
He walked forward and said: "Let me help senior undo it."
Then he activated the "Qian" Writ again. Cracks rapidly appeared on the Immortal Essence Ropes, then they disintegrated and disappeared in front of Luo Zheng's eyes, like silk being peeled from a cocoon.
Gradually, a snow-white naked body appeared in front of Luo Zheng …
Generally speaking, when one cultivated to such a realm, one would have seen through the flesh and skin of the body.
For example, martial artists of the Divine Sea Realm could live for thousands or even tens of thousands of years. They had seen all kinds of beautiful women. As for martial artists of even higher realms, and even True Gods, they had even longer lives. In their eyes, all living things were nothing but skin.
It was rare to see a True God like the big-mouthed monster, Wu Yu, who cared about his appearance.
However, Luo Zheng was born in this world, and his bone age was only forty. Thus, he still felt quite uncomfortable in front of such a woman's naked body. The woman's body emitted a radiance that was as bright as moonlight. It was as if there was a kind of magic hidden within it. For a moment, Luo Zheng's eyes were unable to move away.
Out of respect, Luo Zheng forced himself to turn his face to the side.
When he undid all the Immortal Essence Ropes, rays of soft blue light suddenly floated out of the woman's body. These soft lights formed ribbons of light that intertwined with each other. After covering the important parts of her body, they directly transformed into a simple and fitting blue dress.
As the long skirt slightly rolled up, the tight and well-proportioned legs under the skirt bent slightly, and a pair of bare feet touched the ground.
At this moment, Luo Zheng looked up and carefully sized up the woman's appearance.
Ever since he ascended to the Upper Realm, most of the women that Luo Zheng saw were peerless beauties. With such a cultivation level, it was too easy to change one's appearance. Moreover, it was only a matter of thought to maintain one's youthful appearance. Therefore, the biggest difference between beauties was their temperament. For example, Xun's passion under her silence, or Ning Yudie's grace and delicacy, etc.
However, the woman in front of him gave Luo Zheng an indescribable and unfathomable sense of mystery.
Humans were creatures with strong curiosity. Many times, mystery represented a strong attraction …
Afo said that this woman cultivated the Dao of the Heart and Soul. Presumably, this was the reason why she emitted this feeling.
"What's your name?" Luo Zheng asked.
The woman smiled faintly. There was always a mysterious charm released from her every movement, which made Luo Zheng feel even more confused and uncontrollably attracted. "You're indeed very decent. Even when I was enslaved, you didn't peek into my memories …"
After she was enslaved by Luo Zheng, it was equivalent to stripping off everything about herself and exposing herself naked in front of Luo Zheng. He only needed to search through her memories, and he would be able to understand any minute memory of her life.
"I said before, I don't need to enslave anyone, and I won't peek into your memories!" Luo Zheng replied. "I'm only here because I need your help."
"It doesn't matter." The woman took a step forward barefoot and gracefully approached Luo Zheng. With a faint smile on her face, she stretched out her slender index finger and brushed it across Luo Zheng's face. "I won't tell you my name. If you want to know my name, you have to search for it yourself …"
She shook her finger at Luo Zheng and raised her eyebrows slightly. She immediately turned around and walked towards the exit of the dungeon. Just now, Luo Zheng had forcibly rushed down and tore off a large piece of the moss. However, the growth of the moss was too vigorous, and now the exit was completely blocked.
When the woman walked in front of the moss, it seemed to be very afraid of her and constantly surged in all directions. On the ground where her bare feet stepped on, the moss on the ground separated in an instant, as if afraid of touching the woman. She walked out without any effort.
As for Luo Zheng, he stood there in a slight daze. He could have known the woman's name with just a thought, but he forcibly restrained himself. It seemed that probing into the woman's soul was like a trap. Although it was full of temptation, he was not willing to rashly step into it.
After the woman in blue left, the moss instantly filled the entire passageway layer by layer. Looking at the constantly squirming moss, Luo Zheng frowned. He could only once again operate the power source in his inner world and forcibly squeeze it out …
After leaving the dungeon, Luo Zheng heard a burst of barking coming from outside. It turned out that the big-mouthed monster was teasing Big Black from a certain distance. Big Black was bound by the Immortal Essence Rope and could only bark at the big-mouthed monster. However, it could not do anything to the big-mouthed monster. Big Black's barks could greatly suppress Luo Zheng's soul, but it had little effect on the big-mouthed monster.
When the big-mouthed monster saw the woman in blue appear, it seemed to have seen something extremely terrifying. The sausage-like corners of its mouth could not help but twitch. Soon after, it did not dare to move.
As for Big Black, it whimpered and rushed back into the doghouse, lying on the ground motionlessly.
Such a ferocious dog actually looked pitifully at the woman in blue …
As for the Extreme Evil Old Man and Zhan Beihai, both had nervous expressions. They did not expect that Luo Zheng would dare to release the woman from the second floor of the dungeon!
"Why are you looking at me like that?" The woman in blue glanced at everyone, a hazy smile appearing on her face.
This smile was so enchanting that Luo Zheng found it strange. Why were these people so afraid?
"You, you, don't kill us!" The ferocious-looking Extreme Evil Old Man said as he pointed at the woman in blue. It was indeed rare to see such an expression on the Extreme Evil Old Man's face.
The woman in blue chuckled. "We are now considered to be on the same side. Naturally, there is no need to kill you. If we meet in the future, who knows? Let's talk about … how to help this young man."
Returning to the main topic, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. "Two people. I only need two people!"
"Can't all of us go together?" Zhan Beihai immediately said.
"That's right. Anyway, as long as we tear the other party apart, the mission will be completed," the big-mouthed monster hurriedly agreed.
In their hearts, they thought that if the woman in blue stayed in the Immortal Mansion, the other fellows who were not chosen by Luo Zheng would be unlucky. It was better for everyone to stay together. At least the woman in blue would not act rashly. It was clear that they were extremely afraid of her.
"No." Luo Zheng shook his head. "I need you to fight in the universe. The other party only has three True Gods. We can only have three on our side. This is the rule."
"Universe?"
"You mean the Inner World constructed by the Sage?"
"Isn't the universe only allowed to have one True God?"
"Who is Mu Haiji fighting with in the universe?"
These people's faces were filled with astonishment. They were caught in the Immortal Mansion and naturally knew that this place was Long Xuan. They did not expect Luo Zheng to bring them to fight in the universe.
Previously, they had already guessed that the opponent was Mu Haiji's disciple. Since they were going to fight in the universe, Mu Haiji must have started a universe war with a True God …
"His surname is Luo." The woman in blue smiled charmingly again.
With this sentence, the few True Gods found the answer in an instant.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1704
The Divine Territory had a long history.
The time span of one Divine Era was unimaginably long. Since the Divine Territory was created, countless Divine Eras had passed.
In every Divine Era, countless living beings had become Gods. After becoming a True God, their inner world would form a perfect closed loop. Their lifespan would be endless, which meant that they would exist forever.
True Gods were born continuously, but very few died.
After countless God Epochs, the True Gods in the God Domain had formed an extremely large group.
However, the number of True Gods who could become Saints was very small!
Among them, there was only one with the surname "Luo" — Luo Xiao.
The few Real Gods at the scene immediately understood what the lady in blue meant when they heard her.
They had heard that Firmament Saint and North Saint had a good relationship. Even when the Divine Territory's Saint Hall had caused such a huge commotion, North Saint had stood firmly on Luo Xiao's side and withstood great pressure … Luo Zheng must be Luo Xiao's son. It was no wonder that he could become Gu Bei's disciple.
The Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster had sensed Luo Zheng's potential and wanted to take him in as a disciple. However, when they heard that Luo Zheng was Gu Bei's disciple, they naturally gave up on the idea. Now that they knew that Luo Zheng was Luo Xiao's son, they knew that they had thought too much.
"This involves a lot of things, tsk tsk …" The Evil Old Man clenched his teeth and said.
The big-mouthed monster nodded as well …
If they had killed Mu Haiji's three disciples in the Divine Territory, it would not have been a big deal. At most, they could just run away. Moreover, the Divine Territory was so vast. Although Mu Haiji was a Saint and had great abilities, it would not be easy for him to find out the truth.
However, this Universal War was happening in the inner worlds of two Saints. Mu Haiji was not the only one watching. The Saints of the Saint Hall were watching as well!
Originally, the two of them were very enthusiastic, but now they had already retreated.
"I'll go," Zhan Beihai said calmly.
"Aiya! Brother Beihai is indeed suitable. He has a noble status and is not afraid of Mu Haiji's revenge! I'll have to trouble you … "The Evil Old Man said with a smile.
Zhan Beihai rolled his eyes. His background was different from these two. These two were notorious villains in the Divine Domain. Once they were exposed, everyone would want to kill them. Even if Mu Haiji didn't make a move, the other Saints would.
However, Zhan Beihai was captured by Gu Bei because of other reasons, so he was not afraid of Mu Haiji.
"Then I'll go too." The woman in blue smiled faintly. Compared to Mu Haiji, this woman was even more unrestrained. A faint black mist appeared in her eyes and escaped along her eyelashes. It was as if she had applied black eyeshadow to her eyes. It was extremely moving.
"Alright!" Luo Zheng nodded.
It didn't matter who accompanied him to battle. He needed to solve the problem in the Great Yan Universe as soon as possible!
Thus, the Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster were left in the Immortal Abode, while Zhan Beihai and the woman in blue followed Luo Zheng out of the Immortal Abode and headed to the Great Expansion Universe.
…
…
"Roar!"
A demonic red dragon breath soared into the sky.
This dragon breath was like a flood that broke through a dam, dyeing half the sky crimson red …
A figure constantly shuttled through the dragon breath. With a leap, he was already above the Ancestral Gragon's head. He stretched out his leg and lightly stepped on the Ancestral Gragon's head!
"Boom!"
An invisible force pressed down, and the Ancestral Gragon's huge head smashed downwards.
"This little fellow is indeed powerful. Unfortunately, he's still too young," Mu Han said faintly. Previously, he had been trapped in the bamboo forest by the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin. He had spent a lot of effort to break through the bamboo forest's maze. He didn't expect to encounter the Ancestral Gragon as soon as he broke through the maze.
"Rumble …"
The head was heavily stomped into the ground of a great world, sinking deep into the ground. It directly drilled through this great world!
"Roar roar roar …"
Mu Han had just stepped on the Ancestral Gragon when nine dragons of different colors intersected and breathed out different dragon breaths. There was acid fog, fire, lightning …
"You guys? Even more useless! "
The corners of Mu Han's mouth curled up. His body spun rapidly in the air, and a gentle light appeared on his body. He actually went against the dragon breaths. When he rushed towards the five-clawed golden dragon, he reached out and grabbed at the air!
A pale yellow giant hand grabbed the five-clawed golden dragon's vital point and exerted force!
"Ka ka ka …"
The five-clawed golden dragon was instantly crushed into two!
However, at the moment of the rupture, a bright light flashed out of the five-clawed golden dragon's fracture. It turned into countless Heaven's Divination Essence, which flowed like mercury and connected back together.
The bodies that the nine dragons had formed were Heaven's Evolution Bodies. Although it wasn't as good as their original bodies, it was difficult to destroy this Heaven's Evolution Body. After a few exchanges, the Heaven's Evolution Bodies had been destroyed several times. However, they were able to repair themselves and form a whole body.
However, if this body was continuously destroyed, the Heaven's Evolution Essence would weaken to a certain extent. It would eventually be difficult to reform this body. Now that they couldn't do anything to Mu Han, the situation was naturally worrying.
On the other side …
Heavenly Venerable Oracle and Xun shuttled back and forth like two comets in the sky, circling around Mu Jiu.
"Tsk, tsk, tsk. I didn't expect a human woman to have such a temperament. I, Mu Jiu, am deeply honored to have two beauties as my companions!"
Mu Jiu held the Soul-Searching Banner and narrowed his eyes. His gaze was firmly locked on the two women!
"Shut up!"
With Heavenly Venerable Cloud Fox's help, Xun's wings suddenly flapped, and she suddenly appeared beside Mu Jiu. Her eyes were crimson red, and her entire body was wrapped in a layer of dark red fog. The power of faith that had accumulated in the Pool of Faith for countless years surged wildly into the Holy Spear of Massacre!
"Swoosh!"
This spear contained an extremely terrifying killing intent. Under the condensation of the spear intent, it seemed to be able to penetrate all things.
"The power of faith that focuses on massacre? I like this feeling! "Mu Jiu smiled slightly. He didn't dodge at all. Instead, he grabbed Xun's Holy Spear of Massacre!
At the same time, Heavenly Venerable Oracle also appeared behind Mu Jiu. She shuttled through space like a shadow, and the dim light in her hand silently swept towards the back of Mu Jiu's neck!
A trace of excitement appeared on Heavenly Venerable Oracle's beautiful face. At this distance, she had never failed before!
However, at this moment, a strong sense of danger assaulted her heart. A gray trajectory instantly appeared and collided with her mind. She had no time to dodge. She only felt a wave of dizziness, then lost consciousness and fell down.
The speed of the Light-Sweeping Bat was unimaginably fast. Previously, it had killed Heavenly Venerable Thunder Punishment with a single blow. However, it had only knocked Heavenly Venerable Oracle unconscious. Naturally, Mu Jiu had shown mercy!
On the other side, Mu Jiu had already grabbed Xun's Holy Spear of Massacre. With a sudden tug, the Holy Spear of Massacre flew out of Xun's hand like a sharp arrow. Xun wanted to flap her wings and escape. At the same time, Heavenly Venerable Cloud Fox also wanted to pull her back.
However, Mu Jiu's eyes flashed. With a casual slap of his hand, he locked the space around Xun. Then, he reached over Xun's shoulder and grabbed, crushing all of Xun's skeletal wings. Immediately after, he shook the Soul-Searching Banner!
A gray ribbon of light shot out from the banner and quickly wrapped around Xun!
The moment the ribbon of light wrapped around her, Xun felt that her soul couldn't move. Even her thoughts became extremely slow. She couldn't even control her body to escape …
"It's a pity that such a beauty has fallen." Mu Jiu chuckled. Then, he fell backward and shot downward with the Soul-Searching Banner in his hand.
Heavenly Venerable Oracle had already fallen quite a distance. However, she had unconsciously landed on the ground. Mu Jiu caught up with her in no time. As Mu Jiu shook the Soul-Searching Banner, another gray ribbon of light shot out from the banner and wrapped around Heavenly Venerable Oracle.
Mu Jiu hung the two women on the Soul-Searching Banner like trophies of war …
Heavenly Venerable Oracle, who had slowly regained consciousness, and Xun, who couldn't move, felt their hearts sink. It was better to die in battle than to fall into the hands of such a person!
"Tsk, tsk …" Mu Jiu had a frivolous expression on his face. He reached out and gently pinched Heavenly Venerable Oracle's face. Although Heavenly Venerable Oracle couldn't move, he stared at Mu Jiu with a cold, murderous gaze.
"I like that look in your eyes." Mu Jiu patted Heavenly Venerable Oracle's face. After saying that, he looked at Xun. Although the two women both had cold expressions, their temperaments were worlds apart.
Just as he was about to toy with them, he suddenly heard the sound of wind behind him. His heart skipped a beat. He pulled the Soul-Searching Banner and flipped to the side!
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
A sword ray that stretched for hundreds of miles slashed past Mu Jiu. The sharpness of this sword ray made Mu Jiu's heart turn cold!
If it weren't for his sharp reaction, he might have died under this sword!

Chapter 1705
Mu Jiu was slightly taken aback when he saw Luo Zheng appear here out of nowhere. "Eh? Aren't you the kid that senior brother is looking for? Where did you come from? "
Mu Han had used Mu Jiu's soul-searching flag to search for Luo Zheng in the entire Great Yan Universe!
However, Luo Zheng had disappeared without a trace. It was rather strange.
Now that he had suddenly appeared, Mu Jiu was naturally astonished.
However, although Mu Jiu found it strange, he didn't take Luo Zheng seriously. In Mu Jiu's opinion, Luo Zheng was just a slightly stronger Dao Disciple … He didn't even take the many Heavenly Venerables in the Great Yan Universe seriously, let alone a Realm Lord Dao Disciple? He wasn't worth mentioning! Now that Luo Zheng had appeared here, he was just courting death.
"But that's fine too. My senior brother is stubborn. If he can't find you, he'll be nagging for tens of thousands of years!" Mu Jiu continued.
Luo Zheng stared coldly at Mu Jiu. After Mu Jiu finished speaking, he said indifferently, "Kill him. Try to make it as painful as possible."
Mu Jiu was astonished. "Who are you talking to? Could it be … that you've invited help? "
True Gods in the God Realm never took second-level lifeforms seriously. Moreover, Mu Jiu and the others were Mu Haiji's disciples, so they were even more prideful. Mu Jiu didn't think that Luo Zheng would be able to invite any serious help!
However, as soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, a steady voice came from behind Mu Jiu. "Congratulations, you're right!"
"Boom!"
Then, a monstrous battle intent surged!
That battle intent contained an invincible momentum, as if there was nothing in the world that could make it afraid. There was no other choice but to press forward courageously!
The moment Mu Jiu sensed the battle intent, he realized that he was in trouble!
Although Mu Jiu was frivolous and boastful, his talent was indeed outstanding. Otherwise, he wouldn't have been taken in as a disciple by Mu Haiji. The moment he sensed the battle intent, his right hand began to shake. The Light-Grazing Bat perched on the soul-searching flag instantly turned into a gray streak and shot towards the person behind him!
How terrifying was the speed of a Light-Grazing Bat?
Before this, Empyrean Thunder Punishment and the others simply couldn't catch a trace of it. All they could see was a gray line …
However, the person who appeared behind Mu Jiu suddenly reached out and grabbed the grey line. The Light Sweeping Bat struggled in his hand, but it was unable to break free from his grasp!
"Light Sweeping Bat? It's extremely rare, it's a pity to kill it like this … "A surprised smile appeared on the man's face, but he then squeezed his hand and the soul of the Light Grazing Bat instantly dissipated …
The Light-Skimming Bat itself was not powerful. In fact, it could be said to be extremely fragile. It only relied on its extremely high speed. Ordinary True Gods would find it difficult to capture its trajectory, let alone shoot this little thing down.
But to Zhan Beihai, this was not a big deal.
"You, you dared to kill my Light-Sweeping Bat!"
Mu Jiu's eyes widened. He had not refined the Light-Grazing Bat in the first place and had always regarded it as an extremely precious item. He had thought that there was probably no one in this universe who could capture it, so he had been so confident in summoning it.
He did not expect that a guy who had suddenly appeared would have abilities that far exceeded his imagination!
"Such a strong battle intent, you're from the War God Temple!" Mu Jiu gritted his teeth and questioned.
Zhan Beihai shrugged. "I don't deny it." As he spoke, the battle intent on his body grew stronger. Once Zhan Beihai entered a battle, his entire person would be in an extremely excited state. It looked like he was ready to attack at any time.
Mu Jiu maintained a strong vigilance and said coldly, "I'm Mu Haiji's disciple!"
"I know," Zhan Beihai replied.
"This is a universal war between two Sages. My master's gaze is on this place!"
"I know."
"Do you know the price of interfering in this matter?" Mu Jiu asked again.
"I know …"
"Then why aren't you scramming?" Mu Jiu shouted coldly.
Zhan Beihai smiled slightly and stared at Mu Jiu with pity in his eyes. "I'm just telling you that I know, but I'm definitely going to interfere in this matter." After speaking, he stepped out in the air!
"Dong!"
A dull sound exploded from under Zhan Beihai's feet.
The space in the universe was not as solid as in the Divine Territory. With this step in the air, the space was instantly compressed and collapsed. The compression force was so great that it formed an annihilation force that exploded in the air, instantly enveloping a large area under Zhan Beihai's feet!
This was just the power of a single step!
Seeing the strong annihilation force explode, Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. This Zhan Beihai was really too strong!
Although Luo Zheng had one-third of a Sage's power source, he had always been unable to unleash this power source to the extreme. This was due to the difference in cultivation level, as well as the difference in Luo Zheng's control of power.
However, this could not be blamed on Luo Zheng. It was like someone giving a saber to a three or four-year-old child. No matter how smart the child was, it would be difficult for him to unleash the full power of this saber!
Therefore, Luo Zheng might have strength that far exceeded Zhan Beihai's, but if he used the same strength to compete with Zhan Beihai, he would still not be Zhan Beihai's match!
Mu Jiu was already on full alert against Zhan Beihai's movements. When he heard Zhan Beihai's reply, his expression turned ugly. "My master can turn you into meat paste in the blink of an eye. Aren't you afraid of death?"
"Hmph …"
A cold smile appeared on Zhan Beihai's face. He continued to charge forward at an extremely fast speed. Every step he took in the air caused the space to collapse, releasing a strong annihilation force!
Mu Jiu looked at the approaching Zhan Beihai, and his pupils contracted rapidly.
Should he fight or flee?
Even if he fled, Zhan Beihai would arrive in an instant. At this moment, Mu Jiu no longer hesitated. The Soul Searching Banner in his hand trembled continuously, and powerful True God souls floated out of the Soul Searching Banner!
This Soul Searching Banner only had a total of seven True God souls. Furthermore, each of them had been refined using a soul mystic technique for countless years. They were the Soul Searching Banner's trump card!
Before this, Mu Jiu would never have dreamed that he would use these True God souls! Summoning the Light Sweeping Bat could be considered an eye-opener for the Heavenly Venerables in the Great Evolution Universe. However, he was now worried whether these seven True God souls could keep him safe.
The seven Souls of the True Gods each had their own unique characteristics. There was a general wearing armor, an old man with a long beard, a woman, and a True Spirit … But no matter which Soul of the True Gods they were, they were all powerhouses when they were alive.
Many experts were unwilling to let their souls be enslaved and driven by others. Before they were enslaved, they would think of ways to destroy their own souls. Capturing the soul of a True God was not an easy task, but it was extremely powerful when it was really driven.
Although the Extreme Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster Wu Yu were much stronger than Zhan Beihai, if the Extreme Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster were to deal with them, they might not have it easier than Zhan Beihai. This was because Zhan Beihai was from the War God Temple!
The War God Temple mainly cultivated the War God Dao. Once their fighting spirit surged, they were unstoppable. Even their souls would be enveloped by the raging fighting spirit. They were not afraid of any soul-level attacks at all!
When the first True God soul rushed over, Zhan Beihai faced it head-on with a punch. This punch, which was filled with fighting spirit, smashed into the void and instantly shattered thousands of feet of space. Everything within it started to be annihilated …
Dong!
When the light produced by the annihilation disappeared, the first True God soul had already been defeated and disappeared without a trace.
Zhan Beihai did not stop at all. He had already sent out a second punch!
Dong!
Dong!
Dong!
Every time a dull sound spread out, a large area of space would be annihilated. At the same time, a True God soul would be defeated.
At this moment, Mu Jiu's heart was bleeding. These True God souls were his trump cards, but they were nothing in front of this fellow. Where did this Luo Zheng find help from? How could he be so powerful?
Heavenly Venerate Oracle and Xun, who were bound by the Soul-Searching Banner, also widened their eyes when they saw this scene. They looked at this fellow who was like a War God, and their brows were filled with disbelief!

Chapter 1706
Xun thought of something. She knew Luo Zheng better than Celestial Lord Shen Yu, and she had spent a lot of time in that Immortal Mansion.
When Luo Zheng went to the Illusory Battlefield, Xun often walked in the Immortal Mansion. There should be many secrets hidden in that Immortal Mansion, but these secrets had not been revealed.
Occasionally, she could hear the sound of a dog barking.
Although Xun had never seen Big Black before, the power contained in Big Black's roars had left a deep impression on Xun, who was still in her spiritual form at that time. Although she was fearless, Big Black's barks gave her great pressure even though they were separated by a great distance.
This person should have walked out of the Immortal Mansion …
After Zhan Beihai defeated the souls of the True Gods one by one, he slowed down his speed and walked toward Mu Jiu at a leisurely pace.
His long black hair was messily draped over his shoulders, and the muscles all over his body were swollen, as if stones were embedded in his body. His muscles even emitted a faint light, as if they contained some strange power that could explode at any time.
Mu Jiu, who was holding the soul-searching flag, finally showed a look of fear on his face.
He already understood that the person in front of him did not take him seriously at all. He was not even afraid of his own master!
Mu Haiji might be paying attention to this universal war, but he would not act rashly.
Starting a universal war was not a trivial matter. It had to be agreed by the entire Saint Hall, and Mu Haiji was not the only Saint who was paying attention to this universal war. There were other Saints as well. Mu Haiji could not openly violate the rules of the Saint Hall and descend his true body!
In other words, if this guy from the Temple of War attacked, no one would be able to help Mu Jiu block it!
Many True Gods had peacefully lived for a very long time. According to the calendar in the Dayan Universe, it could be tens of billions or even hundreds of billions of years. In the Divine Realm, there were even True Gods who had lived long enough and committed suicide.
But he, Mu Jiu, was a rising star. He was definitely a very "young" True God. He still had an endless lifespan to enjoy. He could experience ten thousand reincarnations. He could enjoy the worship of trillions of living beings.
Bury himself in the Dayan Universe? He had never thought that there would be such a possibility!
Due to the fear of death, Mu Jiu only had one thought left, and that was to escape. He still had one place to escape, and that was the Dayan Universe. Although the two universes were at war and he would not be safe even if he escaped to the Dayan Dayan Universe, his master was the Dayan Dayan Universe. He could set up a powerful obstacle in the Dayan Universe to protect his life!
As soon as this thought popped up in his mind, his body began to flicker with a blazing white light, and his entire body began to disappear like a human silhouette.
"Heh." The corners of Zhan Beihai's mouth curled up slightly. His arm, which was as thick as a bucket, suddenly raised and punched towards the air not far away!
"Boom!"
With a muffled sound, a certain space suddenly exploded, and Mu Jiu's figure staggered out of the space. There was a trace of blood at the corner of his mouth, and his eyes were filled with resentment!
Zhan Beihai's punch was exactly the same as when Ancestor Gragon had pulled Demon Night Clan's Heavenly Venerable Extreme Flame out of the spatial tunnel!
However, Ancestor Gragon had used his comprehension of Space to block Heavenly Venerable Extreme Flame's teleportation. Zhan Beihai had used his astonishing speed and precise perception to directly destroy Mu Jiu's teleportation. Moreover, Mu Jiu was a True God, so the teleportation he used was a hundred times more profound than that of a Heavenly Venerable Extreme Flame. The difficulty of the two could not be compared.
Seeing Mu Jiu's gaze, Zhan Beihai's eyes showed a hint of mockery. "Use the most painful method possible …" After he said this, the muscles all over his body doubled in size!
On his arms, chest, and between his legs, a dim light flashed!
"These are eight Dao Pillars …" Seeing those dim lights, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. There was more than one way to refine one's body in the God Realm. Eight Dao Pillars and nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier were only one of the more mainstream methods. Zhan Beihai used the same conjoined body method as Luo Zheng.
"Boom!"
With a muffled sound, Zhan Beihai had already disappeared from where he stood, instantly closing in on Mu Jiu.
Mu Jiu knew very well that he was on the brink of death. After he abandoned the Soul Searching Banner, his body suddenly twisted in the air, trying his best to avoid Zhan Beihai's charge. At the same time, he roared, "Senior Brother! Quickly come save me! "
In this Great Evolution Universe, only Mu Han and Mu Tian could help him, but Mu Jiu felt that his two senior brothers were probably no match for this person!
However, just as he roared, Zhan Beihai's large hand had already grabbed Mu Jiu's neck. At this moment, Zhan Beihai was already three times his usual size. In comparison, Mu Jiu was like a skinny dwarf in his hand.
"My War God Temple has a punishment called Battle Intent Dismemberment," Zhan Beihai said as he stared at Mu Jiu in his hand.
Mu Jiu had obviously heard of this punishment before. The pupils in his eyes rapidly contracted, and the fear on his face constantly magnified. "You, you dare to treat me like this … You will definitely regret it!"
Before he could finish speaking, endless battle intent surged out from Zhan Beihai's body. Every True God had their own "influence," and the "influence" that the people of the War God Temple had was battle intent.
At this moment, streaks of battle intent burst out from Zhan Beihai's body like sharp knives, pouring into Mu Jiu's mind.
The blood on Mu Jiu's face disappeared without a trace. His teeth kept chattering, and beads of sweat came out from the pores all over his body.
The most terrifying punishment was always directed at the soul. The Fighting Spirit's Death by Thousand Cuts was like that. Every time the Fighting Spirit entered Mu Jiu's mind, it would cut Mu Jiu's soul. Even if a True God's soul was attacked directly, it would produce the most intense and real pain. In just a breath, Mu Jiu's soul had been "cut" by the Fighting Spirit thousands of times.
The "cutting" of this Battle Intent Dismemberment was extremely precise. It would try its best not to cause the other party's soul to collapse, thus delaying the pain.
Just now, Luo Zheng had asked him to use the most painful method to end Mu Jiu's life. Zhan Beihai had suddenly thought of this cruel method in the War God Temple!
Thus, Luo Zheng, Xun, and Heavenly Lord Oracle saw an extremely terrifying face.
The expression on Mu Jiu's face could no longer be described as twisted. From Mu Jiu's eyes, there was a fervent desire — a desire to die as quickly as possible!
However, since it was a punishment, Zhan Beihai naturally wouldn't end his life so easily.
This painful process lasted for a full 30 breaths. After enduring tens of thousands of cuts, Mu Jiu's pupils had expanded to the limit. His entire body trembled as he gradually died …
Zhan Beihai casually threw Mu Jiu's body aside and turned to Luo Zheng. "Are you satisfied?"
Luo Zheng nodded. Although he thought that Zhan Beihai's strength was above Mu Jiu's, he didn't expect the whole process to be so easy. He had underestimated this group of people in the dungeon!
"Swoosh!"
Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he instantly teleported to the side of the Soul-Searching Banner. Xun and Heavenly Lord Oracle were still wrapped in the brown ribbons of light, hanging motionlessly on the banner.
The moment he grabbed the Soul-Searching Banner, he felt a strong repulsive force attack him. Then, as if his head was being torn apart, there was an intense pain …
Although Xun couldn't move or speak, there was a look of worry on her face.
"This Soul-Searching Banner isn't an ordinary Primordial Treasure. Although I've killed seven True God souls, there are still tens of thousands of souls enslaved within it." Zhan Beihai stepped forward and reached out to grab the Soul-Searching Banner. With a violent shake, the two ribbons of light binding Xun and Heavenly Lord Oracle instantly broke apart, and the two women instantly regained their freedom!
After Heavenly Lord Oracle was freed, the tip of his foot tapped lightly in the air. Then, he cupped his hands and bowed to Zhan Beihai. "Thank you, righteous man, for saving us!"
Zhan Beihai smiled faintly. "You should thank him. I'm only here because I'm following his orders. I don't have any thoughts of morality." There was no right or wrong in this battle of the universe. Although he had chosen to help Luo Zheng, it didn't mean that he had chosen a side. Zhan Beihai had been imprisoned by Gu Bei in the Immortal Abode for quite some time. He didn't know what the War God Temple's stance on the battle between the two Sages was. Under such circumstances, he wouldn't make a decision easily.
Then, Zhan Beihai threw the Soul-Searching Banner to Luo Zheng. "This thing is also a very powerful treasure. I've already erased the imprint on it. It'll be quite useful to you."

Chapter 1707
This Soul Searching Banner was once one of Mu Haiji's most precious treasures. However, after he became a Saint, this treasure was no longer of much use to him, so he naturally bestowed it to his favorite disciples.
After several rounds of passing, the Soul Searching Banner naturally fell into Mu Jiu's hands!
Not only could this banner capture souls, but it could also imprison them and continuously refine them, ultimately enslaving them. Apart from that, it also had the ability to amplify one's divine sense!
After Zhan Beihai erased the soul imprint on the Soul Searching Banner, Luo Zheng held it in his hand without any problems.
This was a soul-type grandmist treasure, and it was indeed quite useful to Luo Zheng. After thanking Mu Jiu, he safely placed it in his spatial ring.
At this moment, a majestic roar came from the depths of the Realm of the Gods!
"Everlasting War! How could someone from your War God Temple appear in the Great Evolution Universe! He actually killed my beloved disciple! Hurry up and get him to leave! "
The person who spoke was naturally Mu Haiji.
In Mu Haiji's eyes, the Great Evolution Universe's defeat was already a foregone conclusion.
He wasn't the only one who felt that way. The saints of the Hallowed Halls felt the same way as well. The living beings in the World of Divination were only struggling at death's door. Unless Luo Xiao could come back to life in a single night, only then would it be possible to save the World of Divination.
Just as the Saints were silently watching this universal war, another True Divinity suddenly appeared!
The moment Zhan Beihai appeared, the Saints had already figured out his origins. This fellow who had suddenly appeared actually came from the War God Temple … And the War God Temple was one of the few major powers in God's Domain. Moreover, the entire War God Temple had three Saints that had joined the Holy Hall. In terms of size, they were much stronger than Mu Haiji!
"Hahahaha," Suddenly, a hearty laugh came from the clouds. "Beihai! Beihai actually didn't die! "That voice contained a hint of ecstasy.
"Everlasting War! I want you to give me an explanation. What is the meaning of this? "Mu Haiji's voice turned cold.
"Explanation? Hehe! Mu Haiji, you changed the rules on the stone slab, so let's not talk about it. After all, the purpose of the Holy Halls was to restrict you and me. Everyone should act according to the rules! Did the men of my Zhan Clan break the rules when they entered the Great Evolution Realm? "Everlasting War sneered.
"Of course not. According to the rules on the stone slab, the Great Expansion Universe can send three True Gods," Saint Jiu said.
"Since we didn't break the rules, why should my North Sea retreat? Just because of one sentence from Mu Haiji? Just because your head is bigger than mine? Hahahaha! "Everlasting War laughed maniacally.
The members of the War God Temple had always been arrogant and unbridled. As for Everlasting War, he was the head of the War God Temple. How could he be willing to show weakness in front of Mu Hai?
In reality, the Saints had already seen the entirety of the Divine Dominion. Normally, everyone would not speak with such a thick smell of gunpowder. However, this Zhan Beihai was also very special to the War God Temple. Naturally, Everlasting War would do his best to protect him.
Mu Haiji calmed down after hearing Everlasting Zhan's retort.
It was just Zhan Beihai. His Great Evolution Realm might not necessarily lose. After all, Mu Tian was still inside …
Thus, the depths of the Divine Dominion regained its calm. However, the Saints all had their own thoughts. Some Saints sighed that Luo Xiao's life was not meant to end, while others were abnormally angry. Originally, this matter had been in the bag, but now, such a variable had suddenly appeared. This made them anxious!
…
…
At this moment, the Ancestral Gragon was already riddled with wounds. Large areas of its beautiful scales had been peeled off, and half of its tail had been broken. Even the pair of sharp sword-like horns on its head had been neatly chopped off!
However, the Ancestral Gragon displayed an extremely strong vitality. Every time he was smashed down by Mu Han, he would climb up again and again. Even if he was riddled with wounds, he did not cower in the slightest!
Mu Jiu's desperate shout did not reach here. Moreover, Mu Han did not think that there was anything in the Great Expansion Universe that could threaten Mu Jiu …
"Still not dead?"
This time, a trace of malevolence appeared on Mu Han's face. These nine true dragons and the Ancestral Gragon were incomparably tenacious. They had stubbornly tangled with him until now.
However, this Ancestral Gragon was already an arrow at the end of its flight. He was prepared to end the Ancestral Gragon's life. A Six-clawed Golden Dragon was extremely rare in the Divine Dominion, but this fellow had already grown to this extent. He could not take it as his spirit pet or contract beast. He could only kill it first and then crush those annoying true dragons later!
"Roar!"
The Ancestral Gragon, who was crawling above the Great Expansion Universe, raised his dragon head. He had almost no strength left to fly. He could only lie on the ground and spit dragon breath at Mu Han. However, the balls of demonic red dragon breath had lost their threat and dissipated before they could even get close to Mu Han …
Mu Han leaped toward the Ancestral Gragon again. This time, there was a ghost halberd in Mu Han's hand. When he waved the halberd, the ghost halberd above him emitted a dark red light. This ghost halberd was also a grandmist artifact and a treasure that was spiritually connected to Mu Han!
After easily dodging the Ancestral Gragon's weak dragon breath, Mu Han once again stood on the Ancestral Gragon's head and raised the ghost halberd high up …
"Stop!"
"Roar roar roar …"
Not far away, the two true dragons rushed over at the same time.
The nine true dragons' Heaven's Evolution Physiques had been destroyed many times and the color on their bodies had dimmed significantly. Moreover, every time their Heaven's Evolution Physiques were destroyed, their recovery speed was much slower. The other seven true dragons' Heaven's Evolution Physiques were slowly reforming. Only the Five-clawed Golden Dragon and Qinglong were still able to move!
Facing the two enormous figures, Mu Han spun the ghost halberd in his hand and shot two streams of killing power at the two true dragons. The moment the true dragons' Heaven's Evolution Physiques came into contact with the two streams of power, they were completely shredded and turned into countless droplets of Heaven's Evolution Essence that splattered everywhere. However, after the droplets of Heaven's Evolution Essence landed on the ground, they continued to gather in one place. It was just that it would take a long time for them to reform their bodies.
"Roar!"
When the Ancestral Gragon saw this, he raised his head angrily and actually pushed Mu Han up.
Mu Han stood on the Ancestral Gragon's head and steadied himself. With a cold snort, he suddenly stomped on the Ancestral Gragon's head …
"Boom!"
The Ancestral Gragon's enormous head smashed into the ground once more …
The Ancestral Gragon had finally lost all his strength. He tried his best to raise his head, but to no avail. His enormous dragon eyes were filled with sorrow, and he kept whimpering from his mouth.
Mu Han spun the ghost halberd slightly and aimed one end of the halberd at the Ancestral Gragon. Rings of red light spread from the ghost halberd as Mu Han condensed a strange power!
No matter how tenacious the Ancestral Gragon's vitality was, he probably wouldn't be able to withstand the damage from this halberd!
"Die, I really don't have the time to play with you …" Mu Han had been trapped by the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin's treasure and had been entangled by the nine true dragons and the Ancestral Gragon for a long time. He had indeed wasted a lot of time.
After saying that, Mu Han viciously stabbed the ghost halberd at the Ancestral Gragon's head!
However, just as the ghost halberd was about to pierce Mu Han, a bronze fist suddenly appeared in front of him. Just this fist alone was bigger than Mu Han's brain! How could Mu Han have the time to kill the Ancestral Gragon when this bronze fist came crashing down? He relied on his instincts to block the attack with the ghost halberd in front of him!
"Boom!"
With the first punch, Mu Han's ghost halberd was bent!
A grandmist artifact artifact was bent by a single punch! Mu Han's heart trembled.
"Boom!"
Without any delay, the second punch landed on the ghost halberd …
The ghost halberd was bent even more severely!
"Boom!"
When the third punch landed, the ghost halberd was directly smashed into pieces, and the massive fist landed on Mu Han's chest!
"Pu!"
The three punches seemed unremarkable, but they contained an unimaginable amount of power. Mu Han spat out a mouthful of blood as he flew backwards, leaving a long trail of blood behind him.
However, Mu Han did not die. He had a feeling that these three punches could have killed him, but at the last moment, the power had weakened. It was most likely deliberately suppressed!
The attacker was very strong, but it seemed like he wanted to keep him alive. Why?
Mu Han's mind spun rapidly. After coming to this conclusion, he looked up and saw a burly man with disheveled hair standing not far away. The three punches just now were given by this long-haired man.
"Alright, that guy is almost done. Go and fulfill your wish," Zhan Beihai said calmly.

Chapter 1708
Because everything had happened so suddenly, Mu Han had suffered three punches in a daze. Before he could clearly see the situation in front of him, his mind was still in a daze.
When he heard Zhan Beihai's words, he was even more stunned!
Then, a person appeared beside Zhan Beihai. It was actually Luo Zheng …
"You!" Mu Han's eyes flashed with a sharp light.
He had originally planned to clean up these Empyreans and then carefully search for this brat.
Although the universe was vast, with Mu Han's cultivation, there was no place for this brat to hide.
He had never imagined that this brat would actually take the initiative to appear and invite such a powerhouse.
Just now, he had been puzzled as to why Zhan Beihai had held back 60% of his strength when his third punch could have killed him. Now, he naturally understood. Luo Zheng had once made a great wish for him. The reason Luo Zheng didn't kill him wasn't because he was afraid of anyone, but because he wanted Luo Zheng to fulfill his wish!
Luo Zheng held the Boundless Sword and slowly walked towards Mu Han. His eyes were as deep as two ancient wells. When he arrived in front of Mu Han, Luo Zheng gently placed the Boundless Sword against Mu Han's neck.
With the support of the Great Wish Art, Luo Zheng felt his strength almost double. This was the uniqueness of the Great Wish Art. However, its weakness was obvious. If Luo Zheng didn't kill Mu Han with his own hands, his cultivation would forever stagnate and there would be no possibility of him advancing in this lifetime.
Thus, in the Realm of the Gods, many martial artists knew the Great Wish Art. However, they were very cautious when making a wish …
"I still have a great wish. Killing one person in front of me is enough to appease the resentment in my heart and extinguish the anger in my heart," Luo Zheng softly muttered. Then, he suddenly raised the Boundless Sword.
At this moment, Mu Han no longer had the strength to struggle. Zhan Beihai's punch seemed ordinary, but the War God Temple's secret technique was extremely domineering and powerful. The surging battle intent directly extinguished the "force" in Mu Han's body. At the same time, it poured into Mu Han's internal organs through his meridians. After flowing through his internal organs, it rushed into his dantian's internal world!
Saints used the "object of heaven" to cover the Heavenly Dao, while True Gods used the Godhead to cover their internal world. Once the Godhead was born, it couldn't be destroyed. However, after the battle intent poured into Mu Han's internal world, it completely enveloped the Godhead. This was equivalent to sealing Mu Han's "force".
At this moment, Mu Han knew that he was definitely going to die. However, the smile on his face grew wider and wider. "Do you really think that you can save Huan Yu? Do you still think you can save your father? "
.............. Luo Zheng.......................... Luo Zheng.................... com...........
"Then why are you struggling?" Mu Han asked again..
Luo Zheng frowned. He was actually thinking about this question …
Zhan Beihai, Xun, Heavenly Sovereign Shen Yu, and the others saw that Luo Zheng was actually hesitating. Their faces were filled with strange expressions. The Ancestral Dragon whimpered softly. There wasn't a single part of its huge body that was intact. Even its huge dragon eyes had lost their luster. However, when it saw Luo Zheng, its originally dim dragon eyes shone brightly. Although the Ancestral Dragon was no longer affected by the imprint after reaching adulthood, it still had a natural affinity with Luo Zheng.
Ten breaths …
Twenty breaths …
Thirty breaths …
The Heavenly Dao shook violently again. Another Heavenly Sovereign had fallen.
Luo Zheng was still holding the Great Thousand Sword up high. He maintained this stiff posture and stared blankly at Mu Han. There was an indescribable sense of desolation on his face.
Mu Han's question was too broad. What was the reason for this?
From the beginning, Luo Zheng had been constantly struggling and struggling in all kinds of predicaments for his sister. Now, he understood that every predicament and crisis was personally arranged by his father …
Why was he moving forward? He hadn't thought about this question for a long time.
The purpose of Mu Han's question was to disturb Luo Zheng's mind and stall for some time. If Mu Tian had appeared here, he would still have a chance to survive. He naturally wouldn't give up. Seeing that his question had caused Luo Zheng to stand there in a daze, he felt extremely lucky.
In fact, Mu Han had helped Luo Zheng ask a very crucial question. That was the meaning of living …
"Boom, boom …"
Booming sounds came from afar. Those were Heavenly Sovereigns fighting. Huge balls of light exploded. Even without using the Roaring Token, the fluctuations of energy, the sounds of explosions, and the light produced could travel very far.
Dozens of great worlds were torn apart, and countless living beings perished.
Among these fallen creatures, there were hundreds and thousands of mortal kingdoms, emperors who had just ascended to the throne, women who had just given birth, babies who had yet to open their eyes to take a look at this world, and scholars who had just participated in the imperial examinations and were filled with longing for the outside world.
Many warriors were paying attention to this war in the universe. However, even more mortals were immersed in their own lives apart from sighing at the strange phenomena of the immortals and gods.
They didn't know whether the great worlds they lived in could be torn apart or not. Even if they knew, it would be useless. Mortals could spend a hundred lifetimes and still find it difficult to leave a great world. They were like ants on a large ship. Even if they knew that the ship would sink, they didn't have the ability to escape to another ship.
What was the meaning of their lives?
As second-class creatures at the lowest level, these mortals also pursued beautiful things.
More beautiful women, more pleasant songs, higher status, more respect, more gold, silver, and jewels. For many people, this was the meaning.
Then what was the meaning of his life?
Compared to these mortals, he might be much stronger. He could easily control the fate of thousands or even tens of thousands of mortal kingdoms …
However, Luo Zheng was also a second-class creature in the universe. Compared to the sages of the divine realm, what was the difference between him and these mortals?
Then what about the gods and sages of the divine realm …
Compared to the higher-class creatures, would the true gods be like mortals and the sages be as insignificant as the emperors of mortals?
Thinking was unlimited, and so was the world …
"Unlimited …"
Luo Zheng had a hint of understanding.
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, Luo Zheng's mouth suddenly curved into a smile. His originally slightly perplexed expression suddenly became firm.
Seeing Luo Zheng's gaze, Mu Han's heart sank slightly.
"It's for my Dao …" Luo Zheng said with a smile. The Great Thousand Heavy Sword in his hand moved with the wind.
"Your Dao, what Dao is it?" Mu Han asked again.
However, Luo Zheng shook his head and smiled mysteriously at Mu Han. "My Dao is too far, too far. You … don't have the qualifications to know!"
After saying this, he waved the Great Thousand Heavy Sword. A majestic power poured out. Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with killing intent. God Yi suddenly slashed out …
"Pu!"
Blood splattered for five steps. The sword intent produced by the God Yi Sword pierced through Mu Han's body and spread along the surface of this large world.
This large world was called "Autumn Mountain World." There were many mountain peaks in this large world. There was almost no plain. As Luo Zheng slashed out with his sword, the plains within a radius of a thousand li were all cut off, forming a large plain with a radius of a thousand li.
In the future, this sword strike of Luo Zheng's was vividly recorded down by some people. Many martial artists and even mortals knew that in the past, Luo Zheng had killed a True Divinity here. It was said that the blood of that True Divinity had seeped into this plain. Whoever could find the blood-soaked stone would be able to obtain the inheritance of that True Divinity. This attracted countless martial artists to come here and dig, causing the entire plain to be riddled with holes.
And this plain was called "God Execution Plain" by later generations.

Just now, Luo Zheng had been in a daze for a long time. His behavior was extremely abnormal. She was afraid that Luo Zheng had gone mad!

Chapter 1709
Just as Luo Zheng looked up at the sky, another massive star exploded in the depths of space.
The moment this star exploded, it released a blazing light that covered the stars in the sky. When everyone looked up, they could only see a vast expanse of whiteness. If one stared at it for too long, they wouldn't be able to see anything.

And in the Dreamsound Realm, the other two Empyreans were also fiercely fighting. They were the old Patriarch and the Grand Empyrean.

At this moment, lotus flowers were constantly blooming in the Dreamsound Realm. On the walls, mountain peaks, plains, city walls, and even in the furnaces of mortals …
The entire Dreamsound Realm was like a sea of lotus flowers …

Although the old Patriarch held the absolute advantage against the Grand Empyrean, it was impossible for him to defeat the Grand Empyrean in a short period of time. After all, as long as the Grand Empyrean's' momentum 'wasn't extinguished, it was impossible for the Grand Empyrean to be defeated!
As the two sides continued to clash, the entire Dreamsound Realm was a mess.

The old Patriarch's complexion sank as he saw this. "Lord Star Tail …"

In this war between universes, the battle between Empyreans had already lost its meaning. The most important thing was the contest between the True Divinities. Since Star Tail had been defeated, the Sacred Race had already won!

Under his summons, the saint Empyreans that were still fighting stopped. Before long, a demonic red space crack appeared behind the saint Great Empyrean and a total of 13 saint Empyreans walked out.
The saint race Grand Empyrean had been in high spirits, but when he discovered that the saint race only had 13 Empyreans left, his expression sank once more …
In this war with the Great Extremity Cosmos, over 90% of their Empyreans had perished. The saints had also paid a considerable price!
On the old Patriarch's side, the power of Space Laws also flashed. The Empyreans in the Great Expansion Universe also followed. Before long, over 30 Empyreans had gathered here, and Empyrean Original Sin and the others were also present.

From the beginning, Mu Tian and Startail exchanged blows. Mu Tian had the absolute advantage, but Mu Tian had still underestimated his opponent.
Although he could suppress Startail in every aspect, Mu Tian's mentality couldn't compare to Startail's. As Luo Xiao's favorite and youngest disciple, Startail had long been prepared to sacrifice himself. Therefore, every attack he made was a method that would cause both sides to suffer.
Because of this, Mu Tian found it very troublesome …
Later on, Startail directly lured Mu Tian into a star maze that he had already set up. Then, he detonated his inner world and launched a desperate attack, the 'astral tribulation waves'.

"Ka!"
Mu Tian casually grabbed the ball of light and it fell into his hand.

Chapter 1710
Xing Wei hadn't completely fallen.
The soul of a True God was exceptionally tough. Even if he was left with only his head, he could still find a way to rebuild his body.
However, this was a long process. Rebuilding his body and dantian was equivalent to starting from scratch.
Xing Wei's head suddenly opened his eyes and looked at the old patriarch and the Celestial Venerables on the other side of the river. His eyes, which had been as bright as the stars, were now dim and lifeless.
In order to fight against the Sacred Clan, he had led the Celestial Clan in planning for countless years. In the end, he had welcomed such an outcome.

Xing Wei's originally dim eyes suddenly shone with exquisite starlight. Although he didn't know the origin of the long-haired man, there was no doubt that this man was invited by Luo Zheng and was on his side!
All of the Empyreans in the Grand Yan Universe were stunned.
