Many Empyreans wanted to rush out and snatch Startail's head when he was thrown high up in the air.
But even if they snatched Startail's head back, what use would it be?
Would it be able to change the situation at all?
If the Grand Yan Universe collapsed, everyone in the universe, from an ant in the lower realm to countless Empyreans in the upper realm, would die. There would be no exceptions. The only difference was who would die first and who would die later. Everything would lose its meaning …
Some Empyreans even planned to leave. Since there was no point in resisting, they might as well make use of the last bit of time to do something meaningful with their clansmen. At least they would be able to peacefully spend the last bit of time.
No one expected Luo Zheng to appear at this moment.
When they saw Luo Zheng and Celestial Venerable Shen Yu, a glimmer of hope rose in their hearts. After all, Luo Zheng had created many miracles.
But no matter how many miracles he had created, he was still just a Realm Lord Dao disciple. His influence on the entire war wasn't that great …
However, from the spatial crack, a burly long-haired man walked out!
"A True God!"
"And a very powerful True God!"
"Is this the helper Luo Zheng invited?"
The moment the Empyreans saw the long-haired man, they could distinguish the unique aura of a True God from Zhan Beihai's body. Furthermore, the battle intent emanating from Zhan Beihai's body made them feel his strength.
After Zhan Beihai appeared, he didn't take any further action. He was waiting for Luo Zheng's orders.
He still had a calm smile on his face. His muscles were faintly glowing with azure light. That was the embodiment of his intense battle intent!
Mu Tian's smile had already stiffened on his face …
The group of Sacred Clan Empyreans behind Mu Tian also stiffened. Even their thoughts came to a standstill.
They didn't expect such an unforeseen event to occur at this moment!
"Who are you? This is a dispute between two Sages. If you get involved … "Mu Tian said coldly. His words were exactly the same as Mu Jiu and Mu Han's. He wanted to use Mu Haiji's name to threaten Zhan Beihai.
"If I get involved, Mu Haiji won't let me go, right? I've already heard it three times in a day. Can't you change the way you say it? "Zhan Beihai yawned and smiled faintly.
"Three times …" A sharp light flashed in Mu Tian's eyes. "Mu Jiu, Mu Tian, what happened to them?"
"What do you think?" Zhan Beihai asked back.
Before this, Mu Tian had never worried about Mu Jiu and Mu Han's safety. In addition, he was focused on fighting Star Tail. How would he know that the two of them had already fallen?
Now, Mu Tian released his divine sense to search for the two of them in the universe. However, how could he still see them?
Although Mu Tian's divine sense couldn't cover the entire universe, he couldn't rule out the possibility that the two of them were too far away for him to sense them. However, he knew that his two junior brothers were most likely dead!
"How dare you!" Mu Tian roared, and clouds of black fog escaped from his body. Although he, Mu Han, Mu Jiu, and Mu Tian often had conflicts, they had cultivated together in the Great Extreme Universe and had a deep relationship with each other. Who knew that the two of them would fall in the blink of an eye? One could imagine Mu Tian's current mood.
Looking at the angry Mu Tian, Zhan Beihai rolled his eyes. He turned to Luo Zheng and asked, "This guy is a little noisy. Can I kill him now?"
"…"

Luo Zheng had already flown back to the top of the cliff and handed Star Tail's head to the old patriarch. Then, he turned his head and said faintly, "Do it …"

"Boom …"
The two True Gods began to fight each other …
In the battle between Mu Tian and Star Tail, more than half of his True Essence had been consumed. Moreover, Star Tail's "Astral Calamity Wave" had caused him to be seriously injured!
Even if Mu Tian was in peak condition, he wouldn't be a match for Zhan Beihai. What more now?
Therefore, from the start of the battle, Mu Tian was retreating step by step. In the face of Zhan Beihai's attacks that could tear apart heaven and earth, he had almost no power to fight back!
As for Luo Zheng, he didn't even bother to look back …
The Heavenly Venerables of the Sacred Race and the Heavenly Venerables of the Great Extreme Universe were worried and hopeful. The Heavenly Venerables of the Great Extreme Universe were worried that Zhan Beihai might be defeated. Who knew what other trump cards Mu Tian had? On the other hand, the Heavenly Venerables of the Sacred Race hoped that Mu Tian could receive Mu Haiji's protection and turn the tables at the crucial moment.
However, Luo Zheng was very clear that even if Mu Tian really defeated Zhan Beihai, so what?
He still had that mysterious blue-clothed lady beside him …
Although Luo Zheng couldn't see through the true strength of these True Gods, the Extreme Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster were definitely stronger than Zhan Beihai. As for the blue-clothed lady, her strength was far above that of the Extreme Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster.

Chapter 1711
"Bang!"
There was a dull roar.
Mu Tian was blasted into the thick plates of the Great Realm by Zhan Beihai's punch. The Great Realm, which was originally broken and slowly collapsing, now had another crack …

Luo Zheng did not watch the battle. Instead, he walked towards the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin and handed him a spatial ring …
The Heavenly Lord of Original Sin's eyes flashed. He stared at Luo Zheng with a trace of gratitude.
He had already taken the ring and clasped it in his hand. He nodded slightly at Luo Zheng. The two of them looked to the other side at the same time. Behind this team, the Heavenly Lord of Resurrection was standing there.
The Heavenly Lord of Resurrection's Mandate of Rebirth was very important and very special. Therefore, he did not take the initiative to participate in the battle. At the critical moment, he could revive the fallen Heavenly Lords.
Since the start of the battle, he had saved the lives of seven Heavenly Lords.
In the spatial ring that Luo Zheng gave to the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin, Maha's spiritual pets, the Holy Moon Wolves, were stored. These Holy Moon Wolves had already been killed by Luo Zheng. Naturally, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpse was mixed in with the fragments of the Holy Moon Wolves' corpse.
At that time, Luo Zheng was also making a desperate effort. He first stored the fragments in the spatial ring. As for whether the Heavenly Lord of Resurrection could save Xuanyuan Chenfeng, it still depended on luck.
Besides Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Luo Zheng also used another spatial ring to store Xi Youqin's corpse. Compared to Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Xi Youqin's corpse was very well preserved. In the past, with the power of the Mandate of Heaven, even an Empyrean could easily revive. Reviving Xi Youqin shouldn't be too much of a problem. However, Luo Zheng was still somewhat worried.
As the two True Divinities engaged in a fierce battle, the saint Empyreans had no plans to intervene. Even if they did, they had no chance of winning. The Great Expansion Universe still had the old Patriarch, Empyrean Original Sin, and Empyrean Oracle. Moreover, the middle and lower Empyreans had the numerical advantage. By the time the True Divinities finished their battle, the end of this war would have already been decided!
Both Empyreans were well aware of this. Thus, both sides welcomed a brief moment of peace …
Therefore, Luo Zheng took advantage of this time, hoping that Resurrection Heavenly Lord would resurrect Xi Youqin and the others.
Luo Zheng took out Xi Youqin's corpse, held it horizontally in his arms, and carefully presented it to Resurrection Heavenly Lord.
Xi Youqin's eyes were tightly closed. She was originally a submissive woman, but now her expression was serene, as if she had fallen asleep.
Thinking of her chattering non-stop, Luo Zheng gently held her hand, feeling a trace of pity in his heart. Then, he made a 'please' gesture to Resurrection Heavenly Lord and gently placed Xi Youqin on the ground.
Resurrection Heavenly Lord nodded, then reached out and gently pressed his forehead. From the top of his head, a ring of light slowly rose up. This was the Mandate of Heaven.
The wheel of light drifted and circled around Xi Youqin's corpse a few times. After some investigation, the Resurrection Heavenly Sovereign quickly replied, "There is no problem in resurrecting her. In the past, the Heavenly Mandate of Life can reactivate her life force, but it will take some time."
Hearing Resurrection Heavenly Lord's answer, Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief. Since victory was in his hands, there was certainly plenty of time …
Beside him, Original Sin Heavenly Venerate also poured out the corpses from his spatial ring. The Holy Moon Ash Wolves' corpses fell to the ground, mixing with each other. The stench of blood immediately spread out. One could even see some golden blood mixed in. This should be Xuanyuan Chenfeng's golden blood.
Seeing this, the surrounding Empyreans frowned slightly.
Although Resurrection Heavenly Lord's Mandate of Heaven was very powerful, it still had its limits. For someone like Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who had been torn into pieces and swallowed into his stomach, it was unknown whether there was any possibility of revival.
Resurrection Heavenly Lord's face showed hesitation. Naturally, he didn't have much confidence.
Original Sin Heavenly Lord's face was full of anxiety. When had this upper level Empyrean, who led Gods Without Thought, ever been so anxious? Even in the face of an undefeatable True God's disciple, he could at least maintain his composure. But when it came to Xuanyuan Chenfeng's life, he couldn't remain calm.
"I can only do my best," Resurrection Heavenly Lord sized up the corpses, then gently waved his hand. The light wheel fluttered again, hovering above the corpses …
Under the continuous fluttering of the light wheel, the corpses seemed to have a life of their own. They constantly wriggled, looking for their original positions, including the Holy Moon Ash Wolves' corpses.
The Mandate of Heaven should belong to the Law of Life, but this kind of magical ability could only be used on second-level life forms. This was because the mysterious ability of "revival" didn't exist in the Realm of the Gods. Compared to the Realm of the Gods, the universe was too small. Only Saints could bestow this kind of ability to the Inner World. Therefore, Resurrection Heavenly Lord's Mandate of Heaven had no effect on Startail.
The corpses that had been minced by Luo Zheng wriggled and gradually pieced together. The gentle life force emitted by the Mandate of Heaven also endowed the corpses with vitality …
When Luo Zheng had killed these Holy Moon Ash Wolves, in order to avoid missing anything, he had meticulously placed all the fragments into his spatial ring. After a while, the several Holy Moon Ash Wolves were actually pieced back together, but there were visible cracks in them.
As for Xuanyuan Chenfeng, although he was also pieced together, after all, he had passed through the Holy Moon Ash Wolves' stomachs. Only the shape of a person could be seen …
Original Sin Heavenly Lord's face was gloomy. He tried his best to control his emotions. Anyone who saw such a pile of corpses would feel uncomfortable, let alone the most important great-grandson of Original Sin Heavenly Lord.
The Resurrection Heavenly Lord waved his hand again, and a round plate above his head began to emit a pale green light of life. The Resurrection Heavenly Lord did not simply use the Law of Life, because the Resurrection Heavenly Lord did not simply heal them. After all, they were already dead. The light also seemed to contain the Law of Time.
As the light continued to spread, it finally formed a pale green light tower that enveloped Resurrection Heavenly Lord and the corpses of the two True Gods. Bystanders could no longer see anything.
With the consciousness of the Heavenly Lords, they could naturally investigate what was happening in the light tower. However, the Heavenly Lords present knew that it was not appropriate to disturb them. Most of the Heavenly Lords were concerned about the outcome of the battle between the two True Gods.
Compared to Mu Jiu and Mu Han, Mu Tian's strength was indeed much stronger. He had actually managed to hold on for so long despite being seriously injured.
However, he was ultimately an arrow at the end of its flight. The Heavenly Lords understood this, Zhan Beihai understood this, and Mu Tian himself understood this even more!
When Mu Tian was forced to his limit, the two lines of black tears in his eyes finally stopped flowing. All the black fog that had dissipated outside was also absorbed into his eyes …
"You've given up?" Zhan Beihai asked calmly.
Mu Tian's self-esteem was extremely strong. His talent and willpower were excellent, but he had never been forced into such a sorry state before. Moreover … this was a battle that he had to win!
At this moment, he stared at Zhan Beihai with a hint of madness on his face. "I, Mu Tian, have never known what giving up is in my entire life!"
After saying this, Mu Tian suddenly slapped his own head. A strange dharma image appeared above his head. The dharma image was formed by lines of gold, and it quickly outlined a strange humanoid creature!
The moment this humanoid creature appeared, it actually grabbed Mu Tian and stuffed him into its mouth.
Seeing this, Zhan Beihai, who was in front of Rao Yong, suddenly changed his expression and actually retreated quickly!

Chapter 1712
"It really is the Spirit Absorbing Evil Dharma Idol …"

Because this "Spirit Absorbing" originally used pain as energy, the stronger the power of the Spirit Absorbing, the more pain the soul would suffer!

At this moment, all of the creatures in his inner world could sense the crisis of the entire world. The True God had taken control of the world with his Godhood. Under the constant prayers of the creatures, every single one of them was emitting fighting spirit. Countless rivers were also converging, finally forming a huge river …
Countless rivers eventually turned into an ocean and fused into Zhan Beihai's Godhead!
This "Fighting Spirit" was actually the same as the power of faith that Xun possessed. The True God needed to absorb the power of faith of the creatures in order to unleash his strongest power.
However, after reaching the level of a Saint, one could use the item of heaven to cover the Heavenly Dao and get rid of the reliance on the power of faith. He had finally killed Xing Wei with great difficulty and was about to help his master win this universal war. Chapter 1714

Chapter 1715
If the saint Grand Empyrean's mood was like this, then the other saint Empyreans could be imagined.
The dozen plus saint Empyreans stood where they were like blocks of wood, their minds already in chaos.
The Great Development Lotus Flower spun as the old Patriarch stood atop it and floated towards the saint Grand Empyreans. His eyes flashed with a sharp light as he stared at the Grand Empyreans and lightly said, "Do you want to commit suicide here or do you want to make a futile struggle?"
The Grand Empyreans looked up at the old Patriarch with a complex expression. As for the Qiong Ji Tree behind him, it had already dimmed. As a saint Grand Empyrean, the most correct course of action was for everyone to flee back to the Great Development Universe, but could they escape?
But at this moment …
A strange sound echoed through the air.
Pop, pop, pop …
This sound was like a woodpecker knocking on a hollow tree.
This sound came from all directions, omnipresent. Everyone present could clearly hear it, but they couldn't find the source of the sound!
Pop, pop, pop, pop, pop …
The sound continued …
Soon, Luo Zheng and Xun both looked up at the same time.
The other Empyreans also looked up …
This voice actually came from the deep space of the Great Evolution Universe.
The height of the Great Development Universe was limited.
High up in the sky, the stars slowly revolved, and above the stars was the void. But at the very top of the void was the dome of the Great Development Universe. And this dome was also sealed off from the outside world by the Wall of Sighs.
From the lowest point of the universe, which was the deepest part of the boundless universe, to the highest point of the universe, which was the dome, was 180 trillion miles.
This sound was transmitted from such a high distance …
It wasn't just the Empyreans that heard this sound, but nearly every living being in the Great Development Universe!
In the 100,000 great worlds, countless mortals looked up at the deep space, a trace of fear on their faces.
Although this sound wasn't anything strange, it shook the entire universe!
It was like the crew of a large ship suddenly hearing a strange sound coming from the hull. They would naturally be deeply worried.
Pop, pop, pop …
"What the hell is that sound?"
"Is someone using the Roaring Command to play a prank?"
"We don't hear much of the Roaring Command, it's completely different from this sound!"
The Roaring Command relied on the laws of karma to force its way into the ears of every living being. Even if they covered their ears, they couldn't resist this sound.
But at this moment, this sound was transmitted from the dome of the universe …
Empyrean Oracle and Luo Zheng looked at each other in dismay, not knowing what was happening.

The Wall of Sighs, which was over a thousand miles thick and extremely sturdy, actually cracked open!
Ka Ka Ka Ka …
The sound of the Wall of Sighs exploding spread throughout the universe. It was like the sound of a gigantic ship breaking. It made people's hearts palpitate.
As the sound continued, the crack in the dome continued to extend for millions of miles.
The crack reflected the bright light of the Wall of Sighs. In the eyes of the countless mortals in the great world, a strange star seemed to have appeared in the sky. This star was far larger than an ordinary star. It was like a huge spider lying in the sky ….
Along with a loud "Boom" sound.
The entire Great Evolution Universe shook. A voice came from the crack in the dome of the Great Evolution Universe. The voice was solemn and dignified, as if it could command all living beings and determine life and death!
"No matter who it is, die for me!"
"Huala …"
Then, a gentle light flashed out from the crack in the dome.
It was a cross-shaped sword light!
This cross-shaped sword light seemed to have the power to destroy the world. From top to bottom, it slashed down at Luo Zheng!
Along the way, the sword light collided with many stars …
However, as long as the sword light came into contact with the stars, they would all be destroyed in an instant. There were no exceptions!
"This is …"
Seeing that sword light, the old patriarch sucked in a deep breath of cold air.
"This is troublesome. A Saint has made a move," Zhan Beihai said with a frown. He had a new guess about Luo Zheng's identity.
Once the Universal War began, it was almost impossible for the Saints of the two Universes to interfere. The stone slab that recorded the rules was not for show. Once the rules were violated, even the Saints would be punished, and they would also have to pay a terrifying price.
Zhan Beihai had heard a little about the enmity between the two Saints, Mu Haiji and Luo Zheng. Even if these two Saints were life and death enemies, Mu Haiji did not have to be so anxious. He was willing to accept the punishment of the slate and kill Luo Zheng?
This was probably a business deal that made him suffer a great loss …
Mu Haiji was willing to accept the punishment of the slate to kill Luo Zheng. There was probably something else going on.
"Is it really a Saint?"
"Let's run!"

The Heavenly Venerables present were also scared silly by this scene.
At the beginning of the Great Universe War, the Heavenly Venerables thought that they were the protagonists of the Great Universe War. But not long after, they realized that it was the disciples of the Saints who would decide the outcome of the war. In other words, the True Gods.
Finally, they realized that it was Luo Zheng who would decide the outcome of the war …

Plop, plop, plop …
The Heavenly Venerables of the Sacred Race all knelt down. This was the God of Creation of the Great Universe and the spiritual home of all the Sacred Race. At this moment, they realized that Mu Haiji had not abandoned them.
He had personally made a move!
With a Saint personally making a move, did the Great Universe War still have a chance of winning?
Rumble, rumble …
As the cross-shaped sword light continued to descend, it touched more and more stars along the way, and naturally, more and more stars were shattered by the sword light. Rumbling sounds constantly reverberated throughout the universe.
Luo Zheng looked up at the sky without moving. The cross-shaped sword light was reflected in his pitch-black eyes. He knew that he could not dodge this sword light. How could a Saint's sword be so easy to dodge? He then said, "Xun, leave this place …"
When Xun heard Luo Zheng's words, she suddenly grabbed Luo Zheng's hand and shook her head. "I won't leave!"
"There is no meaning in dying here with me." Luo Zheng said resolutely.
His father had calculated many things and left plenty of escape routes for Luo Zheng, but he never thought that Mu Haiji would personally make a move.
But then again, even if Luo Xiao had calculated that Mu Haiji would make a move, there was nothing he could do. After all, Luo Xiao's current state was no different from that of a dead man. It was impossible for him to fight against another Saint!
"Alas, I didn't want to reveal my true body here, but it seems that there is no other way …"
A faint voice suddenly sounded in the ears of the Celestial Venerables. That voice contained a mysterious quality. Just hearing it once would leave an indelible impression!

Luo Zheng stared at the blue-clothed woman, smiled, and answered honestly, "Yes!"

Chapter 1716
The moment the blue-clothed woman appeared, the dense fog in the depths of the Divine Realm exploded once again …
These Saints were the strongest existences in the Divine Realm. It was rare for them to be surprised even once in hundreds or thousands of years. At this moment, all the Saints left in the Sacred Hall were dumbfounded. Their voices were filled with endless astonishment, as if this woman's appearance in the Grand Yan Universe was something inconceivable.
"This woman … isn't she Shi Xiaoqiao!"
"This is really terrible! Shi Xiaoqiao is still alive! "
"How could she appear in the Grand Yan Universe? Didn't she disappear after she commanded the Soul Wilderness? That infatuated Canon Sage even stepped into the depths of the Soul Wilderness to search for her. Not only did he find nothing, but after he came out of the Soul Wilderness, his vitality was greatly damaged … "
This blue-clothed woman's true name was Shi Xiaoqiao. This woman had extremely legendary stories circulating in the Divine Realm.
She was also the only True God to step into the depths of the Soul Wilderness and control the entire Soul Wilderness. Commanding the Soul Wilderness was something unimaginable even for Saints before her, but she did it!
Therefore, it was not an exaggeration to call Shi Xiaoqiao the Divine Realm's number one miraculous woman.
This woman had once attracted the pursuit of a Saint. That Saint was called "Monarch Clipper Canon", which was the "Canon Sage" in their mouths.
However, Shi Xiaoqiao had hidden in the Soul Wilderness. Even Saints found it difficult to easily enter that place. The Canon Sage had searched for her several times, but to no avail. He even encountered no small amount of trouble in the Soul Wilderness, causing his vitality to be greatly damaged. He returned to the Sacred Hall to recuperate for two divine epochs before he recovered. There were some forbidden areas in the Divine Realm that even Saints could not enter!
In addition to the Monarch Clipper Canon, several other Saints were also searching for this woman. She was involved in a very important secret of the Soul Wilderness.
However, several divine epochs ago, Shi Xiaoqiao suddenly disappeared from the Divine Realm. Even Saints were unable to find her.
There were rumors that Shi Xiaoqiao had already escaped from the Divine Realm and stepped into the endless chaos outside the realm. There were also rumors that Shi Xiaoqiao's corpse had already disintegrated and fallen.
These rumors circulated for several years and finally sank down after a long time.
However, when True Gods roamed the Soul Wilderness, they would occasionally mention the woman who controlled the Soul Wilderness. After all, before her, even Saints were unable to control the Soul Wilderness.
Shi Xiaoqiao was gradually forgotten by history …
However, the memories of the Saints were surprisingly good. They would not forget any details of the past!
Therefore, when Shi Xiaoqiao appeared, all the Saints in the Sacred Halls were quite puzzled. Where did this woman come from? Could it be that she had always been hiding in the Great Yan Universe?
Outside the dome of the Great Evolution Space, a tall middle-aged man stood there.
This middle-aged man wore a golden battle robe and held the Truth Sword in his hand. His eyes exuded a gentle light that was very similar to the light from the tree, only purer and denser. This was Supreme Saint, Mu Haiji!
When he used the Truth Sword to slash out, his anger had already calmed down …
He had gone through so many twists and turns to break through the Wall of Sighs, sent out so many saint Empyreans, consumed so much 'potential', and even consumed countless resources to change the rules of the stone slab and send in three disciples!
In the end, it was better for him to do it himself …
This sword strike was enough to extinguish Luo Xiao's hopes.
However, he would have to pay a huge price!
Even a Saint would not dare to violate the rules of the stone slab. Next, he would suffer the punishment of violating the rules, the punishment from the stone slab!
But it was still worth it!
Luo Xiao was like a centipede. Even if he died, he wouldn't fall down. Mu Haiji was still afraid that Luo Xiao would strike back. If he cut off the most important link, then all of Luo Xiao's plans would lose their center of gravity. Only then could he rest easy!
Crackle …
Deep within the Sacred Halls, at the edge of the Sea of Time, above a shallow beach, there was a 10,000 foot high stone slab.
The stone slab was engraved with tiny characters. Some of them were ordinary characters, and some of them were golden Sanskrit characters …
This stone slab recorded all of the rules of the Divine Realm, large and small!
These rules were different from those on the Dao tablets. The rules on the stone slab were like the laws of the entire Divine Realm. They could not be disobeyed, they could not be violated. Not even a Saint could do so!
However, to a certain extent, a Saint could draw up and change these rules. Even so, they still had to pay a considerable price.
For instance, Mu Haiji had changed the rules of the Great Universe War. From sending one True Divinity to three True Divinities!
When Mu Haiji shattered the Great Universe's Wall of Sighs and slashed out with his sword, a deep blue bolt of lightning appeared on the stone slab …
This bolt of lightning seemed ordinary. Any martial artist that had comprehended the first level of the Thunder Laws could use it. But, this bolt of lightning that escaped from the stone slab was something that even a Saint would fear!
Crackle crackle crackle …
The deep blue bolt of lightning instantly surged out, forming a chain of lightning that shot out from the Sea of Time at an extremely fast speed. It passed through the Sacred Halls as well as the floating islands of the various Saint families, straight towards Mu Haiji!
"Swish …"
In an instant, Mu Haiji was bound by this chain of lightning.
Mu Haiji didn't resist. He also knew that it was impossible for him to disobey the punishment of this stone slab. When he shattered the Great Universe's dome, he was already prepared to accept his punishment. But as he was bound, that blue-clothed woman suddenly appeared in the Great Universe, appearing within his field of vision …
"Shi … Shi Xiaoqiao?"
Mu Haiji's eyes widened. There was actually a trace of panic in the depths of his eyes. His mind was currently blank. He hadn't expected this at all.
Then, he saw Shi Xiaoqiao spread out her arms. Flowers bloomed and danced around her, forming a huge array that spanned thousands of miles …
Was she trying to dissolve his sword?
"No, no! Mu Xiaoqiao! We can exchange, we can talk. You can't stop me … "
Mu Haiji's roar spread out from the top of the Great Universe.
He wanted to struggle free from the lightning chains, wanted to charge into the Great Expansion Universe, but the lightning that shot out from the slate possessed absolute power in the Divine Domain. Even if he was a Saint, there was no way he could break free from it at all …
"No!"
"No …"
Amidst his continuous roars, he was finally dragged away by the chain of lightning …
But his voice still echoed in the Great Universe. This Saint's final roar caused the hearts of all the secondary lifeforms in the Great Universe to palpitate incessantly …
At this moment …
There was a mysterious smile on Shi Xiaoqiao's face. She looked at the dome of the Great Universe and said indifferently, "Apologies, Supreme Saint. I actually didn't mean to make things difficult for you. I was also enslaved by this Luo Zheng and was under his control …"
Of course, Mu Haiji couldn't hear her words. If he could, he would probably explode from anger.
Then, a sea of flowers appeared around her.
A pair of hands formed an orchid shape, and the flower petals began to flutter in the air like snow. They formed a petal-like tornado that met Mu Haiji's slash head on!
The might of Mu Haiji's sword was extremely terrifying. Anything that came into contact with this sword light would be instantly destroyed.
The same went for the beautiful petals. The moment the soft petals came into contact with the sword strike, they disappeared into nothingness …
But the moment the petals disappeared, they were able to leave behind a coin-sized dark spot on the sword strike!
Every petal was able to leave behind a dark spot. With so many petals rushing towards the sword strike like a rain of petals. The sword strike destroyed the petals, but the petals also destroyed the sword strike at the same time!
When the two sides collided, countless petals dissipated. The huge sword strike was like a block of ice in summer, rapidly shrinking!
"Huff huff huff …"
The sword strike's momentum was extremely shocking. When it was a million kilometers away from Luo Zheng, only half of it was left.
When it was three hundred thousand kilometers away from Luo Zheng, the majority of it was destroyed by the petals. Only a third of it was left …
When it was a hundred thousand kilometers away, the sword strike was only a tenth of what it was before …
When the sword strike landed in front of Luo Zheng, the petals had already wrapped around Luo Zheng. At this moment, only a billionth of the sword strike was left. It was only the size of a finger, and its power was negligible!

Chapter 1717
Although the power of this several-inch-long sword ray was minuscule, it was still exceptionally sharp when used by the Truth Sword and a Saint.
It actually cut open the space between Luo Zheng's eyebrows, leaving a three inch long wound on his forehead!
"Chi!"
Golden blood gushed out from Luo Zheng's forehead …
The three-inch-long sword ray was about to cut open Luo Zheng's forehead and cut into Luo Zheng's brain.
Shi Qiaoqiao reached out and gently waved her hand. A jade light flashed in her hand and intercepted the sword ray. Then, she waved her hand around the sword ray. Specks of jade light flowed out from her nimble fingers and finally formed a jade-colored ball of light that trapped the three-inch-long sword ray inside!
However, Xun's expression didn't look too good. "You clearly could have eliminated this sword ray. Why did you leave it behind and injure Luo Zheng?"
Although Luo Zheng was injured, this wound didn't affect him at all. Xun only felt a bit of heartache.
Shi Qiaoqiao glanced indifferently at Xun, then turned to Luo Zheng and said, "You're also a sword cultivator. The Truth Sword Divine Dao that Mu Haiji cultivates contains a supreme sword intent. It's extremely powerful. This sword strike on your forehead will allow you to comprehend the true meaning of his Sword Dao. How much you can comprehend will depend on your own ability."
A Saint's attack was bound to shock the world. How many people could survive a Saint's sword strike?
Moreover, Mu Haiji had used all his strength in this strike!
However, Shi Qiaoqiao had used ingenious methods to neutralize it, leaving only the three-inch-long sword ray for Luo Zheng to comprehend.
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat when he heard the blue-clothed woman's words. His comprehension of the Sword Dao had indeed come to a standstill. He couldn't even make any progress in the Emotion Severing Divine Dao. This was Luo Zheng's bottleneck!
Shi Qiaoqiao's jade-like finger gently flicked the jade-colored ball of light, causing it to float in front of Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng's forehead was cut open, what transmitted to him wasn't a cold sword intent, but an extremely gentle one.
Usually, the sword intent Luo Zheng comprehended was extremely sharp. However, the Emotion Severing Divine Dao that Luo Zheng cultivated pursued the extreme of sharpness. Therefore, the Emotion Severing Divine Dao required one to cast aside all emotions. There couldn't be any distractions in one's heart. Only in this way could one display the strongest sword strike.
Mu Haiji's Sword Dao took the opposite path....................... s pursuit....................

Luo Zheng's forehead throbbed as the wound rapidly healed. Luo Zheng felt that he had grasped some key points. However, these key points seemed to be hidden in a fog, making it difficult for him to see the whole picture.
Shi Qiaoqiao smiled faintly. "It's already good that you were able to comprehend something after being cut by a sword. If you really want to rely on this three inch sword light to comprehend Mu Haiji's Sword Dao, that's just wishful thinking. That three inch sword light can still be preserved in my Time Cage for some time. You can make the best use of that time to comprehend it."
Luo Zheng nodded.
When a martial artist broke through their bottleneck, they either had to cultivate for months and years to comprehend something, or they had to go through hundreds of battles to gain an epiphany.
Luo Zheng had only suffered a small sword strike, and yet he had already comprehended it. That was just wishful thinking.
However, he had indeed gained a little from his comprehension. This three inch sword light was extremely useful to his Sword Dao. Naturally, he carefully put away the jade-colored ball of light …
The blue-clothed woman had suddenly appeared and dissolved Mu Haiji's sword.
Whether it was the Empyreans in the universe or the Sacred Race Empyreans, they were all left speechless.
That woman was there, casually conversing with Luo Zheng. But from the outside, besides her beauty and her mysterious charm, there didn't seem to be anything special about her. But when every Empyrean looked at her, their faces were filled with awe.
This woman was too terrifying …
Even the old patriarch and the others didn't say a word. They would let Luo Zheng talk to her about how to deal with this situation!
The Sacred Race's Great Heavenly Venerates were still kneeling on the ground. Being able to see their creator was a great honor for every Sacred Race martialist. However, Mu Haiji's appearance made them sink into even deeper despair.
Even the Supreme Saint had been blocked by this woman. What hope did the Sacred Race have?
The Grand Empyrean slowly stood up, and the remaining Sacred Race Empyreans behind him also stood up …
The Grand Empyrean licked his dry lips and softly said, "We have lost, but the mission the Supreme Saint gave us must continue. He told us to pay attention to the Dao Children in the Great Yan Universe. They are the most important! Now I understand. The most important person is that person! There's only one person! "
The Great Realm fragment beneath the feet of the Sacred Race Empyreans continued to move further and further away from Luo Zheng and the others. Although the distance was great and the Grand Empyrean didn't specifically point out who it was, the Sacred Race Empyreans behind him all understood that the Grand Empyrean was referring to Luo Zheng.
This person was too important …
Mu Haiji had gone all out just now to kill this youth, but he had still been stopped by someone.
Now that the Sacred Race had been thoroughly defeated in the universe war, their last bit of value was to find a way to kill Luo Zheng. Although the Sacred Race Empyreans present also understood that this was an impossible task.
But what they pursued was a worthy death!
"Kill," the Grand Empyrean said.
The remaining Sacred Race Empyreans also erupted with intense killing intent as they took out their magic treasures and heavenly wills …
Blood-colored spatial laws condensed as they used greater teleportation to rush towards Luo Zheng!
"Are these guys in a hurry to die?" Zhan Beihai chuckled as he stood up.
"Even if they risk their lives, they don't have the qualifications, right?" Empyrean Oracle frowned. Because of the Sacred Race's invasion, the entire Human Dao Alliance had been destroyed. She naturally hated the Sacred Race Empyreans to the extreme!
"They just want a dignified death. Let's fulfill their wishes." The old patriarch understood what the Grand Empyrean was thinking.
The path of martial arts was filled with helplessness. Even the True Gods of the Divine Realm had no choice. Although they had removed the shackles of their lifespan, there were still more shackles placed on them. Even a Saint would have to face this helplessness …
The final wave of attacks organized by these Holy Race Empyreans was aimed at Luo Zheng. Unfortunately, as soon as they used greater teleportation, they were intercepted by the Old Patriarch and the others. Then, Zhan Beihai stimulated his own fighting spirit and joined the battle, instantly turning it into a one-sided battle.
As the Heavenly Dao of the Great Extreme Universe continued to tremble, the remaining Empyreans were like moths drawn to a flame, falling one after another.
The Heavenly Dao's tremors were originally only transmitted through heavenly wills, so only Empyreans could sense it. But when the Grand Empyrean died, the Qiongji Tree was also smashed to pieces by Zhan Beihai!
Although the object that the Empyrean controlled to collect the heavens wasn't the true body of the Limitless Cyathea Tree, it still brought a tremendous impact to the Limitless Realm. As the Heavenly Dao wildly shook, the tens of thousands of saint martial artists in the Limitless Realm also felt it!
Since then, the Great Extreme Universe no longer had a single Empyrean.
However, the fall of the Empyreans wasn't a huge blow to Mu Haiji. After these heavenly wills dissipated, they would automatically return to the Heavenly Dao. After a period of time, they would reappear in various places in the Great Extreme Universe in some form.
For example, the heavenly wills in the Great Evolution Universe appeared in the form of crowns of heavenly wills, while the heavenly wills in the Great Extreme Universe were born in some corner of the universe as seeds of heavenly wills.

Chapter 1718
The remaining Empyreans of the saint race had all perished, announcing the end of the war.
To the Grand Evolution Universe, this was also a great calamity.
Countless great worlds were shattered, countless kingdoms were destroyed, and the number of martial artists that perished was uncountable.
Destruction also meant new life …
Now, there were only 30 some Empyreans left in the Grand Evolution Universe. In other words, there were over 90 vacant Empyrean positions!
Many World Lords had perished in the Grand Evolution Universe, but there were still many World Lords left. The remaining 200,000 World Lords would fight for the position of Empyrean with all their might.
However, fighting for the position of Empyrean wasn't an easy task. After all, inheriting the crown of Empyrean required time, and the Empyreans of the Grand Evolution Universe hadn't all perished yet. It could be imagined that the right to distribute the 90 crowns of destiny would still be held in the hands of these 30 some Empyreans!
Because of the invasion of the saints, the Empyreans present had all cast aside their prejudices. But when it came to the interests of their own race, there would likely still be disputes between them.
But since these Empyreans had survived such a great calamity together, the distribution of the crowns of destiny wouldn't be decided through force. It would likely be decided through negotiation.
Not long after, the old Patriarch used his roar command to announce the news of victory to the entire universe.
As the old Patriarch's voice echoed through the universe, the Great Evolution Lotus Flower became heavy once more. The 'potential' contained within it suddenly doubled. All of the intelligent life forms in the Grand Evolution Universe were celebrating …
In the mortal courts, in the temples, in the alleys, in the countless holy lands, in the Divine Kingdoms …
It wasn't just the intelligent life forms that were affected by this atmosphere. Some vicious beasts, strange insects, strange grasses, and even ordinary mountains and rivers seemed to emit a fresh aura, a new atmosphere. This victory belonged to the entire universe. This world was alive!
Luo Zheng lay down on a great world fragment, feeling his entire body relax.
In order to survive the crisis of the universe, he had been rushing about for dozens of years. Finally, the result he welcomed couldn't be considered bad …
Luo Zheng also understood that for everyone in the Grand Evolution Universe, this was the end. But for Luo Zheng, this was only the beginning!
He still had to jump out of this universe and face his true enemies.
It wasn't just him who needed to jump out of this universe, but also the other Daoists …
"Youqin!"
A thought flashed through Luo Zheng's mind. He raised his eyebrows and stood up.
To be safe, the Resurrected Heavenly Sovereign took Xi Youqin and Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpses and left.
Although Luo Zheng was very confident in reviving the Primogenitor, he didn't know what the current situation was.
"Crackle …"
Right at this moment, a spatial rift suddenly split open ….
A nimble figure quickly squeezed out from within. A pair of embroidered shoes tapped the air a few times, and with an extremely nimble posture, she threw herself into Luo Zheng's arms.
"Wuwuwu … I thought I was dead for sure!"
Xi Youqin lay in Luo Zheng's arms and cried loudly. Not far away, the Celestial Venerables looked at each other when they saw this scene. On the side, Xun touched her delicate ears and looked away with her red eyes. Xun had been by Luo Zheng's side for the longest time, so she had long been used to Xi Youqin's appearance.
Being tightly wrapped around by Xi Youqin like an octopus, Luo Zheng was a little embarrassed and helpless at first. It was a little inappropriate to be intimate in front of these Celestial Venerables. But he seemed to have understood something. He smiled slightly and then hugged Xi Youqin tightly ….
Xi Youqin was so cute because of her frankness. Luo Zheng had no right to ask her to change anything.
However, just as Luo Zheng hugged her tightly, she struggled out of Luo Zheng's arms. Her shallow eyebrows knitted together as she glared at the blue-clothed woman behind Luo Zheng. "Luo Zheng, who is this woman? Where did she come from?! "
With Xi Youqin's keen sense of smell, she would be hostile to any woman by Luo Zheng's side!
If the blue-clothed woman was very ordinary, it would be fine. But this woman gave Xi Youqin a very special feeling. She was both powerful and mysterious. Thus, Xi Youqin, who was like a docile little lamb a moment ago, turned into a fierce lioness in the next.
However, Xi Youqin's actions frightened the old patriarch and the others.
The Celestial Venerables present couldn't see through this blue-clothed woman's cultivation, but she was a terrifying figure who could even block Mu Haiji's sword. They didn't doubt that this woman could slaughter all the Celestial Venerables present in an instant with her own strength!
This kind of powerhouse was usually temperamental. They were able to obtain victory in this Universal War all because of this woman's help at the critical moment. If they accidentally angered her, they might not be able to bear the consequences ….
The old patriarch, Celestial Venerable Oracle, and the others even wanted to cry in their hearts.
"Youqin, don't talk nonsense. This senior saved our lives!" The old patriarch scolded with an unprecedentedly stern attitude.
Although Xi Youqin belonged to the Demi-Immortal Tribe, the old patriarch had always treated her with a very kind attitude in order to coax her into controlling the God-Slaying Sword Array. He was as gentle as her grandfather. The sudden change in his expression startled Xi Youqin.
If it were another woman, such as Ji Luoxue, she would probably have shut up at this moment. She knew the big picture and knew what to prioritize.
However, if Xi Youqin knew what to prioritize, she wouldn't be her …
After being scolded by the old patriarch, she pursed her lips and said, "So what if he saved my life! He saved my life, so he can snatch my man? That resurrected Celestial Venerable saved me, so can he snatch my man too? "
"Pfft …"
Even the usually serious Xun couldn't help but laugh out loud.
As for Zhan Beihai, although he wanted to laugh, he didn't dare to. Of all the people present, he was probably the only one who knew how terrifying this woman was …
As for the group of Celestial Venerables, their faces were now completely dark. Among them, there were even Celestial Venerables who carefully sized up Shi Xiaoqiao, afraid that this woman would really be angered!
"Youqin!" Luo Zheng saw that Xi Youqin was becoming more and more mischievous and could only call out to stop her.
"Am I wrong?" Xi Youqin replied with a frown.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to scold her even more severely, but when he remembered that she had just narrowly escaped death, his heart suddenly softened and he didn't know what to say.
After Shi Xiaoqiao sealed Mu Haiji's three-inch-long sword light, she kept looking at the dome as if she was sizing up something. She didn't pay any attention to Xi Youqin. Only now did she come back to her senses and glanced at Xi Youqin indifferently. Then, her face was like a blooming green lotus. A mysterious smile bloomed on her face, exuding a different kind of attractiveness, and this attractiveness was almost fatal to men.
The Celestial Venerables present were tens of thousands of times more knowledgeable than the emperors of the mortal world. The beauties they had once owned were all stunning, and there was no lack of them who were more beautiful than this blue-clothed woman. It was just that after cultivating to the Divine Sea realm, it was very easy to change one's appearance, and appearance became secondary. However, the aura this woman exuded made all the Celestial Venerables present palpitate with eagerness. Even Celestial Venerable Oracle, who was a woman, was no exception!
Shi Xiaoqiao smiled faintly and slowly took two steps towards Xi Youqin. There seemed to be countless souls circulating in her brown eyes. As the controller of the Soul Wilderness, no one in the Divine Realm could surpass her in the study of souls. With just a glance, it seemed like she could take away Xi Youqin's soul.
Xi Youqin felt as though she wasn't wearing any clothes and was standing naked in front of the blue-clothed woman. In her nervousness, she hid behind Luo Zheng …
"Senior, Youqin's personality is just that. She speaks without thinking. I hope Senior will be magnanimous enough to forgive her." Luo Zheng immediately cupped his hands and said.
Although he didn't understand this mysterious blue-clothed woman, Luo Zheng felt that she didn't have any evil intentions towards him. She wasn't the kind of person who would seek revenge for the smallest grievance. Moreover, this was just a small matter, so he wasn't very nervous.
Unexpectedly, Shi Xiaoqiao smiled faintly and said, "It's nothing. I just wanted to tell her … so what if I stole your man?"
As soon as she said this, not only was Luo Zheng stunned, but the Celestial Venerables present were also slightly stunned.
Zhan Beihai was even more dumbstruck. "As expected of the Divine Realm's number one wondrous woman. She can even say such words …"
"However … your man is still a bit too inexperienced." Shi Xiaoqiao smiled faintly. "Right now, I'm not interested in him, but I can't say for sure in the future!"
After saying this, her orchid-shaped fingers lightly brushed Luo Zheng's forehead. The wound on Luo Zheng's forehead had already more or less healed. There was only a small crack left, but with this brush, it rapidly recovered and disappeared.
However, even Luo Zheng himself didn't notice that she had quietly implanted a small petal into the wound.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1719
Of course, Xi Youqin was afraid of this woman, but when she saw her action, it was as if she had touched her exclusive property. She poked her head out from behind Luo Zheng's back and glared at the blue-clothed woman with a fierce expression …
However, after Shi Qiaoqiao planted that petal, she clearly lost interest in teasing Xi Youqin. She said to Luo Zheng calmly, "I can't leave the Universe of Great Expansion. Let me return to the Immortal Mansion."
Shi Qiaoqiao had no choice but to take Mu Haiji's sword and expose herself.
Those Sages didn't dare to barge into the Universe of Great Expansion, but if she left through the crack in the dome of the Universe of Great Expansion, she would probably attract endless trouble …
Once she returned to the Immortal Mansion, she could leave through the Takixuan Forest.
Luo Zheng naturally agreed to Shi Qiaoqiao's request. However, he was puzzled. "But the boundary of my Immortal Mansion hasn't been opened yet. Can you pass through it?"
The entire Immortal Mansion was shrouded in a special barrier. Although Luo Zheng had already obtained seven tokens, he still had the last token of the True Impasse. He did not have complete control over this Immortal Mansion, so he could not remove the barrier …
Moreover, Luo Zheng wasn't a Dao Disciple yet, and the Takixuan Forest where the Immortal Mansion was located was indeed somewhere in the divine territory.
Once the boundary was removed, it would probably lead to unpredictable effects. For example, Luo Zheng might directly disintegrate into a ball of vital essence and disappear. This was also possible.
"That boundary can stop them, but it can't stop me." Shi Qiaoqiao shook her head slightly.
The "them" she was referring to was naturally Zhan Beihai, the Big Mouth Monster, and the Evil Old Man …
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded. Since this woman in blue was in a hurry to leave, she naturally had her reasons. He extended his hands and bowed deeply. "It was all thanks to Senior's help that the Universe of Great Expansion was able to pass through such a crisis. Luo Zheng thanks you on behalf of Father!"
Seeing Luo Zheng express his thanks, the old Patriarch and the other Empyreans also bowed deeply towards Shi Xiaoqiao. None of them could repay her kindness, so they could only express their thanks verbally …
Shi Xiaoqiao was not polite at all. She slightly raised the corner of her mouth and said to Luo Zheng, "You must thank me, but not now. You must repay me in the future!"
"That's only natural. If Luo Zheng can enter the Godly Realm in the future and achieve something, I will definitely repay you with all my might!" Luo Zheng liked this woman's frankness and straightforwardness. He then asked, "But I still don't know senior's name …"
Unexpectedly, Shi Xiaoqiao smiled. "Aren't you my master? With the enslavement order in your hands, you can check everything in my soul at any time. Why bother asking me for a mere name? "
"I said that I won't peek at any of senior's secrets. Of course, I'll keep my word," Luo Zheng replied firmly.
Shi Xiaoqiao's beautiful eyebrows raised slightly. She sized up Luo Zheng with a gentle gaze, but there was an indescribable feeling in her eyes. She then said, "Alright, let me go back."
Luo Zheng took out the Gen token and put her into the immortal residence. As for how she would leave the immortal residence's boundary, that was her own business …
After Shi Xiaoqiao disappeared, Xi Youqin said hatefully, "That coquettish vixen!"
As soon as she said that, the Empyreans broke out in cold sweat again.
Although that almighty expert had left, he might have heard her. What if he turned back?
Fortunately, what the Empyreans were worried about didn't happen …
Xi Youqin looked at everyone's expressions and felt bored. She quickly locked onto Xun. "Hey! Aren't you angry? "
How could Xun pay attention to Xi Youqin? She rolled her eyes and turned her body to the other side, not even looking at her.
Xi Youqin didn't stop nagging. "You must be jealous. Why don't you show it! It's not good for a woman to keep her emotions in her heart … "
Xun was completely helpless as she listened to her nagging. With Xun's personality, if it was someone else, she would have already attacked!
Seeing Xun's expression become more and more unbearable, Luo Zheng could only drag Xi Youqin to the side …
When Original Sin Heavenly Lord saw that Xi Youqin was safely sent over, he knew that Resurrection Heavenly Lord was trying his best to revive Xuanyuan Chenfeng. He was naturally very nervous, but he didn't want to disturb them. Fortunately, another spatial crack appeared at this time. Resurrection Heavenly Lord took the lead and walked out of the spatial crack.
Original Sin Heavenly Lord's eyes flashed. He asked, "Chenfeng, he …"
Before he finished speaking, a figure floated out of the spatial crack. It was Xuanyuan Chenfeng himself!
However, in comparison, Xuanyuan Chenfeng looked quite weak at this moment. His face was pale and he didn't have the slightest bit of energy. Once Xi Youqin was revived, she was alive and kicking …
"Greetings, great-grandfather." Xuanyuan Chenfeng looked excited when he saw Original Sin Heavenly Lord. Like Xi Youqin, he thought that he was going to die. He never thought that there would be a day when he would see the light of day again.
"Why are you still greeting me at this time?" Original Sin Heavenly Lord stepped forward and supported Xuanyuan Chenfeng. In the entire Xuanyuan clan, he only regarded Xuanyuan Chenfeng as his own son. Original Sin Heavenly Lord didn't have much hope, but Xuanyuan Chenfeng was still alive. It really made him feel relieved …
After Xuanyuan Chenfeng was killed by Maha, he didn't know about the disputes in the universe. However, he learned some details from Resurrection Heavenly Lord. This time, when he looked at Luo Zheng again, his eyes were completely different.
Before this, Xuanyuan Chenfeng had gradually been convinced by Luo Zheng and treated him as his friend. Now, he completely worshipped him!
As far as the other Daoists were concerned, none of them were qualified to participate in the battle between Heavenly Lords. Hua Tianming was still bitterly cultivating. Ji Luoxue and Lie Qianhan were hiding. The other Daoists were also in seclusion …
However, Luo Zheng not only defeated Daoist Maha, but also killed many Holy Race Heavenly Lords!
What was even more frightening was that this guy could participate in a battle at the True God level!
This was no longer a question of who was the number one person in the Universe of Great Expansion. He alone decided the outcome of this battle. Now, when he looked at Luo Zheng again, he was already full of worship.
"Thank you." Xuanyuan Chenfeng smiled. He also knew that his life was saved by Luo Zheng.
It was just that his physical body was shattered too badly. Even if Resurrection Heavenly Lord used Resurrection Heavenly Lord's Life Reversal, his dantian was still somewhat damaged. For ordinary martial artists, this was probably irreversible damage, but for the Xuanyuan clan, it wasn't too big of a problem …
…
…
Zhan Beihai did not go to the Immortal Mansion.
After bidding farewell to Luo Zheng, he fled to a high place in the Universe of Great Expansion and left through the crack in the Wall of Sighs that Mu Haiji had smashed open.
As a member of the War God Temple, he did not have the same concerns as Shi Qiaoqiao.
After the remaining Heavenly Lords agreed to meet up again, they returned to their respective territories.
Although the Holy Race Heavenly Lords had been completely wiped out, there were still many Holy Race survivors in the Universe of Great Expansion. The Heavenly Lords led their own clansmen to carry out the final round of cleansing.
Luo Zheng brought Xun and Xi Youqin back to the Rising Sky Saint Land to reunite with Ning Yudie, Luo Nian, and the others.
After surviving the calamity, the various major clans also launched their own unique celebrations that lasted for quite a long time.
During this time, Heavenly Venerable Oracle led his clan to reclaim the lost territories of the Human Dao Alliance. At the same time, the Heavenly Dao in the Universe of Great Expansion sent down another Crown of Heavenly Fate. This Crown of Heavenly Fate was obtained by Deity Wu Nian. However, in the end, it was handed over to the Hidden Star Temple in the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region.
After all, the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region had suffered the most this time. Not only did they lose one of the four upper Heavenly Venerables, the Thunder Punishment Heavenly Venerable, but the other four Heavenly Venerables had also died. There was no longer a Heavenly Venerable in the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region.
In the past, when faced with such a situation, the other forces would have been eager to take action and try to carve up the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region. However, after experiencing this crisis, the other clans showed more modesty.

Chapter 1720
This day became the most lively day in the Grand Evolution Universe.
Because of the existence of roaring tokens in the universe, the roaring tokens continued to exist.
However, this was also the most extravagant way to congratulate. After all, other than Luo Zheng's roaring token, the other roaring tokens were used one less.
But on this day, the voices congratulating Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin repeatedly echoed in the Grand Evolution Universe.
"Today is the day that Luo Zheng and Xi Youqin get married. I, Wang Qing, represent the West Spirit Holy Land to wish you a happy life and a long life together …"
"Growing old together for a hundred years, the joy of the zither forever, the prosperity of the five, the celebration of the bear early, the Long Chen Holy Land congratulates Luo Zheng and his wife …"
"The sound of the phoenix, the prosperity of the five generations, the brilliance of the peach blossoms, the harmony of the song for a hundred years …"
One congratulatory message after another constantly echoed in the Grand Evolution Universe …
At the foot of Soaring Heaven Mountain, a grand palace stood tall. Attendants in ceremonial robes shuttled back and forth, and tens of thousands of musicians played in unison. Melodious and festive music continuously drifted through the air.
The Celestial Venerables in the universe were mostly pragmatic and low-key. They had long since grown tired of the glory and wealth that mortals yearned for, but that didn't mean they weren't good at it …
Luo Zheng had kept his promise for Xi Youqin. He hadn't wanted to be so ostentatious. However, with the Demi-Immortal race taking care of everything and Xi Youqin adding fuel to the fire, he could only brace himself and cooperate …
"Grandfather! This way! "
The person who had come was the genius of the Central Domain's Void Spirit Sect, Xi Xiaojie.
Xi Xiaojie had already stepped into the Life and Death realm and was one of the most important figures in the Void Spirit Sect. Back then, he had been young and immature, but now he had a trace of calmness. He was the one who guided Daoist Qing Xu to the entrance of the palace.
With the Demi-Immortal race's methods, it wasn't difficult to invite people from the Central Domain to the higher realms.
Xi Xiaojie, who had stepped into the higher realms for the first time, was extremely curious about everything around him. "Divine Sea experts! So many powerful warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm … are just gatekeepers? "
On both sides of the palace, 300 golden-armored warriors were lined up in a row. All of them were at the Spirit Sea Realm. Any one of them could be titled as a War Emperor or War Emperor in the Mortal World. But here, they were only gatekeepers. Xi Xiaojie was also dumbfounded.
Although Taoist Priest Qing Xu was only a Life and Death Realm cultivator, he had lived for much longer than Xi Xiaojie. He more or less had some understanding of the situation in the Upper Realm. A Spirit Sea Realm cultivator who was called an emperor in the Lower Realm was nothing in the Upper Realm. However, he was still quite shocked in his heart, but he did not show it on his face.
The number of guests who had come to offer their congratulations was enormous. There were more than a hundred thousand of them, but the Demi-Immortal Clan had people who organized the guests into groups.
Among them, the Celestial Venerables of the various races, as well as the newly ascended Celestial Venerables in the past year, were the leaders of the various large powers, the Great Sector Lords, and then the Holy Lords of the Rank 10 Holy Lands, followed by the ordinary Sector Lords …
On the square paved with jade in the palace, guests from all walks of life were scattered in picturesque disorder.
When Xi Xiaojie entered the square, he saw ten towering jade pillars erected on one side of the square. He was puzzled. "What are these pillars for?"
"Heh, you're really ignorant!" A warrior sneered. That warrior indifferently sized up Xi Xiaojie and Taoist Priest Qing Xu, and a strange expression appeared on his face. "Where did you two come from? How did two Life and Death Realm little guys sneak in? "
It was reasonable to call Xi Xiaojie little guys, but Taoist Priest Qing Xu was also a thousand years old. In any case, it was difficult to associate him with the word "little".
However, in the Upper Realm, seniority had always been measured by cultivation base. Bone age was only secondary.
Hearing this, Xi Xiaojie glared at that martial artist. He naturally didn't care if he was reprimanded, but Daoist Priest Qing Xu was also reprimanded. How could he endure it? "What are you saying!?"
As soon as his voice fell, Taoist Priest Qing Xu waved his hand, indicating Xi Xiaojie to stop talking.
Just now, Taoist Priest Qing Xu was unable to see through this warrior's cultivation base at all. This meant that this warrior's cultivation base was far above the Spirit Sea Realm.
In Taoist Priest Qing Xu's eyes, everyone present was a big shot. He was naturally afraid that Xi Xiaojie would be impulsive and offend these big shots!
That warrior was a Realm Lord. In the eyes of a Realm Lord, Life and Death Realm warriors were martial artists who had not "entered". In the Holy Land under his jurisdiction, they could only be regarded as apprentice disciples. How dare he refute him?
This warrior chuckled. "Look at you two sneakily. I'm afraid you want to take advantage of the crowd to get some benefits. I'll send you away on behalf of the Demi-Immortal Clan!"
After speaking, that person stepped forward. With a wave of his hand, a spatial crack appeared in front of him. Then, he grabbed Xi Xiaojie and was about to stuff him into the spatial crack … This person actually used greater teleportation to drive Xi Xiaojie and Taoist Priest Qing Xu away!
With this warrior's cultivation base, how could Xi Xiaojie and Taoist Priest Qing Xu have any room to resist?
But before he could throw it, another spatial crack suddenly appeared beside him. At the same time, a cold voice came from the crack!
"Stop!"
The person who came was the Demi-Immortal Clan's Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao.
When the warrior saw the Huan Miao Grand Sovereign appear, he was stunned.
When the other warriors saw the Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao, they all stood up and cupped their hands in greeting.
"Lord, Lord Heavenly Sovereign," the warrior said in a daze. He seemed to realize that he had done something wrong, but he still said, "These two little thieves wanted to sneak in here, but I sent them away for you …"
Xi Xiaojie was still being held in the hands of the martial artist. But with just a light grasp, he discovered that he couldn't revolve his true essence. His face was filled with depression. Hearing this martial artist call out the words' Empyrean 'in fear, Xi Xiaojie was shocked.
However, when he looked up, his eyes flashed and he smiled. "I've seen you before! You've been to the Cloud Hall! "
"Xiaojie, don't be rude, greet Lord Heavenly Sovereign." Taoist Priest Qing Xu knew that Heavenly Sovereigns were the pinnacle of the universe.
Xi Xiaojie's appearance was much calmer, but his nature couldn't be changed. He still had a smile on his face.
He had indeed seen the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign. Back then, when the Central Domain gathered in the Cloud Hall, they were invaded by the ancient witch clan. At that time, the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign attacked in the Cloud Hall.
Empyrean Miao coldly glared at the martial artist. With a casual grab, Xi Xiaojie felt an invisible force pull him over. Then he politely said to Daoist Qing Xu, "I'm really sorry, there are too many guests this time, so it's inevitable that there will be some mistakes. I hope the Xi family can forgive me!"
The surrounding sector lords saw the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign's attitude and were all dumbfounded. The Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign was so polite to two Life and Death Realm fellows. They suspected that their eyes were playing tricks on them.
What was going on with the Xi Clan?
They had heard of the Xuan Yuan Clan, Ji Clan, Ji Clan … but they had never heard of the Xi Clan.
However, some people were quick-witted and instantly understood. Wasn't Luo Zheng's bride surnamed Xi? These two people were from the bride's maiden family!
After the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign expressed his apology, he suddenly stretched out his hand and grabbed the warrior. He actually used a greater teleportation and threw the warrior into the crack. However, the distance of the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign's teleportation was countless times greater. He directly threw the warrior out of the Rising Sky Realm.
Under the lead of the Huan Miao Heavenly Sovereign, Xi Xiaojie and Taoist Priest Qing Xu were arranged to sit at the front of the plaza with the other Heavenly Sovereigns.
After sitting down, Xi Xiaojie was completely obedient. Although he was full of curiosity about everything around him, he still felt a considerable amount of pressure from the auras of the Heavenly Sovereigns.
Not long after, Xi Xiaojie finally understood the use of the ten pillars.
Thunderous dragon roars came from the sky, and ten True Dragons circled in the sky.
The Nine True Dragons were once able to transform into humans. However, it was difficult for them to do so with the current Heaven's Evolution Body. As for the Ancestral Dragon, although he had gained intelligence, he was still unable to transform at this stage. Therefore, he could only maintain his original form.

Chapter 1721
In the side hall at the back of the palace, Xi Youqin, who was wearing bright red makeup, was lying lazily on a rocking chair. Her pair of red embroidered shoes swayed gently, and there was a silly smile on her face.
This wedding dress was made of Blood Silk. This kind of silk was peeled off from the cocoon and thrown into the air. It did not fall to the ground for many years and was weightless. It was also a top-grade material for making "rope" weapons. A Dharma treasure refined from Blood Silk would definitely be listed on the Universal Myriad Spirit Tablet.
The material that countless weapon refiners coveted had turned into a piece of silk on Xi Youqin's body.
Every woman pursued a different kind of marriage. Ru Xun did not care about status at all. She could not understand Xi Youqin's mentality at all. She was also like Su Lingyun, who only silently accompanied Luo Zheng. She only silently worried and prayed for Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng failed, she was gratified.
In this regard, Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin were very similar. However, regarding status, the two women had fought many times in the Lower Realm. They had already made a decision, so naturally, there was no dispute. However, today, Ning Yudie's heart was still surging with jealousy. Others could not see it, but Luo Nian noticed his mother's temper. However, Luo Nian did not know why Ning Yudie was unhappy.
Not long after, a few streams of light rushed into the sky, splitting into thousands of fireworks. When the fireworks bloomed, it was enough to illuminate the entire Rising Sky Realm.
The wedding ceremony began as scheduled.
Not long after the wedding ceremony began, an extremely sharp aura suddenly emerged from the depths of the Rising Sky Realm.
"Whoosh!"
The moment the aura appeared, it turned into a milky white sword ray that rushed into the sky. It directly pierced through a star three hundred million miles high.
After piercing through the star, the milky white sword ray did not weaken at all. It continued to move upward until it reached the dome of the Great Evolution Realm. It cut into the Wall of Sighs several hundred feet deep before dissipating.
The dome of the Great Evolution Realm was once shattered by Mu Hai. However, if the Wall of Sighs was not interfered with by special means, it would gradually heal like an animal's skin and flesh. In the end, only a thick scar would be left behind.
The guests were originally laughing and chatting, but at this moment, after seeing this sword intent, they fell silent.
The power of this sword was far beyond the ability of a Celestial Venerable.
Some martial artists who did not know what was going on thought that a strong enemy had invaded.
Luo Zheng stood in the center. As he watched, his eyes lit up. "What a strong Sword Intent!"
"That's where Hua Tianming secluded himself!" Celestial Master Qian Lan of the Demi-Immortal Clan said.
The Celestial's faces were full of anticipation.
When these Dao Children returned from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they all went into seclusion one after another. Only Xuanyuan Chenfeng was left to fight Dao Child Maha.
These Dao Children's lotuses were all in full bloom. They were no longer restricted by the Heavenly Dao and had cultivated to the Realm Lord realm. In theory, they were only one step away from the True Immortal Realm.
However, this step was an extremely difficult one.
In the Divine Realm, there were many Realm Lords who comprehended the Divine Dao. After all, they were creatures of the same level and were not restricted by the Heavenly Dao. Therefore, the number of Dao Children was quite large. However, it was very rare for a martial artist to be able to condense a Godhead and step into the True Immortal Realm. Most Realm Lords relied on the Godheads left behind by other True Immortals to ascend to the True Immortal Realm.
These Dao Children were seizing the time to enter seclusion and break through to the True Immortal Realm. They hoped to seize that slim chance and condense a Godhead to fight against the Sacred Race. However, whether it was Xingwei or the old patriarch, they all understood that this was almost impossible in a short time.
However, no one expected that today, a year later, one of the Dao Children would really condense his own Godhead!
After the sword-light soared into the sky, a figure shot out from the depths of the Rising Sky World. After circling in the air, the figure turned into a sword-light and flew toward the Rising Sky Mountain!
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and then rose from the ground.
Not only Luo Zheng, but all the Celestial Venerables present also flew into the air …
The other Realm Lords and Great Realm Lords were eager to give it a try. However, when they remembered that this was Luo Zheng's wedding venue, it would be rude of them to leave rashly. They could only look up.
The sword ray was extremely fast. In a short while, it had already reached the top of the Rising Sky Mountain. Then, it went straight for Luo Zheng!
"It's really Hua Tianming!"
"What a strong momentum. He actually condensed a Godhead!"
"From now on, his lifespan is unlimited and he can freely enter the Ao Xiang Divine Realm!"
When the Celestial Venerables saw who it was, they all looked envious.
From now on, this sword-wielding junior would become an existence they looked up to …
The Celestial Venerables were in high positions, so they understood the importance of lifespan. After all, eternal lifespan meant unlimited possibilities. Although the lifespan of a Celestial Venerate was long, there would come a day when they would fall into the Five Decays of Heaven and Man. They would still not be able to escape the great limit of reincarnation.
"Brother Tianming! Congratulations! "
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt that Hua Tianming's momentum had undergone an earth-shaking change. A kind of momentum naturally flowed out from his body. This momentum came from the countless living beings in his Inner World.
In fact, this kind of "momentum" had already been formed after the birth of living beings in his Inner World.
However, before he had a Godhead, he could not sense the existence of this "momentum" at all, let alone use it.
"Mm." Hua Tianming was also pleasantly surprised. He himself did not expect that the process of condensing a Godhead would be so easy!
He looked around and noticed the momentum at the foot of Soaring Sky Mountain. Then, he joked, "I know that today is Brother Luo Zheng's wedding. In a moment of desperation, I was lucky enough to condense a Godhead. However, in my haste, I did not prepare a gift. My apologies."
Although he was in seclusion, the sound of roaring could be heard everywhere. Today, those congratulatory words had reverberated in the universe for several hours, so he naturally heard them clearly …
"Brother Tianming, you are too polite. You being able to break through this barrier is the greatest gift," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, the Celestial Venerables on the side also congratulated him …
Xi Youqin was still wearing a red veil, but she was pouting. She did not care whether Hua Tianming had broken through to become a True God or not. She was silently criticizing Hua Tianming for not breaking through earlier or later. Of all days, he had chosen to break through on this day, interrupting her perfect wedding ceremony.
Just as she was feeling depressed, someone shouted, "Good, today is a double blessing!" Then, it was followed by everyone's agreement. Xi Youqin's mood improved.
After Hua Tianming took his seat, the wedding ceremony continued …
Half a month after the wedding ceremony ended.
The 13 Celestial Venerables in the universe, including Hua Tianming, Luo Zheng, the Ancestral Gragon, and the nine True Dragons, all escaped into the True Dragon Realm.
The Zhenlong Realm was not a world in the universe, but an independent realm. It was similar to the Extreme Purple Realm, but it was many times larger than the Extreme Purple Realm. The sound of the roar could not reach it.
Strictly speaking, the entire True Dragon Realm was a huge spirit beast bag that belonged to Luo Xiao!
When the nine True Dragons' bodies were destroyed and they came with the cauldron, the conflict involved was similar to that of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. Mu Hai had long planned, including the True Dragon Realm.
However, Luo Xiao beat them at their own game. With the nine True Dragons as a guide, he opened up a martial path for Luo Zheng.
Now that the Sacred Clan in the Extreme Universe had been defeated, it was naturally time to settle accounts with the True Dragon Realm.
