Chapter 1722
Every Saint had a different method of raising their contract beasts.
For example, Sage Jiu directly threw the Black Shepherd Weasel into his inner world to sleep. If Sage Jiu needed the Black Shepherd Weasel's help, he would use a transcendent divine ability to summon it.
But different lifeforms had different ways of growing.
True Dragons were a massive race, completely different from the solitary Black Shepherd Weasels.
Thus, when Luo Xiao constructed his inner world, he directly carved out a space for the True Dragons to live in. He also used this world as his spirit beast pouch.
Around the True Dragons, many other races gradually appeared, such as the Dragon Bloodline, the Human Race, and so on …
True Dragons were Super God Beasts, but the most powerful of them were True Spirits. For example, if the Ancestral Dragon reached adulthood, it could be considered a True Spirit.
After the powerhouses of the Great Yan Universe followed Luo Zheng into the True Dragon World, they immediately caused a great uproar!
The mystery of the Nine Dragons was a huge mystery to ordinary lifeforms, but to the True Dragons, it was a conspiracy. And this conspiracy was led by another five-clawed Black Dragon of the True Dragon World.
Back then, the five-clawed Black Dragon had received Mu Haiji's help to kill Jin Chen, the five-clawed Golden Dragon, along with his eight brothers.
However, the True Dragon World didn't directly intervene in the war between the two universes. It only closely watched the outcome of this war. From the standpoint of the five-clawed Black Dragon, it naturally hoped that the Saints would win. True Dragons were only contract beasts of Saints. They weren't born in the universe, so when the Great Yan Universe collapsed and Luo Xiao completely perished, the contract they signed in the past would also become invalid. The five-clawed Black Dragon would then be able to freely enter the Divine Realm.
Unfortunately, the final battle situation was far beyond the five-clawed Black Dragon's expectations. When it learned that the Saints had been defeated, it understood that true trouble had arrived.
But as a contract beast, it couldn't leave the True Dragon World without Luo Xiao's permission. There was nowhere it could run. Now, it could only gather its tribe and defend this dangerous place to the death …
However, the most powerful array formation in the True Dragon Realm had been set up by Qinglong. Furthermore, among the dozen or so Celestial Venerables who had gone with him, there were the Oracle, the Original Sin, and the Patriarch, the three upper Celestial Venerables. In addition, there was also the newly ascended True God Hua Tianming, the Ancestral Dragon, and the nine True Dragons with Heavenly Derived Bodies.
Such a powerful force was enough to start a global war.
Due to the huge disparity in strength, the True Dragon Tribe was quickly defeated. Those 40 True Dragons were not completely on the side of the five-clawed Black Dragon. When the five-clawed Black Dragon attacked back then, many True Dragons did not know what was going on. By the time these True Dragons realized what was going on, the nine True Dragons had already fallen. Although these True Dragons were furious at the five-clawed Black Dragon's actions, there was nothing they could do. Therefore, when they were defeated, only about 20 True Dragons gave up resisting …
It had only taken him seven days to kill the Five-clawed Black Dragon after entering the True Dragon Realm.
In these seven days, he even had four days to rest and reorganize …
Hua Tianming had also displayed his strength for the first time in this battle. During the crusade against the True Dragon Realm, he had only used one sword move to kill the Five-clawed Black Dragon.
After quelling the rebellion in the True Dragon Realm, the Universe of Great Expansion finally stabilized.
However, for Luo Zheng, for Hua Tianming, and the other Dao Disciples, this was merely the beginning, a starting point …
After returning to the Universe of Great Expansion, Xingwei once again summoned Hua Tianming.
Back then, Xingwei only had his head left. After his entire body had self-destructed, the world within his dantian had also been completely destroyed.
Although Xing Wei had once again condensed a shell and looked no different from an ordinary person on the outside, he had already lost his Godhead and also lost the cultivation of a True God. However, his soul was still powerful, but he was destined to be unable to achieve anything in the future. He could only spend the rest of his life peacefully in the Dayan Universe.
After a detailed discussion with Xingwei, Hua Tianming bid farewell to Luo Zheng. Half a month later, he ascended to the divine realm according to Xingwei's instructions.
When they parted, Hua Tianming was somewhat evasive, which made Luo Zheng very suspicious.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that Hua Tianming's fate was even heavier than Luo Zheng's. After ascending to the divine realm, they would probably never meet again …
…
…
Several years later …
All one hundred and eight Heavenly Wills had appeared in the Universe of Great Expansion.
Many races had also redistributed their factions. Perhaps as compensation for the Human Dao Alliance, the Human Dao Alliance now had as many as thirteen Celestial Venerables. In terms of Celestial Venerables alone, the Human Dao Alliance could be considered the largest faction in the universe.
Yun Luo had also obtained an extremely powerful Heavenly Will and become the fourth upper Celestial Venerable in the Universe of Great Expansion, alongside the Oracle, Original Sin, and the old patriarch.
In addition, the Fiend Night Clan and the Demon Race had also received generous rewards!
In comparison, the Myriad Buddha Sacred Realm was gradually declining.
As for the four great clans of the Gods No Thought, they were originally the disciples of the Sages who had secretly left their legacies in the universe. Their goal was to help the Universe of Great Expansion survive this crisis. Now that the threat of the Sacred Race had been removed, they no longer needed to expand. Thus, they didn't fight for the Heavenly Will crown too much. They even gave away a few Heavenly Will crowns!
During this period of time, apart from cultivating in seclusion, Luo Zheng enjoyed this peaceful life in the universe with the three women.
The flow of time in the divine realm was different from the Universe of Great Expansion. Luo Zheng knew that once he stepped into the divine realm, he didn't know how long it would take him to return to the Universe of Great Expansion.
After cultivating to the True God Realm, time was in a state of limitless.
A thousand years, ten thousand years, a million years, a hundred million years, these didn't mean much to a True God because they wouldn't age with the passage of time.
However, Ning Yudie, Su Lingyun, and even Luo Nian were different. Their lifespans were limited.
If he stepped into the divine realm and crossed a long period of time, he would only see piles of bones when he looked back.
The strongest killer that living beings below the True God Realm faced was time. Even Celestial Venerables couldn't escape from it.
However, not long after, Luo Zheng found a way to break it in the Immortal Abode …
Four years after Hua Tianming left the Universe of Great Expansion, Luo Zheng swallowed the Emotion Severing Pill he'd obtained from the Immortal Abode. Although Afo repeatedly warned Luo Zheng not to casually swallow this Seven Transformations Golden Pill unless it was absolutely necessary, Luo Zheng still swallowed it.
Now, the gap between Luo Zheng and the other Daoists had widened.
Apart from Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, who was slightly less talented, the lotuses of Luo Xixuan, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Ji Luoxue, and the other Daoists had all fully bloomed. Half a year ago, a bolt of lightning had rushed out of Luo Xixuan's seclusion area and transformed into the Lightning Lock Sky Domain.
Although Luo Xixuan hadn't condensed a Godhead yet, she had probably made a big breakthrough. After Hua Tianming, Luo Xixuan would probably be the second Daoist to become a True God.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't a Daoist yet. His Great Expansion Lotus still hadn't fully bloomed …
Judging from Luo Zheng's talent and foundation, he didn't fall behind the other Daoists at all. The problem definitely didn't lie here. The key was probably still in Luo Zheng's inner world!
After all, the other Daoists all cultivated the Vital Essence system, while Luo Zheng cultivated the Chaotic Qi. There was an essential difference between them. On the path of cultivation, no one could guide Luo Zheng. He could only rely on his own comprehension.
In desperation, Luo Zheng could only rely on the Emotion Severing Pill, hoping to break through the shackles of being a Daoist first.
However, as soon as Luo Zheng swallowed the Emotion Severing Pill, he suddenly felt a powerful aura rushing into his mind!
It turned out that Afo's warning was right. As a Seventh Transformation Golden Pill, this Emotion Severing Pill was a spiritual item of heaven and earth. In the few years since Luo Zheng took it out, it had quietly condensed its own consciousness.
This Seventh Transformation Golden Pill actually wanted to possess Luo Zheng's body!
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's Golden Battle Soul was extremely tough. Coupled with the powerful soul pressure released by the Emperor Soul Imprint in his soul, it gradually suppressed the Emotion Severing Pill's consciousness …
However, the danger of this battle between souls was no less than the danger Luo Zheng had experienced!
Because of this battle, Luo Zheng had been in a coma for nearly half a month. With the help of the Demi-Immortal Clan, he slowly woke up!

Chapter 1723
The Sovereign Spirit Seal was originally a method used by Sage Jiu to cultivate the soul.
When the power of the soul was released, the Sovereign Spirit Seal would release a unique pressure.
This strong pressure was even comparable to the Divine Dao's Great Ruling Technique. If the opponent's strength was above his own, he could disturb the opponent's mind. If the opponent's strength was equal to his own, he could suppress the opponent. If the opponent's strength was below his own, he could even make the opponent submit, achieving the effect of subduing the enemy without fighting!
However, this kind of aura did not match Luo Zheng's character. After all, Luo Zheng was not a particularly arrogant person.
However, in order to cultivate the Golden Battle Soul in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had no other choice after stepping into the Heart Refining Pagoda. After all, this was the only soul cultivation method that Sage Jiu had given him!
Luo Zheng's consciousness examined the soul in his mind. The small sword and the monarch spirit engraving shone together, releasing a different kind of golden radiance. He was curious about what kind of changes his soul had undergone.
Wasn't the Emotion Severing Pill supposed to help him comprehend the Emotion Severing Divine Dao? Why would it bring about such changes in his soul?
Out of curiosity, Luo Zheng was eager to give it a try.
However, just as he became excited, he suddenly felt a sharp pain in his head. His vision darkened and he almost fainted again …
His soul was still abnormally weak. Although Xingwei had personally nurtured Luo Zheng's soul, it would take a long time for it to fully recover.
Although Xingwei was only left with his soul, his True God's soul was far stronger than the souls of Empyreans. Naturally, his methods were many times more brilliant. He knew how important Luo Zheng was to Luo Xiao. Naturally, he used the last of his strength to display the Star Cycle Technique.
Although the injury to Luo Zheng's soul was serious, with Xingwei's methods and daily nurturing, it took almost a month for his injured soul to recover.
On the second day after his soul recovered, Luo Zheng found a secluded valley. He sat on a boulder in the valley and closed his eyes tightly …
Luo Zheng was unable to measure the power of the Seven Transformations Golden Pill. However, this Emotion Severing Pill had tormented him to the brink of death. In the end, the small golden sword that was left in his soul could not be just for decoration.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
As Luo Zheng closed his eyes and entered a meditative state, the power in his soul began to be stimulated.
A soft chirp began to slowly reverberate through the valley. The rustling of the grass in the surroundings was also reflected in Luo Zheng's mind.
"Sovereign Soul Seal!"
Luo Zheng didn't know how to activate those small golden swords, so he could only release the power of the Sovereign Spirit Seal.
The moment Luo Zheng activated the emperor soul mark, a powerful soul aura crazily reverberated throughout the entire valley …
The valley that Luo Zheng was searching for was located in a remote area in the Rising Sky Realm. In order to avoid causing chaos and trouble, Luo Zheng tried his best to stay away from those cities.
However, as the most prosperous Great World of the Demi-Immortal Clan, the Rising Heavens World had almost no barren land. Even within a thousand miles of the valley, there were many small towns and villages.
Luo Zheng's Spiritual Aura suddenly spread out in all directions in a manner that could topple mountains and overturn seas. Although the valleys blocked Luo Zheng's Spiritual Aura to a certain extent, a portion of it still managed to escape.
For a moment, the snakes, insects, and ants around the valley were greatly frightened and fled in all directions. As for the mortals within a thousand miles, they suddenly felt dizzy and a sense of inferiority arose in their hearts. Those with weak willpower couldn't help but kneel down …
This was still Luo Zheng's soul aura. He only released 40% of his soul aura.
However, just as he activated the emperor soul mark, a strange scene occurred.
The small golden swords on the surface of his soul actually began to slowly rotate. Under this constant rotation, the small golden swords even began to swim on the surface of his soul like small swimming fish. They looked quite agile!
"These small golden swords are also alive?" Luo Zheng was dumbfounded. When he thought about it, it was impossible. After all, these small golden swords were the same as emperor soul marks. They were just imprints left on his soul.
However, these small golden swords swam for a while and actually pierced towards the emperor soul marks. The small golden swords actually merged with the emperor soul marks.
At this moment, it seemed to be the case. However, in the next moment, Luo Zheng quickly denied this idea!
All of the Sovereign soul engravings were shrinking at an extremely fast speed, but those small golden swords were getting bigger and bigger. It was not that the small golden swords were merging with the Sovereign soul engravings, but that the Sovereign soul engravings were being devoured by these small golden swords!
The devouring speed was extremely fast. In a short while, the emperor soul marks that were originally the size of a fist became the size of a walnut, then the size of a cherry, then the size of a sesame seed, and finally disappeared. They were completely devoured by these small golden swords!
As for the small golden swords, their size skyrocketed by ten times. After devouring, the small golden swords no longer moved. Instead, they quietly floated on the surface of Luo Zheng's soul …
"What the hell is going on?" Luo Zheng's face was full of doubts, and his expression became cautious.
He didn't know whether this change was good or bad. However, the emperor soul marks that he had painstakingly cultivated in the Heart Refining Tower had indeed disappeared.
With doubts in his heart, Luo Zheng once again operated his soul power!
Without the emperor soul marks, the small golden swords began to move. The small golden swords rapidly flowed on the surface of his soul. Under the flowing golden light, Luo Zheng's eyes also flickered with golden light …
At the same time, a strange feeling struck his heart.
He felt that his soul was constantly moving away from him, and his perception was also beginning to disappear. At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes were also ice-cold, and there was no longer the slightest emotion contained within them!
"I understand now …"
"The Emotion Severing Divine Dao is actually closely related to the soul!"
"This Emotion Severing Pill is indeed the most important part of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao …"
Luo Zheng thought silently in his heart, but his face was still silent and cold, without the slightest excitement or joy.
Previously, when Luo Zheng used the Emotion Severing Divine Way, he relied on the baleful aura. He used the baleful aura in his body to suppress his emotions. However, this was an unorthodox method. Even the baleful aura could not completely cut off his emotions. This was because the baleful aura contained the emotion of "anger."
"The Emotion Severing Divine Dao ultimately requires the cultivation of the soul. After all, the soul is the source of the seven emotions and six desires. With the help of this Emotion Severing Pill, can I be considered to have just entered the Divine Dao?" Luo Zheng murmured with a cold expression.
He stretched out his hand and pointed at the Sumeru Ring. He casually pulled out a long sword, gently circled it in the air, and suddenly pointed it toward the sky.
A bit of sword intent condensed on the surface of the long sword.
This bit of sword intent was only the size of a needle tip, and it constantly flickered around the tip of the sword!
"Sword intent condensed?"
Luo Zheng observed coldly. He had no emotions now, so naturally, he didn't have much expression. Under the influence of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, Luo Zheng's negative and positive emotions had all dissipated!
"Wu wu …"
As the sword intent continued to condense, the silver light it emitted became more and more intense.
It was as if a star had condensed on the tip of this ordinary long sword!
"It's not enough. To enter the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, I need to let go of all emotions … Those small golden swords have indeed cut off my emotions, but I haven't really let go of everything," Luo Zheng murmured as he stared at the silver light emitted by the sword intent.
At this moment, there was a spiritual connection in Luo Zheng's heart. He suddenly thought of something. His eyebrows suddenly raised, and he said coldly, "I understand! Even if I cut off the seven emotions and six desires, I still have my own consciousness! "
"The ultimate comprehension of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao should be …"
At this moment, the small golden swords on the surface of Luo Zheng's soul flowed faster and faster.
Luo Zheng's eyes shot out beams of golden sword light as he continued to hover in the air.
"Shua shua shua …"
Just the sword light emitted from his eyes had directly pierced through the mountain walls of the valley, leaving fine sword marks on the mountain walls!
This was the power of Luo Zheng's eyes!
"This is … Anatta Sword!"
Luo Zheng's sword shot straight toward the sky …

Chapter 1724
As Luo Zheng slashed out with his sword, he had already completely forgotten himself.
In his mind, everything in the world was rapidly flying away from him!
Only this sword intent existed!
A sword strike that even he had forgotten was the most decisive sword strike.
As the sword slashed out, a sword light appeared.
At this time, Luo Zheng was like a wooden statue, standing where he was without moving at all …
If he faced his enemy head-on and his sword was dodged, that would mean Luo Zheng's defeat. Because in this state, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to dodge or resist.
This was also the essence of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao. When the sword came out, it would decide life and death!
Woosh!
Luo Zheng's sword thrust towards the skies didn't have too intense of an aura. But, this two inch wide sword light also soared into the skies, rapidly piercing through the depths of space.
Not long after, the thin white sword light pierced through the first star, leaving a two inch wide hole in the star.
To a star that was tens of thousands of miles wide, a two inch wide hole wasn't a problem at all. The star didn't even shake in the slightest.
But not long after that sword light left the star, the star began to sway in the depths of space. Several thousand mile long cracks began to appear on the surface of the star.
There were still many Divine Transformation realm martial artists cultivating on this star. As the star continued to sway, these Divine Transformation realm martial artists were all puzzled. They didn't know why this star would have such a reaction, so they all turned into beams of light that flew away from the star.
"Boom …"
Not long after, the star began to disintegrate and the entire star began to collapse …
The beam of light that pierced through the star didn't stop at all. It continued to rapidly soar upwards, but no one in the universe noticed its existence. After all, compared to the entire universe, this sword light was far too insignificant!
It was just that this insignificant sword light contained an unimaginable strength.
After piercing through the star, the sword light didn't weaken at all. It soared into the sky and pierced into the Wall of Sighs.
Hua Tianming's sword had left behind a wound a hundred zhang deep on the Wall of Sighs, but Luo Zheng's sword was even sharper, and its penetrative power was even stronger. It had penetrated as deep as three thousand zhang into the Wall of Sighs!
It was not until the sword light completely disappeared that Luo Zheng's consciousness gradually returned.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes, which were flickering with golden light but incomparably indifferent, regained their spirituality. The corners of his mouth curled up slightly, and a satisfied smile appeared on his face. After comprehending the "Anatta Sword", Luo Zheng had a deeper understanding of the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao!
"Rumble …"
The mountain wall that he was facing suddenly collapsed in front of his eyes.
The golden light emitted from his eyes earlier had already cut the mountain wall into pieces. After the structure of the mountain wall was destroyed, it naturally collapsed in front of his eyes.
Now that Luo Zheng was operating those small golden swords, he could really kill people with his "eyes" …
At the same time that the mountain wall collapsed, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a buzzing sound. From behind the collapsed mountain wall, a lotus flower slowly rose!
"The Great Lotus Flower of Expansion!"
Luo Zheng's eyebrows slightly raised.
The lotus flowers of the nine Dao Children had all bloomed. Only Luo Zheng was missing!
In fact, after the lotus flower bloomed and became a complete Dao Child, they already had the ability to transcend the Heavenly Dao. At the very least, they wouldn't disintegrate in the Divine Domain.
However, the secondary life forms in the universe were basically only able to ascend to the Divine Domain after being conferred the title of True God, just like Hua Tianming.
Luo Zheng didn't know how long it would take for him to condense a godhood, but before that, he needed to become a true Dao Child. In other words, he needed to have a considerable understanding of the Divine Dao and escape the fetters of the Heavenly Dao …
The Great Lotus Flower of Expansion slowly rotated. Luo Zheng stared at it without blinking.
Many martial artists in the Third Transformation of the Divine Transformation would have these lotus flowers appear when they were transcending their tribulations. However, the appearance of the lotus flower only meant that it had the possibility of blooming, but it didn't mean that it would definitely bloom. Many times, the lotus flower would appear and slowly rotate a few times before quietly disappearing …
Now, Luo Zheng was naturally still somewhat nervous. He was afraid that his understanding of the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao wasn't enough. This lotus flower was still unwilling to bloom. After all, Luo Zheng didn't know the exact criteria for the lotus flower to bloom.
For example, Luo Zheng had already comprehended the "Great Wish Technique". In theory, he was also a genuine Dao Child. However, the Great Wish Technique didn't seem to be recognized by this lotus flower.
While Luo Zheng was paying attention to this lotus flower, the old clan leader and Xingwei were also paying attention to it.
Previously, they didn't know that Luo Zheng had a new understanding of the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao. However, as soon as the lotus flower appeared, the old clan leader immediately sensed it.
As the master of the heavenly treasures, the old clan leader was very familiar with every single lotus flower that bloomed in the Great Expansion Realm. In the entire universe, there were only a few hundred people who possessed a Mahayana Lotus Flower. As for Luo Zheng, he was the only one who had a thirty-five-leaf lotus flower.
Therefore, as soon as the old clan leader sensed it, he had already called Xingwei over …
"This time, Luo Zheng should succeed," Xingwei said indifferently.
"The lotus flowers have all bloomed, but he still needs time to condense a Godhead. Who knows how long that will take?" the old clan leader said with a frown.
The crisis in the Great Expansion Realm had indeed come to an end. However, he knew that Mu Haiji wouldn't give up so easily. Time was never enough for Luo Zheng.
"No." Xingwei shook his head. "I won't wait until he condenses a Godhead …"
The old clan leader was taken aback. "Sir Xingwei, do you mean to send the Realm Lord Luo Zheng to the Divine Realm?"
Xingwei nodded silently.
"But the competition in the Divine Realm will only become fiercer. Although Luo Zheng's talent is unparalleled, the risk is too great," the old clan leader said.
Xing Wei smiled faintly. "This isn't my idea. It's Master's idea. Luo Zheng … is an anomaly. He might not even be able to condense a Godhead." His eyes flickered. "He might not even need a Godhead!"
Godheads corresponded to the pinnacle of the cultivation system. Through the Dao Steles of the Divine Realm, one could comprehend the supreme Law of the Divine Realm — the Divine Dao. Then, through the Godhead, one could envelop one's inner world and absorb the "potential" within to unleash the strongest power possible.
Everything was cleverly linked and complemented each other.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't cultivating the True Essence system. Instead, he was cultivating the even more powerful Chaos Energy.
In Xingwei's opinion, Luo Zheng was indeed a "secondary life form." In fact, no one could determine what level of life form Luo Zheng was. According to Xingwei's understanding, even if Luo Zheng hadn't comprehended the Divine Dao, he probably wouldn't be disintegrated by the Divine Realm if he was thrown into it!
The old clan leader was astonished. The old clan leader had seen countless people. Although he felt that Luo Zheng was indeed different, he hadn't expected Sir Xingwei to give such a strange evaluation.
"Something's happening!" The old clan leader's eyes flashed.
The Lotus Flower of Great Derivation floating above Luo Zheng's head suddenly spun faster!
Luo Zheng's eyes focused on the Lotus Flower. His eyes grew brighter and brighter, as if two flames were burning fiercely within them …
Whoosh!
"Eh? What's going on? "
The Great Derivation Lotus Flower controlled by the old patriarch was no longer under the old patriarch's control. It suddenly condensed and flew in Luo Zheng's direction!
The old clan leader was about to make a hand seal to summon the Lotus Flower of Great Derivation back, but he was stopped by Sir Xingwei. "Don't interfere. This is Master's arrangement."
Whoosh …
The Lotus Flower of Great Derivation flew at an extremely fast speed. It only took one or two breaths to cross millions of miles of distance. Soon, it arrived above Luo Zheng's head.
"A second lotus flower?"
Seeing the Lotus Flower of Great Derivation flying towards him, Luo Zheng was dumbfounded. "This seems to be the one controlled by the old clan leader!"
The Lotus Flower of Great Derivation controlled by the old clan leader was slightly different from the Lotus Flowers floating above the heads of ordinary warriors. For example, the Lotus Flower above Luo Zheng's head was pinkish-white, while the old clan leader's Lotus Flower of Great Derivation was crimson. In addition, there were many differences in details. For example, the old clan leader's Lotus Flower of Great Derivation had always been in a budding state.
After the Lotus Flower of Great Derivation flew above Luo Zheng's head, it directly overlapped with the Lotus Flower above Luo Zheng's head and eventually replaced the original Lotus Flower of Luo Zheng!

Chapter 1725
This item had already escaped the old Patriarch's control.
Now that it overlapped with Luo Zheng's lotus flower that had already bloomed, it meant that Luo Zheng was about to reopen the Great Lotus Flower of Development …
Ever since the old Patriarch had shouldered the Mandate of Heaven and obtained the item, the Great Lotus Flower of Development had never been opened.
The Treasure of Heaven was originally the Life Source Magic Treasure of the Saints, and only the Saints themselves had the qualifications to fully use it. Letting the strongest Celestial Lord in the universe obtain the Treasure of Heaven as a magic treasure was also a convention among the Saints, but it did not mean that they could use it at will.
Thus, whether it was the old Patriarch or the Saint Race's Grand Empyreans, they could only use a portion of the item's ability.
The old Patriarch watched as the Great Lotus Flower of Development flew away. With a casual wave of his hand, he tore open a spatial rift and entered with Startail. In the blink of an eye, they appeared at the side of the valley where Luo Zheng was!
"The Great Lotus Flower of Development … is opening!" The old Patriarch was flabbergasted.
On the other hand, Startail's expression was calm. "In the Divine Realm, the Divine Dao of Development ranks near the top of the 3000 Dao tablets. It is ranked 13th, but there are very few people who cultivate this Divine Dao! In the vast Divine Realm, there are countless True Gods, but since ancient times, only a few hundred people have cultivated the Divine Dao of Development. Do you know why? "
The old Patriarch didn't know much about the Divine Realm. What he knew came from Startail and his own bloodline. When he heard Startail speak of the origin of the Great Lotus Flower of Development, the old Patriarch asked, "Why?"
"The chaos is one. It is the number one of heaven, number two of earth, number one of taiji, number three of heaven … In addition to the original number eight of earth, all the even numbers are derived from taiji. The sum of these derived numbers is fifty. This is the origin of the number fifty, "Startail said.
The old Patriarch naturally knew of the number fifty, but he had shouldered the Mandate of Heaven, so he didn't know the reasoning behind it. Startail's words left the old Patriarch a little dizzy. "I wonder what Lord Startail's words have to do with the small number of people who cultivate the Divine Dao of Development?"
Startail smiled. "Because this Divine Dao attempts to encompass the entire Divine Realm. In other words, the vast space outside the Divine Realm …"
"The entire Primal Chaos …" The old patriarch's eyes flickered as his thoughts drifted.
In the eyes of mortals, a Greater Thousand World was already big enough. It was boundless, and they would never be able to see the edge of this world in their lifetime.
When the old Family Patriarch reached the Spirit Sea Realm, he felt that a big world was very big, as big as a thousand big worlds. Some warriors would have to spend hundreds of years to travel across a big world …
After reaching Sector Lord Realm, he already felt that a great realm was too small. He could traverse it in just a few days. The truly vast realm was the universe! A hundred thousand Great Worlds, a million Boundless Worlds, and an endless starry sky …
After becoming a Celestial Venerable, in his opinion, the universe was nothing more than this. The largest should be the Divine Realm.
But now, the old Patriarch knew that beyond the Realm of the Gods, there was an endless primal chaos. The boundaries of this primal chaos were nearly impossible to explore. Not only was it infinite in terms of space, but it was also infinite in terms of thought!
"If the Primal Chaos can be summarized, then it means that the Primal Chaos has a boundary." The old Patriarch thought for a moment and muttered, "Then, what lies beyond the boundary?"
Lord Startail faintly smiled. "Good question. This is a paradox. Even Saints cannot answer this question. Or, it could be said that Saints' understanding of the Primal Chaos is still in an unenlightened period. Let alone the boundaries of the Primal Chaos and beyond, according to my guess, besides a very small number of Saints that never returned, the vast majority of Saints that wandered through the Primal Chaos only circled around the Realm of the Gods …"
"…" Hearing Startail's evaluation, the old Patriarch didn't know what to say.
"So, from ancient times until now, besides those forbidden Dao tablets, the Divine Expansion Dao tablet is likely the one with the least number of martial artists observing it." Startail continued.
Of the 3000 Dao tablets of the Divine Dao, every Dao tablet had the name of an ancient Dao cultivator engraved on it. This was the name of the first person to comprehend the Divine Dao!
Most of them were saint martial artists. This was because the saints were the first race to flourish in the Realm of the Gods. Although the saints had vanished, the names of the ancient Dao cultivators still remained …
However, the name of Luo Xiao was engraved on the Divine Expansion Dao tablet!
This was because Luo Xiao was the first person in history to comprehend the Divine Expansion Dao. Not only did he comprehend it, but he also relied on this Divine Dao to become a Saint, creating an inconceivable miracle in the Realm of the Gods!
Because of Luo Xiao's legend, the Divine Expansion Dao that no one cared about suddenly became popular.
If one could become a Saint by cultivating this Divine Dao, which True Divinity wouldn't be tempted?
However, the Divine Expansion Dao was far too elusive. After being popular for a period of time, the vast majority of True Divinities eventually gave up. Only a small number of people managed to comprehend the profound principles of this Dao tablet. But, none of these people were able to achieve the same accomplishments as Luo Xiao. Because of this Dao, there was no second Saint.
"Honorable Master spent his entire life exploring the Divine Expansion Dao. It is also because of this that …" As Startail spoke to here, he began to choke with emotion.
In the past, to be accepted as a disciple by Luo Xiao, what sort of glory was that?
Even Yu Taibai, who had been famous for a long time, was willing to enter Luo Xiao's name. Now that he had fallen to his current state, Startail was naturally sad.
However, he knew that his Honorable Master hadn't given up. He also saw hope in Luo Zheng …
As he knew, what his Honorable Master wanted to do now was the true inheritance!
"Huala …"
The Great Expansion Lotus Flower was constantly unfolding.
The lotus flower's leaves didn't unfold bit by bit. Instead, like an octopus, they spread out in all directions.
"Weng …"
From the center of the lotus flower, a fresh and tender stamen was revealed. Motes of light constantly revolved around the stamen.
Luo Zheng raised his head to look at this scene. Just now, Startail had explained so much, but the Old Patriarch only understood 30% of it. As for Luo Zheng, he didn't hear anything at all. Naturally, he was still confused by the scene in front of him.
However, out of instinct, Luo Zheng also understood that this must be related to his father's arrangements. No matter what happened, he would gladly accept it.
The motes of light around the stamen were like fireflies the size of rice grains. They revolved around the lotus leaves and sprinkled down on Luo Zheng. Like a warm drizzle, Luo Zheng was completely enveloped in it.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a gentle aura constantly blowing on his cheeks.
This lazy feeling made him feel a little tired. His eyelids felt as if they weighed a thousand pounds, and he couldn't help but close them. Then, he naturally escaped into the Dream Realm …
"The number of the Great Expansion Realm is fifty, and it has forty-nine uses. The number of the Great Expansion Realm is fifty, and when one moves, it becomes two. One is a variable, and one is a fixed number …"
A dignified voice rang out … …
"I've heard of this saying before." In the dream, Luo Zheng still had clear thoughts. It was as if he was in a lucid state, but he was dreaming.
Back then, in the Illusory Battlefield, Jiang Yu 'er had said this saying. However, no one, including Luo Zheng, had been able to comprehend the profound meaning behind it.
"The Great Expansion Realm is the origin of the Great Expansion Realm …"
The dignified voice continued to speak. These words were incomprehensible to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng could only remember each word in his heart.
After an unknown period of time, the voice finally stopped … and when the voice stopped, Luo Zheng suddenly had a clear feeling in his heart. He suddenly discovered that he seemed to have a very deep understanding of the Great Expansion Realm!
This wasn't an epiphany of Luo Zheng's, but a kind of understanding that came naturally when he thought about it according to the dignified voice!
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally understood his father's painstaking arrangements.

Chapter 1726
That blooming flower continued to hover above Luo Zheng's head …
As the light continued to pour in, it finally turned into a pale golden book and entered Luo Zheng's mind.
There were four large black words engraved on the book, and it was called the Divine Art of Dayan. The voice that had been constantly pouring into Luo Zheng's mind was a part of the Divine Art of Dayan. It was also Luo Xiao's lifetime comprehension.
This' Divine Creation Technique 'had the miraculous effect of strengthening the soul and divine sense. After cultivating it to an extreme level, one's divine sense would be several times stronger than an ordinary martial artist's. Therefore, Luo Xiao's soul and divine sense were unparalleled among Saints!
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that when he began to comprehend the 'Divine Creation Technique', the aura emitted from his soul was constantly increasing!
The Golden Battle Soul was the strongest realm a soul could reach in the universe.
After reaching the Golden Battle Soul, one's soul could freely leave the body to defend against enemies. It could even attach the soul to a sword to increase the power of one's weapon.
For example, the Old Patriarch and Original Sin Heavenly Venerable had Golden Battle Souls …
However, what was stronger than the Golden Battle Soul was the 'Soul of a True God'. The Soul of a True God was just a general term for the soul of a True God. In fact, there were differences in the strength of the soul among True Gods. In the Divine Domain, there was another division of the soul.
For example, Xing Wei and Mu Haiji's disciple Mu Jiu's soul was considered relatively strong among True Gods. However, Mu Tian and Mu Han's souls were slightly weaker!
"Luo Zheng's aura … is greatly increasing!" Xing Wei had already sensed the changes in Luo Zheng's soul.
Luo Zheng's soul was left with those golden sword shadows. His soul was naturally mixed with a sharp aura. When his soul aura was released, it was like a sharp arrow to others. The sharp aura was oppressive!
Facing the sharp aura emitted by Luo Zheng, Xing Wei was still fine.
However, the Old Patriarch was extremely uncomfortable.
The invisible sharp aura penetrated in all directions. Some of the sharp aura directly entered the Old Patriarch's mind. The sharp aura penetrated the Old Patriarch's soul and brought him heart-wrenching pain!
"The aura of this soul is so powerful …"
The old patriarch's face was filled with astonishment. Then, he leaped backward and retreated three hundred feet …
It was no wonder that the Old Patriarch was surprised. If Luo Zheng used his full strength against someone, wouldn't he be able to destroy the other party's soul in an instant?
However, the old patriarch's retreat was far from the speed of Luo Zheng's soul strengthening.
When the old patriarch retreated to a distance of three hundred feet, the aura of the soul became even stronger. Several of the formless "aura arrows" pierced through the old patriarch's soul again. He groaned a few times. Then, he stretched out his hand and entered the space crack. His figure appeared dozens of miles away. He looked at Luo Zheng with a pale face.
"Luo Zheng's soul is still a Golden Fighting Soul … However, the strength of his soul is no less than a True God's soul," Xing Wei said faintly. "Master's skill in the Soul Doctrine is unparalleled!"
In the distance, the old patriarch's eyes were filled with awe. This was the remarkable power of the Master of Dayan!
If one's soul was powerful, one would definitely have the upper hand in a battle. Just like how the Thunder Punishing Heavenly Master had fallen under Mu Jiu's Soul Searching Banner. Even with the deep foundation of the Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region, there was almost no way to fight back!
If Luo Zheng fought with a Heavenly Master at this stage, he could easily kill him with just his soul.
Luo Zheng did not know that his aura had forced the old patriarch to retreat. He was still immersed in his own mind …
The faint light sprinkled down and did not stop after condensing the Divine Dayan Technique in his mind. Instead, it continued to condense and gradually formed a green jade ruler in his mind!
When the entire jade ruler was condensed and formed, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. He saw three large words on the ruler's body.
"Infinite Ruler?"
"Why would such a ruler appear?"
"Father said that the Divine Dayan Technique is actually the origin of counting. How is it related to this ruler?"
Luo Zheng had many doubts in his mind …
However, no one could answer Luo Zheng's doubts. After that majestic voice recited the Divine Dayan Technique once, it did not speak again. If there were any mysteries in it, Luo Zheng could only rely on himself to slowly explore it.
After a while, the faint light from the stamen of the Great Lotus of Dayan finally stopped …
The Great Lotus of Dayan circled around for a while, and the originally blooming lotus leaves closed together.
"Huff … huff …"
After circling slightly in the air, it flew to the old patriarch's side …
At this moment, Luo Zheng opened his eyes and turned his head to look. Only then did he discover that Xing Wei and the old clan leader had rushed over. He cupped his hands and smiled, "Luo Zheng greets Lord Xing Wei and the old clan leader."
Xing Wei smiled faintly, "Congratulations. It seems that you have comprehended the Divine Dao again?"
Luo Zheng nodded, but his face revealed a regretful expression, "It's a pity that I haven't condensed a Godhead yet." As he spoke, he looked towards the deep sky, his gaze penetrating the deep darkness and staring at the pale blue spot of light above the dome of the universe …
In the eyes of many mortals, that spot of light seemed to be a special star that had suddenly appeared.
Some people called this spot of light the "Spider Star", while others called it the "Ominous Star", but in short, it was a bad omen.
Only a few cultivators knew that this spot of light was not a star, but a scar left behind on the Wall of Sighs after Mu Haiji shattered the universe. The Wall of Sighs in the universe had the ability to heal itself, but it would take tens of millions of years for this scar to completely heal. Naturally, these mortals would not be able to see the day this spot of light disappeared …
Looking at that spot of light, Luo Zheng's heart was filled with hostility. He couldn't wait to step into the Divine Realm and kill Mu Haiji with his own hands.
But how to condense a Godhead, and when it would condense, were all unknown!
Xing Wei smiled, "There's no need to wait until that day."
"What?" Luo Zheng looked at Xing Wei in confusion.
"You have already removed the restrictions of the Heavenly Dao. I can now send you to the Divine Realm," Xing Wei said.
"You're sending me? Isn't it natural ascension after condensing a Godhead? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
When Luo Zheng had broken through to the Life and Death Realm in the lower realm, he had been rejected by the world and forcibly sent into the Ascension Passage by the world's power …
In the upper realm, if one could condense a Godhead, they would also be rejected by the universe and ascend to the Divine Realm!
It had been like this for many years …
Xing Wei smiled, "Actually, that's not the case. True Gods who condense a Godhead in the universe are usually received by the Jiang Family's emissaries to the Divine Realm. However, the emissaries in the Great Evolution Universe have been forced to return to the Jiang Family …"
"That little girl from the Jiang Family?" Luo Zheng asked.
Xing Wei nodded, "A thousand divine epochs ago, the Jiang Family used that stone tablet to write down this rule. Therefore, the Heavenly Dao in the various universes is controlled by the Jiang Family alone. Even the Great Sages can't go beyond this rule. However, this time, we exploited a loophole and forced that little girl to stay in the Jiang Family! Speaking of which, your sister has a part to play in this … "
Hearing Xing Wei mention Luo Yan, Luo Zheng's sword-like eyebrows jumped up. His eyes were as sharp as swords, and his expression immediately became agitated.
"Don't be agitated. I'm just guessing." Xing Wei smiled mischievously. "If I'm not wrong, it should be Yu Taibai who did it. Otherwise, the Jiang Family wouldn't have allowed the Heavenly Dao in the Great Evolution Universe to be empty for so long …"
Luo Zheng calmed himself down. He was indeed too sensitive. He didn't know where Luo Yan was in the Divine Realm. However, at this moment, he asked another question that puzzled him, "Then why did the Jiang Family send their own disciples to control the Heavenly Dao?"
Sending their own family members to various universes to help other Sages control the Heavenly Dao seemed to be an arduous and thankless task.

Chapter 1727
The Divine Realm had developed for countless Divine Epochs.
It had been so long that it was unimaginably long …
A set of strict rules had been established, and some rules could not be disobeyed even by Saints.
For example, after Mu Haiji attacked him in anger, he received the punishment he deserved. There was no room for disobedience!
Xingwei said that he would help Luo Zheng smuggle out easily. In fact, Luo Xiao's disciples had planned for a long time to force the little girl from the Jiang Family to return.
In fact, Hua Tianming had also entered the Divine Realm through "smuggling."
If they had entered the Divine Realm through normal means, both Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng would have been known by the Mu Family immediately.
The purpose of helping Luo Zheng and the others change their blood was also to avoid being tracked …
If Mu Haiji tracked them down, the ten Daoists would not be able to escape.
"To enter the Divine Realm, we need to smuggle …" Luo Zheng frowned and pondered.
"This is a path that has been set long ago. Even if I die, someone else will help you smuggle out," Xingwei said.
Xingwei was lucky to have kept his life. If he died, the heavy responsibility of helping Luo Zheng and the others smuggle out would naturally fall on the old patriarch and the people from the Demi-Immortal Clan.
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded slightly. It did not matter to Luo Zheng how they entered the Divine Realm.
"If you're ready, you can come and look for me. It's best to enter the Divine Territory as soon as possible. Although they can try their best to delay the Jiang Family, who knows when the Jiang Family will send someone else …" Xingwei added.
"Jiang Yu 'er is a little girl … They won't be so strict, right?" Luo Zheng thought that he had some friendship with Jiang Yu' er.
Unexpectedly, Xingwei smiled faintly. "Don't think that Jiang Yu 'er is easy to talk to. The people who can make the Jiang Family send to guard the Heavenly Dao in the universe are not as simple as you think. Moreover, she is not a little girl. Judging by the lifespan of a True God, she is relatively young. If measured by the time of the Great Evolution Universe, she is more than 20 billion years old."
"…"
"…"
Luo Zheng and the old patriarch were speechless.
Not to mention that Luo Zheng's bone age was only a few decades old, even the old patriarch couldn't be any younger than Jiang Yu 'er.
Twenty billion years old, it had already experienced several epochs.
She had lived for so long, yet she could still communicate with Luo Zheng like a little girl. It was hard to imagine how she had spent all these years …
Now that Luo Zheng had met the requirements to go to God's Domain, all that was left was to settle down his family.
Before this, Luo Zheng had been struggling with the issue of time.
However, in the Immortal Mansion, Luo Zheng found a solution.
Afo told Luo Zheng that after obtaining the last "Kun" token, he could open the Cultivation Pagoda. The law of time in the Cultivation Pagoda was constant. Back then, Gu Bei had cleverly designed the Cultivation Pagoda to be divided into levels by the degree of time compression.
The Cultivation Pagoda was divided into four levels. On the first level, time flowed at a normal rate of one to one. On the second level, time flowed at a rate of one hundred to one. On the third level, time flowed at a rate of one thousand to one. On the fourth level, time flowed at a rate of ten thousand to one. Generally speaking, due to the distortion of the law of time, this kind of space was not suitable for cultivation, because other laws would also be distorted.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had asked the Craftsman Saint to help him temper his physical body. The law of time there was distorted beyond recognition, and it was almost impossible to cultivate in that state.
However, the Cultivation Pagoda was an exception. With Gu Bei's ability, the Cultivation Pagoda was naturally extremely stable. After opening the Cultivation Pagoda, Luo Zheng would have plenty of time to cultivate in the future … According to Afo, the Immortal Mansion was located in the Takiguan Forest, and the Takiguan Forest was a large forbidden area in the Divine Realm.
The flow of time in the Takiguan Forest was the same as the flow of time in the entire Divine Realm. However, after opening the Immortal Mansion's barrier, the flow of time inside matched the flow of time in the Divine Realm. This was because the barrier enveloping the Immortal Mansion isolated the Divine Realm's space and even time!
However, once Luo Zheng obtained the "Kun" token and gathered all eight tokens, he would remove the barrier enveloping the Immortal Mansion. Then, the flow of time in the Immortal Mansion would no longer match. The flow of time in the Immortal Mansion and the Divine Realm would be the same.
In this way, Luo Zheng's only choice was to arrange for his family to live in the Divine Realm.
…
…
During this period of time, every time Luo Zheng stepped into the Immortal Mansion, the Old Man of Evil and the big-mouthed monster would stick to Luo Zheng like flies.
Last time, when the woman in blue returned to the Immortal Mansion, she pierced through the Immortal Mansion's barrier and left on her own.
The Old Man of Evil and the big-mouthed monster naturally wanted to leave the Immortal Mansion as well, but they saw the woman in blue as a snake. When the woman in blue returned to the Immortal Mansion, the two of them had already hidden in the dungeon. When the woman in blue left the Immortal Mansion, the two of them wanted to follow her, but the barrier had automatically closed. With their strength, they didn't have the ability to break this barrier, so they could only wait for Luo Zheng to let them out.
The problem was that Luo Zheng couldn't control the opening and closing of the barrier. He still hadn't obtained the "Kun" token and couldn't completely control the Immortal Mansion.
After being interrogated by the two of them, Luo Zheng wanted to throw them into the Great Evolution Universe and let them use the universe to travel. He didn't expect the two of them to be even more afraid. These universes were right under the eyes of the Saints. How could they leave the Great Evolution Universe? It would be better to force them to die.
Thus, Luo Zheng could only make the two of them wait.
Now, Luo Zheng finally made up his mind to head to the True Path of Extinction again.
Facing the last section of the True Path of Extinction, Luo Zheng was fully prepared. With his current strength, he believed that it wouldn't be difficult to pass through the True Path of Extinction.
However, Gu Bei's "ingenuity" in setting up the final section of the True Path of Extinction made things very difficult for Luo Zheng.
That True Path of Extinction was no longer a path, but a huge maze!
The Divine Clan warriors in this maze weren't very strong. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could easily deal with them.
However, the maze built on the True Path of Extinction was very real. From the beginning, it contained the Dao of the Five Elements. Luo Zheng wandered around in it for dozens of hours before finally returning to the starting point.
Halfway through, Luo Zheng had to leave the True Path of Extinction. This was also the only time that Luo Zheng gave up halfway on the True Path of Extinction.
"I don't know why Master set up such a maze at the end of the True Path of Extinction," Luo Zheng frowned and pondered.
"Because this maze is very important." As Luo Zheng had more tokens, Afo didn't seem to hide anything from Luo Zheng. "Master wants you to remember this maze."
"What's the point?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Who cares what the point is, as long as we can solve this maze!"
"I can't wait any longer. I've wasted another few years here!"
The Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster dreamed of leaving Immortal Mansion. Luo Zheng had finally released them from the dungeon, but they didn't expect to be trapped in the barrier of Immortal Mansion again!
If they weren't afraid of triggering some restrictions in Immortal Mansion, they would have destroyed it.
They were now eagerly watching Luo Zheng break through the True Path of Extinction.
"Because this labyrinth is related to the disappearance of the Sacred Race. This labyrinth is not something that master made up but actually exists," Ah Fu explained. "However, the labyrinth left behind by the Sacred Race is countless times larger and more complex than this one. Back then, master was trapped in the labyrinth for twelve Divine Epochs without being able to pass through it. Afterwards, when he finally managed to leave the labyrinth, he copied a small part of the labyrinth from his memories …"
"Even Master couldn't pass through the Divine Clan's maze?" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a strange look.
"Yes, so this maze does have an exit. Master's goal is for you to firmly engrave this maze in your mind," Afo said.
Since Afo said so, Luo Zheng naturally had no other choice but to go through it again and again. In fact, with Luo Zheng's current memory, he could remember the path no matter how complicated it was. However, this maze was an illusionary type of maze. Once you walked through a path, you would never repeat it a second time!
Now, Luo Zheng could only use the most stupid method, which was to go all the way to the right. In theory, even if it was a complicated maze, he could still walk out by going all the way to the right.
In the end, Luo Zheng spent about four months and after countless failures, he finally passed through the final maze.
Just as Afo said, this maze was already engraved in Luo Zheng's mind. It was just that Luo Zheng couldn't figure out what the use of memorizing the path of this maze was …
After getting the "Kun" token, before Luo Zheng could open the Cultivation Tower, the Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster hurriedly urged Luo Zheng to open the barrier!

Chapter 1728
Before opening the barrier, Luo Zheng made a trip to the weapon workshop and invited Shenzi Lian out of the residence, settling him down in the Rising Heavens Realm for the time being.
Shenzi Lian was quite displeased with Luo Zheng's behavior. He'd been fiddling around in the workshop day and night, but no one knew what he was forging. Naturally, he was displeased that Luo Zheng had interrupted him midway.
However, Luo Zheng had done so just in case.
Second-level lifeforms would disintegrate when they stepped into the Divine Realm. Shenzi Lian's fate would probably be the same if he stepped into the Divine Realm as a puppet.
It could even be said that all matter in the universe would disintegrate and dissipate when brought into the Divine Realm.
If he didn't remove the barrier, the power of the Divine Realm would probably disintegrate even his iron shell and soul.
After chasing Shenzi Lian away, Luo Zheng returned to the residence.
According to Afo's instructions, he placed the eight tokens neatly together.
As Luo Zheng activated the tokens one by one, the eight tokens seemed to come alive. They hovered in the air for a while before finally arranging themselves according to their order.
The order was Qian, Kun, Zhen, Xun, Kan, Li, Gen, and Dui.
The tokens connected head to tail in the air and finally formed a ring. Black and white light flashed from the ring. It was the Yin and Yang Qi that Luo Zheng had encountered on the True Extinction Path. The Yin and Yang Qi spiraled and blended together, then became distinct and finally formed a perfect eight trigrams.
The eight trigrams floated slowly in front of Luo Zheng.
"Put your hand in it," Afo said.
Not far away, Old Man Evil and the big-mouthed monster's eyes glinted with greed when they saw this.
Controlling the eight trigrams meant controlling the entire residence!
This was the immortal abode left behind by Saint Gu Bei. They might not be clear about the mysteries and various resources within, but just the dungeon that had trapped them for countless years had left an indelible impression on them. How could the other places be any worse than that?
However, this greed only lasted for a moment before it faded away.
Not to mention whether or not they would really be recognized by this Eight Trigrams, they had previously sworn an oath of the Heart Demon in front of Luo Zheng. If they casually violated it, it would probably cast a shadow over their future lives. Moreover, Gu Bei was not Luo Xiao. Gu Bei was not dead. If this Saint came back at any time, even if they occupied the Immortal Mansion, they would not be able to resist the Saint … That would be purely courting death.
Thinking that they would soon be able to escape, they suppressed the greed in their hearts.
The moment Luo Zheng put his hand into the Eight Trigrams, the Eight Trigrams slowly rotated around Luo Zheng's arm. The eight tokens also rotated along Luo Zheng's arm all the way up to Luo Zheng's head.
From afar, it was as if Luo Zheng was wearing a strange hat.
Not long after, the Eight Trigrams and the tokens began to slowly dissipate. They merged with Luo Zheng's mind and fused with Luo Zheng's soul.
While fusing with the tokens, Luo Zheng sighed. Many things had stayed in his mind. Now that the Ancestral Gragon and Xun had left, there was the Divine Creation Technique and the Immeasurable Ruler whose use was unknown. Now, this Eight Trigrams had once again entered his mind …
After the Eight Trigrams merged with his soul, Luo Zheng immediately felt that his soul was connected with the Immortal Mansion. Everything in the Immortal Mansion seemed to have come to life out of thin air, just like his arms and legs. With a thought, he could open any door in the Immortal Mansion!
"These formations …"
"Flying Dragon Formation, Tripartite Formation, Divine Scorpion Formation …"
Only now did Luo Zheng realize that there were several extremely powerful restrictions and formations in the Immortal Mansion. These restrictions and formations were very hidden. Previously, Luo Zheng's range of movement in the Immortal Mansion was limited, so he did not notice them.
Because Luo Zheng could freely control these formations and restrictions, Luo Zheng was even a little puzzled.
Only a small portion of the restrictions and formations in the Immortal Mansion were used to defend against external enemies, but most of them faced the interior of the Immortal Mansion. To be precise, they faced the depths of the Immortal Mansion!
Realizing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression once more.
If that was the case, was there something incredible suppressed in the depths of the Immortal Mansion?
"Luo Zheng, can you hurry up?"
"If there's a problem, we can talk about it later. Can you let us out first …?"
Old Man Extreme Evil and the big-mouthed freak said in almost pleading voices.
Seeing their appearance, Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. He wanted to ask Afo, but he was afraid that the things beneath the Immortal Mansion were too important to mention in front of Old Man Extreme Evil and the big-mouthed freak. He could only suppress his curiosity and say, "Okay, I'll open it for you now!"
Opening the Immortal Mansion's barrier meant that Luo Zheng had truly stepped into the Realm of the Gods!
But just as Old Man Extreme Evil said, the Takeshi Swirl Forest was an extremely dangerous forbidden area. Even with their strength, they had to be careful in this forest. The slightest carelessness could lead to death. Naturally, Luo Zheng could not use the Takeshi Swirl Forest.
When it was time to open the barrier, Luo Zheng was still a little nervous.
When he first ascended from the lower realm to the upper realm, the chaotic spiritual energy of heaven and earth had already made Luo Zheng uncomfortable. The Realm of the Gods was a "real world" for second-level life forms. What kind of pressure would it bring him?
"Then hurry up …" The big-mouthed freak saw that Luo Zheng was not moving and urged him again.
Luo Zheng nodded and closed his eyes.
Gu Bei had named the pink enchantment that protected the entire Immortal Abode the "Bubble Enchantment". This name was not hard to understand. The space within this pink enchantment was a separate entity. It could become the space of the divine domain, and it could also become the space of the universe. It could even act as a transition between two spaces...
To be able to arrange such a barrier, it also showed that Gu Bei was indeed a rare super genius. Although this bubble barrier did not cover a large area, the significance of its existence was actually equivalent to a miniature universe!
"Buzz buzz …"
The pink "bubble" covering the Immortal Mansion swayed slightly. Like a huge soap bubble, it burst …
The moment it burst, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. This was the first time he felt the aura of the Realm of the Gods. But he himself did not feel anything special. It seemed that the Realm of the Gods was also sparse and ordinary.
But in the blink of an eye, he suddenly felt a trace of strangeness coming from his finger!
"Bang …"
A crack suddenly appeared on the surface of one of his sumeru rings. The ring directly broke and jumped away from Luo Zheng's hand. At the same time, the ring turned into a boundless white mist!
This sumeru ring was actually cracking on its own!
"Oh no, I forgot that this sumeru ring was also brought from the universe!"
Unfortunately, it was useless at this moment. The sumeru ring turned into a cloud of white mist in the blink of an eye and dissipated in all directions.
The moment the sumeru ring cracked, an even more bizarre scene appeared.
Generally speaking, if the sumeru ring was damaged, the space inside would also collapse. The things hidden in the sumeru space would be directly lost because after the space collapsed, they would never be found again.
Now that the sumeru ring had cracked, even the "space" in it was also brought from the universe. Therefore, the space did not collapse, but it also cracked. Therefore, the pile of messy things that Luo Zheng still had in the sumeru ring also rushed out in an instant!
Various grades of Celestial Weapons, Sacred Weapons, sabers, swords, bows, and so on were squeezed out by the cracked space and scattered on the ground in front of Luo Zheng …
Fortunately, as a precaution, Luo Zheng had previously placed some important items in the Rising Sky Realm and did not bring them to the Immortal Mansion. The items stored in the sumeru ring were not valuable.
Mottled cracks began to appear on the sabers and swords. White mist constantly escaped from them and just like that, they disappeared in front of Luo Zheng.
The only things that did not disappear were the Da Qian Heavy Sword and its scabbard that Luo Zheng carried with him. This was because the scabbard and the sword were forged by Refined God Child using materials from the Immortal Mansion's Equipment Refining Workshop. These materials were originally taken from the Divine Territory, so they could not be restored.
Old Man Extreme Evil and the Big Mouth Monster were overjoyed when they saw that the barrier had disappeared.
They cupped their fists and thanked Luo Zheng. Then, they prepared to leave amidst a burst of laughter.

Chapter 1729
Once the boundary was opened, there was nothing that could stop them. Even though the Takeshi Xuan Forest was full of dangers, as long as they did not seek death, it was not very dangerous for them!
"Is there anything else?" Old Man Extreme Evil asked with a smile.
"You two should remember that you promised to help me with something," Luo Zheng said.
He had forced them to swear an oath of their inner demons in order to fight against the True God in the Dayan Universe. These two were stronger than Zhan Beihai but weaker than the woman in blue, so they did not fight in the end.
"Of course we remember." Old Man Extreme Evil laughed. "If we meet in the divine territory in the future, Luo Zheng, if you need anything, I, Old Man Extreme Evil, will naturally help!"
"That's right, that's right!" The big-mouthed monster nodded hurriedly.
But that was not what they were thinking. The divine territory was so vast. If they escaped from Immortal Mansion this time, they might never have the chance to meet Luo Zheng again …
Luo Zheng shrugged and smiled. "Since you two are so understanding, I'm relieved."
The two of them did not hesitate in the slightest as they strode towards the entrance of the Immortal Mansion …
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's eyes widened! Staring at their backs, he asked in a daze, "They … can't fly?"
Seeing Luo Zheng's confusion, Afo seemed to have expected it. He smiled. "Of course they can't fly. If you don't believe me, you can try it."
Flying in the universe was a relatively easy thing to do.
After cultivating to the Divine Reflection Realm in the lower realm, one had to use their vital essence to merge with the wind and soar into the sky. At a higher level, it was even more incredible. One could travel through space, teleport, and cross millions of miles in one step …
But in the divine territory, it was not so simple.
Hearing Afo's words, Luo Zheng condensed the aura of chaos and tried to fly …
But the moment he condensed the wind element law, the power of the law instantly dissipated, as if an invisible force had imprisoned him in place.
Luo Zheng originally thought that he would float with the wind, but now his body was immovable!
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a touch of surprise. He waved his hand again and wanted to travel through space!
However, this time was the same as before. Whether it was the Wind Laws or the Space Laws, they were all extinguished in a flash. It was as if there was a mysterious force that extinguished the power of Laws that Luo Zheng cultivated!
"It's useless," Afo said flatly. "The space in the divine realm is very different from the space you know. In fact, the universe you secondary lifeforms live in is just a space created by the saints. It's full of unreality, just like how people can do everything in their dreams. The divine realm is the real world!"
When the Saints were constructing the universe, they were naturally filled with many beautiful imaginations and did not impose any restrictions on the laws.
The universe that secondary lifeforms lived in was equivalent to the ocean. All kinds of lifeforms could swim freely in the ocean, and even newborn fish could float in the air. But now, Luo Zheng was like a lifeform that walked on land for the first time. When he jumped up again, he found that he could not float like he did in the ocean …
Luo Zheng was still unconvinced. At the same time, he was also puzzled. "But the laws I cultivate are inherited from the saints. Why is it ineffective here?"
In the Universe of Dayan, whether it was a Celestial Venerable or a Realm Lord, they all had a deep understanding of the laws.
This understanding came directly from the Lord of Dayan, which was Luo Xiao's understanding of the laws. Since Luo Xiao could use the power of the laws in the divine realm, why couldn't the secondary lifeforms who obtained his inheritance?
"It's not ineffective," Afo said flatly. "If you don't believe me, you can try to condense a flame."
At Afo's words, Luo Zheng reached out and flicked his finger. A fireball rolled out from his fingertip and kept rolling forward. After rolling for several hundred feet, it suddenly exploded …
"Boom!"
After the fireball exploded, it formed a raging fire that illuminated Luo Zheng's face. Luo Zheng stared at the fire and murmured, "So it's the laws of wind and space that are being suppressed?"
Generally, when a cultivator flew through the air, they used the Wind Laws, and when they traveled through space, they used the Space Laws. Although these two Laws were not particularly useful in defending against enemies, they were still indispensable to a cultivator.
"There's also the laws of time. These three laws are being suppressed in the divine realm. This is the restriction of the rules on the stone slab," Afo reminded him.
"Saints can't fly?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Afo shrugged. "Not really. In fact, when you cultivate to the upper level of True God, you can resist this suppression and fly. That Evil Old Man and the Big Mouth Monster are both at the peak of the middle level of True God, but they can't resist this suppression. Naturally, they can only run on the ground …"
"Understood." Luo Zheng nodded.
As soon as he finished speaking, he suddenly heard a sharp whistling sound coming from outside the Immortal Mansion.
"Dong dong dong …"
Following that whistling sound, the ground under Luo Zheng's feet began to tremble!
Everything in this Takeshi Xuan Forest was inconceivably huge. Moreover, the divine realm's heaven and earth were extremely stable. Something that could cause such a disturbance must be abnormally powerful.
Soon, Luo Zheng's gaze passed through the tall eaves of the Immortal Mansion and saw a huge white ape!
The giant white ape was about two thousand feet tall. It stood at the edge of the outer wall of the immortal abode, its slender and powerful arms dangling on the ground. It looked at Luo Zheng with its yellow eyes and opened its bloody mouth slightly. "Awooo, Awooo" sounds came out of its mouth.
The moment Luo Zheng was stared at by this giant ape, he felt as if his entire body was bearing the weight of millions of pounds. Even moving was very difficult!
"Be careful, every vicious creature in this Takeshi Xuan Forest is quite powerful. Ordinary True Gods are not their opponents. These white apes … are summoning their own kind. It may attack at any time." Afo cautiously whispered to Luo Zheng, "You should release Blackie …"
The range of the Immortal Mansion's "Bubble Barrier" was quite large. This white ape had already rushed to the edge of the Immortal Mansion, so it was useless to open the barrier.
When Luo Zheng first stepped into the Immortal Mansion, he had encountered a huge white tiger at the edge of the barrier. Fortunately, this "Bubble Barrier" could divide the divine realm's space into two worlds. Otherwise, with Luo Zheng's strength at that time, a single glare from that white tiger was enough to destroy him thousands of times over …
Even the current Luo Zheng felt a strong pressure when facing this white ape!
Luo Zheng's face was expressionless, but a thought flashed through his mind. Now that Luo Zheng was in complete control of the Immortal Mansion, a single thought from him could affect the entire Immortal Mansion!
"Clang …"
In the backyard of the Immortal Mansion, the Immortal Essence Rope binding Blackie quietly broke.
The lion-sized Soul Mastiff rushed out of the dog house, turned into a black streak of light, and quickly rushed up the walls of the Immortal Mansion. It was shuttling through the roof of the Immortal Mansion at an extremely fast speed!
"Awoo!"
Probably sensing the Soul Mastiff, the white ape that was originally facing off against Luo Zheng suddenly waved its hands. A pair of hands hundreds of meters long reached into the Immortal Mansion and grabbed at Luo Zheng!
"Retreat," Afo said.
Although Luo Zheng felt a great pressure, seeing the huge hairy hands grabbing at him, he naturally understood that it was best to retreat first!
Unable to fly, Luo Zheng could only jump backward. His whole body rose from the ground, drawing an arc in the air, and was about to crash into the pond in the Immortal Mansion …
The white ape's reaction was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. The moment Luo Zheng jumped, the huge hands changed direction and suddenly grabbed at the air, grabbing Luo Zheng. Then the white ape dragged him out of the Immortal Mansion!
The Takeshi Xuan Forest was a forbidden area for True Gods. Not only were there these white apes, there were also some unimaginably ferocious creatures. If Luo Zheng was really caught in the Takeshi Xuan Forest, it would be difficult for him to protect himself with his current strength.
But before the white ape's hands could retract, a black shadow suddenly jumped up from the roof of the crafting workshop. The Soul Mastiff let out a series of roars in the air!
"Woof woof woof woof!"
The power contained in the roars could make many creatures feel fear, and the white ape was no exception. The white ape's huge hands that grabbed Luo Zheng involuntarily trembled.

Chapter 1730
This Soul-Shocking Mastiff was much smaller than the white ape, but as a True Spirit, it was extremely powerful!
He bit down, and a crisp "Kacha" sound was heard!
The thick ape arm was bitten off, and Luo Zheng naturally fell from the sky with the broken arm.
"Awoo!"
The giant white ape howled in pain. Flames of fury shot out from the round eyes on its ape face. It swung its other arm and slapped the Soul-Shocking Mastiff!
But the Soul-Shocking Mastiff was extremely agile.
After biting off the white ape's arm, it jumped again and dodged the white ape's slap. Then it climbed up the white ape's remaining arm. When it reached the white ape's shoulder, it pounced again and opened its mouth to bite the white ape's throat!

With a loud noise, the Soul-Shocking Mastiff screamed and was directly smashed into Immortal Mansion from the sky …
Outside Immortal Mansion, another giant ape appeared!

Although the Soul-Shocking Mastiff was hit by the second white ape, the moment it landed, it took advantage of the momentum to roll on the ground. It lowered its body and vigilantly looked at the two giant apes outside Immortal Mansion, showing two slender fangs and growling in a low voice, but it didn't dare to pounce again.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had just struggled out of the white ape's broken arm. Seeing the Soul-Shocking Mastiff's cowardice, his face showed a strange expression.
In Luo Zheng's view, this "Big Black" should be extremely powerful. Although the two giant apes were powerful, it shouldn't be afraid, but now Big Black didn't dare to rush out of Immortal Mansion.
But in the next moment, Luo Zheng realized what the Soul-Shocking Mastiff was afraid of!
Outside the immortal abode, giant apes popped their heads out from the outer walls. In just a short while, there were actually more than ten of them, and each of these giant apes was bigger than the last!
The first Giant Ape that attacked Luo Zheng was completely white. The second Giant Ape had a tuft of flame-like hair on its head. It was three times bigger than the first Giant Ape, and its strength was also much stronger. The Giant Apes that appeared later were even more ridiculously huge, and their heads were covered with red hair.
The first giant ape was merely a cub of this kind of ape. These red-haired giant apes stood outside the immortal mansion like a row of tall mountain ranges, tightly surrounding one side of the immortal mansion!
"Yo!"
After these gigantic apes appeared, gigantic black birds appeared in the sky. These black birds had four pairs of wings, and the feathers on their bodies were like long needles. It was difficult to condense Wind Laws to fly in the Divine Domain, but these gigantic birds relied on their enormous wings.

There were at least fifty to sixty giant black birds circling above the immortal abode. Judging from the aura emanating from them, they were not any weaker than the giant apes.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
After the appearance of the giant black birds, a dense swarm of giant gray insects appeared.
These giant gray insects were not ridiculously huge like the black birds and giant apes, but each one was as tall as a person. They were so densely packed that they blotted out the sky and covered the earth. Looking from afar, they looked like a huge gray cloud, making one's scalp tingle.
In the center of these giant gray insects, there was a giant crimson insect, being carried by other giant gray insects. That crimson insect had a pair of compound eyes, staring at Luo Zheng from afar, as if thinking about something. Clearly, whether it was the giant apes, the black birds, or the insects in this Takeshi Spiral Forest, they all had extremely high intelligence.
After all, Luo Zheng had just taken control of this immortal abode. The aura emanating from these strange creatures of the Divine Domain was almost comparable to that of a True God. Faced with this scene, he could not help but feel a little flustered …
"These guys are still thinking about what's under Immortal Abode," Afo thought to himself. At this moment, he said to Luo Zheng, "Master, just activate all the killing formations in Immortal Abode." Previously, he had wanted to guide Luo Zheng to familiarize himself with how to control Immortal Abode, so he had let Luo Zheng release Big Black. Unexpectedly, as soon as the bubble barrier enveloping Immortal Abode was opened, these monsters rushed out together. Their reaction was really fast. After all, it had been countless years since the bubble barrier last opened …
"Yes! Those formations! "
This was the first time Luo Zheng had taken full control of Immortal Abode, so after all, he was still a little unfamiliar with it. After hearing Afo's reminder, Luo Zheng suddenly realized.
