"Awoo!"
The leading white ape suddenly howled. It spread its arms and was about to climb over the outer wall of Immortal Abode, causing the black mass of strange birds and giant insects to become restless.

Afo's expression changed, but he hurriedly said.

"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
These rainbow-colored sword shadows were extremely fast and almost indestructible. When they hit the white ape's body, they left a bloody hole. If they hit the giant gray worms, they would directly turn them into dust!

"Awoo!"

All kinds of screams rose and fell in the Immortal Abode. All kinds of monsters died tragically under the streaks of sword shadows!

Several streaks of sword shadows hit the crimson giant insect from a distance, but produced a crisp sound of metal clashing. The crimson giant insect was actually able to block the attack of the streaks of sword shadows!

But when they crossed the square of Immortal Abode and rushed into the inner courtyard, an illusory little person and a sledgehammer emerged from the pavilion. "Clang!"
The sledgehammer seemed to hit a large gong, producing a loud bang. At the same time, an invisible torrent of power erupted and spread in all directions.
This power had no effect on Luo Zheng, Afo, and Blackie, but it seemed to have a miraculous effect on the monsters in the Takeshi Vortex Forest. Those monsters fell into the torrent of power and were quickly pushed out of Immortal Abode by the invisible torrent!
"Squeak squeak squeak squeak …"
The crimson giant insect was not willing to be driven out. It was keenly aware of Luo Zheng's existence and seemed to know that Luo Zheng was the key figure in Immortal Abode. At this time, it spread out its six pairs of bright red wings and rushed toward Luo Zheng against the torrent of power. But the invisible power was very vast. Its flight path in the air was also crooked, but its speed was still astonishing. In the blink of an eye, it was only two or three feet away from Luo Zheng!
At this distance, Luo Zheng could clearly see its sickle-like mouth and smell the fishy smell of the insect.
When the crimson giant insect opened its mouth and wanted to bite off Luo Zheng's head, a rainbow-colored light flew again …
It turned out that the rainbow-colored sword was constantly shooting out sword shadows to kill those monsters, but most of the monsters were pushed out of Immortal Abode by the torrent of power. The rainbow-colored sword naturally aimed at the crimson giant insect!
"Clang clang clang clang clang …"
The crimson giant insect's surface instantly emitted an arc of blood light, blocking the rainbow-colored sword. But its body was involuntarily forced back by the sword shadows, and it was getting farther and farther away from Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng, who finally reacted, turned around and ran. He quickly rushed into the pavilion.
"Clang!"
Accompanied by a second loud noise at the top of the pavilion, another torrent of power rushed in all directions. The crimson giant insect wanted to resist the rainbow-colored sword shadows, but it was no longer able to resist the torrent of power. Its body, which was taller than a person, involuntarily rolled in the air and was thrown out of Immortal Abode in the blink of an eye!

Chapter 1731
The killing formations in the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion were triggered in a certain order.
But Luo Zheng was in a panic, and there seemed to be a few powerful killing formations that had yet to be activated.
But the seven-colored sword and the sledgehammer on the pavilion's roof had already driven these monsters away, leaving behind only the corpses of birds, insects, and giant apes …
Although those monsters were driven out of the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion, they still kept circling around, unwilling to leave for a long time!
Especially that crimson giant insect, it kept staring at Luo Zheng with its pair of bright red compound eyes, and at the same time made a hissing sound. It was actually communicating with Luo Zheng outside the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion!
When that sound rang out, a strange feeling arose in Luo Zheng's heart.
He obviously couldn't understand what the insect was saying, but he could understand it in his heart. This seemed to be a unique way of communication!
"Release the thing underground, and we will let you leave the Takeshi Xuan Forest. We won't make things difficult for you, and we can also give you three Spirit-Splitting Fruits. This is a very good deal for you," the crimson giant insect said to Luo Zheng.
Feeling this strange message, Luo Zheng's face was full of confusion.

"What is buried under the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Previously, he had discovered that there were many prohibitions and killing formations in the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion, and they were all set up for things below the Holy Realm Dweller's mansion. These monsters were comparable to True Gods, and it must not have been easy for them to grow to such a level.
Afo took a deep breath, but shook his head, "Letting you know now won't do you any good. This thing is even related to the ranking of the floating islands in the Sea of Time. You will know in the future when it's time for you to know. For now, let these monsters leave quickly!"
If it was in the past, Luo Zheng would definitely keep asking questions he was curious about.
However, after experiencing so much, Luo Zheng also understood that it was not necessarily a good thing for him to know many things in advance. He only needed to follow his own path to the end. If he knew too much, he could not solve it with his current ability. It was meaningless except for adding to his troubles.
Therefore, Luo Zheng shook his head at the crimson giant worm outside the immortal mansion and said softly, "I refuse."
His voice was very low, and he didn't use a true essence sound transmission. However, that crimson giant insect seemed to have a powerful innate ability to understand. It could only look at Luo Zheng with its compound eyes and then open its shell, spreading its wings and leading the countless gray insects away.
"Ahhhhh …"
"Yo! Yo … "
Those Giant Apes and Black Birds also let out resounding cries and left in an instant.
Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief...
In God's Domain, just a single forest was hiding such powerful monsters. Moreover, there were so many of them.
Indeed, it was as his father had said. The road to God's Domain would only be more difficult.
Previously, Luo Zheng could not understand. Since his father was a Saint, why would he create so many difficulties for him, and even let him experience countless life and death situations? Now, Luo Zheng finally understood.
In the universe, Luo Xiao could indeed help Luo Zheng rise to the top, and he could also help Luo Zheng walk on a smooth path.
However, Luo Xiao could not protect him forever. After reaching God's Domain, he could only rely on himself for everything. If he did not give Luo Zheng so many life and death situations, with Luo Zheng's mental state, it would be very difficult for him to establish himself in God's Domain!
From this point, it could be seen that Luo Xiao had indeed given a lot of thought …
"Why can't I open this bubble enchantment again?"
Luo Zheng could also feel the existence of the bubble enchantment in his mind, but he sensed that this enchantment was in a state where it could not be used.
"This bubble enchantment is equivalent to cutting off a unique space in God's Domain. Naturally, it cannot be opened and closed at will like ordinary enchantments," Afo explained. In order to show Kubei's superb skills, he continued, "It was already very difficult for Master to make this enchantment back then."
After all, this bubble enchantment could even prevent secondary creatures from splitting and returning to their original state. This was equivalent to erasing the rules of God's Domain to a certain extent. Although it was only limited to God's Domain, it was already extremely valuable.
Luo Zheng nodded to show that he understood. He still planned to bring his family into God's Domain, so he had to completely restore this bubble enchantment.
He himself was fine, but it would be a tragedy if the others went in together and disappeared before his eyes.

With such misgivings, sending his family into the Immortal Mansion was probably a foolish choice.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng also asked Afo about this concern.

If the enchantment is broken or the enchantment has to be removed, they can hide in the Cultivation Pagoda!"

That Mu Haiji may not let it go. In the future, he may use other means to endanger the universe. Leaving your family in the Universe of Dayan will be even more dangerous."
Afo's words comforted Luo Zheng a little. There was no such thing as an absolutely safe place in the world. Even Saints had people they couldn't protect, not to mention that Luo Zheng's current strength was far from that level. He could only do his best. At least from the power displayed by this God's Domain, only those at the level of the blue-clothed woman could break in. However, whether the blue-clothed woman could resist the various killing formations in the God's Domain was still uncertain.
After making up his mind, Luo Zheng returned to the Universe of Dayan from the God's Domain.
After staying in the Rising Sky Saint Land for a while, Luo Zheng sent Ning Yudie, Luo Nian, Xun, Mu Mingxue, and the others into the God's Domain. When Xi Youqin learned that Luo Zheng was preparing to enter the Divine Territory, she also clamored to enter the God's Domain with him. After all, Luo Zheng could 'return' to the God's Domain at any time. If she stayed in the God's Domain, she could still see Luo Zheng.

Naturally, Xing Wei could not let Xi Youqin stay in the God's Domain like this. Instead, she urged her to continue cultivating so that she could step into the True God Realm one day.
Luo Zheng also agreed with Xing Wei's approach. Because of this, Xi Youqin and Luo Zheng argued for a long time. It took a lot of effort to appease them, but it took a lot of time to prepare.
About one and a half months later, Luo Zheng finally settled everything and went to look for Xing Wei.
Since Xingwei's physical body had been destroyed, he was no longer able to travel freely in the universe. Therefore, the old patriarch showed up and led Luo Zheng to travel through the universe. When the Old Patriarch stopped, Luo Zheng asked, "Did Hua Tianming also sneak in from here?"
Xing Wei shook his head. "He didn't take the same route as you."
After saying that, Star Tail slowly walked to the front of the Wall of Sighs. He reached out his hand and placed it on the Wall of Sighs. A red light slowly spread out from his hand. When the red light spread out, the Wall of Sighs seemed to melt, forming a passage.

Chapter 1732
Before stepping into the passage, Xingwei gave him some key information. "In theory, Daozi are allowed to enter the divine realm. However, if they try to sneak in, they'll be punished by the divine realm. That's why very few second-level lifeforms have chosen to sneak in."
"Sneaking in will be punished?" Luo Zheng was stunned. "But I was in the Immortal Mansion …"
"That's because you didn't leave the Takiguan Forest. If you used the Northern Sage's Immortal Mansion to enter the divine realm, you'd still be punished even if you could safely leave the forest," Xingwei said. "This is the rule that the Jiang Family modified."
After the Jiang Family took control of ascension, they helped the Saints of the Sage Halls guide the second-level lifeforms who ascended. Everyone who ascended to the divine realm was recorded in the records.
However, the ascenders of the universe were a precious resource to the various Saints. After all, the martial artists that ascended from their own universe were all True Gods that relied on themselves to condense their own Godhood. This type of True God was also a very popular genius in the Realm of the Gods.
However, after countless years of development, the Sage Hall had come up with a set of detailed rules to balance the benefits of the various great clans and Sages. These Ascenders were distributed according to the rankings of the floating islands. Some Sages were nurtured in their own internal worlds, but they were being fought over by others. Naturally, they were unhappy.
Thus, for a period of time, a large number of stowaways appeared in the divine realm!
There were even Saints who directly brought their Daozi from their internal worlds into the divine realm to nurture.
The Jiang Family was so angry that they directly modified the rules on the slate to restrict stowaways.
Any martial artist who dared to sneak into the divine realm would definitely be punished by the slate. After that, very few second-level lifeforms dared to sneak in. After all, the slate's rules were irresistible. The price of stowaways was too high. If it was light, they'd lose all their strength. If it was heavy, they'd die.
"There's such a serious matter … Why did you only tell me now?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
Xingwei smiled. "Master has already considered this problem."
Luo Zheng's brain worked quickly. "You mean my bloodline?"
"Mm." Star Tail nodded. "The bloodlines passed down from the Demi-Immortal Clan were prepared for Dao Children like you. They can help you evade detection and avoid punishment, but they can also lead to unpredictable consequences."
If he hadn't changed Luo Zheng's blood, Mu Haiji would have found out the moment Luo Zheng stepped into Divine Realm. The consequences could be imagined.
Luo Zheng didn't know that the Golden Bloodline had such an effect, so he asked, "What are the unpredictable consequences …"
Startail shook his head. "That's up to fate. After all, it's not easy to avoid the punishment of the slate. Even Mu Haiji couldn't do it!"
"Even Saints can't do it?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
Xingwei smiled as if it was only natural. "This might be the only chance for you. You don't know how much effort your father put in to achieve this!"
Luo Zheng nodded silently, with a glimmer of light in his eyes. "I see."
After bidding farewell to Star Tail and the old patriarch, Luo Zheng stepped into the passage.
This passage didn't break through the Wall of Sighs. It was just a very special space passage built on the Wall of Sighs.
The Laws within were completely different from the Laws of Space that Luo Zheng had come into contact with. He couldn't even sense any fluctuations of the Laws.
The moment Luo Zheng stepped into the passage, he felt an irresistible force wrap around him. At the same time, he was constantly pulled into the depths of the passage. His whole body spun rapidly, like a top.
During this process, Luo Zheng tried to control his body and stop spinning. However, he soon found that he couldn't use the Laws of Space, nor could he fly in the passage.
Thus, Luo Zheng understood that after stepping into the passage, he had actually entered Divine Realm. After all, this situation was exactly the same as the Divine Realm he had felt in Immortal Mansion!
Thus, Luo Zheng could only spin along with the passage, drifting with the flow.
Regardless of whether it was the sense of space or time, Luo Zheng's mind gradually became fuzzy.
He felt that he had experienced an instant, but also felt that a long time had passed, and that he had traversed an endless path …
After an unknown period of time, Luo Zheng suddenly felt his body become lighter. The force that wrapped around him suddenly disappeared. Then, with a "bang", he was suddenly thrown out and fell rapidly!
"Midair?"
"Vegetable field!"
Luo Zheng's reaction was extremely fast. Although he fell down and the world spun, he could still see the surrounding scenery. Below him was a square vegetable field!
"Boom!"
Not long after, Luo Zheng smashed into the vegetable field.
With Luo Zheng's body, if he smashed into the vegetable field, he could probably create a huge hole with a radius of ten thousand zhang!
However, that was in the universe!
The universe was constructed by true essence, while the Divine Realm was constructed by primal chaos.
The difference between the two was like the difference between a second-level life form and a level-level life form. The materials in the Divine Realm were naturally different.
When Luo Zheng smashed down, he only created a small hole and crushed a few seedlings in the vegetable field.
With the strength of Luo Zheng's body, he naturally didn't have any problems. However, he still had a very deep impression of this hard vegetable field.
Previously, he thought that only the Takeshi Xuan Forest was so hard. Now, he understood that perhaps the entire Divine Realm was similar …
Just as Luo Zheng fell down, in the depths of the Divine Realm, on a stone slab behind the Templars, a line of small characters suddenly flickered with a faint halo. After this halo flashed, it transformed into a palm print and shot out from the stone slab!
After that lightning shot out, voices of astonishment once again sounded from the depths of the Divine Realm.
"What's going on? Why did the stone slab send down a punishment? "
"The 833rd law has been activated. Someone has snuck in from a certain universe …"
"How strange, this is already the second one!"
"…"
After that palm print shot out, it disappeared into the sea of time and quietly escaped through the vast space of the Divine Realm. Soon, it appeared in a certain region …
"Crackle!"
Luo Zheng had just recovered from his shock when he saw a huge palm print suddenly appear in the sky!
When he noticed this lightning, he was so scared that he broke out in a cold sweat. This black palm print contained enough power to kill him a thousand times over!
Thus, when he saw this lightning, he silently mourned in his heart. This time, he was probably finished. How could he be so unlucky as to be slapped to death by this palm print just as he entered the Divine Realm?
After this palm print appeared, it directly slapped down on Luo Zheng. However, halfway through, Luo Zheng felt his body suddenly warm up. The golden bloodline within his body was burning!
At the same time, the palm print seemed to have lost its aim. It was like a headless fly, constantly circling in the air dozens of miles above Luo Zheng's head. However, it was unable to lock onto Luo Zheng!
"So that's how it is!"
Luo Zheng looked up at the sky with a smile on his face.
He instantly understood that Xingwei was right. This golden bloodline could indeed help him escape punishment …
This black palm print circled for a while but still couldn't find Luo Zheng. After a while, the palm print turned into a black ball and fell from the sky!
"Oh no!"
When Luo Zheng saw this, he knew that things weren't looking good. He turned around and fled to the other side of the vegetable field.
The moment the black ball hit the ground, it exploded. From the black ball, black threads as thick as hair shot out and intertwined into a huge net that spread out in all directions. This net spread out extremely quickly. Even Luo Zheng couldn't escape!
"Swish swish swish …"
The hair-like black threads sank into Luo Zheng's body.
These black threads didn't cause any substantial damage to Luo Zheng. However, they tightly wrapped around his dantian. After his dantian was wrapped, he suddenly felt that he had lost contact with his inner world!
"This …"
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a headache coming on.
Although Xingwei warned him that there might be unpredictable consequences, Luo Zheng was still mentally prepared.

Chapter 1733
Those black threads were like dense strands of hair. Some of them merged into Luo Zheng's body and wound around his dantian, while the rest of them disappeared into thin air.
Luo Zheng looked at the blue sky and let out a long sigh.
Although it was rather depressing to have his dantian sealed, in the end, he didn't lose his life. It could be considered a blessing in disguise.
At this moment, Luo Zheng raised his head and looked into the distance. He discovered that this vegetable field was larger than he had imagined. These lush green seedlings stretched far into the distance. It was as vast as the ocean, and he couldn't see the end of it.
And he himself was standing in the middle of this lush green ocean …
"What the hell is this place?" Luo Zheng curled his lips.
The Realm of the Gods was a place ruled by True Gods and Saints, but there were also some weak mortals. The vegetables planted in this vegetable field were presumably for mortals to eat, but how could ordinary mortals cultivate the land here?
Although Luo Zheng was stepping on soil, it belonged to the Realm of the Gods. Not to mention ordinary mortals, even Divine Sea Realm or Divine Reaching Realm martial artists would have to expend a considerable amount of effort to cultivate this vegetable field. Ordinary mortals wouldn't even be able to lift a single piece of soil!
"Then these seedlings …"
He bent down and reached out to grab an emerald green seedling. He couldn't identify what kind of plant this seedling was, but when he casually pinched it, he unexpectedly didn't break it. It wasn't until Luo Zheng used a bit of strength that he heard a crisp "crack" sound, that the seedling was forcibly broken.
"This unremarkable seedling is even tougher than a Divine Tool in the universe …"
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng concluded that these things weren't for ordinary mortals to eat. What kind of mortal could chew a Divine Tool to pieces?
Now, Luo Zheng was a bit regretful. His understanding of the Realm of the Gods was still too little. If he had known this would happen, he would have asked Xingwei about the local customs and practices of the Realm of the Gods. It would have been better than knowing nothing …
Luo Zheng chose a direction and then moved forward along this boundless vegetable field.
Although one couldn't fly or travel through space in God's Domain, Luo Zheng had an extremely strong physique. His running speed wasn't too slow.
However, what Luo Zheng didn't expect was that even after running for six to eight hours and covering at least ten thousand li, there was still a lush green vegetable field in front of him. It seemed that this vegetable field would never end!
Looking at the boundless vegetable field, Luo Zheng wanted to cry but had no tears. No matter how he looked at it, he felt that this world was unreal. It was as if he had fallen into some absurd illusion. However, this place was the real Domain of the Gods …
After adjusting his mood, Luo Zheng didn't stop and continued on his way.
No matter how large this vegetable field was, he believed that it would eventually have its boundaries. It was just a matter of expending a little more effort!
As he moved forward, Luo Zheng constantly observed the black lines that wrapped around his dantian.
At first, Luo Zheng wanted to use his strength to remove these black lines. However, the dantian was originally the most important and also the weakest part of a martial artist. Luo Zheng was very careful even when he used his strength. Only when Luo Zheng used his strength did he discover that these black lines seemed to have become one with his dantian. It was impossible for him to remove these black lines unless he wanted to destroy his dantian as well.
However, several hours later, a pleasantly surprised expression appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
He discovered that under the circulation of his golden bloodline, it seemed to be eroding these black lines. Several of the black lines on one side of his dantian had become much thinner and seemed to be on the verge of breaking!
"The golden bloodline also has such a use!"
If his dantian was sealed, it would be a huge hidden worry for Luo Zheng. If he encountered a powerful enemy, Luo Zheng wouldn't even have the means to resist.
It seemed that the golden bloodline contained some ability to avoid the black palm print. It also had a miraculous effect on these black lines!
Luo Zheng used his strength to force the blood in his body to surge towards his dantian.
If an ordinary person did this, it would undoubtedly be an act of suicide. However, Luo Zheng's body wouldn't have much of a problem even if his internal organs were displaced. Gathering a large amount of golden blood in his dantian wouldn't cause any harm to him!
Therefore, Luo Zheng madly ran forward while trying to dissolve these black lines …
Unknowingly, the sky gradually darkened. The lush green vegetable fields disappeared, replaced by a pitch-black world. Meanwhile, above the sky was a hazy night. A pure white moon hung in the sky. God's Domain also had moons in the game.
Every Saint would do their best to construct the perfect world they imagined. However, they would more or less be influenced by the Realm of the Gods. This was the mindset of living creatures.
Just like when someone saw a ship sailing on the sea for the first time and asked that person to build another ship, the ship he built would more or less be influenced by what he saw before …
Therefore, the moon in front of him was the true and only moon.
When night fell, a thick layer of fog gradually descended on the vegetable field. The surroundings were terrifyingly quiet. Only the rustling sound of Luo Zheng running in the vegetable field could be heard!
"Da da …"
A slight sound came from the side of Luo Zheng's ear.
"Hmm?" Luo Zheng suddenly stopped. Vigilance flashed in his eyes. However, he held his breath and listened attentively.
The surroundings were still silent. There was no wind and no movement.
Luo Zheng frowned. He could only continue running forward …
"Da da da …"
Just as he started walking, those voices rang out again.
However, when Luo Zheng turned around to look, the sounds quickly disappeared again!
Now, Luo Zheng was sure that something in the surroundings was following him. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng slightly closed his eyes. He stimulated the power of his soul and released his divine sense!
Within the range of his divine sense, everything was reflected in Luo Zheng's mind. However, he still only saw the solid soil and the unchanging seedlings.
"What the hell …"
Luo Zheng muttered to himself as he strode forward again.
However, as soon as he lifted his foot, the "da da da" sound came again. As soon as he stopped, the "da da da" sound disappeared again. The situation was very strange!
In the end, Luo Zheng simply ignored it and continued to dash forward at high speed. His speed was also extremely fast. In a breath's time, he could dash out a few miles. It was comparable to an arrow that was shot forward!
However, he didn't run far before he immediately understood where the "da da da" sound came from!
In the vegetable field in front of him, a black cricket jumped out. This cricket was about the size of a puppy. When its pair of strong hind legs bounced, it would make a crisp "da da" sound!
"This cricket is following me!" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. Out of the corner of his eye, he noticed that there were dense black insects bouncing out of the vegetable field. They were all crickets the size of puppies. It turned out that he was surrounded by these insects!
"Da da da da da …"
The dense bouncing sounds came, as if there was an army of crickets marching, completely surrounding Luo Zheng.
"Zhi zhi zhi zhi zhi zhi …"
After encircling Luo Zheng, the crickets began to chirp. The chirping sounds of these crickets were extremely loud. With hundreds and thousands of crickets chirping together, it was even more deafening. It was as if these crickets were the only ones left in this world …
However, after these crickets communicated for a while, they kept quiet in unison. The surroundings were quiet again. At this moment, the crickets' black eyes were looking at Luo Zheng with a hint of greed. Obviously, they were treating Luo Zheng as food!
"Were you just discussing how to divide me up?" Luo Zheng stood in place with a sneer on his face.
"Da da!"
One of the crickets jumped up and took the lead to pounce on Luo Zheng. This puppy-sized cricket jumped in the air and spread its wings. With a flap of its wings, it swooped down at Luo Zheng from a higher angle!
However, when this cricket just got close to Luo Zheng, there was a "bang" sound!
That cricket had already been sent flying!
Luo Zheng clenched his right fist and looked at the cricket that was sent flying. His eyes revealed a hint of surprise.
Even though his dantian was sealed, Luo Zheng's physical body and eight Dao pillars were not to be underestimated. At the very least, his strength was ten times that of a normal sector lord. Under this punch, he originally thought that it would be able to split the cricket into pieces. He didn't think that this cricket would be unharmed!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1734
After the first cricket was sent flying, all the other crickets heard the commotion and moved!
"Da da da da da …"
Those crickets swarmed forward, their speed incomparably fast. In the blink of an eye, this swarm of black crickets had densely surrounded Luo Zheng. Clearly, they wanted to rely on their numbers to cleanly devour Luo Zheng!
However, these crickets quickly discovered a serious problem. Their originally indestructible mouthparts were unable to tear apart the prey in front of them. They couldn't even leave a single wound on Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng, who was pressed down at the bottom, sighed slightly. Although he wouldn't be injured by this swarm of black crickets, his heart was still flustered …
He flipped over, lifted his foot, and stomped heavily on the ground!
The cricket under his foot was directly stomped into the soil by Luo Zheng …
If this was the universe, Luo Zheng's stomp would have caused the earth to quake and mountains to sway. Perhaps a valley would have directly collapsed. However, at this moment, he only stomped a small hole in the vegetable field. Moreover, he didn't even trample a single cricket to death!
However, with the help of this force, Luo Zheng directly soared into the air. He shook off all the crickets attached to his body, and then landed heavily not far away.
These crickets seemed to have extremely powerful concealment abilities. They could even avoid the divine sense released by Luo Zheng. However, their strength couldn't be considered strong. Luo Zheng estimated that they were the lowest level creatures in the Realm of the Gods …
"Da da da da da …"
Seeing Luo Zheng escape, the crickets once again jumped, spread their wings, and continued to chase after Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng sighed. In any case, this lush green vegetable field seemed endless. Since these reckless insects were rushing over, he was too lazy to escape. He would just treat it as killing time.
Immediately, he closed his eyes. The surface of his soul in his mind suddenly flashed with a golden light. After that golden light lit up, it instantly spread to the surface of his soul. Small golden swords slowly revolved around Luo Zheng's soul!
In addition to his dantian, Luo Zheng could also borrow the power of his soul.
When the large group of crickets flew over, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes!
At this moment, his eyes began to emit a surging golden light!
"Swish swish swish swish swish …"
The golden light contained a sharp aura. These auras were actually small, shadowless arrows. These small arrows seemed to be real, and they had even cut a mountain wall into pieces.
However, the most powerful aspect of these Shadowless Swords was their ability to attack the soul!
The defensive power of these crickets could be considered extremely strong. They were able to withstand Luo Zheng's physical attacks. However, as the lowest level of life forms, their souls were pitifully weak. Luo Zheng's aura spread out in an overwhelming manner, like thousands of tiny arrows shooting out. The moment they pierced through the heads of these crickets, their souls were completely destroyed!
"Puff, puff, puff, puff …"
After the souls of the crickets were destroyed, their bodies fell to the ground one after another. There was no room for resistance at all.
Batches of crickets in front were "stared" to death by Luo Zheng's gaze, but the crickets in the back fell one after another.
Gradually, the corpses of these crickets became a small hill, piled up in front of Luo Zheng. These crickets only had their souls destroyed, but they were not completely dead. At least, their bodies were still alive. Those thick crickets' legs were still kicking and twitching …
These crickets also had a certain level of intelligence. Seeing that their companions had suffered heavy casualties, the crickets in the back finally understood that Luo Zheng was not their prey.
Thus, with a dense "Da Da" sound, the crickets that had gathered around all ran away.
Luo Zheng smacked his lips. Then, he turned around and prepared to leave this place and continue moving forward …
However, when he glanced around, he saw a flame faintly floating toward him. Because of the mist, Luo Zheng could not see clearly. He thought that his fight had attracted other ferocious creatures. Naturally, he became vigilant …
But when that flame penetrated the mist, Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief.
He saw a giant bull the size of an elephant. This giant bull had a pair of red horns and its eyes emitted a faint green light. It looked extraordinary. However, what really made Luo Zheng lower his guard, and even feel more relaxed, was the thing on the back of the giant bull …
On the back of the bull, there was a little boy holding a torch.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, this little boy was simply like an oasis in the desert.
Although Luo Zheng had made up his mind to walk in one direction and eventually reach the end of this vegetable field, Luo Zheng had no idea how far the end was.
If there was someone who could guide him, it would be best.
Moreover, Luo Zheng had just entered the Domain of the Gods. He was unfamiliar with the people and the place. He did not know the rules and customs of the Domain of the Gods. If he could understand it through this little boy, he would be an excellent candidate!
"Ah! So many crickets are dead! What's going on! "
This little boy had a pair of big black eyes and white skin. He looked very cute. Luo Zheng stood behind the pile of insect corpses. The little boy did not notice him, but for some reason, after seeing these insect corpses, the little boy was very happy!
So these crickets were called crickets. The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth slightly raised. He had already obtained the first piece of information.
Seeing the little boy so happy, killing these crickets should be something beneficial to him. It could be that these crickets were very precious, or it could be that he was getting rid of a pest in this vegetable field. It could also be both.
With a thought, Luo Zheng took a step forward and said, "I was the one who killed these Locusts."
"Ah …"
The moon was dark and the wind was strong. The surroundings were pitch-black. The little boy was familiar with this vegetable field, so he dared to ride the bull alone. However, when a person suddenly appeared, he was startled. The torch in his hand trembled!
"You, who are you!" The little boy stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands in greeting. "I was passing by this place, but I accidentally got lost. I'm really sorry."
"Lost?" The little boy's eyes turned. There was a hint of cunning in his eyes. Although it flashed, Luo Zheng still saw it. Then the little boy laughed. "Even if you got lost, why did you come to my vegetable field? How do you plan to get out?"
"Go all the way north. I think I'll eventually find a way out," Luo Zheng answered truthfully. He felt that this little boy was somewhat cunning. Although he was vigilant, he still answered truthfully.
"Haha, you can find a way out all the way north?" The little boy laughed out loud. "You can run to your death if you go all the way north. Let's see if you can run out in a hundred years!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was secretly shocked.
Although he had heard that the Realm of the Gods was extremely vast and could not be compared to the universe, he did not expect it to be so vast. With his speed, it would take him a hundred years to walk out of a vegetable field?
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the little boy laughed again. "I'm telling the truth. You're lucky you met me today. Otherwise, you wouldn't be able to get out even if you died! Come on up, let my Er Huang take you out! "
After saying this, he patted the giant bull's back.
This giant bull was three zhang tall, and its back was extremely wide. It could easily seat ten people.
Seeing the little boy's sincere appearance, Luo Zheng seemed to suspect that he was overthinking things. Without any hesitation, he took a step forward and jumped, landing on the bull's back. He heard the little boy pat the bull's back and say crisply, "Er Huang, take me back!"
The giant bull lowered its head and walked forward.
The cow moved very slowly, just like an ordinary cow walking in the fields. However, Luo Zheng felt that the space around him had changed. It was as if it was an illusion. The cow was clearly crawling slowly, but it was carrying him at an extremely fast speed. He secretly sighed in amazement in his heart.
"There are so many seedlings in this vegetable field. I wonder what they are planted?" Luo Zheng asked carefully. Although he was facing an innocent little boy, he had to be careful in the Realm of the Gods.
"You don't even know this? Are you from the outside?" the little boy asked back.
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment. "You can say that."
"These vegetable fields are all planted with Jade Fairy Rice. Those are the food of the Gods," the little boy replied. "Only our Vast Sky Territory has Jade Fairy Rice! It's a special product! "

Chapter 1735
The Realm of the Gods was divided into many Domains.
The largest Domains were the East, West, South, and North Domains. Corresponding to them were the East Pole Fusang, the West Pole Ruo Wood, the North Star Jianmu, and the South Dipper Xunmu.
These four Divine Trees each had their own unique and mystical abilities. They were the four pillars of the Realm of the Gods. It was by relying on these four Divine Trees that the Realm of the Gods was able to continuously draw in chaotic energy from outside and transform it into Heaven Earth Yuan Qi.
Besides the four Domains and the central Sea of Time, there were many other Domains in the Realm of the Gods.
Although Luo Zheng had a rough understanding of the Realm of the Gods, it was impossible for him to remember every domain name. However, he at least knew that he was in the Vast Sky Domain.
On the back of the cow, Luo Zheng carefully asked the little boy about the situation. However, the little boy didn't know much, and many of his questions were irrelevant. Even so, Luo Zheng quickly gained a general understanding of the situation.
It turned out that the so-called Citizens of the Gods were the true mortals of the Realm of the Gods.
These Citizens of the Gods didn't cultivate, or it could be said that their cultivation talent wasn't good. But even so, they were much stronger than the mortals of the universe.
For example, this vast and boundless vegetable field was reclaimed by the Citizens of the Gods!
In the universe, even Life and Death Realm martial artists wouldn't be able to easily dig up this vegetable field. Only Divine Sea Realm martial artists could rely on their physical strength to barely reclaim it. However, the ordinary Citizens of the Realm of the Gods could do it.
In other words, any Citizen of the Realm of the Gods was born with divine strength!
It was no wonder that when Luo Zheng obtained Saint Jiu's power source, Saint Jiu warned Luo Zheng that pure strength was nothing in the Realm of the Gods.
"Such a large vegetable field, how many Citizens of the Gods would it take to reclaim it?" Luo Zheng asked again.
This vegetable field was boundless. If the Citizens of the Gods were to reclaim it, it would take a very large group. Luo Zheng wanted to know how many Citizens of the Gods there were.
Who knew that the little boy would roll his eyes at Luo Zheng. "Who said we had to personally dig up the land? Don't we have cows … "As he spoke, he patted his' Er Huang '. This giant cow also responded with a' moo '.
Luo Zheng was a little embarrassed by the little boy's words.
It turned out that the village that the little boy belonged to only had three to four thousand gods. It was also a small village that was not well-known in the Vast Sky Domain.
These Gods made a living by farming, which was to grow these "Jade Immortal Rice".
Although the Divine Folk possessed considerable strength, they were still unable to absorb the Heaven Earth Yuan Qi in the Divine Realm. Therefore, they did not practice Inedia and still had to eat the five grains. However, the five grains in the Divine Realm were naturally different. This was probably the source of the Divine Folk's strength.
It was the same as the Mountain Collapsing Clan that Luo Zheng encountered in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Those Mountain Collapsing Clan members fed on the flesh and blood of all kinds of ferocious beasts. Even if they didn't cultivate much, they still had innate divine strength.
The cow wobbled and finally reached the edge of the vegetable field!
After Luo Zheng entered the Divine Realm, what entered his eyes was either a vegetable field or a vegetable field. It even gave Luo Zheng the misconception that the Divine Realm was a vegetable field. Only now did he see some different scenery.
At the edge of the vegetable field, there was a mountain road that extended upwards. Halfway up the mountain, there was a lonely village.
Although it was already night time, the lights of every household in the village were still shining.
When the 'Er Huang' reached the entrance of the village, the little boy suddenly stood up and smiled at Luo Zheng. He extended his hand and clapped according to a certain rhythm.
After the clapping stopped, numerous figures flashed out from the darkness. These figures carried spears on their backs.
Although Luo Zheng did not release his divine sense, his soul perception was now extremely sharp. Almost in an instant, he felt the killing intent of these figures. However, these people were all hiding in the darkness and did not rush out!
"This little boy …" Luo Zheng remained calm and collected. However, he roughly understood that this little boy wanted to lure him to this place and then use the clapping sound to send a signal. He was afraid that this little boy wanted to harm him!
However, he had no enmity with these God Citizens. Why would he do this? Luo Zheng was confused …
At this moment, the little boy suddenly rushed out. The 'Er Huang' under his feet lowered its head. He took advantage of the momentum and slid down the cow's neck to the ground. He rolled on the ground and rushed into the village. As he rushed, he shouted, "Everyone, hurry up and do it!"
This series of movements was extremely nimble. Of course, if Luo Zheng wanted to stop the little boy from escaping, it would only take a thought. However, he did not do so. He wanted to see what this little boy was selling in his gourd!
"Moo —"
The 'Er Huang' under Luo Zheng's feet cried out and began to shake its back. It actually wanted to shake Luo Zheng off.
However, Luo Zheng's feet were like nails, firmly nailed to the cow's back. No matter how the 'Er Huang' shook, Luo Zheng still stood on the cow's back without moving an inch!
"Hurry, hurry, hurry!"
"Surround that brat!"
"Don't let him escape!"
"…"
The God Citizens in the darkness carried their spears and rushed out at the same time. The person in the lead held a spear and stabbed it towards Luo Zheng's face without any hesitation!
"Whoosh!"
"The strength is not bad. Ordinary warriors of the Divine Sea Realm would be injured if not killed by this spear. Unfortunately, the accuracy is too bad …"
Seeing these God Citizens attack, Luo Zheng's previous conjecture was verified!
However, the difference between the Mountain Crasher Clan and the Mountain Crasher Clan was that the Mountain Crasher Clan was a proper martial artist who was constantly training. On the other hand, these God Citizens had obviously not received any training. They only had a body full of strength. In Luo Zheng's eyes, they were just randomly stabbing their spears. How could they possibly hit Luo Zheng?
Facing this spear, Luo Zheng only tilted his head slightly. The spear stabbed past Luo Zheng's cheek, missing by less than half an inch.
The God Citizen was slightly taken aback. However, he thought that he had almost hit Luo Zheng and became even more excited. He retracted his spear and stabbed at Luo Zheng again. The result of this second spear was the same as the first. It was still only half an inch away from Luo Zheng's cheek!
At the same time, the other God Citizens also stabbed with their spears …
Amidst the chaotic stabbing of the spears, Luo Zheng simply used the "Eight Winding Flying Smoke" movement. His whole body fluttered lightly on the back of the ox like a willow catkin. All the spears that were stabbing at Luo Zheng almost all slid past him.
Every spear was just a little bit off, but the difference of a millimeter was huge. From beginning to end, not a single spear could touch Luo Zheng!
"Come on, everyone! This guy can't avoid it! "
"Alas, that spear almost killed him!"
"He's pretty lucky. He was just a little bit off …"
These God Citizens always thought that they were just a little bit off. Instead, it stimulated their courage and made them strenuously attack with their spears.
The little boy who was hiding nearby was looking at Luo Zheng's calm expression. A look of shock flashed across his big eyes. He realized that the man on the back of the ox was just toying with them!
These God Citizens had not received any professional training. Although they were very strong, they could not hold on for long if they used their strength recklessly. After twenty to thirty breaths, many God Citizens were already panting. They carried their spears and stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
The few remaining divine citizens who were still persevering also stopped. They weren't fools. A bunch of them had been stabbing Luo Zheng for so long, yet they still hadn't managed to touch his clothes. The difference in strength was too great. Luo Zheng was just toying with them!
Luo Zheng stood on the back of the ox and looked down at these God Citizens. His face was slightly cold as he asked, "Why are you attacking me?"
As soon as he finished speaking, the leader of the God Citizens shouted angrily, "There's no need to say anything. If you want to kill us, do as you please. We, the people of Meng Village, will not leave this place!"
"That's right. Meng Mountains is the land of our clan that has been passed down for generations. Why should we be driven away by you?" Another God Citizen said angrily.
"I, I'll fight you to the death!"
The strongest God Citizen carried a spear and rushed toward Luo Zheng again.
Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly rose. He turned his hand and grabbed the spear. The eight-layered Dao Pillars in his body suddenly opened, and a powerful force was released. With this squeeze, an explosion sounded. The spear was crushed by Luo Zheng!

Chapter 1736
Luo Zheng's display of power immediately intimidated these excited Divine Citizens.
Hearing these Divine Citizens' words, Luo Zheng understood a few things.
Someone must be looking for trouble with this "Meng Village" and wanted to drive these Divine Citizens away. Since these Meng Village villagers refused to leave, the other party naturally hired some people to cause trouble for them.
When Luo Zheng had just entered the Divine Realm, he had fallen into a vegetable field in this village and had easily exterminated a large group of "Horseshoe Beetles" in the field. This had been seen by the little boy. The little boy had a plan in mind and had brought Luo Zheng to the village in an attempt to kill Luo Zheng together.
After sorting out his thoughts, Luo Zheng naturally understood the Divine Citizens' motives.
"I'm just a passerby who accidentally stumbled into the vegetable field. I didn't mean to offend anyone, so why do you want to attack me?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
After thinking it through, Luo Zheng wasn't angry at all.
"Is there a mistake?"
"Aren't you someone sent by the Sun Moon Village?"
When these Divine Citizens saw Luo Zheng's innocent expression, they were somewhat shaken.
"Indeed, I'm not. I don't even know what the Sun Moon Village is," Luo Zheng shrugged.
"How can we believe you?" A Divine Citizen questioned.
"Believe me?" A smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. "Then you'll believe me!"
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng's figure flashed and finally left the back of the ox.
Although he couldn't use the Law of Wind to fly, he could still use the ingenious movement skills that he had previously cultivated. His movement was like a ghost and he instantly disappeared from these Divine Citizens' eyes.
The Divine Citizens only felt a gentle breeze whistling past them. Then, they loosened their grip on their spears and the spears disappeared.
In a breath's time, Luo Zheng was standing in the middle of the village gate with a bundle of spears in his hand.
Every Divine Citizen turned pale with fright and stared at Luo Zheng in fear. Their mouths were slightly agape as if they didn't know what to say. "If I was sent by the Sun Moon Village, you would already be corpses," Luo Zheng said indifferently. After saying this, he casually threw the bundle of spears on the ground.
In the face of Luo Zheng's explanation, no one uttered a word.
They also felt that Luo Zheng's words were reasonable. The strength that Luo Zheng had displayed was too powerful. If he wanted to kill them, it would be as easy as turning his hand over... There was no need to explain things to such a powerhouse!
However, the villagers' eyes were still not so friendly. Instead, they were filled with hostility. Luo Zheng was the one who was really depressed.
If it wasn't because he had just arrived, he wouldn't have bothered with these Citizens..
. However, Luo Zheng also reminded himself that this was the Divine Domain, not the universe. The first thing he needed to do was to integrate into this world and get used to it. He couldn't be impulsive!
"Everyone, believe him! He's not an assassin sent by the Sun Moon Village! "
At this time, a voice came from the village. The person who came was a black-haired youth with a long sword hanging from his waist. His eyes were calm. He was a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist.
This person was also a martial artist. Was he a martial artist among the Divine Citizens? Luo Zheng thought to himself.
He wasn't afraid of this black-haired youth. These Divine Citizens didn't have any cultivation, but they all had brute strength. They gave Luo Zheng a strange feeling. Now that he saw a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist, he suddenly felt a sense of familiarity.
"Little Wu, what took you so long?!" A Divine Citizen said angrily.
Little Wu smiled embarrassedly. "I've been here all this time. I just didn't make a move …"
"We were almost killed by this person just now. As the only martial artist in our village, you actually hid in the back. You're a disgrace to our Meng Village!"
"That's right, that's right. Little Wu, why didn't you make a move?!"
Hearing Little Wu's words, these Divine Citizens became even angrier.
They were almost killed by him …
Luo Zheng suddenly realized that these Divine Citizens were very difficult to communicate with. He was the one who showed mercy, so why did they say they were almost killed?
Little Wu rolled his eyes and said, "I'm not his match either. Let alone me, even the chief of the Sun Moon Village doesn't have full confidence in taking him down!"
Little Wu's evaluation shocked the Divine Citizens once again.
After all, Little Wu was the strongest warrior in their village, and also the only hope of Meng Mountain Village. Therefore, these Gods would naturally believe what he said.
But even the chief of the Sun Moon Village couldn't take him down? This was too much!
"How is that possible?!"
"I think this kid has some strength, but he's far from that stage!"
"Little Wu, are you saying this because you're afraid of fighting?"
Little Wu blinked his eyes and continued, "Because he's also a Divine Proof Realm martial artist, just like the chief of the Sun Moon Village."
"Divine … Divine Proof Realm martial artist!"
"Realm Lord?"
"Is he really that strong?"
The Divine Citizens widened their eyes again and looked at Luo Zheng with fear, as if they were looking at a monster.
Luo Zheng didn't understand at first. What was a Divine Proof Realm martial artist?
But when someone mentioned the Realm Lord, Luo Zheng suddenly understood!
The strongest level in the universe was the Celestial Lord, and the Celestial Lord was an achievement that could only be achieved by being bestowed the Mandate of Heaven. In fact, it was essentially a title.
However, True Gods were extremely rare in the universe. Even if there were, they would be brought to the universe by the Jiang family after they were apotheosized. Not to mention ordinary people, even the Realm Lords rarely knew about it. For example, Hua Tianming's apotheosizing this time was only known by a very small number of people in the Demi-Immortal clan.
However, the number of True Gods in the Divine Realm was quite large, and True Gods were not rare here.
After cultivating to the Sector Lord level, the 'living beings of the same level' who wouldn't be restricted by the Heavenly Dao would naturally have to prove their Divine Dao and charge towards the True God level. Therefore, the Sector Lord level was the so-called Proving God level martial artist …
"Am I right?" Little Wu smiled at Luo Zheng, as if he was also seeking confirmation.
In fact, Little Wu couldn't see Luo Zheng's cultivation level. As a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist, he could only see through the cultivation level of the Extreme Divine Realm. However, since he couldn't see through Luo Zheng's cultivation level, and he couldn't feel the unique "Force" of a True God from Luo Zheng, it was easy for him to determine that Luo Zheng's cultivation level was at the Realm Lord.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, "Yes, I am indeed a Divine Proof Realm martial artist."
Little Wu cupped his hands and bowed to Luo Zheng, "We Meng Village people were reckless just now. I apologize on behalf of everyone. Please come in."
The little boy who had been hiding in the back rushed out and said to Little Wu, "Big brother, is he really not a bad guy sent by the Sun Moon Stockade?" His big blinking eyes were filled with doubt. He thought he had spent a lot of effort to trick Luo Zheng into coming here, but he didn't expect it to turn into a farce.
Little Wu reached out his hand and heavily knocked on the little boy's head, "You still have the nerve to say that! You are the one who caused all of this! If he was really sent by the Sun Moon Stockade, you would have been dead by now … "
The atmosphere suddenly relaxed, and all the Gods seemed to believe Little Wu's words. At least, Luo Zheng didn't have any hostility.
After Little Wu entered the village, Luo Zheng was led to the biggest pavilion in the village.
In the middle of the pavilion, there was a brazier, and above the brazier, there was a big wok. It seemed like something was cooking in the wok, and it emitted a thick meaty aroma.
Luo Zheng's gaze lingered on the flame for a moment. He couldn't see through the law hidden in the flame at first glance.
Then, he looked at the wood that was burning, and there was a faint golden light. He roughly understood that the wood used to make the fire was probably an extremely precious material in the universe.
No wonder Refined God's son was so excited when he saw the materials in the immortal abode. The materials used by ordinary Gods in the Realm of the Gods were all extraordinary in the universe …
However, in the eyes of these Gods, a Divine Warrior seemed to have a very high status. At least, at this moment, everyone was very respectful to Luo Zheng, and treated him as a guest.
At this moment, Little Wu asked, "Friend, where did you come from? Why did you come to our Meng Village? "
Earlier in the universe, Xingwei had taught Luo Zheng a set of excuses. It was necessary to hide one's identity. In the Realm of the Gods, one had to make up a fake identity.
Luo Zheng organized the information in his mind, and said in an orderly manner, "I am Luo Tianxing, and I came from the outer realm. But to be honest, I got lost in the Vast Sky Domain, and I accidentally stumbled into that vegetable field …"
"Lost in the Vast Sky Domain …" Little Wu was slightly stunned, and there was a faint look of disbelief on his face.

Chapter 1737
Of course, Luo Zheng knew that if he casually made up a story, there would always be some parts that would not hold water.
But the less information he revealed, the lower the possibility of him being exposed. So, it didn't matter whether they believed him or not.
Furthermore, these people were just some God's people, and it was impossible for them to find out anything about him.
Just as Luo Zheng had expected, Little Wu didn't linger on this issue. Instead, he told Luo Zheng that their small village was very far away in the Vast Sky Domain. Even the nearest God's City was very far away. Therefore, he wanted to invite Luo Zheng to stay in Meng Village for the time being.
Meng Village was facing a great enemy, and Luo Zheng was a Divine Warrior. Luo Zheng had expected this kind of invitation. So, Luo Zheng gladly agreed.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had agreed, Little Wu was overjoyed. "My friend, you must be tired after coming all the way here. Ah Wen, go and get two pots of good wine …"
After that, Little Wu opened the big pot in front of him, and a rich meat aroma immediately spread out in the loft.
Luo Zheng had already abstained from eating for a long time, but when he smelled the meat aroma, his appetite was aroused. According to Little Wu's introduction, the meat in the pot was peeled off from the Iron Fang Beast.
As a Divine Realm warrior, he had also abstained from eating. But, eating the meat of this ferocious beast could increase his strength. Although this beast was nothing in the eyes of those True Gods, it was still a precious ingredient among the God's people.
After being polite, Luo Zheng swallowed a few pieces of meat. Immediately, he felt a warm feeling in his body. The energy emitted from this meat was even purer than the energy from the top level ferocious beasts in the Forbidden Land of God!
But, Luo Zheng wasn't surprised. The Forbidden Land of God was the world inside Saint Jiu's body, and the living beings there were created by Saint Jiu's Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi. So, they were still second-level living beings. Although the Iron Fang Beast's level was low, it was still a living being of the same level. It was created from Chaos, and there was still a big difference between them.
After eating and drinking, the people of Meng Village arranged another comfortable loft for Luo Zheng to stay.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't need to sleep. He hadn't forgotten that his Dantian had been sealed …
Along the way, Luo Zheng tried his best to control the golden blood in his body to wash the black lines on his Dantian. These black lines were extremely stubborn. After washing for so long, only two or three black lines were broken, which made Luo Zheng very anxious.
At this stage, Luo Zheng's greatest goal was to recover his strength as quickly as possible. Otherwise, it would be very difficult for him to advance in God's Domain.
And from the looks of it, although the village was peaceful, there was still a great enemy before them. According to what Little Wu had said, there were many martial artists in the Sun Moon Stronghold and there were even Proof of Divinity cultivators, which was the Sector Lord Realm!
It wasn't that Luo Zheng hadn't come into contact with World Lord Realm martial artists in the God Domain. A few years ago in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, there were quite a number of God Domain geniuses that had been defeated by Luo Zheng. Moreover, they were all from large clans in the God Domain.
However, with his Dantian sealed, Luo Zheng had too few means to rely on. His strength was greatly reduced. Originally, it was not a problem, but now it was a problem for Luo Zheng.
One night passed …
Luo Zheng continued to wash it with his blood, but he only managed to break four black threads. However, there were more than a hundred black threads entwined around it. It seemed that it would take at least a month for it to be done.
"Huff huff …"
"Huff huff …"
"The sword guards the Dantian, the eyes determine the universe, strength meets strength, step on the square!"
At dawn, there was the sound of swords clashing outside the house. Someone was practicing swordsmanship outside.
Luo Zheng got up and opened the door. He saw Little Wu holding a shiny cyan-steel sword and practicing swordsmanship …
Although Luo Zheng didn't recognize that set of swordsmanship, his understanding of swordsmanship had reached the level of "Anatta Sword", which was far beyond the level of a great grandmaster. How sharp was his vision? At a glance, he could see that this set of swordsmanship was quite simple, so simple that it made people's hair stand on end.
It seemed that these divine citizens didn't surpass second-level life forms in all aspects, Luo Zheng thought to himself.
In this universe, let alone a Divine Transformation realm martial artist, even a Life and Death realm or Divine Sea realm martial artist would have reached the level of a great grandmaster in their comprehension of the sword. The swordsmanship they practiced was ever-changing, each with its own subtleties!
But although this Little Wu had a Divine Transformation realm cultivation, the swordsmanship he used was quite ordinary. It was only a variation of the basic swordsmanship, and because of this variation, it was far inferior to the basic swordsmanship in terms of flexibility.
Thinking about it carefully, these divine citizens were born with an innate divine strength. If they could use the heaven and earth origin energy of the Realm of the Gods, then they would be able to open the Divine Sea realm and become a Divine Sea realm powerhouse. This wasn't strange at all.
After all, a Divine Sea realm martial artist in the Realm of the Gods was roughly equivalent to a "Xiantian life form" in the lower realms of the universe. They had just started on the path of martial arts. As for Little Wu, as a Divine Transformation realm martial artist, he had only taken the second step. Moreover, this Little Wu was only an ordinary villager from a remote mountain village. To be able to cultivate to the Divine Transformation realm was already an astonishing talent, so how could he possibly have any good swordsmanship to practice?
Besides Little Wu, there was also a group of sword-wielding children by his side.
These children probably couldn't get their hands on a sword, so they could only use twigs and sticks as substitutes. But, they followed Little Wu's example …
The twigs in the children's hands, if placed in the universe, could be exchanged for a decent quality Saint artifact?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled. No matter where you went, there would always be people who were inclined towards the path of martial arts. Then, he walked down the stairs of the loft.
"Be serious!" Little Wu put even more effort into his swordsmanship. As for the children, they couldn't keep up with Little Wu's rhythm and began to sway left and right. The only one who could barely keep up with Little Wu's movements was the little boy who carried Luo Zheng out of the vegetable field last night.
Luo Zheng already knew that this little boy's name was Little Wen. He was Little Wu's younger brother.
Seeing Little Wu's messy footwork, Luo Zheng stood with his hands behind his back and said indifferently, "If you want to practice the sword, you have to practice the footwork first. This mnemonic, 'Take square steps', is wrong. Although the sword is hard, it can also be straight. If you can only take square steps, it will inevitably affect the flexibility of your hands. If your swordsmanship loses its spirituality, then what is the point of practicing the sword? It's better to use a spear! "
Although these words sounded a bit harsh, with Luo Zheng's understanding of the way of the sword, he was completely qualified to make such an evaluation.
However, Little Wu, whose back was facing Luo Zheng, suddenly gave Little Wen a meaningful look!
Little Wen immediately put away the wooden sword in his hand. "Hmph, what nonsense are you talking about? My brother's swordsmanship was learned from Jing Cheng. This' Soaring Eagle Swordsmanship 'is extraordinarily ingenious. How can it lose its spirituality in your mouth?"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. He opened the door and saw Little Wu leading the group of children to practice swordsmanship methodically. He knew that Little Wu had already discussed with them and wanted him to teach them a few moves. He was probably afraid that he wouldn't agree, so he used this method to provoke him.
He stared intently at Little Wen. Although there was a smile in his eyes, a faint aura was released. This aura wasn't enough to kill, but it could give the child a certain amount of pressure.
Little Wen was very scheming, but under Luo Zheng's stare, his expression immediately became flustered and his face turned pale. The other children also stared blankly at Luo Zheng, their faces revealing fear.
Seeing the children's expressions, Luo Zheng's heart softened. Forget it, there was no point in arguing with a group of children. He said to Little Wu, "If you need guidance, just ask. I, Luo Zheng, am not a stingy person. Why bother to belittle me?"
When Little Wu and the other children heard this, their faces were filled with surprise. They had rehearsed for this moment for a long time, but now it seemed that their efforts had been wasted …
Even in the Realm of the Gods, cultivation wasn't something that ordinary Gods could come into contact with.
Golden Cores, cultivation methods, resources, teachers …
To the Gods, these were things that they could only hope for but not reach. As for the legendary Dao Tablets, they were nothing more than legends to the Gods.
Although Luo Zheng wasn't a True God, as a Proof of Divinity martial artist, they naturally didn't want to let him go. It was just that they didn't expect Luo Zheng to be so easy to talk to …
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, his figure flashed slightly. He casually touched Little Wen's hand, and the wooden sword was already in Luo Zheng's hand.
Although this wooden sword was a child's toy, its weight was actually more than ten thousand pounds. Although Little Wen was only seven or eight years old, he was still a God. He couldn't be measured by the eyes of the universe.
"Practice the sword first. Watch carefully!"

Chapter 1738
When this set of sword steps was displayed, Little Wu, Little Wen, and the other children's eyes shone brightly.
The Flying Eagle Swordsmanship that Little Wu had used previously also allowed Luo Zheng to understand the bizarre aspects of the Divine Region. Those disciples who relied on the True God's family practiced extremely exquisite martial arts. Perhaps they had been comprehended by the Sages, and subsequently passed on to the Divine Path in the Tablet of Dao!
However, the distance between them and ordinary God citizens was extremely far, so the gap between them was frighteningly large.
Even if these God citizens were naturally born with divine strength after they reached adulthood, and had a stronger perception than the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods, it was still very difficult for them to come into contact with some of the basic martial arts in the universe! As for why this situation had occurred, Luo Zheng did not know.
Perhaps it was because this small mountain village was really too remote, or perhaps it was because the Realm of the Gods had formed such a situation over countless years. This question could only be solved by himself in the future.
However, although these God citizens did not possess any powerful martial arts manuals, their physiques were there, and their vision was not bad. When they saw Luo Zheng's sword steps, their eyes immediately lit up!
"Huhuhuhu …"
After ascending to the Upper Realm, moves gradually became less important when fighting against martial artists. Luo Zheng could only abandon many techniques.
However, there were many restrictions in the Realm of the Gods. Luo Zheng also understood that when fighting against those True Gods, these methods could often play a role in turning the tide!
After this set of sword steps was completed, Luo Zheng put away his wooden sword and said indifferently, "This set of sword steps is only the most basic steps on the path of the Sword Dao. However, if you can master it, you can lay a solid foundation. You can use your heart to experience it …"
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, Little Wu and the other children's eyes, which had been shining, became confused again …
They had too little contact with martial arts. Although they knew the profoundness of this sword step, how could they comprehend its true meaning in a short period of time?
Seeing their expressions, Luo Zheng curled his lips. These guys seemed to be even more stupid than he had imagined. When he had first comprehended the sword steps, he had purely followed Hua Tianming's rhythm. It was almost equivalent to learning without a teacher. These God citizens' perception was still somewhat lacking.
"Let me demonstrate it again …" Luo Zheng said helplessly.
"Wait a minute! Let me try! "
Xiao Wen stood up and asked for his wooden sword from Luo Zheng. With a light shake of the long sword, his left leg slid forward as if he was sliding on ice. With this step, his whole body immediately became incomparably agile.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Xiao Wen's movements were much slower than Luo Zheng's. After she took a step, she had to think for a while before she could take the second step. Sometimes, she even took the wrong step.
But in the end, he still managed to perform the sword steps that Luo Zheng had just demonstrated!
After Xiao Wen sheathed her sword, Luo Zheng praised, "Not bad. Although you are not flexible enough, you have already grasped the pattern. Otherwise, you would not have been able to remember all my steps. You have the highest comprehension ability!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's praise, Xiaowen was so happy that she almost jumped up from the ground.
On the other hand, Xiao Wen's brother, Xiao Wu, revealed a gloomy expression!
In the deep mountains of the Vast Sky Domain, every village would train their own warriors. After all, there would occasionally be ferocious creatures appearing in the divine mountains. Sometimes, there would also be ferocious creatures wreaking havoc in the vegetable fields. Sometimes, there would even be conflicts with the divine people of other villages. Therefore, the warriors of the village would have to act.
Xiao Wu was a warrior that Meng Mountain Village had spent a lot of resources to train. Unfortunately, Xiao Wu's talent was average. Plus, Meng Mountain Village was not rich, so the resources they could provide were quite limited. Therefore, Xiao Wu's strength was also average.
Now that he saw his brother's talent, which seemed to have far surpassed his own, he felt both gloomy and happy for his brother. In his mind, he was wondering if he should let Xiao Wen become his disciple …
For the next few days, Luo Zheng had been teaching them the sword steps.
On one hand, he could learn more about the Vast Sky Domain. On the other hand, this place allowed him to feel at ease and untie the black lines in his dantian.
The villagers of Meng Mountain Village were very simple and honest. They knew that Luo Zheng was a Divine Warrior, and he taught the children swordsmanship every day. Naturally, they were very grateful. Every day, they would cook the best mountain products in the village and give them to Luo Zheng.
At the same time, these villagers also had all kinds of speculations about Luo Zheng's background.
The divine people were not stupid. An unknown Divine Warrior staying in their small mountain village was most likely because he had done something wrong and was hiding from his enemies. However, Meng Mountain Village was so remote that even if there were any enemies, they would not be able to find this place. Therefore, they were not too worried.
Unknowingly, Luo Zheng had been in Meng Mountain Village for more than half a month, and half of the black lines in his dantian had already been dissolved. In another half a month, he should be able to untie all of the black lines. Moreover, Luo Zheng finally had a relatively trustworthy understanding of the Vast Sky Domain. Once the black lines were completely dissolved, he would leave.
These divine people's martial talent was actually not weak. It was just that their foundation was too weak. Even though they had a body full of strength, it was difficult for them to display it. Xiao Wu had just barely grasped the knack of the sword steps. With his Divine Transformation realm cultivation, he could barely use it, but it was still not flexible enough.
As for Xiao Wen's talent, it was indeed beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. Although he had yet to open his Divine Sea, he had only spent three days to master the sword steps. This talent could not be measured by ordinary standards. He had a very outstanding talent.
Thus, according to Xiao Wen's characteristics, Luo Zheng derived a few basic sword skills and created a set of sword skills to teach him …
These days, Xiao Wen had been practicing his sword skills day and night. On this day, as he saw Luo Zheng walk out of his loft, he suddenly threw away his wooden sword and knelt down in front of Luo Zheng. "Master, please accept a bow from your disciple!"
He actually wanted to become Luo Zheng's disciple!
If this were the universe, Luo Zheng indeed had the qualifications to establish his own sect. He even had the ability to create a great force in the universe. Taking Xiao Wen as his disciple would not be a problem.
But in the Realm of the Gods, Luo Zheng was only a Proof of Divinity martial artist. His future path was still very difficult. Even if he took Xiao Wen as his disciple, it would still be difficult to take care of him. He faintly smiled and said, "I'm sorry, but I am unable to take you as my disciple right now."
It was Xiao Wu's idea to let Xiao Wen become his disciple. Xiao Wu knew that his talent was not enough to catch Luo Zheng's eyes. If his brother could be taken as a disciple by a Proof of Divinity martial artist, of course, it would be much smoother than blindly cultivating on his own. This was an opportunity that should not be missed.
But they did not expect that Luo Zheng would reject them without even thinking about it.
"Why, why, I, I'm very obedient! Master! "Xiao Wen raised his head and said with a stutter.
Xiao Wu also chimed in, "That's right, why can't you take my brother as your disciple?"
Of course, Luo Zheng could not explain his background to them. He just shook his head faintly, then looked at Xiao Wen and said, "Why do you cultivate martial arts? If it is to protect the people of Meng Village, then the sword skills I've taught you now, and your talent is enough. "
Although Luo Zheng had not come into contact with the other martial artists of the Vast Sky Domain, but from the reaction of the people of Meng Village, he could make a rough guess. Judging from the cultivation techniques, other than the Divine Dao and the top cultivation techniques, the Divine Realm was not much stronger than Huan Yu in other aspects. After all, almost all the inheritances in Huan Yu came from the Sages!
Of course, this was only for the ordinary God's people and martial artists. For example, the cultivation techniques of the Han Jia sisters that Luo Zheng encountered in the Forbidden Land of God were also extraordinary. After all, they also had the inheritances of the Sages.
"No!" Xiao Wen denied Luo Zheng's words, "The reason I cultivate martial arts is not only to protect Meng Village! I, I want … to attain the Divine Dao! "
Xiao Wu, who was standing at the side, was so surprised that he could not close his mouth. Xiao Wu had never thought that his brother had such a dream.
In the Divine Realm, the God's people and the True Gods were the absolute dividing line!
Once one succeeded in attaining the Divine Dao, it meant that one would have an unlimited lifespan and unlimited possibilities.
However, although there were a lot of True Gods in the Divine Realm, it did not mean that attaining the Divine Dao was easy.

Chapter 1739
Within Xiao Wen's black eyes, there seemed to be two balls of fire burning.
Looking at this child's resolute eyes, Luo Zheng's heart was touched. This little guy had a shadow of himself in the past, firm and resolute in his beliefs!
Whether it was the Martial Dao or the Dao of Godhood, it was a path to constantly grow stronger. Whether this path had an end or not, Luo Zheng could not answer. However, once one stepped on this path, one must have the belief to constantly improve.
If Xiao Wen only wanted to protect Meng Village, there was no need for him to become Luo Zheng's disciple.
"Sorry … Now, not yet," Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words turn for the better, Xiao Wen's eyes flashed slightly as he asked, "Now is not the time, then when?"
Luo Zheng took a deep breath and raised his head to look at the sky of Changkong Domain. A bright sun hung in the sky, emitting a dazzling light. That was the real sun, the only sun in the Realm of the Gods …
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng replied, "If one day, you can hear my name in Changkong Domain, come and find me!"
Luo Zheng came here for revenge, and the possibility of him dying was too high.
If one day, he could face Mu Haiji and protect himself, then he would be qualified to establish a sect in the Realm of the Gods and protect his family!
"The day you become famous in Changkong Domain!" Xiao Wen smiled.
"That's too difficult …" Xiao Wu curled his lips. He felt that although a Divine Proof Expert was powerful, he was not a True God. Although Changkong Domain was not a large domain, there were still many True Gods. Even if Luo Zheng succeeded in proving himself as a True God, it would still be difficult for him to become famous in Changkong Domain!
What Little Wu didn't know was that Luo Zheng's goal wasn't to make a name for himself in a single domain. He wanted to make a name for himself in the entire Divine Domain, on the floating island in the Sea of Time, and in the Sage Hall!
"It's okay, I can wait! I believe you can do it, no, I believe you can do it, Master! "Xiao Wen was full of confidence in Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled slightly. Just as he was about to speak, he suddenly heard a scream coming from the entrance of the village. "Ah —"
When the miserable screams were heard, the entire Meng Village was shocked.
"Save me!"
"It's the people from the Sun Moon Village!"
"Someone is dead!"
These divine people were not warriors. When they saw someone being killed, they immediately panicked like ants on a hot pan. Even the villagers of Meng Village were howling in grief, covering their heads and fleeing like rats.
Even Little Wu, who was at the Divine Transformation Realm, was flustered, let alone the ordinary Divine Citizens. "They really did come …"
