The only one who remained calm was probably Luo Zheng. He slightly frowned and rushed towards the top of the pavilion. At the same time, Luo Zheng lightly hooked the wooden sword on the ground with the tip of his foot. At the same time, Luo Zheng jumped towards the top of the pavilion and caught the wooden sword.
After jumping onto the top of the pavilion, Luo Zheng was like a grasshopper, jumping up and down on the pavilion. He was as agile as a cat, not making a single sound.
In just a few breaths time, he had found his way to the entrance of the village. When he glanced around, a wisp of anger burst out from his eyes!
In front of the entrance of Meng Village, a group of seven warriors was blocking the entrance with green blades in their hands. Under their feet were two divine people. These two divine people were the villagers of Meng Village. One of them had his neck chopped off, and the other was chopped in half. This method was extraordinarily cruel!
"Hmph, our Village Chief gave you three months to leave Meng Village. Since you refused a toast only to be forced to drink a forfeit, don't blame us, the Sun Moon Village!" The leader of the group waved the green blade in his hand and grinned hideously. The reason he killed these two divine people was to frighten the villagers of Meng Village and drive them away.
"Since you don't want to leave, then you don't have to. Our Village Chief has said that the blades in our hands need to be constantly fed with blood in order to become sharp. Brothers, except for the women, kill all of them, including the elderly and the children!" The warrior waved the blade in his hand and led the other six warriors behind him in a charge.
Luo Zheng had stayed in this village for more than half a month. The villagers of Meng Village were simple and hospitable, so naturally, Luo Zheng was very angry when they were killed for no reason. However, this anger quickly disappeared from his eyes. Being able to suppress the emotions in his heart at any time was an ability that a mature warrior should have!
Most of the villagers were running away in panic, but there were still some brave villagers who held up their spears and went up to meet the warriors. Unfortunately, these villagers only had powerful strength, but their overall strength was pitifully weak. How could they be a match for those warriors?
Thump! Thump!
A green blade flashed, and the spears in the hands of those villagers were instantly cut in half. The leader of the group had a cold smile on his face. He waved the green blade in his hand again, and was about to chop off the head of one of the villagers!
"Now is the time …"
Luo Zheng shot down from the roof of a building on the side. Before he landed on the ground, he hit the ground with his elbow, then he spun forward and thrust the wooden sword in his hand forward!
This wooden sword weighed more than ten thousand pounds, so the green blade in the hands of this Divine Realm warrior must weigh at least a few hundred thousand pounds. Luo Zheng's Dantian couldn't circulate, so naturally, he couldn't channel the Chaotic Energy into the wooden sword. Therefore, he didn't want to fight this green blade head on.
However, it was just as Luo Zheng had predicted. The cultivation methods of ordinary martial artists were simply too weak. Even if these martial artists were at the Ultimate Divine Realm, their saber techniques were still too horrible to look at in Luo Zheng's eyes!
Thud!
Luo Zheng's wooden sword was like a spirit snake coming out of its hole, and he took the initiative to touch the warrior's wrist.
Although he was only using the strength of his mortal body, it was still enough to make this Divine Realm martial artist suffer. The moment the wooden sword touched the ground, there was a crisp "Kacha" sound. The blue saber in the martial artist's hand fell to the ground with a "Clang" sound. The martial artist clutched his wrist and howled in pain. His wrist bone had already been shattered!
Alas, these Divine Realm warriors were too weak …
In the universe, not to mention Divine Realm warriors, even Life and Death Realm and Divine Sea Realm warriors wouldn't change their expressions even if they lost a wrist. Even if they lost an arm, they would still be able to face the enemy calmly.
Luo Zheng thought so in his heart, but he still dashed forward and attacked without the slightest hesitation!
The rest of the Divine Realm warriors could only see Luo Zheng's figure flashing, and they all swung their blades at Luo Zheng. But with their lousy blade techniques, how could they hit Luo Zheng?
Luo Zheng was like a nimble deer, constantly dodging these rough blades, and whenever he saw an opportunity, he would reach out his hand and thrust his sword! Every time the wooden sword was thrust, it would accurately hit the opponent's wrist. Along with the sound of bones breaking, the green blade fell to the ground.
In just a few breaths of time, the seven Divine Realm warriors were all holding their wrists and screaming in pain …
When the villagers of Meng Village saw this scene, they were all stunned, including Little Wu and the others. They had just arrived, but the battle had already ended!
"Brother, is our master really a Divine Realm warrior?" Little Wen asked in a low voice.
Little Wen had never seen the strength of a Divine Realm warrior, she had only heard about it from her brother. But a Divine Realm warrior shouldn't be as strong as Luo Zheng.
"I, I'm not sure," Little Wu's heart was beating like a drum. He couldn't see through Luo Zheng's cultivation level, but a Divine Realm warrior shouldn't be able to defeat seven Divine Realm warriors in a few breaths of time, right? Could this Luo Tianxing be a True God? But he didn't have the unique aura of a True God …
"Kid, who are you? We are from the Sun and Moon Stockade, and you are our stockade chief … "The leader of the group of Divine Realm warriors held his arm and stared at Luo Zheng furiously. Of course, he had noticed that Luo Zheng was a Proving Divinity Warrior. At this moment, he wanted to use the name of the Sun and Moon Stockade to drive this kid away. A small village like Meng Village would never have a Proving Divinity Warrior!
However, this Extreme Divine Stage martial artist had only opened his mouth halfway when Luo Zheng raised the tip of his foot, and a stone had already arrived with a whistling sound, heavily smashing into his lower jaw. With his entire lower jaw shattered, let alone speak, he couldn't even let out a blood-curdling scream.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1740
In the Realm of the Gods, even a small stone could weigh about four to five hundred pounds. Therefore, the force of Luo Zheng's kick was not weak at all.
Since the leader of the group couldn't threaten Luo Zheng, another warrior beside him could take over the conversation. After all, the Sun and Moon Village was their backing, and the source of their confidence …
But just as this warrior was about to open his mouth, Luo Zheng kicked again, and another stone flew out, accurately hitting the warrior's jaw.
"Thud!"
Thus, this warrior's jaw was also shattered.
When the other warriors saw this, how could they dare to open their mouths to threaten others? They all shut their mouths tightly, fearing that a stone would come flying at them if they opened their mouths!
"You guys deal with these people," Luo Zheng said calmly.
"They killed Sixth Brother and Uncle Lin! We must avenge them! "
"Kill them!"
"… …"
A life for a life, this was a universal principle.
The angry villagers rushed forward with spears in their hands.
Although the villagers of Meng Village were kind, they didn't show any mercy to the murderers who killed their own people.
When the warriors saw this, they immediately turned around and ran. Although Luo Zheng had broken their wrists, their legs were still intact.
But just as they turned around, Luo Zheng kicked the stones with one foot, one by one.
The pebbles carried a great force as they accurately hit the back of their legs. The impact of the pebbles directly broke their leg bones!
"Plop, plop, plop … …"
For a moment, the six cultivators all fell to the ground. Since the cultivators with broken legs were unable to fly, they could only drag their broken legs and slowly crawl.
The villagers carried their spears and rushed forward, poking these warriors into a hornet's nest … …
Luo Zheng didn't stay to watch this bloody scene. In fact, ever since he ascended to the Upper Realm, Luo Zheng had rarely killed anyone. Moreover, in a high-level fight, even if he killed someone, it didn't look so cruel. Unlike now, where blood was spilled all over the ground, it was extremely cruel.
After killing all the warriors from Sun Moon Camp, the villagers of Meng Village buried the bodies of Sixth Brother and Uncle Lin.
Only then did the angry villagers feel a lingering fear.
Ever since the conflict between Meng Mountain village and Sun Moon Village broke out, no one had died. Before this, Sun Moon Village only taught a few Meng Mountain villagers a lesson, then gave them a deadline to move out.
Who would have thought that when the time came, the Sun and Moon Village would start killing people. If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, perhaps Meng Village would have already been massacred by Luo Zheng.
Inside the biggest pavilion, all the influential villagers of Meng Mountain Village had gathered together. They were discussing how to deal with the Sun Moon Village, as killing the warriors of the Sun Moon Village was tantamount to breaking off all pretense of cordiality. What if the Chief of the Sun Moon Village came here to kill them?
Luo Zheng sat in a corner of the pavilion with his eyes closed. He silently operated his own blood vessels, rushing towards the black line above his dantian …
After being bound by the black line, he couldn't connect to the world inside his body. However, during this period of time, he could vaguely feel that something had changed in his world inside his body. This made Luo Zheng feel worried.
The flow of time in his world inside his body was extremely fast. Normally, when he was cultivating, Luo Zheng would peek into his world every few hours to prevent the creatures in this world from fighting each other. Occasionally, some clans would start a war, wanting to carry out a large-scale massacre, but they would be stopped by Luo Zheng in time.
During this period of time, after his dantian was sealed, he couldn't find out how far his world inside his body had evolved, or what had happened inside. He could only wait until these black lines were broken.
"Why don't we just leave? If the Sun and Moon Stockade attacks us, won't all of us die?! "
"Leave? Why should we leave? This place has been our Meng Mountain village's territory for generations. How can they just take it away like that? What kind of logic is that? "
"Speaking of which, our Meng Mountain village is a desolate place. What's the point of the Sun Moon Village coming here? They just have to go against us!"
The voices of those Gods became louder and louder. When Luo Zheng heard the last sentence, he slightly opened his eyes.
Indeed …
There was nothing important in this village. Outside of Meng Mountain village, there was only a flat and wide vegetable field. Why would the Sun Moon Village want this vegetable field?
"What are you afraid of? Why don't we just ask our benefactor to destroy the Sun Moon Village?" A God suddenly said with a loud voice.
This God said what everyone was thinking. Almost at the same time, all the people of Meng Mountain village turned their heads and looked at Luo Zheng. This' Luo Tianxing 'was their only hope. But, would Luo Tianxing be willing to take such a big risk to help them stop the Sun Moon Village?
Little Wu and Little Wen sat on the side, not saying a word.
This Luo Tianxing had suddenly appeared in this remote mountain village, taught them martial skills, taught them the Dao of Sword, and even helped them stop the Sun Moon Village. He had done more than enough. If they still hoped that he would help them eliminate the Sun Moon Village, it would be too much.
The entire attic suddenly became quiet. No one dared to ask Luo Zheng for help.
Sitting in the corner, Luo Zheng looked at the ceiling and said, "I'll be staying in Meng Village for another fifteen days. After fifteen days, I'll leave. Thank you for taking care of me. If those people from Sun and Moon Camp dare to come again, I won't stand by and do nothing."
This was the guarantee that Luo Zheng could give. He didn't know where the Sun Moon Village came from, but judging from the subordinates of the Sun Moon Village, the leader of the Sun Moon Village couldn't be very powerful. Even if Luo Zheng couldn't use the Chaotic Energy now, it wouldn't be difficult to deal with them.
However, Luo Zheng guessed that the Sun Moon Village might have a higher level of power behind it. With Luo Zheng's current strength, if he offended a True God, he would be in big trouble.
He didn't want to be chased around by a group of True Gods after entering the Divine Realm. If he wasn't careful, he might even lose his life. That would be too unfair.
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he closed his eyes again. Meanwhile, the people in the attic were whispering to each other.
With the Sun Moon Village's rude and unreasonable character, if they killed their men today, they would probably come to find them tomorrow. Luo Zheng's promise was already a great help!
The villagers of Meng Mountain village spent the night in anxiety …
The next day, Luo Zheng still had a calm expression on his face as he taught Xiao Wen, Xiao Wu, and the others how to practice the sword.
Halfway through her practice, Xiao Wen blinked her big eyes and said to Luo Zheng, "Actually, you should leave Meng Mountain village now!"
"Why do you say that?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
"The Sun Moon Village won't let this go easily, and you are alone." Xiao Wen's face showed a worried expression.
Xiao Wu and the others probably had the same thought. They knew that Luo Zheng's background was definitely not ordinary. Perhaps, he had no choice but to hide in Meng Mountain village. If he got involved in the dispute of the Sun Moon Village, he might expose himself. This was definitely not a wise choice.
The villagers also understood this, but Luo Zheng was their only hope. How could they be willing to let him go?
Luo Zheng smiled and said, "It's okay …"
He already knew that the nearest big city to Meng Mountain village was called Mirror City, but it was still hundreds of billions of miles away.
Mirror City was one of the seven Divine Cities in the Vast Sky Domain. Only Mirror City had True Divinities. Even if the Sun Moon Village invited strong reinforcements, they wouldn't be able to find him in a short period of time.
Just as the villagers of Meng Mountains had expected, the Sun Moon Village had sent seven warriors here a day ago, but none of them had returned. This had aroused the suspicion of the Sun Moon Village. Therefore, they sent someone to investigate and found that the seven warriors had been killed by the Divine People, stripped naked and thrown into the wilderness.
The chief of the Sun Moon Village was furious. He immediately led his men and charged toward Meng Mountain village.
As a Proof of Divinity Warrior, the chief was still very careful. He knew the abilities of his men. Seven Ultimate God Realm warriors were more than enough to slaughter the entire Meng Mountain village. But now, all seven of them were killed. There must be something strange going on.
When the chief arrived at Meng Mountain village with thirty to forty warriors, he saw a young man sitting quietly at the entrance of the village.
"A Proof of Divinity Warrior!"
As a Proof of Divinity Warrior, the chief's vision was much better than ordinary people. With just a glance, he could tell that Luo Zheng was not an ordinary person. Although Luo Zheng was trying to hide his aura, the aura that was casually leaking out from him still shocked the chief.
"I'll ensure the safety of Meng Mountain village. If you leave this place, I'll spare your life." Luo Zheng was the first to speak.
When the voice was heard, the subordinates of the chief immediately boiled up, as if they had been greatly insulted.
"Who do you think you are! Quickly kowtow to our chief and apologize, then apologize with your life! "
"You're just a Proof of Divinity Warrior, and you think you're the strongest in the world? Our chief will make you regret being born!"
"… …"
"Shut up, all of you!"

Chapter 1741
After countless eras of development, the Martial Dao in the Divine Realm had reached an absolute bottleneck.
Once a God was born, the height he could reach and the achievements he could achieve was basically determined, and all the social classes were basically fixed …
For a small mountain village like Meng Village, the chances of a strong warrior coming out of it was close to zero!
Although the chief of Sun Moon Stockade was furious, his eyes were very sharp. With just a glance, he could tell that Luo Zheng was not an ordinary person. Therefore, he had a strong sense of vigilance in his heart. Therefore, he asked about Luo Zheng's master first, because he wanted to know about Luo Zheng's background.
"My master?"
Luo Zheng glanced at this man indifferently, then said in an indifferent tone, "You don't have the right to know."
When those subordinates who had been scolded by the chief heard Luo Zheng's words, they immediately began to shout and scold him …
But just as those subordinates opened their mouths to speak, Luo Zheng had already flicked the small pebbles that were hidden in his fingers. Along with the sharp sound of breaking through the air, those warriors who were scolding had their jaws broken by Luo Zheng's pebbles, rolling on the ground in pain.
The chief of Sun Moon Stockade stood still. Even if Luo Zheng didn't attack, he knew that he was no match for this man. Since this man didn't want to tell him his master's background, he could only change his words and say, "My friend, to be honest, our Sun Moon Stockade is also working for someone! That person is not someone you can afford to offend. Offending that person for a Meng Village is definitely not a wise choice! "
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a pondering expression.
This chief of Sun Moon Stockade was a man with sharp eyes. He was not like those conceited idiots who wanted to find trouble with Luo Zheng and wanted to kill him.
Since he said he was working for someone, and that person was someone Luo Zheng couldn't afford to offend, that meant this person's cultivation base was most likely a True God expert.
The gap between Realm Lord and True God was too big. Just crossing one realm was like heaven and earth. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he didn't even have the slightest chance of winning when facing a lower True God.
Moreover, his Dantian was now bound by those black threads. It was even more impossible for him to be a match for a true god.
Noticing Luo Zheng's expression, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief laughed in his heart. This person is already hesitating. I'll hurry up a bit so that this person will know the difficulty and retreat!
This Sun Moon Stockade Chief was indeed helping someone, and that person's background was indeed quite big. At the very least, he was once one of the overlords of the Vast Sky Region!
In the past, the thirty-six islands and seventy-two strongholds in the north of the Vast Sky Region were all under this person's control. Moreover, there were seven Lower True Gods who followed this person.
Unfortunately, this person had suddenly disappeared a long time ago, and there was no news of him for dozens of eras. The thirty-six islands and seventy-two strongholds that he had once managed had all fallen apart and were rapidly falling apart.
There were only three strongholds left, and the Sun Moon Stronghold was one of them!
But, just like how the Sun Moon Camp was now in a state of decline, and all the glory and legends of the past had been completely forgotten. Even a tiny Meng Mountain village was not afraid of the Sun Moon Camp …
If it was in the past, not to mention a remote village, even the divine city of the Vast Sky Domain would not dare to underestimate the 72 strongholds and 36 islands!
But now, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief saw a great opportunity.
Because that person had returned.
Once that person was restored, the past glory would be restored. For the Sun Moon Stockade Chief, this was a great opportunity.
If it wasn't because his Sun Moon Stockade was once one of the seventy-two strongholds, he wouldn't even have the chance to talk to that person. It was also because of that person's nostalgia that he, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief, was given this opportunity.
Although the Sun Moon Stockade Chief was a Divinity Realm expert, it was just a way for Realm Lords to flatter themselves. Since the ancient times, there were too many divine people who were able to cultivate to the Realm Lord Realm, but the number of martial artists who were able to attain the Divinity Realm was very small.
If he was really valued by that person, attaining the Divinity Realm in the future wouldn't be a difficult task!
In the eyes of the Sun Moon Stockade Chief, although Luo Zheng was extraordinary, and it was highly possible that he was born in some great power, or even one of the wealthy families on the floating islands, he was still definitely not a match for that person.
However, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief didn't want to be looked down upon by that person. If he couldn't complete the task that was given to him, how could he be valued by that person in the future?
"Besides … we're just taking back the territory that belongs to us," the Sun Moon Stockade Chief added.
"Our Meng Village is your territory?"
"Nonsense!"
"We, the villagers of Meng Mountains, have been rooted here for generations. Don't you have any shame?!"
The villagers of Meng Village were hiding in the houses and pavilions in the village, quietly observing the situation outside the village. But when they heard what the Sun Moon Stockade Chief said, these divine people couldn't hold themselves back anymore. After all, they were the natives of Meng Village.
But to their surprise, when the Sun Moon Stockade Chief heard what they said, he smiled and said calmly, "Generations? How many years has it been? According to what I know, it hasn't even been one divine era since the people of Meng Village migrated here, and seven divine eras ago, this place was indeed our territory, or should I say, that person's territory! "
The lifespan of divine people was not short, but one divine era was already very long, let alone seven divine eras …
"Seven divine eras? Why don't you say one hundred? "
"There's no proof for this, it's just your side of the story!"
"I can even say that one hundred divine eras ago, the ancestors of Meng Village had also built a village here! Haha … "
The villagers of Meng Village refuted again.
But the Sun Moon Stockade Chief just smiled coldly and said, "There's no way you haven't heard of the legend of the thirty-six islands and seventy-two stockades. I'm just telling you that that person has returned. It's up to you to believe it or not!"
When that person first appeared in the Sun Moon Stockade, the Stockade Chief didn't believe it either. To the Sun Moon Stockade Chief, that was already an ancient legend.
Legends could be fake, but strength couldn't be faked. That person had the strength of an Intermediate True God.
Hearing the Sun Moon Stockade Chief's words, the villagers of Meng Village immediately quieted down. At the same time, whispers could be heard, but Luo Zheng heard all of these voices.
"Thirty-six islands and seventy-two stockades. I remember there was such a legend in the Vast Sky Domain. Our Meng Village belongs to one of those regions."
"Hmph, this is just a story made up by someone. How many divine eras ago? How can you believe something passed down from such a long time ago? "
"That's right. That person is the infamous Old Man of Extreme. But according to the legend, he died a long time ago, and those stockades and islands have also collapsed."
"…"
Although the voices of these discussions were soft, Luo Zheng heard every single word. When he heard the words' Old Man of Extreme ', he raised his eyebrows slightly. The corner of his mouth curled up slightly, revealing an indescribable smile. He stared at the Sun Moon Stockade Chief and asked, "The person you are talking about, is he really the Old Man of Extreme?"
It was still inconvenient to spread the news of the Old Man of Extreme's return to the outside world. At the very least, he had to help the Old Man of Extreme retrieve all the things he buried in Meng Village.

Chapter 1742
The people of Meng Village did not believe that the Evil Old Man had returned.
After all, this legend had been around for too long. Most of the people in the legends had already passed away. It was indeed hard to believe that they would suddenly reappear one day.
However, Luo Zheng believed it because Luo Zheng had personally released the Evil Old Man.
Previously, after Luo Zheng had released the Evil Old Man, the Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster had charged into the Takixuan Forest. Then, the Immortal Abode had been attacked by a group of ferocious creatures from the divine realm.
Luo Zheng did not know if the two of them had died in the hands of those creatures. At that time, Luo Zheng was also worried that the Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster would not be able to survive against those creatures in the Takixuan Forest, no matter how powerful the Evil Old Man and the big-mouthed monster were.
Now, it seemed that Luo Zheng's worries were unnecessary. The Evil Old Man had been imprisoned for so long. As soon as he was released, he immediately tried to restore his former glory and status. Coincidentally, the Evil Old Man's lair was in the Vast Sky Territory!
"If you don't believe me, the next person to come here will not be me, but the Evil Old Man himself," the Sun Moon Stockade Chief said.
A peak mid-level True God was not to be underestimated in the divine realm!
With such a backer, the Sun Moon Stockade's status in the future would be greatly improved. The Sun Moon Stockade Chief was naturally very pleased. He believed that Luo Zheng would definitely compromise. No one was willing to court death in front of a True God!
"I believe you," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief and his subordinates instantly heaved a sigh of relief. They obviously did not want to provoke Luo Zheng, a martial artist who had proven himself to be a True God. If they could make Luo Zheng retreat, that would be the best outcome!
"If that's the case, you can persuade the villagers of Meng Mountains to leave obediently. Or you can leave yourself. Leave these villagers to us," the Sun Moon Stockade Chief said with a smile.
The villagers of Meng Mountains did not expect Luo Zheng to actually believe the Sun Moon Stockade Chief's words. They were stunned. "Luo Tianxing, you can't believe what they say! They're just trying to scare us … "
"The Sun Moon Stockade is just a dilapidated stockade. It's been half dead for tens of thousands of years. How can they invite a True God?"
"…"
Luo Zheng did not refute the words of the villagers behind him. He continued to speak to the Sun Moon Stockade Chief, "I believe that the Evil Old Man has indeed returned, but I will not leave. If you really want me to leave, you can ask the Evil Old Man to come here himself!"
The warriors of the Sun Moon Village had relaxed looks on their faces. They thought that since Luo Zheng had compromised, this matter would naturally be resolved. However, when they heard Luo Zheng's words, their faces instantly stiffened.
Luo Zheng had his own plans.
Luo Zheng had just entered the God's Domain, so it would be difficult for him to fully integrate into the God's Domain. If he could obtain the help of the Evil Old Man, he would be able to achieve twice the result with half the effort!
"That's right! Get Old Man Evil to come personally!"
"Let's see where your Sun Moon Village can find a True God! If there really is a True God, why would the Sun Moon Stockade need to chase us away? "
"I also want to see the legendary True God!"
However, the villagers of Meng Mountains misunderstood Luo Zheng's meaning. They thought that Luo Zheng did not believe the words of the Sun Moon Stockade Chief and was just taking advantage of the situation to speak.
The Sun Moon Stockade Chief's face turned ashen, and his voice became cold again. "Do you know the consequences of the Evil Old Man coming here personally?"
"Of course I do." Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
"Very well!" The Sun Moon Stockade Chief sneered and left with his subordinates. Today, he could not persuade Luo Zheng to leave and drive away the villagers of Meng Mountains. He could only leave first and let the Evil Old Man come personally.
Although he would be accused of incompetence, he also knew that with the Evil Old Man's methods, the villagers of Meng Mountain Village and this Luo Zheng before him would all die!
After the Sun Moon Stockade Chief left, the villagers who were hiding rushed out. Most of them had a victorious expression on their faces. They were glad that they had chased away the warriors of the Sun Moon Stockade without shedding a drop of blood.
"The Sun Moon Stockade has been around for god knows how many years. There are only a few people in the stockade. Their strength is average, but their bragging skills are amazing. They insisted that they had a True God!"
"What a joke! According to the legends, the Evil Old Man was a big shot who ruled the north of Vast Sky Domain. Why would such a person appear in a small stockade? "
"That makes sense!"
In the eyes of the villagers of Meng Mountains, the Sun Moon Stockade was not much stronger than Meng Mountain Village. It was just a stockade made up of a bunch of mobs. Before this, they were still afraid of the Sun Moon Stockade Chief. Now that Meng Mountain Village had 'Luo Tianxing', why would they care about the Sun Moon Stockade?
However, Luo Zheng's words poured a bucket of cold water on the villagers' enthusiasm.
"The Evil Old Man should be back," Luo Zheng said calmly.
"He's really back? Are you kidding? "A villager chuckled. He did not believe Luo Zheng's words either.
"I'm not kidding." Luo Zheng shook his head. "The Evil Old Man has indeed returned to Vast Sky Domain. The Sun Moon Stockade Chief is not lying. I've seen the Evil Old Man himself not long ago."
When Luo Zheng said this, the noisy atmosphere at the entrance of Meng Mountain Village instantly froze. Everyone was immersed in silence …
"You, you, you're telling the truth?" Another villager asked with a serious expression.
The people of Meng Mountain Village dared to go against the Sun Moon Stockade, but they would never dare to offend a True God …
"It's true," Luo Zheng said. "But …" He wanted to comfort them. Although the Evil Old Man was powerful, he could not disobey Luo Zheng's orders. Unfortunately, these people were probably frightened. Now, they were extremely agitated.
"Then, then what should we do?"
"Let's go. I heard that the Evil Old Man committed all kinds of crimes. That's why he was named 'Evil Old Man'. Back in the day, he was so evil that an almighty figure subdued him. He has disappeared for so many years. Is he really going to be restored?"
"This is Meng Mountain Village. It was left behind by our ancestors …"
"Ancestors my ass. The Evil Old Man has lived longer than all of our ancestors combined. If the legend is true, we are indeed occupying the Evil Old Man's territory!"
"…"
Since these villagers were so agitated, Luo Zheng did not bother to explain …
Sun Moon Stockade, Turbid Moon Cave.
Turbid Moon Cave was originally an abandoned cave. The cave entrance had collapsed many years ago and was abandoned long ago.
However, during this period of time, the Sun Moon Stockade dug up the cave entrance again and cleaned it thoroughly. Then, it was listed as a forbidden area by the Sun Moon Stockade. No one was allowed to come close!
The Sun Moon Stockade Chief was now kneeling respectfully in front of Turbid Moon Cave. He said softly, "Evil Old Man, it's not that I didn't do my job well. Meng Mountain Village has invited a strong helper from somewhere. Although he is only a Proof God, his strength is indeed extraordinary. I'm very sensitive to soul divine abilities. That Proof God's soul is probably far stronger than a Golden Battle Soul. I'm afraid it's not inferior to a True God's soul!"
After he finished speaking, the Sun Moon Stockade Chief remained kneeling on the ground.
"Bullsh * t! He's just a Realm Lord. It's already very impressive for his soul to become a Golden Battle Soul. Possessing a True God's soul? Even those wealthy families on the floating islands can't do that! " The Evil Old Man's voice came from Turbid Moon Cave. There were not many Realm Lords who possessed a Golden Battle Soul. The Evil Old Man found it hard to believe.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1743
After Old Man Evil left Takeshima Forest, he saw a group of ferocious creatures heading straight for the Immortal Abode.
Although Old Man Evil and the big-mouthed monster were powerful, they would most likely die if they were surrounded by this group of ferocious creatures …
The strange thing was that these beasts only glanced at the two of them indifferently and then ignored them.
After those creatures left, Old Man Evil and the big-mouthed monster quietly left Takeshima Forest and went their separate ways.
Old Man Evil was imprisoned for several divine epochs …
To a True God's world, seven or eight divine epochs wouldn't make much of a difference. At most, two or three more Saints would be conferred and the ranking of the floating islands on the Sea of Time would change slightly.
However, to the Gods' citizens, this change was still rather significant.
When Old Man Evil returned to the Vast Sky Domain, he realized that the thirty-six islands and seventy-two strongholds he had built had long been destroyed. Most of them had disappeared in the river of time.
Old Man Evil had expected this. However, when he learned that there were still three strongholds left among the seventy-two strongholds, he was very touched. After all, these were the legacies he had left behind. After so many years, there were still martial artists bitterly holding on.
Although Old Man Evil had committed many evil deeds, he was a very loyal person. Although these people were just a joke in Old Man Evil's eyes in terms of talent and strength, he still treated them well. He was prepared to take out some of the things he had hidden in the Vast Sky Domain and slowly strengthen this faction!
However, some of Old Man Evil's things couldn't be exposed. For example, the things he had buried in Meng Village. He had just returned to the Vast Sky Domain and his foundation wasn't stable yet. Naturally, he had to be careful.
However, these people from Sun Moon Stronghold were indeed weak beyond his imagination. They couldn't even do something as simple as driving away those Gods' citizens. This was indeed beyond Old Man Evil's expectations.
Now that the Sun Moon Stronghold Chief reported this to him, Old Man Evil naturally had to praise Luo Zheng to the heavens in order to cover up his own incompetence. A Realm Lord's soul was comparable to a True God's soul? This was utter nonsense!
After so many years, he had only seen one person who had cultivated his soul to such a level in the Realm Lord realm. It was that brat Luo Zheng whom he met in the Immortal Abode!
Luo Zheng was the disciple of a Saint and his father was a Saint. There weren't many people like him in the Divine Region …
How could a Proof God martial artist appear in such a desolate village in the north of Vast Sky Domain? Old Man Evil wouldn't believe it even if he was beaten to death.
"If I'm lying, Ancestor, you can chop my head off. If you don't believe me, Ancestor, you can make a trip there personally. Everything will be clear then!" The Sun Moon Stockade Chief said solemnly.
"You're saying that I have to go personally to chase away some divine citizens?" Old Man Evil's voice rang out from Turbid Moon Cave again.
"I did not do my job well. There is indeed no need for Elder Ancestor to step in for such a small matter. However, the problem lies with that martial artist who has proven himself to be a Divinity!" the Sun Moon Stockade Chief hurriedly said, afraid that the Evil Old Man would be angered.
Actually, the Sun Moon Stockade chief's understanding of the Old Man of Evil also came from legends. After all, he had not been in charge of the Sun Moon Stockade for a very long time after becoming a Proof of Divinity expert. Moreover, after so many years, the records of the Old Man of Evil in the Sun Moon Stockade were very vague. However, just from the name "Old Man of Evil", he knew that the Old Man of Evil was definitely not a good person!
Since Old Man Evil had said so, Old Man Evil could only sneer. "It seems like I have to go there personally!"
When Old Man Evil heard this, Old Man Evil was overjoyed. He knew that Old Man Evil did not blame him for his incompetence. With Old Man Evil's strength, killing that Proof God martial artist was just a piece of cake. Old Man Evil could finally see what happened to that Proof God martial artist in Meng Village.
After confirming that Old Man Evil had returned, the God citizens of Meng Village finally decided to give up on Meng Village. No matter how important something was, it was not as important as their own lives. They had no way of resisting a True God, especially one who was at the peak of the middle stage.
However, Luo Zheng stood out and told them not to leave.
Luo Zheng did not tell them the reason, but he was very confident that he could guarantee their safety!
However, most of the people of Meng Village still did not choose to believe Luo Zheng. Perhaps Luo Zheng was indeed an extraordinary Proof God martial artist, but he was only a Realm Lord. He was not at the same level as Old Man Evil.
Thousands of God citizens of Meng Village began to move and hide on the same day. However, there were still a considerable number of God citizens who stayed behind. These God citizens were divided into two groups. One group was those who were unwilling to leave Meng Village no matter what, while the other group was led by Little Wu and Little Wen. They chose to believe in Luo Zheng!
On the second day, when the sky was just beginning to brighten, ninety percent of the residents of Meng Village had already left. Only one-tenth of the villagers were left.
Today, Luo Zheng was still sitting at the entrance of Meng Village. He was grinding out the black lines on his Dantian while waiting silently. He was worried that Old Man Evil would not show up and send another group of weak and ordinary martial artists over.
As for the God citizens who stayed behind with Luo Zheng, they were even more nervous. If Luo Zheng could not deal with Old Man Evil, they would be buried together with him!
Not long after, the group of martial artists from Sun Moon Fort arrived in a mighty manner. Old Man Evil was strolling leisurely at the back of the group.
After crossing a hill, Old Man Evil could see the entirety of Meng Village in front of him.
"We're almost there, Old Ancestor." Sun Moon Fort's chief reminded Old Man Evil. "That Proof God martial artist is probably still guarding the entrance of Meng Village. With Old Ancestor's strength, killing him is just a matter of a blink of an eye!"
Old Man Evil sat lazily on a palanquin with a nonchalant expression on his face. Even if Sun Moon Fort's chief was telling the truth, his opponent was still just a Realm Lord. He was not qualified to be his opponent.
However, after crossing this short hill and arriving at the entrance of Meng Village, Old Man Evil looked in the direction of Sun Moon Fort's chief's hand gesture. His gaze focused on the entrance of the village. His eyes suddenly widened. He, who was originally lying lazily on the palanquin, suddenly stood up with an expression as if he had seen a ghost!
"Luo Zheng!"
Then, he rubbed his eyes with his hands, as if he wanted to see more clearly …
"Old Ancestor?" Seeing Old Man Evil's expression, Sun Moon Fort's chief was slightly stunned. Could it be that the Proof God martial artist from Meng Village was indeed someone with a powerful background?
Old Man Evil did not answer Sun Moon Fort's chief. After he observed for a while and confirmed Luo Zheng's identity, he felt conflicted! When he left the Immortal Abode, Old Man Evil had hoped that he would never meet Luo Zheng again. After all, the Divine Realm was so vast. The possibility of meeting him again was next to nothing. He did not expect to meet him again after such a short period of time. What the hell!
Old Man Evil jumped down from the palanquin and left without saying a word. Sun Moon Fort's chief was baffled by this scene.

Chapter 1744
The Evil Old Man did not expect to meet Luo Zheng here!
The Takixuan Forest was not far from here, but it was still three regions away. The Evil Old Man did not know how to describe his feelings now that he had coincidentally met Luo Zheng in this small mountain village in the north of the Vast Sky Domain. He could only say that he and Luo Zheng were fated to meet.
However, the person he did not want to see the most, other than Gu Bei and the other Saints, was probably Luo Zheng.
In order to leave the dungeon, he had made an extremely unfair Oath of the Demon of the Heart to Luo Zheng. He had to obey Luo Zheng's every word. If he disobeyed the oath, he would be devoured by the Demon of the Heart!
After finally leaving the dungeon, the Evil Old Man naturally hoped to go further on the path of godhood. He was now at the peak of middle-level True God. Just one more step and he would become an upper-level True God. When that time came, his strength and status would rise to another level. Even the Saints would have to be careful if they wanted to harm him.
To be safe, he would definitely not violate his Heart Demon Oath in the slightest. Therefore, as soon as Luo Zheng spoke, his body, which had turned away, suddenly stiffened. Then, he slowly turned his head and chuckled. "I know, of course I know."
"That's good." Luo Zheng nodded. "Since you still remember, you can stay here today. I have something I need your help with."
The Sun Moon Stockade chief and his subordinates were dumbfounded. How high was the Evil Old Man's position in their hearts?
Who was this Proving God martial artist from Meng Village? How dare he speak to the Evil Old Man in such a manner!
Old Man Evil, on the other hand, was neither angry nor annoyed. Instead, he chuckled amiably. His attitude made it even harder for them to wrap their heads around it.
"If you need my help, it's fine. However, I have just returned to the Vast Sky Domain. There are some things I need to take away. Coincidentally, they are buried under Meng Village. Do you want these villagers to leave first?" The Evil Old Man said. In his eyes, these divine citizens were no different from ants. But since Luo Zheng was here, he naturally had to get Luo Zheng's approval first …
When the villagers of Meng Mountains saw that the Evil Old Man had really come here, they were naturally frightened. The Evil Old Man was the legendary Evil God. In the Vast Sky Domain, if any children were disobedient, some adults would use him to scare their own children. "If you continue to be disobedient, the Evil Old Man will come and take you away!" Just this sentence alone was enough to stop a baby from crying.
Now that the legendary Evil God had appeared here, how could the villagers survive? Fortunately, most of the people in Meng Village had already left, leaving behind only these divine people.
But just as Luo Zheng had promised, these people did not expect the Evil Old Man to be so polite in front of Luo Zheng. How could he be worthy of the name "Evil Old Man"?
When they saw this amiable scene, they were even more surprised and speculated about Luo Zheng's background. Now that the Evil Old Man had made such a request, Luo Zheng discussed it with the rest of the divine citizens.
Since the Evil Old Man's original intention was not to chase away the villagers of Meng Mountains but to take away the things buried here, the villagers of Meng Mountains were not so against it …
Therefore, the remaining divine citizens left Meng Village first and met up with most of the divine citizens who were temporarily hiding. The Evil Old Man ordered the Sun Moon Fort's chief to lead a group of martial artists to guard outside before the two of them entered the village!
The Evil Old Man shook his head and lamented to Luo Zheng, "Sigh, this mountain used to be called Blackwind Ridge. It was one of my old bases. Blackwind Ridge used to be famous throughout the Vast Sky Domain, but now, it has fallen to the point where no one knows about it."
Only three of the seventy-two forts he had established were left. Moreover, after so many years, things had changed. Whenever he thought about it after returning to the Vast Sky Domain, he would feel aggrieved but had nowhere to vent his frustrations.
Luo Zheng, who was following behind the Evil Old Man, smiled when he heard what the Evil Old Man said, "If a Real God's lifespan is endless, you will only lose some time. To a Real God like you, time is the most worthless thing, right? As long as you spend some time, you can rebuild some things. Why can't you make a comeback? "
As soon as Luo Zheng finished speaking, the Evil Old Man paused slightly. He turned his head and looked deeply at Luo Zheng before saying slowly, "You're right. However, the environment in the Divine Territory is constantly changing. I don't know if there will be an opportunity like back then …"
Back then, the Evil Old Man had just stepped into the realm of a middle-level True God. It was extremely difficult for him to dominate the Vast Sky Domain. However, the two major forces in the Vast Sky Domain happened to be fighting each other, so they did not have the time to care about the area north of the Vast Sky Domain. That was how the Evil Old Man was able to rapidly expand his power!
However, if he wanted to find an opportunity like that now, it would be extremely difficult.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would smile and say, "There's an even greater opportunity now. Can't you see it?"
"An even greater opportunity?" The Evil Old Man was slightly taken aback. He stared at Luo Zheng for a long while before his eyes suddenly lit up. His breathing seemed to have quickened as well.
He already knew Luo Zheng's identity. He was Gu Bei's disciple and Luo Xiao's son.
Although the Vast Sky Domain was only a small domain in the Divine Territory, the Evil Old Man had heard about the matters on the floating island and the conflicts between the Sages!
Since Luo Zheng had stepped into the Divine Territory, he definitely had a grand plan. Luo Zheng had two Sages backing him up. The Evil Old Man believed that Luo Zheng would be able to rise in the shortest time possible.
If he could follow Luo Zheng, it would indeed be a great opportunity for the Evil Old Man!
"No, no, no …" The Evil Old Man's head shook like a rattle drum as he immediately rejected this idea.
Although Luo Zheng was the successor of two Sages, Gu Bei had disappeared for many Divine Eras, and Luo Xiao was on the verge of death!
And Luo Zheng would have to face Mu Haiji and even the Sage Hall …
The opportunity was not small, but the risk was too great. That woman, Shi Xiaoqiao, had the right to get involved, but he, the Evil Old Man, would only be cannon fodder if he got involved!
Seeing the Evil Old Man's reaction, Luo Zheng only said indifferently, "A True God's lifespan is indeed unlimited, but are you really willing to spend the long years of your life in mediocrity?"
The Evil Old Man chuckled. "That's better than dying!"
The Evil Old Man knew that Luo Zheng's potential was limitless. Even if he could not become a Sage in the future, it was still possible for him to become an upper-level True God or even a perfected True God …
However, not everyone had the courage to go against a Sage!
Since this was the Evil Old Man's attitude, Luo Zheng did not force him. Right now, he only needed the Evil Old Man's help to stabilize himself in the Divine Realm.
After the Evil Old Man entered the center of Meng Village, he reached out and patted the ground left and right, as if he was looking for something.
After searching for a while, he finally found a spot. Then, he walked a few steps to the side of the spot and suddenly slapped the ground!
A huge Divine Rune formed in his hand!
This Divine Rune turned into a huge arrowhead that slowly crawled on the ground!
"Clack!"
At this moment, a muffled sound suddenly came from below Meng Village. It was as if some huge mechanism had been opened deep underground.
The Evil Old Man's face lit up. The Sealing Mechanism he had set up years ago was still effective. This meant that after he had left for so many years, no one had been able to touch what he had hidden underground.
However, as soon as he activated the 'Sealing Mechanism', a powerful aura suddenly erupted from one of the pavilions in Meng Village. At the same time, an arrogant laughter rang out. "Hahaha, Heinous Old Dog, I've waited for seven divine eras, and you've finally returned!"
The Evil Old Man's expression changed drastically when he heard that voice!
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed tightly.

Chapter 1745
The Extreme Evil Old Man's Fengjueji was rather ingenious.
Once the Fengjueji was sealed, there was only one chance to activate it. If the sequence of activation was wrong, the entire Fengjueji would self-destruct, along with the item inside!
Back then, he had sealed an extremely important treasure that he had obtained into it before burying it under the Black Wind Ridge. If someone really wanted to take that treasure, the only result would be that they would not be able to obtain anything.
Seven divine epochs had passed, and the first thing the Extreme Evil Old Man did when he returned was to retrieve this treasure. It was obvious that this treasure was very important to him.
However, the Extreme Evil Old Man did not expect that someone had been waiting for him in the Vast Sky Domain for seven divine epochs …
Luo Zheng had been staying in Meng Village all this time, but he did not notice that there was such a person in the village.
To be able to hide in Meng Village, completely avoid Luo Zheng's sharp divine sense, and even dare to show up now, this person's strength was probably above the Extreme Evil Old Man!
With a creak, the door of a house in Meng Village opened, and a person walked out. This person was skinny and had a face as clear as jade. He held an exquisite feather fan in his hand and had a proud smile on his face. As he walked, he said calmly, "Actually, I never thought that you, Extreme Evil Old Dog, would return one day. This Fengjueji is too ingenious. I've studied it for so many years, and even asked someone to use the Divine Dao of Time to imprison this Fengjueji. I wanted to take that item out after activating this Fengjueji. Unfortunately, I didn't dare to take the risk, for fear of destroying this Fengjueji. I've waited so long for this day! "
The Extreme Evil Old Man's face was covered in a layer of frost. His soybean-sized eyes stared at the person as he spoke word by word, "Yin Yuanzi! You're very patient! "
Looking at the Extreme Evil Old Man's expression, the smile on Yin Yuanzi's face became even wider. "Of course! For that item, I can afford to waste any amount of time. What is seven divine epochs to me? I can wait for seventy divine epochs! "
As Yin Yuanzi spoke, he glanced at Luo Zheng, who was behind the Extreme Evil Old Man. "But I'm curious. As a little fellow called Luo Tianxing, where did you come from? Even among the Proving Divinity experts, he's extremely outstanding. Could it be that he's from some wealthy family on the floating island? "
Luo Zheng frowned slightly and didn't say anything. Since Yin Yuanzi knew that his name was Luo Tianxing, he must have been lurking here for several days. Moreover, during these days, this guy should have been secretly observing him. This feeling made Luo Zheng quite uncomfortable.
The way Yin Yuanzi saw it, only the wealthy families of the floating islands could nurture such a Proof of Divinity martial artist!
Judging from Luo Zheng's background, he was indeed a descendant of a Floating Island Clan. After all, the Luo Clan once owned a Floating Island in the Sea of Time. Moreover, the Luo Clan's Floating Island was ranked quite high. Unfortunately, after Luo Xiao's defeat, the Floating Island was occupied by other clans. Even the disciples on the Floating Island were expelled.
How could the Extreme Evil Old Man be in the mood to explain anything to Yin Yuanzi? He had already activated the Sealing Extinction Mechanism. His mind was racing. His cultivation was similar to Yin Yuanzi's, but he had been locked up in the dungeon for so many years. Although his cultivation was still there, his strength had most likely been reduced!
Although Yin Yuanzi was also a middle True God, he must have improved quite a bit over the years. With one advancing and one retreating, the difference in strength between the two was probably not small.
"Patience is indeed there, but I don't know … if you have the strength!" The Extreme Evil Old Man quietly prepared. Suddenly, he stomped his feet. Black smoke swirled around him, and his entire person seemed to turn into an illusory human figure.
"Extreme Evil Dao!"
As the black smoke swirled around him, the Extreme Evil Old Man's originally snow-white hair suddenly turned black. His entire person revealed his evil side. The wrinkles on his face looked as if they had been cut by a knife. Two illusory demonic claws condensed on his hands. The Extreme Evil Old Man charged at Yin Yuanzi at an extremely fast speed.
The Extreme Evil Old Man was not tall. He was a head shorter than Luo Zheng. Due to his old age, his body was even skinnier. However, he was still a True God. Once he attacked, it would be a fatal move.
The pair of demonic claws condensed and clawed at Yin Yuanzi's head!
This attack was indeed extraordinarily fierce. From Luo Zheng's point of view, the Extreme Evil Old Man's claw seemed ordinary. However, the power contained within it shocked him. At the very least, with Luo Zheng's current strength, there was no way he could withstand this attack.
Yin Yuanzi seemed to be quite familiar with the Extreme Evil Old Man. He also knew how powerful the Extreme Evil Old Man's attack was, so he did not take it head-on. However, as he retreated, he gently waved the feather fan in his hand. Instantly, his entire body became extremely agile. His body shifted, leaving behind afterimages, and he easily dodged the Extreme Evil Old Man's claw.
As the two True Gods fought, Luo Zheng did not have the time to watch the show. If he was not careful, he might be implicated. He quietly retreated and leaped toward a nearby house. Then, he heard the houses and pavilions in Meng Village collapsing. Cracks appeared on the surface of the hill, and there were faint signs of it collapsing.
Luo Zheng was glad that he had evacuated the villagers of Meng Village. Otherwise, if the two True Gods were to fight, the villagers would have suffered.
In the universe, he had seen True Divinities attack. They could be said to be able to destroy the heavens and earth. Their strength was hundreds of times more tyrannical than an Empyrean's. But now, as these two True Divinities fought, they were only able to destroy a single mountain. The Divine Realm's restrictions on strength were not ordinary …
The feather fan in Yin Yuanzi's hand was like a saber and a shield. Not only could it block the Extreme Evil Old Man's claw, but it could also strike at the Extreme Evil Old Man when it found an opening.
The feather fan looked light, but there was a powerful aura condensing within it. It turned into a sharp blade that slashed toward the Extreme Evil Old Man. This blade was fast and ruthless. There were a few times when the Extreme Evil Old Man could not dodge it, and a deep wound appeared on his body.
The smile on Yin Yuanzi's face grew wider. Back then, the Extreme Evil Old Man had hit him when he was down, and he ended up in a miserable state. After lying low for seven Divine Eras, not only did he have his revenge, but he was also very pleased with himself when he thought about how he was about to obtain an important item.
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt even more conflicted. He had encountered the Extreme Evil Old Man in the Divine Territory and wanted to ask for his help. He did not expect that the Extreme Evil Old Man would encounter such a powerful enemy as soon as he returned to the Vast Sky Territory …
With Luo Zheng's current strength, even watching a battle of this level was very risky, let alone helping. The wisest choice for him now was to leave!
"Hehe, Extreme Evil Old Dog, when you stole my fortune back then, you didn't expect this day to come, did you?"
Yin Yuanzi suddenly turned around and dodged the Extreme Evil Old Man's claw once again. However, he waved the feather fan and slammed it down heavily on the Extreme Evil Old Man's body!
"Boom!"
The Extreme Evil Old Man's thin and weak body crashed into Meng Village. The hill, which was already full of cracks, finally collapsed. However, the hill where Meng Village was located was hollow!
When the hill collapsed, the houses and pavilions on the hill quickly caved in as well, falling into a huge cave in the hill. Luo Zheng could not help but fall into the cave as well.
Luo Zheng, who was falling rapidly, was very frustrated. It was indeed very inconvenient to not be able to fly in the Divine Territory!
As for the Sun Moon Stockade martial artists outside Meng Village, they were also dumbfounded. The Extreme Evil Old Man did not allow them to enter Meng Village, so how would they dare to take a step in? However, they had no idea what had happened in Meng Village.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1746
Luo Zheng fell all the way down and was also dizzy from the fall.
The mass of matter in the Divine Realm was tens of thousands of times greater than matter in the universe. When the mountain collapsed, the force of impact was enough to smash a Divine Transformation Realm or even a Divine Pinnacle Realm martial artist to death!
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's body was strong enough to withstand the fall. However, he was suddenly involved in the conflict between Old Man Extreme Evil and Yin Yuanzi. This was indeed an unexpected calamity.
"Rumble, rumble …"
It turned out that there was a huge cave at the bottom of Black Wind Ridge. Old Man Extreme Evil had set this place as his lair because of how well hidden this cave was.
Back then, after he had set up the 'Sealing Mechanism', he had completely sealed off this cave. For several divine epochs, only Yin Yuanzi had secretly snuck into this cave in an attempt to open the 'Sealing Mechanism' and take away the treasure hidden within. However, Old Man Extreme Evil's' Sealing Mechanism 'was extremely ingenious. It was almost impossible to take out the' Sealing Mechanism 'without destroying the treasure. Yin Yuanzi had waited in the Vast Sky Domain for seven divine epochs, but in the end, Old Man Extreme Evil had returned!
The villagers of Meng Village were completely unaware of this. Their ancestors had come to this small mountain at Black Wind Ridge and continuously reclaimed it, turning Black Wind Ridge into a small mountain village and becoming the residence of Meng Village's ancestors.
Now that Yin Yuanzi and Old Man Extreme Evil were fighting, the small mountain had collapsed …
Apart from Luo Zheng, Yin Yuanzi and Old Man Extreme Evil were also unable to fly in the Divine Realm. At this moment, they naturally fell into the cave like Luo Zheng.
However, both of them were middle True Gods. Their strength was naturally much stronger than Luo Zheng's. Amidst the rubble, Old Man Extreme Evil and Yin Yuanzi's figures were still fighting each other …
After a long while, Luo Zheng finally fell to the bottom of the cave. The rubble and sand buried him. The weight of the rubble in the Divine Realm was astonishing. Although Luo Zheng only felt a few large rocks on top of his head, he felt as if tens of thousands of mountains were pressing down on him, firmly pressing him down!
Like an earthworm, he could only wriggle out of the sand bit by bit.
When Luo Zheng stuck his head out and spat out the sand in his mouth, he was able to clearly see the scene in the cave through the dim light from the top of the cave.
The cave wasn't big, only about a thousand feet wide. In the center of the cave, there was a sphere about ten feet wide. There seemed to be a light flickering in the sphere, constantly changing from bright to dark, then from dark to bright, like a firefly constantly emitting light.
"There's actually a living creature inside this sphere? Is this the treasure that the Evil Old Man wants to take away? "Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Outside the sphere, there were also three-foot-long swords of light. They slowly floated around the sphere, but at this moment, the surface of the swords of light was very dim, as if they had lost the power they should have.
This sphere was the Fengjueji that the Evil Old Man had set up. The Fengjueji was a secret technique from the Southern Mountain Range that the Evil Old Man had obtained by chance.
The Fengjueji's design was extremely ingenious. If a treasure was sealed inside, it could only be opened according to the method of the person who set it up. If the order was slightly different, the swords of light around the sphere would activate and instantly destroy the treasure inside the sphere!
Therefore, Yin Yuanzi had hidden here for several days, waiting for the Evil Old Man to unseal the Fengjueji and the swords of light lost their effect before he chose to make his move.
"Swoosh!"
On the other side of the cave, the Evil Old Man brandished his demonic claws. Black smoke billowed as a strong evil intent was released. It was as if a pure evil demon wanted to tear everything in this world apart!
The Evil Old Man probably noticed the scene inside the Fengjueji as well. His face turned cold, and he looked like he was going to fight Yin Yuanzi to the death!
Yin Yuanzi was indeed slightly stronger than the Evil Old Man. His feather fan was like a white cloud that could easily disperse the Evil Old Man's attacks. However, the Evil Old Man's ruthlessness forced Yin Yuanzi to continuously retreat …
"Yin Yuanzi, I, the Evil Old Man, have committed all kinds of evil in the past, but I have never offended your Essence Qi Sect. Back then, when the Ghost Sect wanted to attack your Essence Qi Sect, I even interfered! Today, you must take my treasure? " The Evil Old Man was probably affected by the billowing evil intent. He spoke through gritted teeth, his tone sinister.
Yin Yuanzi waved his feather fan and said indifferently, "I have no choice. The item sealed in your Fengjueji is too important. Let alone me, if anyone else knew of its existence, they would not let it go so easily. You are not my match. If I advise you to give up on this item, I can give you other compensation. How about it?"
"What a joke!"
The Evil Old Man laughed maniacally. His black hair was swept to the back of his head like a waterfall. He sneered and said, "Back then, it was because of this item that I offended Saint Gu Bei. I was imprisoned for seven divine eras before I was able to escape. Am I going to give it to you just like that? Who do you think I, the Evil Old Man, am? "
"Heh." Yin Yuanzi sneered and said, "If you don't give it to me, then I'll take it myself. I won't just take your treasure, I'll take your life as well. Isn't that a good deal for you?"
As soon as he finished speaking, Yin Yuanzi's feet moved rapidly on the ground while he suddenly flipped the feather fan in his hand.
A layer of green light suddenly appeared on the surface of the pure white feather fan. The green light rippled and transformed into a ghost face. The ghost face's eyes were filled with laughter, but its mouth drooped down, causing it to look extremely strange.
When the ghost face appeared, Yin Yuanzi flipped the feather fan again, and another ghost face appeared on the other side of the fan. This ghost face was the exact opposite of the ghost face on the other side of the fan. It had tears in its eyes, but it was laughing without end!
After the two ghost faces appeared, Yin Yuanzi's expression became strange. One side of his face was filled with joy, while the other side was filled with sorrow!
"Temperamental ghost!"
Previously, Yin Yuanzi had been fighting with the feather fan in his hand and did not dare to face the Evil Old Man head-on. When this change appeared on his face, he rushed forward and did not dodge the Evil Old Man's demonic claws. Instead, he formed a palm knife with one hand and slashed horizontally.
"Dong!"
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
With a muffled sound, the demonic claws on the Evil Old Man's right hand suddenly collapsed, and the bones in his right hand shattered into countless pieces.
"I gave you a chance and warned you. Since you're courting death, don't blame me." Yin Yuanzi had a sinister smile on his face as he slashed with his palm knife again!
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
The second palm knife landed on the Evil Old Man's shoulder, shattering the bones on that side of his body. He crashed into the rocks on the side of the cave, creating a huge hole that was several feet deep!
The hardness of the rocks in the divine territory was probably equivalent to the dark stars in the universe.
If Luo Zheng did not use his power source, it would take him some effort to smash the rocks apart with his current physical strength.
However, in the battle between two middle true gods, it was still as fragile as tofu!
"Crack."
An extremely soft cracking sound came from the sphere. Yin Yuanzi and the Evil Old Man were in the midst of their battle and did not hear it.
However, Luo Zheng, who was not far away, saw it clearly. A thin crack appeared on the sphere in the middle of the cave, as though something inside wanted to break the sphere!

Chapter 1747
The Extreme Evil Old Man was on the verge of death after taking that blow.
The energy contained in the palm blade continued to wreak havoc in his body, immobilizing half of his body.
Yin Yuanzi had a faint smile on his face as he slowly walked toward the Extreme Evil Old Man. "As a True God, sometimes you have to give up! Back then, I had the chance to obtain that item. Do you know why you were one step ahead of me? "
The Extreme Evil Old Man was embedded in the crevice, glaring at Yin Yuanzi without saying a word.
"Because I knew I'd offend Gu Bei. Isn't it courting death to steal from a Saint?" Yin Yuanzi said with a satisfied smile, "That's why I decisively gave it up. That's why it fell into your hands. You're just my scapegoat. Seven divine eras … That's indeed a bit long, but our lifespans are limitless. What does a mere time count for? Hahahaha … "
Yin Yuanzi laughed loudly. His laughter echoed in the cave, causing the sand and stones around the cave to fall and rustle. Luo Zheng felt a piercing pain in his ears when he heard that sound. He felt dizzy!
Seeing the Extreme Evil Old Man remain silent, Yin Yuanzi felt rather bored. He sighed softly, then his right hand turned into a palm blade and slashed toward the Extreme Evil Old Man again.
Although the Extreme Evil Old Man did not say a word, he was unwilling to wait for death in the face of Yin Yuanzi's attack. He raised the demonic claw on his left hand again and slammed it toward Yin Yuanzi.
However, the Extreme Evil Old Man was destined to be a dying struggle. Yin Yuanzi countered with a backhand and shattered the demonic claw on the Extreme Evil Old Man's left hand. There was the sound of bones cracking. The Extreme Evil Old Man's hands fell limply to the side, no longer able to resist.
The Extreme Evil Old Man felt a wave of sadness in his heart.
Back then, for the sake of that item, the Evil Old Man had used up all of his wealth, shaking the entire Vast Sky Territory. He had almost lost his life because of it. In the end, he had managed to get his hands on it, but in the end, he had been captured by Gu Bei. It could be said that the North Sage was the most lenient of all the Sages, sparing his life and imprisoning him in the immortal estate's dungeon.
This trial lasted for seven divine epochs. Vast Sky Domain had already changed, but he never thought that there would still be people who remembered the matters of the past, still coveting that item.
In his despair, the Evil Old Man saw Luo Zheng on the other side of the cave. At this moment, Luo Zheng had half of his body out of the cave. He wasn't looking at the Evil Old Man, but at the "Sealing Trap" he had set up.
It was also Luo Zheng's gaze that piqued the Evil Old Man's curiosity. He followed Luo Zheng's gaze and saw more and more cracks on the Feng Jueji!
The light within the sphere was clearly dimming, but its frequency was also increasing!
"That thing … has already hatched?"
Old Man Evil's heart pounded wildly!
That thing had been stored for several divine epochs, so how could it possibly hatch? And it was hatching at this moment?
No! That thing's intelligence was astonishing. After hatching, it would definitely know how powerful the "Sealing Trap" was, so it wouldn't dare to break open the sphere and struggle free. Because once it did, it would definitely be killed by the "Sealing Annihilation Sword" around the Feng Jueji!
And he had just unlocked the "Sealing Trap", and that thing was already hatching!
More and more cracks appeared on the sphere, and the crackling sounds grew louder and louder. Yin Yuanzi was just about to end the Evil Old Man's life when he suddenly heard a strange sound behind him. He had just turned around when the sphere suddenly shattered, and something emerged from it, pouncing toward Yin Yuanzi at an incredible speed.
Yin Yuanzi's reaction was not slow at all. He twisted his body to the side and swung the feather fan in his hand at that thing!
But that thing was abnormally agile. It coiled around the feather fan and climbed up Yin Yuanzi's arm!
At this moment, Luo Zheng, who was not far away, saw clearly that it was a strange-looking mouse. This mouse was only the size of a palm, and its head was extremely sharp. It also had a pair of slender fangs, and a small dot of light hung on its tail, constantly flashing.
After this strange rat pounced on Yin Yuanzi's arm, it suddenly burrowed into his arm!
A true god's physique was far stronger than a realm master's. Even though Yin Yuanzi wasn't a body refiner, his physical body was far from what an ordinary cultivator could compare to. Moreover, after Yin Yuanzi used the feather fan to summon the "temperamental ghost", his physical body had reached a new level.
However, as soon as the rat drilled in, it was as if it had drilled into soft mud. It drilled through Yin Yuanzi's skin without much resistance and kept crawling up his arm. A fist-sized bump could be seen moving rapidly on Yin Yuanzi's arm.
Yin Yuanzi's expression was also very anxious. His eyes flashed with ruthlessness, and his right hand pointed at the bulge on his left arm.
"Pu!"
As a sharp hidden force pierced through his fingertip, a bloody hole appeared on his arm. But that strange rat wasn't injured by this hidden force, and it continued to move along his arm.
As for Yin Yuanzi, he pointed a few more times, each finger leaving a bloody hole on his arm. But each time, he was a little slower. Finally, the rat moved to his shoulder and burrowed into his internal organs!
Yin Yuanzi's expression was extremely distorted, and deep fear appeared on his face.
As a true god, even if his internal organs were hollowed out, he wouldn't die. A true god generally had two life gates. One was the dantian, and the other was the soul.
And the most powerful thing about this rat was that it could suck the opponent's internal world, and at the same time, destroy the cultivator's physical body at an extremely fast speed!
If Yin Yuanzi's internal world was destroyed, he would be equivalent to a cripple.
Thinking of this, Yin Yuanzi gritted his teeth and endured the intense pain caused by the rat moving through his internal organs. He reached out and pointed at his chest several times!
"Pu pu pu pu …"
A series of bloody holes appeared on his chest under the ferocious finger force. Even Yin Yuanzi's heart was pierced, and blood spurted out uncontrollably.
But the rat was still abnormally agile in his body. It kept drilling through his body and quickly burrowed into his lower abdomen!
Immediately after, Yin Yuanzi felt a numbing sensation coming from his dantian. His entire body trembled, and he fell to the ground with a plop. His body couldn't help but tremble!
This scene happened in the span of two or three breaths. Luo Zheng and Old Man Extreme Evil couldn't help but gasp when they saw this scene.
What kind of rat was this? How could it be so powerful?
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw a bulge suddenly appear on Yin Yuanzi's lower abdomen. Accompanied by a splatter of blood, the rat drilled out of Yin Yuanzi's stomach. It shook its small head, and with a crisp "ding dong", a green object flew out of the rat's mouth and fell to the ground!
The object was like an octagonal gem. It flickered with a faint green light and emitted a unique "force". Only then did Luo Zheng realize that it was Yin Yuanzi's Godhead!
A Godhead was extremely sturdy. In theory, it was almost impossible to destroy it, and the rat couldn't swallow it. But it directly tore the Godhead out of his internal world.
After spitting out the Godhead, the rat opened its soybean-sized eyes. They flickered with a faint light as it sized up Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1748
How could the Old Man of Extreme Evil resist?
He could only watch as the rat pounced on him and plunged its head into his abdomen.
The rat was extremely intelligent. It seemed to know that the Old Man of Extreme Evil had no way of resisting and became even more unscrupulous. It buried half of its body into the Old Man of Extreme Evil's body and greedily sucked in the vital essence in the Old Man of Extreme Evil's inner world. A slender tail hung outside, emitting a bright yellow light.
The Old Man of Extreme Evil's reaction was almost identical to Yin Yuanzi's. His eyes widened and his body trembled uncontrollably.
Not long after, blood spurted out and a black Godhead was bitten out by the rat. It threw it on the ground with a "ding".
The Old Man of Extreme Evil used the Godhead to seal his inner world and prevented the rat from devouring the living beings in his inner world. Thus, the rat ripped the Godhead out of the Old Man of Extreme Evil's inner world!
After extracting the Godhead, the rat stuck its head into the Old Man of Extreme Evil's body again and began to devour …
Luo Zheng never expected the "treasure" the Old Man of Extreme Evil was talking about to be such a ferocious creature.
After the rat devoured the Old Man of Extreme Evil, Luo Zheng would be the one to suffer. Although Luo Zheng had the protection of the Concentric Garment and his body was extremely sturdy, he might not be able to withstand this strange rat!
He struggled out of the pile of sand and pebbles and glanced helplessly at the Old Man of Extreme Evil. The Old Man of Extreme Evil's eyes were tightly shut. He probably couldn't keep his body.
He wasn't related to the Old Man of Extreme Evil and couldn't be considered a friend. However, such a tragic death did make Luo Zheng feel somewhat regretful, even though the Old Man of Extreme Evil's death had nothing to do with Luo Zheng.
The Old Man of Extreme Evil was a True God in the Domain of the Gods. In addition, the Old Man of Extreme Evil had sworn an oath. If the Old Man of Extreme Evil could give Luo Zheng some help or even give him some pointers, Luo Zheng's path in the Domain of the Gods would be much smoother.
The rat's exposed tail was still swaying. While the rat continued to devour the Old Man of Extreme Evil's dantian, Luo Zheng carefully walked to the other side of the cave.
Without being able to fly, it would be quite difficult to leave the cave. However, Luo Zheng had already noticed that there was a small hole beside a protruding rock on the left side of the cave. Presumably, that was the way out of the cave.
However, just as Luo Zheng was halfway there, he suddenly felt an intense soul pressure coming from behind him. It was an extremely powerful soul that was fiercely pouncing on him, instantly submerging into Luo Zheng's mind!
"Who would have thought that this Stone Forest Rat would hatch ahead of time, devour my inner world, and cripple my cultivation! Brat, I was also forced to possess your body today! Your body is not bad. I'm going to take it! "The soul entered Luo Zheng's mind.
After Yin Yuanzi's dantian was devoured by the Stone Forest Rat, his physical body was destroyed as well. However, his soul wasn't injured. Yin Yuanzi had noticed Luo Zheng's extraordinary talent from the start. After being left with only a soul, he naturally thought of seizing Luo Zheng's body. Luo Zheng was merely a Sector Lord. At most, he could cultivate a gold Battle Soul. However, it was a piece of cake for Yin Yuanzi.
In Luo Zheng's mind, Yin Yuanzi's soul charged towards Luo Zheng. Naturally, Luo Zheng was shocked!
Although Luo Zheng's soul had improved significantly after devouring the Emotion Severing Pill, Yin Yuanzi was an intermediate True God. How strong was his soul? Yin Yuanzi's strength was far above Mu Haiji's three disciples. The level of his soul was even higher!
In a moment of desperation, small golden swords appeared on the surface of Luo Zheng's soul. Wisps of invisible sharp aura surged in his mind and shot towards Yin Yuanzi!
"Swish swish swish swish …"
Yin Yuanzi's soul level was indeed very strong. His soul had reached a level that Luo Zheng couldn't comprehend. He entered Luo Zheng's mind in a hurry to kill Luo Zheng's soul and occupy Luo Zheng's physical body. He didn't take Luo Zheng seriously.
However, the sharp aura released by Luo Zheng's soul was like tiny invisible swords. If it were an ordinary Golden Battle Soul, it would have been immediately penetrated by these tiny invisible swords. However, it only left a tiny cut on the surface of Yin Yuanzi's soul!
Even so, Yin Yuanzi was shocked. He didn't expect Luo Zheng's soul pressure to be so sharp. Furthermore, the pain from the damage to the soul was the most intense. Yin Yuanzi's face instantly distorted.
"You're already halfway there and you're still resisting. A mere Sector Lord actually managed to cultivate your soul to such a level. It seems like you must be from some wealthy family on a floating island. I'll devour your memories and replace your soul. I'll fulfill all your regrets in this life. Hahaha …"
The sharp small swords formed by the soul pressure couldn't deal any fatal damage to Yin Yuanzi. He actually withstood the soul pressure and advanced forward. He suddenly stretched out his hand and the two hands formed by his soul transformed into a pair of giant claws that grabbed at Luo Zheng's soul!
"Soul Assimilation Memory Retrieval Technique!"
