The moment the pair of giant claws grabbed at Luo Zheng's soul, they suddenly twisted and formed a circular cage that enveloped Luo Zheng's soul.
Usually, possession was just to kill the other party's soul and use one's own soul to occupy the other party's body.
However, not only did Yin Yuanzi want to occupy Luo Zheng's body, he also wanted to devour Luo Zheng's soul and read the memories within. This way, he would be able to completely replace Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng continued to release his soul pressure in an attempt to break out of the cage formed by the pair of hands, but no matter how hard he tried, he couldn't break out of the circular cage. A look of impatience appeared on Luo Zheng's face. If he was really replaced by this person, he really couldn't imagine what the consequences would be!
Not only was he unable to break out of the cage, but it was also becoming increasingly difficult for his soul to move. It was as though there was an invisible force that was restraining his soul while crazily reading his memories.
"You, you … you're actually Luo Xiao's son?"
After reading a portion of Luo Zheng's memories, Yin Yuanzi's soul was slightly stunned. Then, he revealed a look of ecstasy. If Luo Zheng was really the Saint's son, he would most likely have obtained Luo Xiao's inheritance. If this brat knew the secrets of the Saint, it would be a true treasure to him!
If he had known Luo Zheng's identity in advance, he would rather give up his body and possess Luo Zheng.
However, just as Yin Yuanzi was reading Luo Zheng's soul, another soul shot out from the Extreme Evil Old Man's head. After this soul left his body, it also shot into Luo Zheng's mind.
"Yin Yuanzi! You and I will fight to the death! "
The Extreme Evil Old Man had just returned, but he was already so miserably persecuted by Yin Yuanzi. His dantian and physical body were also destroyed by the Stone Forest Rats. Now that his soul had left his body, he wanted to seek revenge on Yin Yuanzi.
Naturally, the Extreme Evil Old Man's soul wasn't weak. After entering Luo Zheng's mind, it went straight for Yin Yuanzi's soul. Without any hesitation, the souls of the two middle-level True Gods fiercely collided.
It was also because of this collision that Yin Yuanzi was forced to deal with it. Meanwhile, Luo Zheng instantly felt relieved and seized this opportunity to break free from the cage formed by Yin Yuanzi's claws.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1749
After escaping with his life, Luo Zheng's mind was also endlessly agitated.
He didn't expect to almost lose his life in this small mountain village. If the Evil Old Man hadn't rushed into his mind at the crucial moment to find trouble with Yin Yuanzi, his soul would've been extracted and refined!
After leaving the universe and losing his father's support, he no longer had so much magical luck. The divine territory was filled with danger!
Old Man Extreme Evil and Yin Yuanzi's souls clashed once more. They were actually locked in battle in Luo Zheng's mind.
Even for a True God, it wasn't easy to cultivate one's soul.
During the time the Evil Old Man was imprisoned, Yin Yuanzi's cultivation had improved by quite a bit. The Evil Old Man was indeed no match for him, but his soul hadn't improved much.
The two Real Gods tangled with each other in Luo Zheng's mind, but they barely fought to a draw!
The Evil Old Man's eyes were bloodshot. Even if he died, he would take Yin Yuanzi down with him. However, Yin Yuanzi had waited for so many years. How could he be willing to die with the Evil Old Man?
"Stop, stop …" Yin Yuanzi roared. "Do you know who this kid is?"
The Evil Old Man didn't listen to him. His face was filled with madness. Countless spikes appeared on the surface of his soul, and he charged at Yin Yuanzi again!
"That person is the son of a Saint. His soul contains a huge secret. After I extract his memories, I can share them with you. In the future, I'll create a powerful body for you …" Yin Yuanzi said as he dodged.
"Hmph, I know what you're saying! But how can I believe a treacherous person like you? Today, I won't rest until I'm dead. I'll definitely destroy your soul! "
The Evil Old Man already knew Luo Zheng's identity. However, he had sworn an oath to his Inner Demon. How could he attack Luo Zheng? Therefore, Yin Yuanzi's words didn't tempt him at all. Moreover, even if the Evil Old Man compromised now, with Yin Yuanzi's character, once he extracted Luo Zheng's soul, he would definitely hurt the Evil Old Man. He wasn't a three-year-old child. How could he believe Yin Yuanzi's words?
"We can discuss …"
Before Yin Yuanzi could finish, the Evil Old Man's soul charged forward again. He opened his hands and grabbed Yin Yuanzi's soul. "Discuss, my ass! Die for me! Soul Shattering Flame! "
"Zi zi zi …"
Wisps of green flames suddenly burst out of Old Man Evil's soul. He was burning his own soul!
The green flames emitted by Soul Ignition seemed to be extremely lethal to the soul. Even Luo Zheng, who was far away, could feel the extraordinary power and kept retreating.
Yin Yuanzi let out a shrill shriek as Old Man Evil's soul burned. At the same time, a layer of yellow light spread out from Yin Yuanzi's body. Within the light, there was a faint layer of a tortoise shell. It was clearly a soul martial skill, but it strengthened the soul's defense.
Unfortunately, Old Man Evil's Soul-Breaking Flame was extremely powerful. The moment the tortoiseshell appeared, it quickly cracked!
Yin Yuanzi also understood that Old Man Evil had gone mad. He clenched his teeth and began to burn his soul ….
However, just as he moved, a sharp aura shot at him from behind!
Luo Zheng's soul pressure once again transformed into small arrows that shot toward Yin Yuanzi … Although these small arrows couldn't penetrate Yin Yuanzi's defenses, he still had to deal with Luo Zheng as well as the Evil Old Man. It could be said that he was being attacked from both sides. How could he deal with this?
"Pu pu pu pu …."
Under the burning of the Soul-Breaking Flame, Yin Yuanzi's soul became extremely weak. Luo Zheng's small arrows could easily tear Yin Yuanzi's soul apart.
"We … can discuss …."
Yin Yuanzi couldn't finish his sentence. His entire soul was shattered by Luo Zheng's small arrows. The Soul-Breaking Flame quickly burned the fragments and burned his soul to nothing.
"Whoosh!"
At the last moment, an extremely small wisp of soul reverberated in Luo Zheng's mind. It was a wisp of Yin Yuanzi's soul. Some soul techniques could make one's soul extremely tenacious. Even if there was only a wisp of soul left, it still retained all of its memories. As long as it could find a suitable body, even if it was just the body of a divine citizen, it could still make a comeback!
Old Man Evil was still in a state of fury after burning his soul and didn't notice this wisp of soul. Thus, it quietly escaped from Luo Zheng's mind.
This wisp of soul was still secretly rejoicing that it was able to escape, but Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes. A wisp of golden light flashed from his eyes, and a wisp of extremely sharp soul pressure shot out like a small arrow. It hit the wisp of soul precisely. This wisp of soul was extremely weak and was instantly destroyed by Luo Zheng ….
Luo Zheng blinked and then closed his eyes. Yin Yuanzi's wisp of soul contained his memories, and there were some secrets that Luo Zheng couldn't reveal. Thus, he had to eliminate the source of trouble.
Old Man Evil's soul was still in Luo Zheng's mind. However, after frantically burning his soul, he was now on the verge of death. The green flames had already been extinguished, and his soul was in a semi-transparent state. It was like a candle in the wind, about to collapse at any time.
He quietly floated in Luo Zheng's mind. This old man who had once committed all kinds of evil was now full of sorrow as he stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
"Sigh …."
Luo Zheng sighed softly. He slowly floated in front of Old Man Evil and sent out his soul power.
Although he knew that Old Man Evil wasn't a kind person, he had never harbored any evil intentions towards Luo Zheng. He had also promised to help him, but he didn't expect that there would be such a sudden change and that he would end up in such a state.
After Luo Zheng injected his soul power, Old Man Evil's soul was finally filled up a little.
Although a True God's soul was powerful, it wasn't easy to repair it if it was severely damaged. However, at least his soul was now far from collapsing.
"Why did you save me?"
Old Man Evil stared at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. According to Old Man Evil's logic, he was definitely not someone Luo Zheng could trust, and Luo Zheng wasn't that stupid.
Luo Zheng said, "I still need your help. You can't die now."
"My physical body is already damaged, my cultivation is gone, and my soul is like a candle. What's the difference between this and death? Hehe …. "Old Man Evil let out a self-deprecating laugh. Perhaps it was because his soul was weak, or perhaps it was because he was really sad, but there wasn't a trace of life on his face. He had lost the courage to live.
"Don't you still have a chance? You can still grab this chance. "Luo Zheng smiled.
"Grab this chance …."
Old Man Evil lowered his head and fell into deep thought. Previously, he was afraid of Mu Haiji and didn't dare to get involved in the battle between Saints. He only wanted to regain his glory in the Vast Sky Domain.
However, he was now just a remnant soul. What else did he have to worry about?
If Luo Zheng really achieved great things and even obtained a place in the Sage Hall, it wouldn't be impossible for him to help him recover his physical body and improve his cultivation!

Chapter 1750
In Old Man Evil's eyes, Luo Zheng's background and status were extraordinary.
Although the floating island that Luo Xiao had single-handedly constructed had already collapsed, Luo Xiao's disciples were still very active. This was especially true for Luo Xiao's chief disciple, Yu Taibai. Even now, he was still a well-known character in God's Domain!
However, Old Man Evil didn't understand why Luo Zheng had come to the Vast Sky Region like a headless fly the moment he stepped into the divine territory. Moreover, he was still loitering in this small mountain village in the north of the Vast Sky Region.
He didn't know that Luo Zheng had been sent to the Vast Sky Region because he was an illegal immigrant. The Vast Sky Region was chosen as Luo Zheng's first stop. After all, the Vast Sky Region was a small region and wouldn't attract much attention in the divine territory. Moreover, it was a relatively stable region and wouldn't be too dangerous.
"Although I, Old Man Evil, am a True God, my life's goal is to dominate a region. My methods back then were too bloody and ruthless, so I took on the name 'Evil'. Now that I've fallen into such a state, there's no hope. I don't care if I don't seize this opportunity. However, Luo Zheng, if you have any requests, I'll do everything I know."
When Old Man Evil said this to Luo Zheng, his sinister and vicious eyes were filled with sincerity. If others saw Old Man Evil's expression, they would be so shocked that their jaws would drop.
Luo Zheng smiled. Although Old Man Evil said this, it didn't mean that he didn't want to restore himself. Old Man Evil's words could be trusted, but there was no need to take them seriously. If there was a chance in the future, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't mistreat this person.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's expression suddenly changed. "Oh no!"
As it turned out, Old Man Evil and Yin Yuanzi had a fierce battle in Luo Zheng's mind. That Stone Forest Rat had already devoured Old Man Evil's dantian. The Stone Forest Rat leaped down from Old Man Evil's body with an unsatisfied expression on its face. Now, it was staring at Luo Zheng at the side of the cave, its bean-sized eyes flickering with faint light.
Even though Luo Zheng was only a Sector Lord, and in the eyes of the Stone Forest Rats, it was still not as delicious as a True God's dantian.
Thus, the Stone Forest Rat once again dashed toward Luo Zheng at an incredible speed.
"Run! This Stone Forest Rat is a unique True Spirit. Even if it has just hatched, it can still inherit most of its strength from its previous life! "
Evil Old Man said anxiously in Luo Zheng's mind.
Many true spirits in the Divine Realm all possessed all sorts of strange forms.
The so-called Unique True Spirit was the only one in the entire Divine Realm!
The Stone Forest Rat was a unique existence in the entire Divine Realm. There was no second Stone Forest Rat in the Divine Realm. It could not reproduce, but it had a miraculous talent of "reincarnation".
Once a Stone Forest Rat was killed, a strange rat-shaped rock would form in one of the mountains in the Divine Realm. After countless years of absorbing Heaven Earth Yuan Qi, this rat-shaped rock would slowly evolve into a Stone Forest Rat. This Stone Forest Rat was actually the "reincarnation" of the previous Stone Forest Rat. After successfully reincarnating, the Stone Forest Rat would possess most of its strength from its previous life. Thus, when a Stone Forest Rat hatched from a rock, its strength would be even stronger than an intermediate True God!
How could the current Luo Zheng be a match for this Stone Forest Rat?
Luo Zheng had seen what happened to Yin Yuanzi and Evil Old Man Evil. How could he dare to stop? He entered the small hole at the side!
"Run to the left!"
This small hole was originally carved out by Evil Old Man Evil many years ago. Evil Old Man Evil was naturally very familiar with the path in this hole. At this moment, he constantly reminded Luo Zheng.
However, even though Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow, rodents were best at speed. The Stone Forest Rat was much faster than Luo Zheng when it caught up.
"Squeak squeak squeak!"
The Stone Forest Rat had inherited most of its strength from its previous life. As it chased after Luo Zheng, it let out a sharp squeak. There was a hint of playfulness in its squeak!
As it climbed along the side of the small hole, its speed was extraordinarily fast. The distance between it and Luo Zheng was constantly closing.
In the blink of an eye, the Stone Forest Rat was only three to four feet away from Luo Zheng. Its small but powerful legs kicked off the ground as it pounced straight at Luo Zheng!
"Eight Winding Smoke!"
Luo Zheng frowned. Unable to fly in the air or perform greater teleportation, he could only use his movement skill!
Although the Stone Forest Rat was fast, it did not expect Luo Zheng's movement skill to be so ingenious. Although its pounce was fast, after Luo Zheng used his movement skill, his entire body was like a piece of weightless paper. The Stone Forest Rat pushed Luo Zheng forward. Its sharp fangs were always two inches away from Luo Zheng.
Seeing this opportunity, Luo Zheng increased his speed and dashed along the cave!
However, the Stone Forest Rat was indeed extraordinarily intelligent. After its first pounce was dodged by Luo Zheng, it seemed to have seen through the ingenuity of the Eight Winding Smoke. It took advantage of the opportunity to bounce off the wall of the cave and pounce again from another angle.
The angle from which it pounced forced Luo Zheng into a corner of the cave. Although Luo Zheng's Eight Winding Smoke was nimble, it still required space to move backward. After being forced into a corner, Luo Zheng could only move along the wall of the cave. This time, the Stone Forest Rat successfully got close to Luo Zheng. It extended its two slender fangs and charged at Luo Zheng's lower abdomen!
Bang!
Following a muffled sound, the Stone Forest Rat's fangs did not pierce Luo Zheng's dantian. This was because of Luo Zheng's Concentric Garment!
Although the True Gods had many methods to penetrate the Concentric Garment and injure Luo Zheng behind it, the Concentric Garment was ultimately made by the Craftsman Saint. The Concentric Garment, which was made from the Divine Spark's Origin, was still an unbreakable existence.
The Stone Forest Rat used its two fangs to charge forward, but it was as if it had hit an iron plate. It was unable to injure Luo Zheng at all.
"Your garment …"
Seeing that Luo Zheng was unable to avoid the Stone Forest Rat, the Evil Old Man thought to himself that he was done for. He did not expect Luo Zheng's garment to be so magical. Naturally, he was extremely surprised.
Squeak! Squeak! Squeak!
The Stone Forest Rat let out an angry squeak. It also understood that Luo Zheng's ordinary garment was indestructible. It pounced again and actually burrowed into Luo Zheng's clothes!
Luo Zheng's Concentric Garment could move as he wished. At a critical moment, it could instantly cover his entire body. However, the Stone Forest Rat's reaction and speed far exceeded Luo Zheng's. Before he could cover his entire body with the Concentric Garment, he felt a sharp pain in his shoulder. The Stone Forest Rat had actually burrowed into his body through his shoulder.
Luo Zheng's body was tougher than the Evil Old Man and Yin Yuanzi's, but the Stone Forest Rat had some kind of magical ability. No matter how tough something was, it was unable to obstruct it. It was actually able to move freely through Luo Zheng's body!
Not long after, the Stone Forest Rat burrowed into Luo Zheng's lower abdomen. Its fangs pierced into Luo Zheng's dantian.
However, the instant the Stone Forest Rat's fangs entered, the black lines around Luo Zheng's dantian suddenly glowed and quickly wrapped around the Stone Forest Rat's fangs. These black lines actually prevented the Stone Forest Rat from devouring Luo Zheng's inner world.
It turned out that the purpose of these black lines was to cut off Luo Zheng's connection with his inner world. Whether it was from the outside in or from the inside out, they would all be blocked by the black lines!
The Stone Forest Rat wanted to burrow into Luo Zheng's dantian, so it naturally triggered these black lines!

Chapter 1751
The Stone Forest Rat suddenly jumped, wanting to use its fangs to break the black threads!
These black threads were very difficult to deal with. Luo Zheng used his golden blood to dissolve them, and now there was only a small portion of the threads left tied to his dantian. He did not expect that the Stone Forest Rat's fangs would be able to break these black threads one by one!
After the last black thread broke, the Stone Forest Rat did not hesitate. It stabbed its fangs into Luo Zheng's dantian and hurriedly sucked in the world within his body.
Now, Luo Zheng felt his body go numb. He could not help but tremble, unable to resist at all. He was extremely anxious.
When Old Man Extreme Evil saw this, he was also helpless. This Stone Forest Rat's ability was too powerful. If he could completely control this little guy before it hatched, it would definitely be a great help!
Unfortunately, the Stone Forest Rat woke up on its own. After inheriting its past memories, it became a newly born demon. Even a True God would have to avoid it.
However, the Stone Forest Rat had just sucked in a few mouthfuls when it suddenly pulled out its fangs. It screeched in pain and actually crawled out of Luo Zheng's shoulder. It fell to the ground and waved its limbs crazily as it crawled away …
When Old Man Extreme Evil saw this scene in Luo Zheng's mind, his eyes widened in disbelief.
"What, what's wrong with this Stone Forest Rat!?"
After the Stone Forest Rat pulled its fangs out of Luo Zheng's dantian, Luo Zheng's body returned to normal. Although the Stone Forest Rat had drilled through his body and caused him many internal injuries, Luo Zheng could still endure the pain. He looked at the Stone Forest Rat with a face full of amazement and understood in a moment.
The Stone Forest Rat's ability to devour the world within its body was actually a method of absorbing true essence. However, the Stone Forest Rat was absorbing the entire world, including the secondary life forms. This was a very unique innate ability.
However, the world within Luo Zheng's dantian was formed directly from the aura of chaos, and the aura of chaos was highly toxic to life forms!
In the divine realm, only the Sages could resist the poison of the aura of chaos and roam the world outside the divine realm.
As for the true essence absorbed by the other True Gods, they relied on the four sacred trees to convert the aura of chaos into Heaven Earth Yuan Qi before they could absorb it normally.
The roots of the four sacred trees extended outside the divine realm. As they absorbed the aura of chaos, the roots that were tens of millions of miles long were also corroded by the aura of chaos. However, the four sacred trees had unique with their unique with their tenacious vitality. When the aura of chaos corroded a portion of their roots, they would quickly grow with it.
The Stone Forest Rat sucked the Chaotic Qi into its body. How could it resist the toxicity of the Chaotic Qi?
The aura of chaos had always been Luo Zheng's most secretive weapon.
However, the implications of the Primal Chaos Secret Technique were too great. Luo Zheng had never used it directly. He only extracted the aura of chaos and used it as true essence.
Some experts in the universe had already guessed that Luo Zheng possessed the aura of chaos, including the Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's broken sword. However, they could not figure out where Luo Zheng's aura of chaos came from.
Now that he was in the divine realm, Luo Zheng could only be more careful. Unless it was absolutely necessary, he would not directly release the aura of chaos!
Although the Evil Old Man was extremely confused, he also guessed that Luo Zheng's dantian might contain poison. However, what kind of poison could kill a unique true spirit like the Stone Forest Rat? Moreover, how could such a poison be hidden in the dantian?
Although Luo Zheng did not explain it to him, no matter what, he had managed to keep his life …
The danger here had been eliminated. Luo Zheng immediately sat down cross-legged and activated his dantian to heal himself.
Just now, the Stone Forest Rat had burrowed into Luo Zheng's shoulder, leaving a bloody wound in his body. Even his internal organs had been damaged.
Fortunately, the Stone Forest Rat had helped Luo Zheng cut off those troublesome black threads, opening up the barrier between Luo Zheng and his dantian. It could be considered that the seal had been broken in advance.
However, just as Luo Zheng was about to extract the aura of chaos to heal himself, his expression suddenly changed. He quickly transformed into an Inner Body Clone and escaped into his inner world.
During this time, Luo Zheng had completely lost contact with his inner world. He was unable to monitor the changes in his inner world.
In this short period of time, the vast inner world had gone through a long period of time. The living beings within had undergone earth-shattering changes!
Under Luo Zheng's protection, humanity had experienced rapid development. Several great Divine Kingdoms had been overthrown and a peerless powerhouse had been born – the Evil Deities.
This Evil Deities had a Divine Transformation Realm cultivation. Within Luo Zheng's inner world, the Evil Deities were the strongest powerhouses there were.
Under the rule of the Evil Deities, the several continents that had been split apart were all recovered by him, forming the largest Holy Land – the Evil Cloud Holy Land!
As for the Evil Deities, he considered himself a 'God'. He announced to the world that it was he, the Evil Deities, who had created the entire world.
At the start, the Evil Deities' words were rejected by the people of the world. However, the Evil Deities were ruthless and ruthless. Anyone who dared to question him was slaughtered. Because of this, the Evil Deities had slaughtered over three million humans.
Luo Zheng's inner world was still small. All the humans within it added up to only a little over ten million. The Evil Deities had actually killed nearly a third of the population.
After learning of this, Luo Zheng was naturally furious!
At this moment, his incarnation was standing high in the sky, overlooking the continents below. His gaze finally locked onto a gray continent. As the master of his inner world, Luo Zheng possessed omniscient and omnipotent abilities. With just a thought, he knew that the Evil Deities were on this continent.
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng's figure immediately floated towards that continent!
What Luo Zheng didn't expect was that just as he was halfway there, the nine stars in the sky suddenly began to revolve. Mysterious runes condensed on the surface of each of the nine stars, and a colorful source of strength shot out from those runes.
These sources of strength gathered together, turning into a colorful arrow that shot towards Luo Zheng!
"Eh!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's face was filled with disbelief!
As the master of his inner world, he had never used these sources of strength before. However, these sources of strength actually took the initiative to attack him ….
Although these runes weren't strong, they had truly extracted the source of strength. Just this arrow alone was enough to kill a normal sector lord.
However, wanting to kill Luo Zheng was a fantasy. After all, Luo Zheng himself was the true master of the nine stars. When the colorful arrow was an inch away from Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng spat out the word "Freeze". The arrow strangely stopped in front of his chest, unable to move an inch further!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1752
Then, Luo Zheng gently waved his hand and the rainbow-colored arrow turned into beams of light that returned to the nine stars!
Luo Zheng looked down once more, a cold sneer on his face.
He never imagined that such a genius would be born in his inner world. The Evil Deities knew of his existence and had created nine divine runes on their own. Then, they placed these nine divine runes into the nine stars and waited for him to enter his inner world before borrowing the power of the nine stars to kill him!
Not to mention that the Evil Deities had no chance of succeeding, just being able to do this was enough to make Luo Zheng praise them.
"Let me see just what sort of amazing and extraordinary character you are." Luo Zheng coldly sneered. Then, he continued to sneak towards the continent …
Because of Luo Zheng's support, the human forces in his inner world were the strongest and most prosperous.
In the center of this continent, there was a Law Tablet that Luo Zheng had thrown down. Now, this Law Tablet had been moved to the highest palace and was enshrined as a divine object. It was impossible for an ordinary martial artist to meditate on this Law Tablet!
Luo Zheng couldn't stop this behavior. Although these life forms were born in Luo Zheng's inner world, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to control their thoughts.
To hide their treasures for themselves, this was the natural instinct of all life forms!
Atop a high mountain, there was a massive sacrificial altar. Hundreds of thousands of martial artists were gathered around the altar, constantly bowing to a statue on the altar. This was the statue of the Evil Deities themselves.
"They're collecting the power of faith …"
After the Evil Deities reached the peak of their cultivation in Luo Zheng's inner world, their cultivation was unable to progress any further.
This was because Luo Zheng's cultivation was only at the World Lord realm. Thus, the Evil Deities could only cultivate to the Divine Transformation realm at most. In order to become stronger, the Evil Deities had actually thought of using the power of faith!
Currently, Luo Zheng didn't need the power of faith. Before he condensed his Godhead, the power of faith in his inner world wasn't of much use to him. Only after he condensed his Godhead did the power of faith turn into 'Force', and his strength rose to a higher level!
However, now that the Evil Deities were trying to collect the Faith Power of the entire world, it was not a good thing for Luo Zheng. After all, the Faith Power was limited. If the Evil Deities continued to build their own totems, it would have a far-reaching impact on the world. When that time came, it would be troublesome for Luo Zheng to eliminate this influence.
A palace was built on one side of the towering mountain.
Under the support of three stone pillars that were two thousand feet long, the entire palace was suspended in the air. On the outermost circular terrace of the palace, one could see the entire coastline of the Eastern Continent.
On this terrace stood a man and a woman. The man's face was as sharp as a knife and his expression was cold and stern. The woman was delicate and beautiful, slender and elegant.
This man was the strongest powerhouse in the world and was given the honorific title of 'Evil Deities' by the people of the world. As for the woman, she was the princess of the Imperial Celestial Dynasty and also the wife of Evil Deities.
"He's here. The power of the Nine Stars really can't do anything to him …" The Evil Deities' eyes were calm and steady. He knew that he was about to face an invincible enemy, the God of Creation of this world!
The woman leaned on the broad shoulders of the Evil Deities, a worried expression on her face. "From the traces left behind in history, this person has a heart of universal love. Why must you make an enemy out of him?"
Hearing this, the Evil Deities revealed a proud and aloof expression. "I, the Evil Deities, have lived my life as a human, so how can I live under someone else's roof? I've always been the one controlling others. No one can control my fate! "
In order to fight against Luo Zheng, the Evil Deities had made many preparations. He had collected some traces left behind by Luo Zheng in history and found some key evidence from legends. He had even estimated Luo Zheng's strength through speculation!
"But he's much stronger than you," the woman said softly.
The Evil Deities sneered. "He is indeed much stronger, but not by much. Although I'm the only one in this world who has cultivated to the Divine Transformation Realm, according to my speculations, there should only be two realms above the Divine Transformation Realm. After crossing these two realms, one's cultivation will reach the end!"
In this world, there were no martial artists at the Extreme Divine Realm or the World Lord realm. But, the Evil Deities had relied on his own abilities to infer that Luo Zheng was at the World Lord realm. It was just that he didn't know the exact definition and strength of the World Lord realm.
Moreover, he didn't know that above the World Lord realm there were Empyreans, True Divinities, and even Saints … the Evil Deities was limited to Luo Zheng's inner world. To be able to infer two realms was already incredible.
"With my current strength, I can even defeat an opponent one realm higher than me. He is two realms higher than me. Combined with my previous arrangements, I still have a chance!" The Evil Deities coldly said, his eyes filled with determination.
What he didn't know was that once Luo Zheng truly died, his inner world would also collapse. Everything in this world would disintegrate, finally dissipating into the chaos.
Woosh …
Luo Zheng drew a thin line in the air, climbed over the mountain, circled around the palace, and slowly approached the outermost balcony of the palace, looking down at the man on the balcony.
"You … are the Evil Deities?" Luo Zheng lightly asked.
The Evil Deities grinned, then grabbed the woman who was hugging him and leaped to a higher level. Looking down at Luo Zheng, he smiled and said, "Yes, I am!"
"Remove the totems you use to gather the power of faith and disband your Evil Cloud Holy Land. Then, I will spare your life. Otherwise, die," Luo Zheng said.
The woman blinked and looked up at the God of Creation of this world from several hundred feet away. She pursed her lips and didn't say a word. According to many historical records, this God of Creation was very kind and amiable. He had also prevented martial artists from killing each other many times. He had also condensed the Laws he comprehended into Law Tablets and erected them all over the world for martial artists to study and cultivate.
Subconsciously, she didn't want to be enemies with the God of Creation. After all, he was the creator of this world, and every living creature in this world was created by him!
However, her husband had great ambitions and was unwilling to be trapped in this small world. He knew that there was a wider space in the outside world, and even predicted that there was an existence more powerful than the God of Creation in the outside world.
Therefore, she could only come up with a crazy plan with her husband, the God Slaying Plan.
Borrowing the power of the Nine Stars to kill Luo Zheng was only the first step. Of course, it failed, but it was also expected. However, the moment she saw Luo Zheng, she had a faint premonition that her husband was destined to lose everything …
"Spare my life?" The Evil Deities' smile was blazing. "Since I, the Evil Deities, was born, no one has ever spared my life. You are not qualified!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
He was very protective of the creatures in his Inner World. If this Evil Deities could agree to his request, he would indeed spare him. Many times, Luo Zheng was unwilling to break the balance of his Inner World and let the creatures develop on their own.
Moreover, this Evil Deities indeed had extraordinary creativity and talent. He was also qualified to be arrogant. Unfortunately, he didn't understand how ignorant his arrogance was …

Chapter 1753
"Hmph!"
Evil Deities snorted coldly, and a sharp light flashed in his eyes. Specks of starlight shot out from the palace.
There were a total of nine specks of starlight, which corresponded to the nine stars in the sky!
"Borrowing the power of the stars?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
After some Saints constructed the universe, they would usually hide the nine stars, and the living beings in the universe would not be able to borrow the power source. It was almost impossible to see a situation like Jiu Sheng, who casually allowed the Mountain Crushing Race to use his own power source.
Luo Zheng constructed the Nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and had already placed the nine stars into his body in advance, so he did not interfere too much. Evil Deities relied on his own talent to observe the heavens every night and also developed a set of star battle techniques!
"Hong long long …"
The nine stars in the sky began to revolve, and in the process, Luo Zheng felt that almost one percent of his power source disappeared!
The nine specks of starlight behind Evil Deities grew rapidly, and those specks of starlight swept towards Evil Deities like a waterfall.
The woman beside Evil Deities floated lightly in the air, closed her eyes, and put her delicate palms together. When her palms separated, a ray of light was pulled out by her hands, and that ray of light quickly turned into a purple longsword.
From this longsword, a rich power of faith was emitted. This sword was actually a weapon of faith.
As she lightly tossed the longsword, the nine specks of white light quickly entered the sword. Evil Deities caught the sword, suddenly turned in the air, and slashed towards Luo Zheng!
Faced with Evil Deities' slash, Luo Zheng smiled slightly and praised, "What a profound sword technique, was this also created by you?"
After he finished speaking, he stretched out a finger and lightly flicked the sword at an extremely fast speed!
"Ding!"
A melodious sound rang out, and the longsword was flicked to the side by Luo Zheng.
Evil Deities' expression darkened, and he took the opportunity to pull the sword back, sending out a Sword Ray. He turned around and slashed at Luo Zheng again. His sword was unrivaled in the world, but Luo Zheng still stretched out a finger and lightly flicked it.
"Ding!"
With this flick, the sword was once again deflected away.
Evil Deities' attacks were getting faster and faster. Luo Zheng's finger attacks were also getting faster and faster. As a result, a series of rhythmic crisp sounds could be heard in the air!
However, Evil Deities had gone all out, while Luo Zheng had only used one finger. The difference in strength between the two was obvious!
"Do you think I'm qualified now?" Luo Zheng asked, pinching his sword with two fingers.
At this moment, Evil Deities suddenly revealed a mysterious smile. "Not enough!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the woman who was watching the battle not far away suddenly closed her eyes. Halos of light spread out from the top of her head. These halos of light were like water ripples.
At the same time that a halo appeared above her head, a halo also appeared above Luo Zheng's head, enveloping Luo Zheng!
"Hmm?"
Luo Zheng was stunned and suddenly felt that he couldn't move!
Those halos actually cut off the connection between Luo Zheng's soul and his incarnation!
"Die …"
As soon as Evil Deities finished speaking, he suddenly pushed his sword forward. The nine halos of light on the sword suddenly erupted with a powerful force, breaking away from Luo Zheng's hand and slashing toward Luo Zheng's incarnation.
"Puchi!"
The long sword pierced Luo Zheng's body, slicing him in two, and fell to the ground.
Luo Zheng's incarnation was formed by chaotic energy and a thought. After this incarnation was cut in two, no blood flowed out. As it fell, it turned into strands of chaotic energy and began to dissipate. Soon, it disappeared from Evil Deities' sight!
"Did he succeed …"
The woman looked at the disappearing body of the God of Creation with disbelief in her beautiful eyes. She didn't expect that killing a God of Creation would be so easy!
Evil Deities's face showed a trace of arrogance. "I said that even a God of Creation has a weakness! This person's character is destined to end up like this! "
Before Luo Zheng arrived, Evil Deities and she had analyzed Luo Zheng's character and judged how Luo Zheng would react. It could be said that they had taken advantage of Luo Zheng's kindness.
In the inner worlds of many martial artists, there would be traitors like the Evil God that would appear. Their method of dealing with these traitors was to directly obliterate them!
After losing his incarnation, Luo Zheng opened his eyes with a dumbfounded expression on his face. He had actually been schemed against by the creatures in his Inner World. Fortunately, he was the only one who knew about this. If word of this spread, he was afraid that others would laugh their heads off.
Just now, that woman had used some kind of secret technique to cut off the connection between his soul and his incarnation. Luo Zheng hadn't expected this move.
However, to Luo Zheng, an incarnation was just a mass of chaotic energy. Creating an incarnation was just a matter of thought for Luo Zheng.
Evil Deities couldn't be ignored, so he closed his eyes again and condensed an incarnation in his Inner World. Then, he disappeared and reappeared in front of Evil Deities.
After killing the God of Creation, Evil Deities had a smug smile on his face. But at this moment, his smile suddenly froze on his face.
"You can continue," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Evil Deities stared at Luo Zheng, speechless for a long time. Although he was extremely smart, he had no concept of the real world. He also couldn't imagine that the God of Creation was just an incarnation that could be created at any time!
As the light in his eyes flickered, he suddenly burst out with a roar. He waved the long sword in his hand again. The nine stars on the sword shone, and the sword rays attacked Luo Zheng like a storm!
This time, Luo Zheng turned his head and smiled at the woman. Just now, Luo Zheng's incarnation was defeated by this woman's divine ability. After Luo Zheng stared at her, she couldn't move at all.
At the same time, he pointed his index finger at the long sword. An irresistible force blocked the long sword. With a light tap of his index finger, the long sword broke into pieces!
Evil Deities was just stunned when Luo Zheng disappeared from his sight. Just like how Luo Zheng had suddenly appeared, Luo Zheng disappeared without any fluctuation of laws. Evil Deities couldn't capture Luo Zheng's figure at all!
"Although you are smart and realize that you exist in an illusory world, your mind is still shackled by an insurmountable barrier … …"
Luo Zheng's voice drifted in from all directions.
Evil Deities raised his head and looked around, but he still couldn't sense any fluctuations of energy. Luo Zheng's methods were completely beyond his comprehension!
Boom!
In the instant that Luo Zheng appeared, his knee slammed into Evil Deities' face.
Boom … …
Under the impact, the Evil God was smashed into the palace below.
In this world, the Evil God's palace was called the Temple of Marvels by the common people. Under this impact, the three 20,000 foot high stone pillars beneath the palace also collapsed!
Smoke and dust billowed, and rubble fell … …
Evil Deities was embedded in the mountain. He looked up at Luo Zheng in the sky, the flames in his eyes still burning fiercely. Even if he was at the end of the road, he wouldn't admit defeat.
As for the woman imprisoned by Luo Zheng, her face was also deathly pale. Just as she had predicted, her husband had suffered a crushing defeat. There was no room for resistance. This wasn't a contest of strength on the same level.
"I … … can let you see the real world, but now is not the time," Luo Zheng continued.
Luo Zheng had already tried to place the creatures in his inner world into the universe. The creatures wouldn't disintegrate. According to Luo Zheng's deduction, his inner world was on the same level as the Realm of the Gods. Logically speaking, the creatures in his inner world shouldn't encounter any obstacles when entering the Realm of the Gods!

Chapter 1754
"I don't need to lie to you. In fact, I only need a thought to kill you," Luo Zheng continued to say to Evil Deities.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Evil Deities' heart suddenly trembled.
His wife had long deduced that the God of Creation seemed to have the ability to kill any living being. He was not someone who could be defeated by strength alone …
However, Evil Deities was extremely stubborn. He was bent on killing the God of Creation, which was why he had come to this step.
Now it seemed that all his arrangements and strategies were simply childish and ridiculous in the eyes of the God of Creation. Luo Zheng could have killed him directly, but he still gave him a chance!
"You … why did you give me such a chance?" Evil Deities asked cautiously. Luo Zheng's way of dealing with things was completely different from Evil Deities' philosophy. Evil Deities believed that once he failed, he would die without a burial place. He could not understand why Luo Zheng would not only spare him, but also help him!
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Do as I say. You are not qualified to collect the power of faith. I gave you a chance, but it has nothing to do with you."
As soon as he finished speaking, Luo Zheng's incarnation quietly dissipated …
His wife also regained her freedom of movement. She hurriedly flew over and called out, "Husband, are you okay?"
Evil Deities was still embedded in the mountain, lost in thought.
"Husband, why did he give you a chance, but tell you that it has nothing to do with you?" The woman also did not understand Luo Zheng's words.
Evil Deities was stunned. He muttered Luo Zheng's words repeatedly …
"It has nothing to do with me … It has nothing to do with me …"
Soon after, Evil Deities narrowed his eyes and revealed a bitter smile. He said to his wife, "If I spare an ant, is it because of the ant itself?"
The woman shook her head. "Of course not."
"In his eyes, I'm just an ant," Evil Deities said helplessly.
The woman blinked her eyes and suddenly understood. She comforted, "He … is a life form that we cannot understand. It's normal for him to see us as ants."
Unexpectedly, Evil Deities also smiled coldly. The two flames in his eyes burned crazily. "I want to prove to him that even if I am an ant, I will be an extremely special ant!"
In fact, in Luo Zheng's eyes, Evil Deities was an extremely special ant.
As the population base in his inner world increased, there would always be some strange creatures born. With the ability of "omniscience", Luo Zheng knew that Evil Deities had completely comprehended the tablet of laws he had transformed into. Not only that, he had also entered the icy continent and comprehended a part of the "Ice Soul Devil Flame" ability. In addition, his attainments in the dao of runes had reached the peak!
Luo Zheng did not transfer the dao of runes into his inner world. However, Evil Deities used his own comprehension to drive the power of the Nine Stars through primitive runes. This kind of talent and comprehension ability would be quite amazing even in the divine realm, let alone in the universe.
Encountering such a person, Luo Zheng naturally cherished his talent. Therefore, he only had him dispel the belief totem and eliminate the belief totem of "Evil Deities". This was to prevent it from affecting Luo Zheng's ability to use the power of faith in the future.
Once Evil Deities set a goal, he would do his best to achieve it. Moreover, he was quite good at guessing people's minds.
He understood that Luo Zheng did not like him collecting the power of faith, so he immediately disbanded his own esoteric religion. At the same time, he also disbanded the sacred land of Evil Deities. However, relying on the core subordinates in the sacred land, he began to rearrange the belief totem on several continents in the world …
However, this belief totem did not belong to Evil Deities. It was Luo Zheng's belief totem. At the same time, it spread all kinds of legends about the God of Creation.
This process went very smoothly. After all, legends about the "God of Creation" and "World Entrusting Venerable" had been circulating since ancient times in this world. In fact, when Evil Deities first started collecting the power of faith, they encountered considerable resistance. After all, all kinds of legends about Luo Zheng had been circulating in this world, and people had always worshipped Luo Zheng as the God of Creation.
Previously, in order to collect the power of faith, Evil Deities needed to erase the legends about Luo Zheng. At one point, they had slaughtered almost a third of the human population … At the current stage, Luo Zheng did not need the power of faith for the time being. However, in the future, when the God of Creation condensed, it would be very important to convert the power of faith into "influence" …
By the time Luo Zheng came out of his inner world, the wound caused by the Stone Forest Rat had almost healed.
He had just gotten up when he heard the Evil Old Man sigh in his mind. He said regretfully, "Although I don't know how terrifying your poison skills are, it's really a pity that you killed this Stone Forest Rat with poison."
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes and retorted, "What's there to pity? If I hadn't killed this rat with poison, I would be dead by now. Besides, your physical body was also destroyed by this Stone Forest Rat. Shouldn't you feel deep hatred for this little thing?"
However, the Evil Old Man continued, "You don't know how precious this Stone Forest Rat is. Back then, a True God was canonized because of this Stone Forest Rat! Seven Divine Epochs ago, in order to obtain this Stone Forest Rat, I gambled everything I had. I thought I had won the gamble, but I didn't expect the Stone Forest Rat to hatch prematurely. I missed the opportunity to sign a contract with it … "
The memories of a true spirit would not be severed.
This Stone Forest Rat had assisted an Ancient Sage in becoming a True God. Later on, that Ancient Sage died, so naturally, the Stone Forest Rat died as well!
However, the Stone Forest Rat would not truly die. After it was "reincarnated", it turned into a strange rat-shaped stone again. After its resurrection, the Stone Forest Rat signed a contract with another Realm of the Gods warrior. Although that Realm of the Gods warrior did not become a True God, he successfully became a True God and cultivated to the Great Perfection Realm …
It could be said that once one signed a soul contract with the Stone Forest Rat, it was equivalent to obtaining the key to a huge treasure vault!
"It's a pity." Luo Zheng shrugged and prepared to leave. The Evil Old Man said, "I've been telling you for half a day … this Stone Forest Rat is so precious. Are you planning to leave it here?"
"Isn't this rat dead? What's the use of it …" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Just as the Evil Old Man said, the Stone Forest Rat's magic did not lie in its magical abilities, but in the memories passed down by the Stone Forest Rat as a true spirit.
What was the use of a dead Stone Forest Rat?
"You know too little about the Realm of the Gods," the Evil Old Man said helplessly. "Even if the Stone Forest Rat was only a carcass left, it would still attract countless True Gods to fight over it. Even the wealthy families of the floating islands would not give up on it!"
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Because through the Stone Forest Rat's carcass, you can find the place where it was' reincarnated '. You can be the first to dig out the rat-shaped stone. Even if you can only use it to exchange, the value of this rat carcass is beyond your imagination!" the Evil Old Man explained patiently.
Luo Zheng suddenly understood. He also knew that the Stone Forest Rat's carcass was valuable. Even if he did not need the rat's memories in the future, he could still exchange the Stone Forest Rat's carcass!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng stepped forward and put away the rat carcass.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1755
Now that Old Man Evil had fallen to such a state, he no longer cared about the Stone Forest Rats. Instead, he was focused on helping Luo Zheng.
After he asked Luo Zheng to put away the rat carcass, he guided Luo Zheng through the narrow cave. When Luo Zheng walked out of the cave, he was already in a mountain stream a hundred miles away from Meng Village.
This path was originally connected to the cave underneath Meng Village. Many years ago, Old Man Evil had dug this path using the 'Art of Getting Lost'. The path extended and changed, forming an unsolvable maze underground.
In the past, some villagers of Meng Village had mistakenly entered the cave, but they were unable to solve the maze and were trapped to death. On his way out, Luo Zheng saw a few skeletons. Most of them belonged to the villagers of Meng Village who had lost their way …
After crossing the mountain stream, Luo Zheng returned to Meng Village. The Sun Moon Fort Chief and the group of warriors were still standing on the same spot, waiting for him.
When they saw the collapse of Meng Village, they knew something was wrong. The Sun Moon Fort Chief led his men up the mountain. Looking at the bottomless pit below Meng Village, no one dared to go down to investigate.
Without seeing Old Man Evil, none of them dared to leave. They could only wait here.
Half a day later, they saw Luo Zheng coming out alone.
"Young … Young Master Luo, where did you come from? Have you seen my Patriarch? "The Sun Moon Fort Chief stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
With Old Man Evil's return, if he could rebuild the seventy-two forts, the Sun Moon Fort Chief would be put in an important position. His day of attaining the Dao of Divinity was just around the corner. Naturally, he was concerned about Old Man Evil's condition.
"You mean Old Man Evil?" Luo Zheng scratched his head and said, "I almost fell to my death. In a daze, I walked out of a cave. As for where he is now, I don't know …"
"You don't know?" The Sun Moon Fort Chief and his men looked at each other. Seeing Luo Zheng's confused face, they didn't worry about him and didn't ask any more questions. Instead, they discussed how to get a rope to go down and look for him.
The Evil Old Man's sigh sounded in Luo Zheng's mind.
He had indeed returned to the Vast Sky Territory to bring glory to the 72 Strongholds. After all, he could not bear to see the Sun Moon Stronghold in such a state. However, the Evil Old Man did not have the ability to do anything now. He could only let Luo Zheng deal with him and let them fend for themselves.
The villagers of Meng Village had also returned one after another. When they saw what had happened to their village, all of them were filled with grief.
Luckily, these villagers had evacuated from Meng Village in advance. If they hadn't, the collapse of Meng Village would have killed more than half of the villagers.
Since the Sun and Moon Village was no longer looking for trouble with them, these God's people had no choice but to build new houses on the other side of the hill. The vegetable fields they used to live in were still there, and Meng Village could be rebuilt.
Now that Luo Zheng's dantian had been unsealed, there was no need for him to stay here.
After Luo Zheng came to Meng Village, Xiaowen's understanding of the Martial Dao had improved by leaps and bounds. However, because it had only been a short time, Xiaowen had not made any breakthroughs in his cultivation. However, the Martial Dao that Luo Zheng had imparted to him had obviously surpassed that of his elder brother, Xiaowen. Of course, Xiaowen was reluctant to let Luo Zheng leave. However, he also understood that with his current strength, it was impossible for him to follow Luo Zheng. He could only secretly encourage himself in his heart. If he could become a Divine Martial Artist one day, he would leave this mountain village immediately and look for his Master, Luo Tianxing!
Half a month later …
In a nameless mountain to the north of the Vast Sky Territory.
Luo Zheng stood in front of a protruding mountain wall halfway up the mountain. He could see the snowy plains in the distance.
The Vast Sky Territory was only a small territory in the Divine Realm. However, it was still so huge that it was beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. The most terrifying thing was that he could not fly in the air, let alone use Greater Teleportation.
With Luo Zheng's walking speed, it would probably take him more than a thousand years to traverse the Vast Sky Territory on foot …
"How far is it?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Soon. It's on top of this mountain …" Old Man Extreme Evil said.
Back then, Old Man Extreme Evil had hidden some things in the mountains to the north of the Vast Sky Territory. What Luo Zheng was looking for now was the belongings that Old Man Extreme Evil had left behind seven divine eras ago.
"Caw caw caw …"
Luo Zheng stopped in front of the mountain wall for a while. Dozens of strange-looking fire birds flew toward Luo Zheng.
He suddenly raised his head, and his eyes flashed with a sharp light!
"Caw —"
Those fire birds let out a series of miserable shrieks and fell into a ten-thousand-foot-tall cliff.
Using soul suppression to kill these ferocious beasts in the Divine Realm was the fastest and most convenient method.
"Tsk, tsk, tsk. You're just a Realm Lord, but your soul is already so powerful. When you condense a Godhead in the future, how powerful will your soul be if it advances further?"
Old Man Extreme Evil said in his mind.
Luo Zheng didn't reply. Old Man Extreme Evil knew some of his secrets. He even knew that he was the son of Luo Xiao and had a grudge against Mu Hai. However, it didn't mean that he would reveal everything to Old Man Extreme Evil, at least not now.
He suddenly stomped on the ground and jumped twenty to twenty feet high. Like an agile monkey, he hung on a rock at the top of the mountain wall. Then, he exerted strength in his arms and swung up again, flipping over and climbing to the next platform.
After climbing like this for two days, Luo Zheng finally reached the top of this nameless mountain.
"Do you see that triangular rock?" Old Man Extreme Evil reminded.
Luo Zheng looked around and saw a triangular rock. It was like a mini pyramid at the top of the mountain.
Following the method taught by Old Man Extreme Evil, Luo Zheng approached the rock and pressed down. At the same time, Luo Zheng smiled and said, "It won't be taken away by someone again, right?"
Four days ago, Luo Zheng had also done the same thing on another mountain under Old Man Extreme Evil's guidance. However, the item on that mountain had already been taken away. Even though Old Man Extreme Evil had hidden the item well, seven divine eras was too long ago. It was inevitable that someone would unintentionally find it …
"It shouldn't be!" Old Man Extreme Evil gritted his teeth. He wasn't very confident.
Just as he finished speaking, the triangular rock sank down. Following a muffled sound, a small pit appeared in front of Luo Zheng. In this pit, there were some black discs!
Luo Zheng jumped into the pit and picked up one of the black discs. He held it in his hand and examined it carefully.
These black discs were rough to the touch. Luo Zheng could faintly feel that they contained some kind of special power. However, Luo Zheng couldn't understand this power and couldn't tell what use it had. "What the hell is this? A magic treasure or a rare material? "
"Neither!" Old Man Extreme Evil said calmly. "This is the currency circulating in the divine realm, Shenwu coins!"
"Shenwu coins?" Luo Zheng raised an eyebrow and couldn't help but roll his eyes. "And there are only a few hundred of them … You want me to travel for half a month?"
Previously, Luo Zheng had planned to go directly to the "Mirror City" in the north of the Vast Sky Territory. Mirror City was one of the seven divine cities in the north of the Vast Sky Territory. However, Old Man Extreme Evil insisted that Luo Zheng find something and told Luo Zheng that without these things, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to move even a single step!
"Hehe, don't underestimate Shenwu coins. Some True Gods' entire wealth might only be worth a few hundred Shenwu coins." Old Man Extreme Evil chuckled. "Without Shenwu coins, even if you reach Mirror City, you won't be able to leave the Vast Sky Territory for at least eight to ten years. Without these things, you won't be able to move even a single step in the divine realm!"
Luo Zheng turned the Shenwu coins over and over again.
The currency of the universe was True Yuan Stones and Zhen Yuanyu. They were both currency and consumables. They were condensed from the heaven and earth's essence and contained the purest form of True Yuan. Directly absorbing them could replenish the True Yuan in one's dantian.
As Old Man Extreme Evil said, Shenwu coins were the currency circulating among True Gods. True Gods didn't need to consume True Yuan under normal circumstances. They relied on the power of faith in their inner worlds.
"Can Shenwu coins replenish True Yuan? Or can they replenish power of faith? "Luo Zheng asked.
Old Man Extreme Evil shook his head. "Neither."
"Then what's the use of Shenwu coins …" Luo Zheng asked again.
No matter what kind of currency it was, it had to have a strong attraction to True Gods. Otherwise, it would just be a pile of trash that no one would want!
Who knew that the Evil Old Man would keep them in suspense. He chuckled. "Shenwu Coins are too useful. Those floating island wealthy families in the Sea of Time fight over them every year. It can be said that having Shenwu Coins is equivalent to having everything in Divine Dominion! As for what it's used for, you'll naturally find out in the future. It's useless even if you know now! "
"Having Shenwu coins is equivalent to having everything in the divine realm?"
Luo Zheng stared at the black round piece, his expression becoming more and more strange. This was too exaggerated and didn't conform to common sense at all.
In the universe, only low-level martial artists fought tooth and nail for True Yuan Stones and Zhen Yuanyu. High-level martial artists didn't need them at all. After all, there weren't many times when they urgently needed to replenish True Yuan after a battle. Moreover, by this time, the outcome of the battle had already been decided. Thus, Realm Lords and Celestial Venerables only needed to carry hundreds of Zhen Yuanyu with them.
Therefore, when high level martial artists exchanged items, they usually bartered for other items. True essence jades weren't very valuable in their eyes …
This black round piece couldn't be eaten or drunk. How could it be used as currency in the divine realm?

Chapter 1756
After collecting the Shenwu Coins, Luo Zheng followed Old Man Evil's instructions and headed south.
As soon as he entered the Divine Dominion, Luo Zheng tried to understand the general outline of the Divine Dominion. However, Luo Zheng could not see the whole picture through the God's citizens of Meng Village. After all, the furthest the God's citizens of the village had ever been was the Mirror City …
Although Old Man Evil had been imprisoned for seven Divine Eras and his information was outdated, his knowledge and experience were quite comprehensive even among the True Gods.
"I just don't understand why a top figure like Yu Taibai didn't come to pick you up directly …"
In Old Man Evil's eyes, Luo Zheng was a proud son of God born in a Floating Island Clan. With Luo Zheng's talent and status, he was qualified to be sent to a Floating Island Clan. Even if Luo Xiao's Floating Island was destroyed, with Yu Taibai's connections, it would not be difficult for him to send Luo Zheng there.
Luo Zheng also knew that his father's disciples were still very active. At the very least, they took good care of Luo Yan.
However, since Xingwei had made such arrangements, they naturally had their own considerations. At this stage, Luo Zheng could only rely on himself!
"I think your first step should be to enter a Floating Island Clan!" Old Man Evil smacked his lips in his mind. Floating Islands were places that everyone in the Divine Dominion yearned for, but a martial artist like Old Man Evil who was born unorthodox did not have the qualifications to enter.
"Floating Islands … What kind of places are they?" Luo Zheng asked. He had heard Xingwei mention them in Huan Yu, but he did not have a deep understanding of them.
Old Man Evil sighed. He could not understand how Luo Zheng, Luo Xiao's son and Gu Bei's disciple, was so ignorant of the Divine Dominion. He could only say, "In the center of the Divine Dominion is a vast and boundless sea called the Sea of Time … I once walked by the edge of the Sea of Time. It's a sea formed by light and time. Any living thing that falls into it will be split apart by time. It's an extremely dangerous sea! And in the center of the Sea of Time are the floating islands built by the various Clans!
"Behind these floating islands are naturally the Saints of the Divine Realm. Only Saints have the qualifications to establish a floating island, so every floating island represents a peak power in the Divine Realm! They have almost monopolized 90% of the resources in the God Domain. Of course, that includes Shenwu coins. "
"Once you enter the floating island, your future will be limitless! However, it was extremely difficult to obtain the recognition of a Great Clan and bring in foreign martial artists! Even I … wasn't qualified to enter the floating island back then! "
Hearing the Evil Old Man's introduction, Luo Zheng asked in surprise, "You're not qualified?"
"Hehe, how could a True God like me, who was born in the wild, be chosen by those wealthy families?" The Extreme Evil Old Man revealed a cynical expression. Even if he hadn't received the favor of a wealthy family, he had still cultivated to the peak of the middle level. This was indeed something to be proud of.
"Enter the floating island …"
As Luo Zheng conversed with the Extreme Evil Old Man, he climbed the mountains. Now that he was standing at the peak of a mountain, he felt that the outline of the Divine Realm had become clearer. He had also set a preliminary goal.
The biting cold wind entered Luo Zheng's nose. He smiled slightly and jumped down from the mountain peak. His entire body was like a piece of paper, drifting downwards with the wind …
Relying on his own strength and methods, Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow. However, being unable to fly was indeed a problem.
Two and a half months later, Luo Zheng was still slowly advancing north of the Vast Sky Region. Finally, he arrived at the first Divine City, Mirror City.
"This Divine City is so beautiful!"
When he was still hundreds of miles away from Mirror City, Luo Zheng had already caught a glimpse of the entire Divine City.
As its name suggested, the walls of this Divine City were built with a type of silver brick. These bricks had been polished to a glossy shine. The entire wall was like a giant mirror. Luo Zheng didn't need the Extreme Evil Old Man to explain.
When Luo Zheng arrived at the entrance of Mirror City, he was stunned to see a dense mass of martial artists in front of him.
There were tens of thousands of martial artists. Most of them were at the Divine Transformation Realm. Above them were a small number of Divine Realm and Proof of Divinity martial artists. However, they were all blocked outside Mirror City!
"Don't make a ruckus! Come over here and register the points you've earned today! " More than ten Proof of Divinity martial artists in black armor stood at the entrance of Mirror City. These Proof of Divinity martial artists held long whips in their hands and were shouting at the crowd of martial artists.
With these shouts, the martial artists present immediately clamored and surged in one direction like a surging tide.
"What the hell is going on?" Luo Zheng's mouth was wide open. His eyes were filled with confusion.
"Do you think it's that easy to enter a Divine City?" The Extreme Evil Old Man chuckled. "Not just anyone can enter the city!"
It wasn't difficult to raise one's cultivation in the Divine Realm. The Divine People could open their Divine Sea as soon as they took the first step on the Martial Path and become a Divine Sea Realm martial artist. Therefore, even if Little Wu's talent was average, he could still enter the Divine Transformation Realm after the entire village gathered together and sent him to cultivate in Mirror City.
However, Meng Village had limited financial resources. Although Little Wu was a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist, it was difficult for him to cultivate any superior martial arts techniques in Mirror City. He didn't even have the most basic understanding of the Sword Dao.
How many Divine People were there in the Divine Realm?
After cultivating for a while, the vast majority of them would be stuck at the bottleneck of the Third Divine Transformation. Some of the martial artists who were determined to walk on the Divine Way would naturally go around looking for masters to learn from. There were a very small number of people who would encounter some unparalleled fortuitous encounters and become the disciple of an expert. However, this kind of luck wasn't something that everyone could have. The vast majority of martial artists would choose to enter the Divine Cities, where there would be more opportunities!
However, a Divine City's capacity was limited. If it was completely opened, the entire Divine City would be flooded by a sea of Divine People … This had happened in the past.
Left with no choice, almost all Divine Cities forbade anyone from entering.
"The rule in most Divine Cities is to pay one Shenwu coin," the Extreme Evil Old Man said.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. "It's only one Shenwu coin …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was relieved. He still had a few hundred Shenwu coins he had dug out from the Extreme Evil Old Man.
"Heh heh, do you think it's that easy to get one Shenwu coin? Look at these martial artists at the city gates, constantly completing the missions given by Mirror Citadel. The weaker martial artists have to work for decades or even centuries before they have the right to enter the city once. This is the value of one Shenwu coin! "Old Man Extreme Evil sneered.
"Hundreds of years of hard work in exchange for one Shenwu coin?" Luo Zheng was shocked when he heard this. After realizing the value of Shenwu coins, he suddenly felt that the black round pieces were heavier. However, he knew that this was just a feeling in his heart.
Just then, a crack suddenly opened in the city gates. A middle-aged man in a brocade robe walked out from the crack.
"True God?"
Luo Zheng, who was standing far away, narrowed his eyes slightly. This middle-aged man was a Lower True God.
The middle-aged man pointed at a Proof of Divinity martial artist in the crowd. The Proof of Divinity martial artist looked ecstatic. He kept nodding and bowing to the middle-aged man. Then, the middle-aged man brought him into the city.
"Bang!"
The city gates closed again. The tens of thousands of martial artists present all looked envious.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Anyone who owns a house in Mirror City can bring three people into the city. That middle-aged True God should be a native of Mirror City. The Proof of Divinity martial artist must be a relative of the middle-aged man. Naturally, he can enter the city with the middle-aged man," the Extreme Evil Old Man said.
"That works?" Luo Zheng was dumbfounded …
"Heh heh, back then, I had seven or eight houses in Mirror City. Unfortunately, after so many years, I'm afraid they've changed owners," the Extreme Evil Old Man muttered. "Let's not talk about it. Let's quickly enter the city."
Luo Zheng had Shenwu coins in his hands. Naturally, he wouldn't be as busy as the martial artists.
After bypassing the martial artists, Luo Zheng squeezed his way to the city gates.
The Mirror City guard looked coldly at Luo Zheng. He grabbed the whip in his hand and pointed to the side. He said impatiently, "Another one who doesn't know the rules! Line up over there to receive your mission! "

Chapter 1757
Shenwu Coins were indeed an extremely rare item in God Dominion.
Ordinary True Gods had very few Shenwu Coins. In fact, these martial artists who were busy outside the city might not even have a single Shenwu Coin in their entire lives. They only needed to accumulate enough points to enter the city, and the Shenwu Coins that were exchanged for points would naturally not fall into their hands.
Hearing a Proof of Divinity warrior say he had shenwu coins, the guard's expression was strange. "You have shenwu coins?"
Luo Zheng nodded and took out a shenwu coin.
When the black round piece was in Luo Zheng's hand, the guard's eyes lit up. With a wave of his hand, the shenwu coin Luo Zheng took out fell into his hand!
The other guards also looked over and sized up Luo Zheng.
For a Proof of Divinity warrior like Luo Zheng to have shenwu coins, it was either a gift from a True God or he was extremely lucky and found some treasure left behind by a True God …
If it was the former, it didn't matter. If it was the latter, this kid probably had more than one shenwu coin!
"Can I enter now?" Luo Zheng asked.
The guard carefully sized up the black round piece in his hand and then looked up at Luo Zheng with a smile. "Not yet."
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked again.
The guard chuckled. "Sorry, it costs two shenwu coins to enter Mirror City. One isn't enough."
Mirror City was a god city. There must have been many true gods there. The guards of Mirror City were only Witness God martial artists, but Luo Zheng did not want to cause trouble there. So, when he faced the arrogant guards, he was very polite.
However, the guard raised the price so suddenly. Luo Zheng frowned slightly. "Didn't you say it only costs one shenwu coin to enter the city?"
"Hehe …" The guard shook his head. "The price has increased today. You need two shenwu coins to enter the city. If you don't have one," he pointed at the warriors next to him. "Go take missions like them and earn enough points before entering the city!"
It wasn't easy to become a guard of Mirror City. The guards at the city gate were inextricably linked to some people in the God City. If Luo Zheng was sure he only had one shenwu coin, they naturally wouldn't let go of this chance to take it for themselves.
If Luo Zheng really took out a second shenwu coin, it would be even easier …
They had seen too many people like Luo Zheng, who came out of nowhere. Especially people like Luo Zheng, who was easy to talk to. They could get rid of him with just a few words. So what if he was a Proof of Divinity martial artist at the entrance of the Divine City?
Luo Zheng obviously wouldn't take out a second shenwu coin. His expression turned cold. "I don't have time for missions."
"Then come back when you earn your second shenwu coin." A hint of mockery appeared on the guard's face as he tossed the shenwu coin in his hand.
When the shenwu coin was tossed up again, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed with a golden light!
Old Man Extreme Evil had warned him along the way not to cause trouble in Mirror City. Therefore, he had restrained his soul to the extreme and hid all his sharpness.
However, the hearts of the people in the God Realm were no different from the universe. Kind people were always bullied and kind horses were always ridden!
The small golden sword above Luo Zheng's soul flashed and an extremely sharp soul pressure shot out. The small invisible arrows brushed past the souls of the guards!
These guards were only Sector Lord warriors.
The difference in power between warriors of the same level in the God Realm was even greater than in the universe!
For example, Little Wu was a Divine Transformation Realm warrior. He only had cultivation but his understanding of martial arts was so low it made one's hair stand on end.
It was the same for Sector Lord warriors. Most of the God Realm warriors cultivated to "Proving God" but in reality, they didn't have the chance to "Prove God". They only had cultivation.
For Sector Lords like them, forget about reaching the level of "Silver Fighting Soul" and "Gold Fighting Soul," there weren't many who had even cultivated "Jade Fighting Soul." As for Luo Zheng's soul strength, although it was still a Gold Fighting Soul, due to the fact that he had cultivated "Great Yan God Canon," the sharpness of his Soul Power was not lower than a Real God's!
The small arrows formed by soul pressure shot out and didn't injure the soul of the guard. Just the pressure contained within the arrows made the guard's hair stand on end. A cold feeling came from his back and his entire body froze from his bones to his muscles!
"Pu, pu …"
The sharp invisible arrows finally hit the city gate behind him. Two thin scratches appeared on the sturdy city gate.
Even though Luo Zheng tried not to let the soul pressure spread, the moment Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with golden light, the row of Mirror City guards still felt the terror and froze in place!
The shenwu coin was still spinning in the air and falling down.
Luo Zheng's right hand reached out like lightning and caught the shenwu coin. Then he waved the shenwu coin in front of the guards. "Keep it well." He said lightly and then stuffed it into the guard's pocket. Then he walked past the guards without looking back and pushed open the gates of Mirror City.
Only when the gates closed did the guards come back to their senses. They looked at each other but didn't say a word …
The God Realm was rarely like the universe, where geniuses favored by the heavens would suddenly appear.
Geniuses that could reach such heights all had this kind of background and power behind them. Just based on the other's potential and talent, it was possible to determine what level of power they came from.
Came from a small mountain village? Born in a normal god city? Or were they trained in the Central Divine City? Even if they came from the four great godly trees or the rich families of the floating islands, there were slight differences. It was very easy for people with foresight to determine!
These Mirror City guards were people without foresight but that didn't mean they were idiots. At least they had the ability to distinguish.
The soul level that Luo Zheng displayed was a level they couldn't fathom. Such a person could be cultivated even in the Central God City of a "domain." At the very least, he came from the four great godly trees. He could even be a genius of the floating islands!
Such a person, not to mention a mere guard, even normal true gods didn't dare to provoke him.
In the end, the Mirror City guard touched the shenwu coins in his pocket and swallowed. In the end, he didn't say anything.
Luo Zheng controlled his actions very well. He warned the guards and didn't cause any commotion. At least, he didn't attract the attention of the true gods in the Mirror City. After all, Luo Zheng's identity was too sensitive.
After entering the Mirror City, Luo Zheng's aura was like snow meeting the sun. It disappeared completely …
Although the Mirror City was one of the seven great god cities in the Vast Sky Region, it wasn't as big as Luo Zheng imagined. He asked the Extreme Evil Old Man why they didn't build a bigger god city. The Extreme Evil Old Man only replied with one question. Why build it so big?
In the universe, some holy cities could be said to be very big. However, the purpose of building a god city in the God Realm was different.
In the universe, dozens of rank ten sacred lands kept absorbing geniuses to expand. More sector lords were born so that they could fight for the position of Celestial Venerate in the future.
The God Realm had developed for countless god eras. It was a stable world.
It didn't mean that there weren't any geniuses in the God Realm. It was just that this concept was so small that it was negligible. Therefore, the major powers didn't have much desire to expand. For example, the wealthy families on the floating islands would only change their ranking every few years. It was very difficult for a true god to become a paragon, and even harder for a true god to become a saint.
Because of this, the distribution of resources went to the major powers. The competition between the lower level warriors was even crueler!
After Luo Zheng entered the city, he followed the Extreme Evil Old Man's instructions and went straight to the west side of the Mirror City. That was the only place that had the transportation channel to cross the Vast Sky Region.
"The Vast Sky Region is only a small region. There are a total of three powers stationed there. These three powers control all the god cities in the Vast Sky Region. However, those who know the details know that these three powers are just Han Jia's puppets. You can go through the transportation channel and go to the Liuren Region in the south of the Vast Sky Region. That's Han Jia's ancestral land," the Extreme Evil Old Man said.
"Han Jia? Ancestral land? "Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback.
The Extreme Evil Old Man nodded and said in a respectful tone, "Yes. The Han Jia's Saint Emperor became a saint 130 god eras ago. Now, the Han Jia is one of the major families on the floating islands. In the few eras that I wasn't around, the Han Jia's floating island ranking probably rose again! The Liuren Region is the Han Jia's ancestral land. You can go there and look for opportunities. "
