Chapter 1758
After hearing Old Man Evil's introduction, Luo Zheng's thoughts drifted back to the Forbidden Land of God.
Han Liusu, Han Chuyue …
Back then, these two sisters had given Luo Zheng a headache.
"Since the Han Clan's Saint Emperor is a Saint, how is he related to Mu Haiji?" Luo Zheng asked.
Old Man Evil widened his eyes and shook his head. "I don't know."
Old Man Evil's influence was limited to the Vast Sky Region. Even there, he wasn't the most powerful existence. In addition, he had never been to any of the floating islands. How would he know the relationship between the top figures in the divine realm?
"But … this shouldn't matter, right?" Old Man Evil said helplessly.
"I've been to the Forbidden Land of God before and met some of the Han Clan's disciples. If I enter rashly, I'm afraid they'll recognize me," Luo Zheng explained.
Luo Zheng didn't just have to pay attention to the Han Clan sisters. He also had to pay attention to their personal guards. For example, He Zuowei and the others recognized Luo Zheng. If Han Clan and the Mu Family had a good relationship and Luo Zheng was recognized, things would become troublesome.
Although Luo Zheng knew that there were tens of thousands of cultivators who had proven themselves to be gods, it would be difficult for the Han Clan to notice him given his current status, Luo Zheng understood that he had to be careful in the divine realm. In the divine realm, Luo Zheng had no way out.
"It's not a problem if you don't go to the Liuren Domain." Old Man Evil seemed to be in a difficult position. "However, there are thirty-six smaller domains around the Liuren Domain. They're all under Han Clan's control. Just leaving these small domains won't be easy for you!"
The floating island families had their own territories in the divine realm. Even the Vast Sky Region belonged to Han Clan.
Now that Luo Zheng couldn't use Greater Shift, the Shift Token was useless, and he couldn't even fly. He could only rely on the long-distance teleportation channel built by Han Clan. However, the cost of teleportation was expensive even for True Gods, let alone the current Luo Zheng.
The few hundred Shenwu coins that the Evil Old Man gave Luo Zheng were not enough to send Luo Zheng out.
Luo Zheng fell into deep thought. Just as the Nefarious Old Man had said, Shenwu Coins were too important. However, Luo Zheng still did not have the ability to earn Shenwu Coins!
"I have a transcendent artifact, Mu Jiu's Soul-Searching Banner. If I sell it, it should be worth some Shenwu Coins." Luo Zheng suddenly remembered that he still had a Soul Searching Banner in his hands. This Soul Searching Banner was left behind by Mu Haiji's disciple, Mu Jiu. This time, Luo Zheng had also brought it into the Realm of the Gods.
Who knew that Old Man Evil would reject his suggestion the moment he suggested it. "You're talking about that soul-searching flag? Hehe, that Soul Searching Flag is indeed valuable. It is a magic treasure that Mu Haiji used in the past. Although Mu Haiji has abandoned it, it is still very famous. If you use it, you will be discovered very quickly. That is even more dangerous! "
Luo Zheng immediately fell into hesitation. This small problem was enough to put Luo Zheng in a difficult position.
"If you ask me, you are just afraid that the Han Family will recognize you. However, the Han Family has so many disciples. I'm afraid that only a few of them have seen you in the Forbidden Land of God. The probability of that happening is not high. I can teach you a face-changing technique. I guarantee that even your father will not be able to recognize you!" Old Man Evil smiled.
Old Man Evil was obviously exaggerating. To some experts, once they locked onto someone's aura, they would be able to recognize them even if they were burned to ashes.
However, in the Forbidden Land of God, the highest cultivation was only a Realm Lord. They probably did not have such methods. Since Old Man Evil's suggestion was not bad, Luo Zheng gladly accepted it …
While Old Man Evil was giving Luo Zheng advice in his mind, Luo Zheng followed the main road of Mirror City to the west.
After walking for a short distance, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a golden light. Old Man Evil said at the same time, "Someone is following you."
"I know … and it's a true god," Luo Zheng calmly replied in his mind.
A few hundred meters behind Luo Zheng, there was a middle-aged man in gray robes. This person was following Luo Zheng at a constant speed.
He had just arrived in the divine realm. Mu Hai could not have found him so early. This person was following him. It must have been the guard at Mirror City's entrance who tipped him off.
"However, it's fine. A mere lower true god wouldn't dare to make a move in Mirror City," Old Man Evil sneered.
The entire divine realm was a hierarchical society. Even saints were restricted by the rules on the stone slab.
The various central divine cities and divine cities of the various small regions were also part of the forest of rules.
Outside the divine city, no matter how much you fought, killed, and robbed, no one would care. However, the divine city's rules were extremely strict!
Back then, Old Man Evil had occupied almost a third of the northern region of the Vast Sky Region. Han Jia naturally knew about this, but they did not send anyone to deal with Old Man Evil. Old Man Evil knew this very well. Every time he entered Mirror City, he followed the rules and never caused any trouble!
To the west of Mirror City, there was a huge square with a row of grey-brown stone walls. The stone walls didn't fit in with the buildings around Mirror City. On the stone walls, there was a door that was ten feet wide.
These doors were the teleportation channels to Mirror City.
"This teleportation channel doesn't seem to be used by anyone?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
There were a total of nine gates on the stone wall, indicating that there were nine teleportation channels here. However, there was not a single person in the square. There were only a few guards in black armor standing in front of the stone wall, their faces filled with boredom.
Old Man Evil smiled faintly. "Nonsense. It's not easy to build a teleportation channel in the divine realm. The cost of teleportation is also extremely high! To teleport from the Vast Sky Region to the Liuren Region, it costs at least a hundred Shenwu Coins! For a normal Lower True God, it's hard to gather that amount even if you have all your wealth! "
"Then what if a True God wants to go to the Liuren Region?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The regions aren't separated from each other. Just walk over … It'll just take three to four hundred years," Old Man Evil smiled.
Luo Zheng was stunned …
This kind of thing happened in the universe. For example, it was not easy for a Divine Ocean Realm cultivator to travel between large regions. If Luo Zheng did not have a teleportation token, it would be very difficult for him to travel between large regions. However, he did not expect to face such a problem after becoming a True God.
Teleportation channels in the universe were extremely cheap. However, just one teleportation in the divine realm would cost a True God all his wealth!
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally understood the importance of Shenwu Coins. If he did not have the few hundred Shenwu Coins that Old Man Evil gave him, he would not be able to easily enter Mirror City, let alone use the teleportation channel.
The middle-aged man who had been following Luo Zheng saw Luo Zheng walking towards the plaza and frowned!
He had just received news from his nephew, who was a guard of Mirror City, that a Proof God martial artist with an extremely powerful soul had entered Mirror City. His nephew also told him that although this person was only a Proof God martial artist, he seemed to be quite wealthy.
Although Luo Zheng did not reveal his wealth, a Proof of Divinity martial artist who could casually take out a Shenwu Coin to enter the city was as rare as a phoenix's feather or a qilin's horn. This True God immediately set his sights on Luo Zheng, hoping to extort a sum from him after Luo Zheng left the city!
Although the middle-aged man understood that Luo Zheng most likely came from some powerful faction, he did not think too much about it.
However, the middle-aged man did not expect Luo Zheng to go straight to the teleportation channel …
The middle-aged man was also a local True God of the Vast Sky Region. He had struggled in Mirror City for three God Eras before he could settle down. He had bought a house in Mirror City, but the total value was only 80 Shenwu Coins. That was all he had!
However, Luo Zheng could easily take out 100 Shenwu Coins to use the teleportation channel! This brat was only a Proof God martial artist!
The middle-aged man suddenly felt his heart ache. It would be great if he could stop Luo Zheng before he entered the city!
Seeing that Luo Zheng was really going to use the teleportation channel, the cooked duck was about to fly away. The middle-aged man gritted his teeth and quickly stopped Luo Zheng. He lowered his voice and said, "Friend, why have you come to Mirror City?"
Luo Zheng had already noticed this person. There was no panic on his face. Although it was his first time entering the divine realm, people's hearts were more or less the same.
Luo Zheng glanced at the middle-aged man. "Does Mirror City belong to you?"
The middle-aged man was embarrassed by Luo Zheng's question. He covered it up and said, "The divine city does not belong to one person."
"Then who does this divine city belong to?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Mirror City belongs to the Vast Sky Region's Qian family. Strictly speaking, the Qian family is controlled by Han Jia, so Mirror City belongs to Han Jia …" the middle-aged man explained seriously.
"Thank you, I understand. Goodbye." Luo Zheng laughed and turned to leave.

Chapter 1759
Just as Old Man Evil had said, the rules in Divine City were strict. Even an upper-level True God would not dare to act rashly, much less a lower-level True God!
Luo Zheng had heard many threats in his life, but this was probably the most powerless one he had ever heard. He even wondered how this person had become a True God with such a brain …
What Luo Zheng did not understand was that becoming a True God in the divine territory was not as difficult as he had imagined.
Moreover, the difference in strength between True Gods was quite exaggerated. For example, Mu Jiu and Mu Tian were both lower-level True Gods, but as Mu Haiji's disciples, they could kill ordinary True Gods in the blink of an eye!
Zhan Beihai was also a lower-level True God, but he was much stronger than Mu Jiu, Mu Tian, Xing Wei, and the others.
This was also why Old Man Evil had suggested that Luo Zheng join one of the Floating Island's wealthy families. Only by becoming a disciple of a Sage could Luo Zheng quickly increase his strength.
"Leave what?" Luo Zheng asked, confused.
The middle-aged man smiled coldly. "Naturally, the Shenwu coins on you!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng casually took out six or seven Shenwu coins. "Are you talking about this?"
Seeing the Shenwu coins in Luo Zheng's hand, the middle-aged man's eyes shone with greed. He hurriedly nodded. "Yes, yes, this is it!" Saying so, he reached out to grab the Shenwu coins.
"Dream on!"
Luo Zheng pulled back his hand and hid the Shenwu coins like a magic trick …
"You! You're courting death … "
This True God had been toyed with twice by Luo Zheng and finally flew into a rage out of humiliation!
Snowflakes appeared in his eyes, and an icy aura surged out. This True God cultivated the "Snow Seal Divine Dao". Luo Zheng had seen it once in the Emperor's Corridor in the Illusory Battlefield.
Although they were both True Gods, a True God who had been tempered in the universe was much stronger than an ordinary True God in the divine territory.
Although the aura was fierce, Luo Zheng's state of mind was not affected at all. At the same time, he quickly retreated!
"Evil Old Man, didn't you say we can't fight in Mirror City?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind when he saw the middle-aged man's posture.
Old Man Evil was also speechless. "You've angered this idiot! But he's going to be in trouble … "
The commotion here also alarmed the guards of the teleportation channel. Those guards were also astonished. They looked at the True God as if they were looking at an idiot.
At the same time, a ray of blue light shot out from the center of Mirror City. The blue light shot into the sky and a stone slab slowly rose from the ground. It quickly spiraled to the west of Mirror City!
After the stone slab appeared, it attracted the attention of countless warriors in Mirror City.
"Which idiot is making a move in Mirror City again!"
"Do you really think that the rules set by the Qian Family are just for show? Aren't you courting death?! "
"Why is it going to the west of the city …"
Because they were afraid of the punishment from the stone slab, all the warriors in Mirror City were very careful. Let alone circulating their true essence, they didn't even dare to use their true essence sound transmissions, let alone condensing their "force" with their Godhead. Normally, when they cultivated or taught martial arts to their disciples, they would go to a designated area in the city. Otherwise, they would be punished by the stone slab!
The True God was angry and impulsive, so he condensed his "force". But before he could do anything, he saw a ray of blue light flash in the sky. The True God was scared out of his wits. His face was bitter as he said, "I, I … didn't want to make a move!"
It was a pity that the stone slab wasn't a human and it didn't listen to the explanation of the Real God. The surface of the stone slab emitted bits of lightning and the lightning quickly gathered together to form a thick bolt of lightning. Then, it struck the Real God abruptly.
"Boom!"
Although the lightning looked very normal, the power contained within was almost irresistible.
"Ahhhh …"
The lightning struck the True God's body. Tiny bits of lightning continued to swim around his body, making him cry out in pain. He rolled on the ground for a while before stopping.
Luo Zheng felt a weak life force from the True God and knew that he hadn't died. But after being struck by the lightning, even if he didn't die, he would be half dead …
"That stone slab …" Luo Zheng looked up at the stone slab in the sky, his eyes full of curiosity.
He had heard from Xingwei that there was a stone slab in the Realm of the Gods that could even restrain a Saint. Mu Haiji had attacked Dayan Yu before and was punished by the stone slab. But this palm-sized stone slab in front of him could restrain a True God, but was it really so powerful that even a Saint was afraid of it?
"This isn't the Ten Thousand Commandments Stone Slab," Old Man Extreme Evil reminded him. "It's just a Ten Commandments Stone Slab."
"Ten Commandments Stone Slab?" Luo Zheng asked curiously. "There's more than one slab?"
Old Man Extreme Evil chuckled. "How else do you think these God Cities in the Realm of the Gods came about?"
Ever since the Dao Tablets of the Realm of the Gods descended, stone slabs appeared all over the Realm of the Gods. These stone slabs came in different sizes. The small ones were called the Ten Commandments Stone Slab, the big ones were called the Hundred Commandments Stone Slab, and the bigger ones were called the Thousand Commandments Stone Slab.
Soon, people in the Realm of the Gods discovered the use of these stone slabs. Rules could be set on these stone slabs, and anyone who violated the rules within the area covered by the stone slabs would be punished by the stone slabs.
From the moment these stone slabs were created, none of them had been damaged. Even a Saint couldn't damage the weakest Ten Commandments Stone Slab.
All the God Cities in the Realm of the Gods were built around stone slabs. Therefore, the size of these God Cities was largely related to the size of the stone slabs …
The Vast Sky Domain was originally a small domain. Mirror City was located in the north of the Vast Sky Domain and was a relatively remote God City. It only had one Ten Commandments Stone Slab. However, the Vast Sky God City in the center of the Vast Sky Domain had one Hundred Commandments Stone Slab.
As for the center of the Han Family's ancestral land like the Liuren Domain, which was the Liuren God City, it had one Thousand Commandments Stone Slab.
"I understand. So … the Ten Commandments Stone Slab is the one that can suppress a Saint?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Smart! Hehe, the Ten Commandments Stone Slab is also known as the Control Stone Slab. Even a Saint can't do anything to it. Moreover, the Ten Commandments Stone Slab covers the entire Realm of the Gods … "Old Man Extreme Evil explained.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was still confused. "Since these stone slabs are so powerful, why hasn't anyone used them as weapons? If I carry a Thousand Commandments Stone Slab and write down rules that are beneficial to me, who would dare to attack me? "
"Hahaha, you're indeed the son of a Saint. You're really smart," Old Man Extreme Evil praised. "It's said that this kind of thing did happen in ancient times. Some upper True Gods used the stone slabs as weapons and wrote some extremely shameless rules on them. For example, 'No one is allowed to attack me.' Once someone attacked me, the stone slab would attack … At that time, the battle between some powerful True Gods was really a battle between stone slabs."
"…"
Imagining the scene of True Gods carrying stone slabs in battle, Luo Zheng was speechless. However, he had to admit that this was indeed an extremely powerful method.
"However, later on, this method was put to an end. Someone rewrote the rules on the Ten Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet. As the Ten Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet is known as the Control Stone Tablet. It is a stone tablet that can control the entire Divine Realm. If I remember correctly, it should be the 104th rule: No one is allowed to use the stone tablet as a personal weapon. After this rule appeared, the True Gods had no choice but to put down their own stone slabs … "Old Man Extreme Evil said.
"This rule is really strange," Luo Zheng sighed.
"It's also something that has to be done. In order to become stronger, everyone is desperately researching. Although the Realm of the Gods is very stable now and the rules are very strict, there are still a lot of people who are looking for loopholes in the rules in order to obtain a chance to advance," Old Man Extreme Evil said.
Luo Zheng nodded to show that he understood.
At that moment, three patrolling True Gods wearing red and black armor rushed out from the side. After the stone slab was activated, some people in Mirror City were alarmed and had to come out to patrol as usual.
The three True Gods sized up the half-dead middle-aged man on the ground and asked Luo Zheng about what had happened. After understanding what had happened, they left. Although the rules on the stone slab were strict, there were always idiots who could not control themselves and tried to break the rules …

Chapter 1760
When Luo Zheng told the guards that he wanted to use the space tunnel to teleport, they still didn't believe him.
In the eyes of these guards, a hundred Shenwu Coins were enough to buy a house in Mirror City, enough to worship a True God as a teacher, and enough to exchange for a considerable amount of cultivation resources. To a Divine Warrior, this was nothing more. Yet, Luo Zheng was using it to use the Space Tunnel. It was a foolish move to burn money!
The guards thought that Luo Zheng was here to amuse them.
When Luo Zheng took out a stack of shenwu coins, the guards' attitude changed 180 degrees.
No matter what, the one who spent money was the boss.
These guards obviously didn't understand that normal true gods couldn't teach Luo Zheng anything. Not to mention Mirror City, even the central god city of Liuren Domain, Liuren Divine City, wasn't Luo Zheng's destination. He wanted to go to the floating island.
When the door on the stone wall opened, Luo Zheng saw a familiar scene. Behind the door was a deep blue space. Spirals spun quickly inside as if it led to an endless abyss. He had snuck in from the Great Yan Universe and used this space tunnel.
Now, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and stepped into the passageway …
After Luo Zheng left …
In the center of Mirror City, there were two sage-like old men standing on top of a golden tower.
This tower had a special position in Mirror City because the owner was from the Qian family and Mirror City was controlled by the Qian family!
"Seems like another wild kid from some rich family on the floating island came out …" one old man said lightly.
"Very few rich families are willing to 'go wild' now. The risk is huge and the results may not be good," the other old man shook his head and said.
The rich families on the floating island usually trained their true elites in the family. The best geniuses were sent to the forbidden land of god cultivation and then brought back to the floating island. The weaker geniuses were sent to the family ancestral land for training. The better ones were sent to normal god cities.
However, there was an exception to this. Some disciples with prideful talent were mostly arrogant and untamed by nature. Such people would be allowed to roam the world freely. This was the so-called 'unrestrained'.
Luo Zheng might have completely retracted his soul spirit, but the two elders of the Qian Clan had extraordinary senses. They might not be able to see through Luo Zheng, but they could sense that Luo Zheng was extraordinary. They also saw how extravagant Luo Zheng was, taking out a hundred Shenwu coins to ride the spatial passageway. Naturally, they thought that Luo Zheng came from some wealthy clan on the floating island.
"Since he is not a disciple of a rich and powerful family, his origin is somewhat suspicious. What is his purpose of going to the Liuren Domain? Should we inform Han Jia? "
"Hehe, what can a mere Proof of Divinity martial artist do? Don't meddle in other people's business …"
…
In the space tunnel …
"Since the Law of Space is unable to be used in the Divine Domain, how was this spatial tunnel built?"
Luo Zheng's body was constantly circling around, his entire being immersed in a strange feeling. Although he could not move, he could still think. He asked the Evil Old Man in his mind about this.
"Hehe, who said that the Law of Space is unable to be used? Those upper true gods, Paragons, and Sages can all use it to a certain extent. It's just that your strength is too low," the Evil Old Man said.
"Is this also because of the Governing Slate?" Luo Zheng asked as a thought flashed through his mind.
"Of course. Sigh, I don't know what those ancient Sages were thinking. They set up so many strange and bizarre rules, which also created the current situation in the Divine Domain," the Evil Old Man said with a sigh.
Migration in the Divine Domain was a huge problem. Many true gods were unable to use the Law of Space, so their range of movement was rather limited. This was equivalent to restricting the flow of Deities, greatly hindering the passage of lower class Deities upwards.
"This way, it is beneficial for those wealthy clans who have landed on the floating island," Luo Zheng said thoughtfully.
The Divine Domain's rules were strict and stable, but they were also like a pool of stagnant water. Very few geniuses were born, so it was naturally difficult for newcomers to become Sages. This way, the status of the wealthy clans on the floating island was much more stable!
"That is indeed the case, but there will always be people breaking this fence. For example, Sage Jiu, who created the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, is one of them! Hehe! "the Evil Old Man laughed.
After Sage Jiu opened up his inner world, he opened the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to the outside world. At first, the wealthy clans on the floating island did not care about it, but it attracted many grassroots Deities in the Divine Domain!
After these grassroots Deities cultivated in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for a while, they came out as peak Proving Gods, causing quite a stir in the Divine Domain … Thus, the wealthy clans on the floating island also set their sights on the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, sending their own elites to cultivate there.
After hearing the Evil Old Man's words, Luo Zheng suddenly realized that there was such a story behind the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It was no wonder he had encountered so many geniuses in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement …
After floating in the spatial passage for five days, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly lit up. His body was already prepared, ready to fall at any time. Last time, when Luo Zheng had snuck in from the Great Evolution Space, he had fallen quite heavily.
But right after that, Luo Zheng felt his body become light. Only then did he realize that he was sliding down a slippery path …
"Sou!"
After sliding down the slippery path to the bottom, he stood up, safe and sound.
"The treatment of spending money is really different …" Luo Zheng sighed.
Luo Zheng looked back and also discovered a huge stone wall. However, this stone wall was carved with all kinds of exquisite sculptures. In terms of appearance, it was much more exquisite than the stone wall in Mirror City.
"This should be Liuren Divine City?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, this is the ancestral land of the Han Clan. It is also the Central Divine City of the Han Clan. It is a hundred times larger than the Mirror City of the Vast Sky Region," the Evil Old Man replied.
There were only three levels of Divine Cities in the Divine Domain. Ordinary Divine Cities were small cities like Mirror City. The larger ones were the Central Divine City of the Vast Sky Region. The largest Divine Cities were the ancestral lands of the rich and powerful families, such as Liuren Divine City.
"After using the spatial passage, don't stay here. Quickly leave!" At this time, a stern reprimand came from one of the guards of Liuren Divine City. He was scolding Luo Zheng.
'This Liuren Divine City is indeed more strictly guarded than Mirror City,' Luo Zheng thought. However, he still cupped his hands towards the guard and walked away.
When the guard saw Luo Zheng walk away, he muttered to himself, 'How strange. There's actually a Proof of Divinity cultivator using a spatial passage …'
Although the Sixth Ren Divine City was a hundred times larger than the Mirror City, it was still not considered large when compared to the Rank Ten Sacred Grounds that Luo Zheng had seen in the Great Evolution Universe. After all, the range of a Divine City was built according to the area covered by the stone slab. Although the Sixth Ren Divine City had the Thousand Commandments Stone Slab, the range of the stone slab's influence was limited.
Following the Evil Old Man's instructions, Luo Zheng spent several days searching Liuren Divine City.
There were many 'Proof of Divinity Residences' in the Divine City.
These 'Proof of Divinity Residences' were similar to the martial dojos and martial houses in the universe. After entering a Divine City, the majority of Divine Citizens would enter the Proof of Divinity Residences to cultivate martial skills and comprehend the Divine Dao in hopes of successfully proving divinity one day.
However, these Proof of Divinity Residences were useless to Luo Zheng. In terms of the Divine Dao, Luo Zheng might not be as good as some of the True Gods in the Proof of Divinity Residences. However, based on his understanding of the Martial Dao, it was likely that many True Gods would want to take Luo Zheng as their master.
After several days of searching, Luo Zheng was not without any results.
Only then did Luo Zheng understand that the largest Proof of Divinity Residences in Liuren Divine City, the 'Heaven-Containing Residence', was opened by the Han Family.
It was said that after entering the Heaven-Containing Residence, there was a slight chance that the Han Family would take a fancy to them and accept them to cultivate. After entering the Han Family, there was also a slight chance that they would become a direct disciple of the Han Family and be sent to the Han Family's floating island …
This slight chance was extremely slim. Ordinary Divine Citizens did not even dare to think about it. Therefore, when Luo Zheng asked around, the Proof of Divinity cultivators stared at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a madman …
As soon as Luo Zheng arrived at the Heaven-Containing Residence, he saw tens of thousands of Proof of Divinity cultivators sitting cross-legged two feet apart with respectful expressions.
At the front of these Proof of Divinity cultivators, there was an old man with a moustache. He had his hands behind his back and was swaying back and forth as he muttered, "Birth, restraint, ride, humiliation, the measurement of the heavens, their meanings are mixed, their forms are complicated …"
Although Luo Zheng did not know what he meant, he still had a certain understanding of the Divine Dao. He immediately knew that this old man was imparting the Divine Dao of the Five Elements.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1761
Not every martial artist had the opportunity to look at the Dao Tablet.
Moreover, comprehending the Divine Dao in front of the Dao Tablet was originally an extremely severe test. If not done properly, one's soul might be destroyed.
In fact, most of the Proof of Divinity martial artists relied on the teachings of True Gods to comprehend the Divine Dao.
However, there were many types of teachings …
The Sages had a deep understanding of the Divine Dao. If they personally taught, they would naturally see more things. They could even obtain some insights that could not be obtained from the Dao Tablet. Those were the Sages' own insights. However, not everyone had this kind of treatment. Only the Sages' children and disciples had this kind of qualification.
This was also the reason why the disciples of the Sages were much stronger than ordinary True Gods.
Below them were Perfection True Gods, Upper True Gods, Middle True Gods …
The lower one's cultivation was, the shallower one's comprehension of the Divine Dao would be. The comprehension one could obtain would also be less. Even if a lower True God imparted the Dao, there was still a difference.
This Heaven Containing Palace had True Gods teaching Dao. There were hundreds of the most common Divine Daos. Therefore, the Heaven Containing Palace was the most famous in Six Ren Divine City. Countless Proof of Divinity martial artists wanted to enter.
However, those who could truly enter the Heaven Containing Palace were few and far between.
These Proof of Divinity martial artists who could not enter the Heaven Containing Palace could only wander around outside.
However, the Heaven Containing Palace was not stingy. If there was free time in the palace, they would invite some True Gods to teach outside.
This was the reason why Luo Zheng saw this scene today. Moreover, today's teachings were the Five Elements Divine Dao. It was also one of the most widely spread Divine Daos in the Divine Realm. Some Proof of Divinity martial artists who could not enter the Heaven Containing Palace naturally wanted to seize this free opportunity to comprehend the Dao and listen attentively.
Luo Zheng had already made up his mind to enter the Heaven Containing Palace. However, there were so many Proof of Divinity martial artists blocking his path. He was not in a hurry. He could wait until the old man finished teaching and then ask. Therefore, he saw an empty spot and sat down cross-legged.
He did not plan to cultivate the Five Elements Divine Dao. However, the Divine Dao inheritance contained the truth. There was no harm in listening more.
Who knew that just as he sat down, a bald martial artist next to him said in a low voice, "Get lost!"
Luo Zheng didn't know the inside story. He only knew that the Heavenly Palace was open to everyone to listen. However, the empty space in front of the Heavenly Palace was very tight. Some Proof of Divinity cultivators who stayed in Six Ren Divine City all year round had taken the best spots.
For these best positions, some of the Proof of Divinity martial artists fought until their heads bled. Some fought outside the city, and some fought through other channels …
As soon as Luo Zheng arrived, he went up and "occupied" one of the relatively good seats. Seeing Luo Zheng's unfamiliar face, how could the bald martial artist have any good words to entertain him?
If this bald-headed martial artist tried to persuade him, Luo Zheng wasn't someone who didn't know how to act tactfully. Although it was indeed overbearing to take the empty space for himself, he also understood that it was first come, first served, so it didn't matter even if he was a bit further away.
This bald-headed martial artist's malicious words provoked Luo Zheng. He glanced at the other party indifferently and calmly replied, "Anyone can sit in this empty space. Why do you want me to get lost?"
"Brat, you're new here, right?" The bald-headed martial artist immediately understood that Luo Zheng didn't know the inside story. "The seat you're sitting in is the seat of the son of the Heavenly Palace's Chamberlain Pang. If your parents don't know the rules after you die, big brother can teach you!"
Hearing the bald-headed martial artist's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a cold light. He tried his best to restrain his emotions.
Although this bald-headed martial artist had the same cultivation as Luo Zheng, the difference in strength between Luo Zheng and himself was like heaven and earth. Among the Proof of Divinity cultivators, perhaps only the top disciples of the Floating Island's wealthy families had the qualifications to compete with Luo Zheng.
If this person hadn't cursed his parents, no matter what he said, Luo Zheng's state of mind wouldn't have changed.
Luo Zheng also knew that making a move in Six Ren Divine City was a very stupid move, but for the sake of his plan, he could only endure it.
Seeing Luo Zheng remain silent, the bald-headed martial artist felt that Luo Zheng didn't know what was good for him. He suddenly stood up, an extremely angry expression appearing on his face as he shouted, "Benefactor Wu! This brat actually dares to criticize your Divine Way! Say that the words you imparted to me are complete bullshit and utter nonsense! "
The old man with the surname Wu from the Heaven-Containing Palace was shaking his head as he imparted the Dao. He spoke of his comprehension in a slow and precise manner. For the person who imparted the Dao, sharing his comprehension with everyone was a form of enjoyment, and it could also lead to new comprehension.
The old man surnamed Wu hated it the most when someone tried to impart the Dao to him, so when he heard the bald-headed martial artist's words, his face immediately filled with anger, "Who said that my Divine Way is complete nonsense?"
"It's him!"
"It's that brat who just arrived!"
"I heard what he said, he's very disdainful of you, Senior …"
In addition to the bald-headed martial artist, several other Proof of Divinity cultivators suddenly jumped out from beside Luo Zheng and pointed at Luo Zheng one after another.
These people were probably in the same group, and their testimony seemed to be quite credible under the influence of public opinion.
In fact, these people were already used to this. If someone sat in a position they shouldn't, they would use this method to drive them away.
Generally speaking, a Dao imparting True Divinity would directly drive the troublemaker out, but this old man surnamed Wu was very serious. He didn't drive Luo Zheng away immediately. After taking a few steps closer, he stared at Luo Zheng and questioned, "Tell me, what part of my Dao is complete nonsense? What part of it is nonsense? If you can't say it, then quickly get the hell out of here. Young people these days are getting bolder and bolder, daring to speak nonsense in front of the Heavenly Palace! "
Everyone's eyes were fixed on Luo Zheng. Except for a few martial artists who knew the inside story, most of the Proof of Divinity cultivators felt that Luo Zheng was here to cause trouble.
In this case, Luo Zheng's explanation was useless, and it would only make things worse.
The bald-headed martial artist's eyes were full of ridicule, but he was secretly happy in his heart. This brat was looking for trouble. He didn't want to get out of here, and he had to force him to leave. Let's see how he will get out of this situation.
Who knew that Luo Zheng slowly got up, cupped his hands towards the Dao imparting True Divinity, and said, "In my opinion, what Master Wu said is complete nonsense …"
"…"
The faces of the Proof of Divinity cultivators present were like flipping through a book. The first page was a lively expression, but the next page was uniformly stunned.
Even the bald-headed martial artist was no exception. His mouth was wide open as he looked at Luo Zheng in a daze.
Some words were one thing to say in private, and another to say it in front of others. The Proof of Divinity cultivators present were all here to learn, and could be considered half of Old Man Wu's students. Luo Zheng saying these words in front of him was equivalent to slapping Old Man Wu in the face!
Even though Old Man Wu was a True Divinity, his face was still deathly pale, "You, what did you say? Say it again!"
"In my opinion, what Master Wu said is complete nonsense," Luo Zheng continued.
"…"
The faces of the Divine Refinement cultivators present stiffened even more, and some began to whisper among themselves.
"Is this brat crazy?"
"Although the people of the Heavenly Palace have always been gentle, they can't tolerate such slander."
"Although Master Wu's cultivation is only that of a lower True Divinity, his comprehension of the Divine Dao of the Five Elements is still quite good …"
Old Man Wu was so angry that he laughed, "Let me hear what nonsense you're spouting. Why don't you give me some advice?"
Ai …
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. He also didn't want to offend Old Man Wu, but in this situation, he had no choice but to go with the flow. He immediately said, "Using the words of life, restraint, opportunity, and oppression to summarize the Five Elements is indeed correct! But Master Wu, what you said is completely copying the original meaning of the Dao tablets! On the path of the Divine Dao, there are tens of thousands of paths. If one were to completely comprehend the meaning of the words and then casually impart them, it would be like a blind man touching an elephant; it would be difficult to see the whole picture. "
After Old Man Wu heard Luo Zheng's criticism, there was a trace of surprise in his heart, but he still coldly questioned, "Humph, in the Realm of the Gods, there are at most True Divinities that cultivate the Divine Dao of the Five Elements. Why can't we use this concept?"
Luo Zheng shook his head, "Of course not! Sticking to the old ways is the greatest taboo of the Divine Dao. The Divine Dao of the Five Elements combines two and five, and there are twenty-five variations. However, fire and wood are taboos, and water and water are taboos. Only by removing these two taboos can one take a step forward. How many lower True Divinities have failed to comprehend these two taboos and stopped at the Divine Dao of the Five Elements? Presumably, Master Wu is also unable to progress on this path because of this, right? "

Chapter 1762
Old Man Wu had been in the True Divinity Realm for many years, and he was stuck between the Two Taboos of the Five Elements Divine Dao.
These two taboos were the Fire and Wood taboos, and the Water and Earth taboos!
Because of these two taboos, Old Man Wu's cultivation was unable to progress, and his godhead was also unable to break through!
Ordinary Proof of Divinity martial artists simply did not know of the existence of these two taboos. Even if a small number of them had heard of them, they would not be able to understand the meaning of these two taboos. But this young Proof of Divinity martial artist in front of him was able to point out these two taboos with just a few words. This really shocked Old Man Wu!
However, there was a reason why Old Man Wu couldn't break these two taboos. There was only one way to break these two taboos in the Divine Domain, and that was to use the "Taboos Removal Golden Pellet". It was to refine the Wood Fusion Golden Pellet and the Water Fusion Golden Pellet from the Fire Bird's Everlasting Fire Crown and the Earth Savage Beast's heart!
Once these two golden pills were combined and refined together, one could obtain the Fire Fusion Golden Pill. After swallowing it, one would have a thirty percent chance of breaking through the two taboos.
However, the Wood Fusion Golden Pill and Water Transformation Golden Pill were both Five Transformations Golden Pills, while the Taboo Golden Pill was a Six Transformations Golden Pill. This was not something a low-level Dao Imparting True God in Han Jia's Heaven-Containing Palace could possess.
Old Man Wu had worked hard in the Heaven Containing Palace, and for more than half a Divine Era, he had only managed to save enough money for one Wood Fusion Golden Pill!
Even if he could save enough money for one Water Transformation Golden Pill, he would still need to spend an even greater amount of money to ask a pill refiner to combine the two golden pills. This was an even greater expense …
People with foresight were always respected. Although Old Man Wu's temperament was a bit harsh, after hearing Luo Zheng's words break through his bottleneck, his tone softened a bit, but he still said coldly, "Even if this old man is stuck under the Two Taboos, so what? What part of the Divine Dao that this old man imparts is bullshit? "
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, and said unhurriedly, "Teacher Wu also knows that according to the Divine Dao you imparted to me, I will definitely be stuck under the Two Taboos. Isn't this what you mean by not understanding?"
"Hmph, Divine Martial has millions of True Divinities who cultivate the Five Elements Divine Dao, but how many of them can break through these two taboos? You are still pestering me endlessly! Men, chase this person away! " Elder Wu shouted, and two people rushed out of Han Tian Manor. These two people were also lower true gods, but their expressions were gloomy and cold. Their gazes were as sharp as knives. They were clearly Han Jia's guards.
"Wait a minute," Luo Zheng waved his hand and continued, "I said that if we follow Benefactor Wu's method to prove the Divine Way, we will sooner or later fall into the two taboos. This is because Benefactor Wu only knows one way to break the two taboos, right?"
The old man surnamed Wu raised his eyebrows and reached out to stop Han Jia's guards. He stared at Luo Zheng and sneered, "Could it be that you have some brilliant ideas today? Come up with the second method? "
The Proof of Divinity martial artists present stared at Luo Zheng with a sneer on their faces.
Just now, Luo Zheng and the old man surnamed Wu had answered so fluently that it had indeed surprised these Proof of Divinity martial artists. The old man surnamed Wu, as a True Divinity martial artist, may not be very strong, but his understanding of the Divine Way was very solid. He was best at theoretical explanations.
These Proof of Divinity martial artists were shocked and secretly jealous of Luo Zheng. After Luo Zheng fooled around with a few words, he was still going to be chased away. This naturally made these Proof of Divinity martial artists very happy.
"This is called showing one's slight skill in front of an expert, haha," the bald martial artist also laughed on the side.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would once again cup his hands in salute to the old man surnamed Wu, and a faint smile appeared on his face as he slowly said, "Although I am a Proof of Divinity martial artist, I can be considered to have some understanding of the Five Elements Divine Way. I really do have a second method to break through the two taboos, and this method does not need to rely on the Golden Core."
"Haha," after hearing Luo Zheng's words, the old man surnamed Wu burst into a series of cold laughter.
A faint smile also appeared on the cold faces of the two guards of the Heaven-containing Mansion beside him.
The Proof of Divinity martial artists present also roared with laughter …
After the old man surnamed Wu finished laughing, he said in a cold voice, "Looking at your bone age, you don't learn anything good, but learn how to deceive people. Today, I want to hear what kind of reasoning you can fabricate to break through the two taboos!"
Luo Zheng's face did not show any signs of panic, and still said in a neither fast nor slow tone, "When one first enters the Five Elements Divine Way, because the five elements are heterogeneous, there are two cracks in the Godhood condensed. Fire and wood are difficult to fuse, and earth and water repel each other, so these two cracks lead to two taboos! The Five Elements Divine Way has been passed down for so many years, and countless Proof of Divinity martial artists have used this ancient method to prove their Dao. They know how to do it, but they do not know how to do it. They have never been able to avoid these two taboos! That's why I said that Master Wu, you should follow the rules and stick to the old ways! "
"If you use the power of the gold form to remedy it, you can create Profound Gold in your Dantian to remedy it. Although this method is time-consuming and energy-consuming, ordinary True Divinities cannot obtain the Golden Core. If you use this method to cultivate, you will be able to do it in less than ten thousand years!"
After saying this, Luo Zheng stood with his hands clasped behind his back, his eyes indifferent.
The Proof of Divinity martial artists present were confused, and did not understand what Luo Zheng was talking about at all.
"This kid is really amazing, fabricating nonsense as soon as he opens his mouth!"
"Some people don't have any real ability, but their ability to talk without thinking is really great!"
"Who is Master Wu? Isn't it courting death to talk without thinking in front of him?"
The Proof of Divinity martial artists saw that the old man surnamed Wu did not speak, so they whispered to each other, waiting to see Luo Zheng being thrown out of the hall …
The old man surnamed Wu stood in the same place. After listening to Luo Zheng's words, he fell into deep thought.
Suddenly, it was as if a bolt of lightning had burst out of his mind and streaked across his soul. At this moment, his face was flushed, and his expression became abnormally excited, as if he had grasped a life-saving straw, and his tone could no longer remain calm, "You, you said the Profound Gold needed to remedy it. How much wood and how much gold does it need?"
Among the Five Elements, the power of Profound Gold was the fusion of gold and wood.
"Thirty percent, seventy percent," Luo Zheng replied indifferently.
"Thirty percent, seventy percent …" The old man surnamed Wu was like a fool, walking back and forth in front of everyone, completely forgetting his demeanor in front of his students, and only lowering his head to think.
Everyone looked at each other in dismay, not knowing what was wrong with the old man surnamed Wu. Why did he suddenly become crazy …
"Using Profound Gold to remedy it, this method … this method is really stupid, but it's still more effective than the method of removing taboos … this method, this method is really feasible!" The old man surnamed Wu deduced in his mind, and actually agreed with the method that Luo Zheng taught him!
In order to break the two taboos, he was willing to stay in the Heaven-Containing Mansion to teach Dao and earn that meager amount of shenwu coins. According to the old man surnamed Wu's calculations, with this meager salary, it would probably take one or two divine eras to accumulate enough shenwu coins. Although the lifespan of a True God was unlimited, who would be willing to do so?
According to Luo Zheng's "stupid" method, it would only take ten thousand years. This would save a lot of time, and it would be good news for the millions of low-level True Gods who cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao!
"Who are you!? Where did you hear this method? "The old man surnamed Wu stared straight at Luo Zheng, his eyes shining as if he wanted to eat him alive.
"This one knows a little about the Five Elements Divine Dao … I comprehended it myself." Luo Zheng cupped his hands and smiled.
"That's impossible, that's impossible. How can you have such comprehension at such a young age, you …" The old man surnamed Wu looked at Luo Zheng in a daze, his face full of disbelief.
The Proof of Divinity cultivators present, including the two True God guards of the Heaven-Containing Mansion, were all dumbfounded.
Previously, the old man surnamed Wu had already said that Luo Zheng was a swindler, and in their hearts, they naturally regarded Luo Zheng as a swindler of real value. They never thought that Luo Zheng would be able to draw such an evaluation from the old man surnamed Wu with just a few words.
As for the bald cultivator beside Luo Zheng, his mouth was also wide open, staring at Luo Zheng in a daze, transfixed like a wooden chicken.
How could he have imagined that in order to drive away this little brat beside him, he would eventually lead to such a bizarre situation. At the same time, he also had a bad feeling in his heart …
Luo Zheng continued, "Allow this one to explain. Just now, this Luo did not ridicule Old Mister's words. It was only because this one sat in the wrong position that I was slandered by this person. As for that nonsense, this Luo did not say anything. It was these people who joined forces to slander this Luo, so this Luo could only go with the flow."
As Luo Zheng spoke, he pointed at the bald cultivator beside him …
The old man surnamed Wu glanced at the bald cultivator. Whether or not Luo Zheng had said anything was no longer important. What was important was that he had pointed out a solution.
But since Luo Zheng had explained himself like this, the old man surnamed Wu would naturally take action. He pointed at the bald cultivator, "Throw him out. I don't want to see this person again in the future."

Chapter 1763
Luo Zheng might have dabbled in the Five Elements Divine Dao, but his own understanding of it was not that deep.
Luo Zheng would not have been able to say what he had just said. He was still relying on the Evil Old Man in his mind.
The Extreme Evil Old Man chuckled in his mind. "The big shots in the floating island families of the Divine Domain don't care about the Golden Six Refinement Pill at all. If their disciples encounter the two taboos, they'll just give them a few more pills and they'll pass easily. They're unlike us grassroots True Gods who can't even produce a Golden Five Refinement Pill after years of hard work. We came up with this idea back then because we had no choice …"
The Evil Old Man had also cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao. Furthermore, he had personally gone to the Dao tablet of the Five Elements Divine Dao and studied it. Naturally, his understanding of the Five Elements Divine Dao far exceeded that of an ordinary lower True God!
As he had said, using the Black Gold method to break the two taboos was a stupid method. The "two taboos" were not a problem for the Sages and their disciples. A mere Five Transformation Golden Pill and a Six Transformation Golden Pill were nothing in their eyes. Therefore, they would not waste time thinking of a way to break the taboos. Even if they came up with the Black Gold method, no one would use it.
The Evil Old Man was considered a genius among the grassroots. He had relied on his own talent to cultivate to the peak of a middle True God. There were not many True Gods like him in the Divine Dominion.
After Elder Wu ordered his men to chase the bald cultivator away, he said to everyone, "Today's lesson ends here." Then, he stared at Luo Zheng as if he was afraid that Luo Zheng would run away. "Come with me!"
Luo Zheng nodded and was brought into the Heaven-Containing Palace by Elder Wu.
Today's lesson ended early. All the Proof of Divinity cultivators present looked regretful. Looking at Luo Zheng's back, their expressions were incomparably complicated. Some were envious, and some were jealous.
Some of the older Testimony Gods had stayed in Six Ren Divine City for countless years and still had not gained anything. Entering the Heaven-Containing Palace to cultivate the Divine Dao was still a distant dream.
They saw that Luo Zheng's bone age was still young, probably less than a hundred years old. With only a few words, he was personally led into the Heaven-containing Mansion by the True God of Dao Impartation. It was no wonder that they were jealous …
The Heaven-containing Prefecture was the Attestation Prefecture of the Han Family. In the eyes of Attestation Gods, it was the best place to attestation a god, second only to the floating islands in the Sea of Time.
Of course, those floating islands were like legends to ordinary Proving Gods and even many True Gods. They never had the extravagant hope of entering the floating islands, so naturally, they would not consider it. Therefore, the Heaven-containing Mansion was the best place to cultivate in Liuren Domain.
Following the entrance of the Heaven-Containing Mansion, Luo Zheng arrived at a spacious and flat plaza. There were corridors on both sides of the plaza. Under the guidance of the old man surnamed Wu, Luo Zheng followed the corridors to the side of the Heaven-Containing Mansion. They passed through a forest with dense vines. The winding path led to a secluded place, and the scenery was pleasant and exquisite.
Not long after, Luo Zheng was brought to a side courtyard. This side courtyard was where the old man with the surname Wu lived.
After inviting Luo Zheng to take a seat, the old man surnamed Wu asked, "Your Excellency, your surname is Luo?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
"May I know Your Excellency's name?" the old man surnamed Wu asked again.
"This one is Luo Tianxing," Luo Zheng replied with cupped fists.
The old man surnamed Wu nodded. "May I know which family on the floating islands Your Excellency is from, and why have you come to Liuren Divine City?"
In Old Man Wu's eyes, let alone a Proof of Divinity martial artist, even a lower True Divinity martial artist wouldn't have such eyesight. For Luo Zheng to be able to come up with such an outrageous method, there was a high chance he came from some floating island family!
"This one has never been to a floating island," Luo Zheng shook his head and denied.
What he said was naturally true. Not only had he never been to a floating island, he had never even heard of what a floating island looked like …
"Is that so?" The old man surnamed Wu chuckled, but his face was still full of disbelief. "If you didn't come from a floating island, then this old man doesn't believe that Your Excellency comprehended the Profound Gold Breaking Technique on your own!"
This was not a matter of talent. If Luo Zheng's comprehension of the Five Elements Divine Dao had reached this level, it was impossible for him to just be a Attestation God cultivator. He would have long since become a True God, and if he used the Profound Gold Breaking Technique to make up for the two taboos, in ten thousand years, Luo Zheng would have already become a Middle True God!
Based on this logic, the old man surnamed Wu believed that Luo Zheng was lying.
Luo Zheng knew that the old man surnamed Wu would have such doubts, so he had already prepared an excuse. "To be honest, the Profound Gold Breaking Technique was not comprehended by me. It was left to me by a nameless senior. Benefactor Wu, think about it. Would the wealthy families on the floating islands really be so bored as to come up with such a stupid method? The Forbidden Gold Pill is something they can easily obtain. Why would they go through so much trouble? "
"This …" The old man surnamed Wu didn't think too deeply about the problem. Hearing Luo Zheng's explanation, it was indeed so. Those upper True Gods, Great Perfection Saints, and Saints monopolized almost ninety percent of the Shenwu coins in the God Domain. A sixth-grade Gold Pill was also an ordinary item in their eyes. How could they come up with the Profound Gold Breaking Technique? That would be extremely boring. The old man surnamed Wu nodded. "Indeed. It seems that nameless senior of yours was also born from a humble background. He's also an extremely intelligent person. I've studied the Five Elements Divine Dao all my life, but I've never come up with such a method. As you said, I've been blinded by my old ways. Alas …"
"This one did come to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture to ask for a favor," Luo Zheng took the opportunity to say.
The old man surnamed Wu glanced at Luo Zheng. Although Luo Zheng hadn't stated his purpose, he could guess. "You want to enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
These days, there were too many Proving Gods who wanted to enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. Too many …
All the Proving Gods treated the Heaven-Containing Prefecture as a dragon's gate. Once they passed this dragon's gate, the matter of becoming a Proving God was basically settled.
Almost every year, every month, every day, there were people who wanted to rely on the old man surnamed Wu to enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. However, those who could truly enter the Prefecture relied on their own strength and talent.
The old man surnamed Wu finally understood. This martial artist called Luo Tianxing wasn't from a wealthy family, nor was he a genius. He was just an ordinary Proving God martial artist who had coincidentally obtained the Profound Heaven Forbidden Breaking Technique. He wanted to use this technique to exchange for entering the Heaven-Containing Prefecture.
After all, Luo Zheng had helped the old man surnamed Wu. Therefore, when Luo Zheng made this request, the old man surnamed Wu couldn't refuse. He just shook his head and said, "Although I am the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's Teaching True God, to enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, you have to go through the Prefecture's selection process. I can't make an exception and take you into the Prefecture."
When the Heaven-Containing Prefecture recruited Proving Gods, there would often be hundreds of thousands of Proving Gods taking part in the examination at the same time. However, there would only be a dozen or so people who were actually accepted. This was truly one in a thousand. The old man surnamed Wu couldn't change the result.
Moreover, those who could enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture were all geniuses among the Proving Gods. Even if the old man surnamed Wu really helped Luo Zheng cheat, Luo Zheng's talent wasn't enough. If he entered the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, he would quickly be exposed.
"However, I can recommend you to fight for a spot in the Prefecture's examination. It's possible." The old man surnamed Wu's expression became unnatural.
A spot in the Prefecture's examination was difficult for normal warriors to obtain. After all, just a spot in the examination could be sold for seven or eight shenwu coins. It wasn't a small sum. However, the Xuan Gold Taboo Breaking Technique that Luo Zheng taught him was far from worth this price. The old man surnamed Wu owed Luo Zheng a big favor, but he could only help with this small matter. He felt bad.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng smiled slightly. "If Benefactor Wu can give me a spot, that would be the greatest help! I won't be picky. "
Benefactor Wu didn't know that Luo Zheng himself didn't care about the chance to enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. The Heaven-Containing Prefecture was just a springboard under his feet. His goal was Han Jia's floating island.
If he couldn't even enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture with his true ability, how could he enter Han Jia's floating island?
To be able to obtain a spot in the examination was enough for Luo Zheng. There was no need for the old man surnamed Wu to worry about the rest …
"If that's the case, then it's easy. You've picked the right time. The Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination is held once a year, and the examination will begin in a month. Right now, more than half of the spots have been given out. I'll ask for a spot for you. Wait a moment!" The old man surnamed Wu left the courtyard.
Not long after, the old man surnamed Wu returned with a white jade token in his hand. He handed it to Luo Zheng. "Just engrave your name on it."
Luo Zheng held the white jade token in his hand and felt a chill come from it. This kind of jade was an extremely rare material even in the universe, but it was probably a very common item in the divine realm. After receiving the token, Luo Zheng stood up and bid farewell to the old man surnamed Wu.
After Luo Zheng left, the old man surnamed Wu shook his head and muttered, "I actually hoped that you could enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture with your own ability, but I'm afraid there's no hope for that this year …"
This year was the Han Jia's big year of examination. The geniuses of the thirty-six regions under Han Jia's jurisdiction would all gather in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. This Luo Zheng's skeletal age was less than a hundred years. Although the old man surnamed Wu didn't have the opportunity to investigate Luo Zheng's innate talent, he knew it was most likely hopeless.
