Chapter 1764
One month later, the Heaven Containing Prefecture's recruitment test would begin. During this time, Luo Zheng stayed in Six Ren Divine City.
During this time, more and more people entered Six Ren Divine City. Most of them were God Refinement Realm cultivators. Naturally, they came for the Heaven Containing Prefecture.
In the vast space outside of the divine city, there were many complicated forces. For example, some sects were willing to invest and send some of their genius disciples into the Heaven Containing Prefecture. They hoped that they could prove their Divine Dao in the Heaven Containing Prefecture and repay the sect.
Other than the large and small sects, the second and third tier clans of the 36 domains also hoped that their disciples could enter the Heaven Containing Prefecture.
Thus, Six Ren Divine City became lively during this time.
The cost of using the spatial tunnel was as high as a hundred Shenwu coins. However, to the clans that controlled the divine city, it wasn't too much of a problem. More and more Divine Dao cultivators came through the spatial tunnel in Six Ren Divine City.
When the number of Divine Dao cultivators increased, it would cause some trouble.
The rules of Six Ren Divine City were set by the Thousand Commandments Slate. Although there was a huge difference between the Thousand Commandments Slate and the Control Slate, it was naturally much stronger than the Ten Commandments Slate. The Thousand Commandments Slate could record a thousand rules. Moreover, the range of the rules was larger, and the power of punishment was stronger.
Thus, some of the rules in Six Ren Divine City were slightly different from Mirror City.
Although Six Ren Divine City also prohibited fighting in the city, there were some additional rules. For example, one could challenge another person in Six Ren Divine City, but the price was one Shenwu coin.
No matter the outcome, this Shenwu coin would be paid to Six Ren Divine City. It was a "fighting tax" collected by Han Jia.
Most of the Proof of Divinity cultivators in the Divine Realm were quite young. They started cultivating from the Divine Sea Realm, so it wasn't too difficult for them to break through to the Third Transformation Stage. These youths were still young and full of vigor, so it was inevitable that they would cause trouble when they gathered together. If they fought, they would have to pay the "fighting tax", and the Sixth Ren Divine City was also adding fuel to the fire.
Thus, before the recruitment test, the Divine Dao cultivators in Six Ren Divine City fought each other. It was an opportunity to test their strength.
Hanyue Restaurant, the largest restaurant in Six Ren Divine City.
This restaurant was built with six pillars that supported the heavens, forming a massive patio. The diners ate at the outermost perimeter, and the isolated patio in the middle was deliberately provided with opportunities for martial artists to fight.
However, those who could enter the Hanyue Inn were basically people of status. After all, it would cost three to five God Martial Coins each time they entered. Even for many Lower True Gods, it would be difficult for them to afford it, let alone Proof Gods.
But for this group of Divine Warriors who came from famous families, it was not a problem …
Luo Zheng was also in the Hanyue Building today.
The Evil Old Man was very excited when he mentioned the Hanyue House. He strongly recommended Luo Zheng to come and broaden his horizons no matter what.
Luo Zheng had abstained from eating for many years, so he did not really care about eating. Moreover, the Evil Old Man did not leave him many God Martial Coins, so he seemed to be uninterested.
The universe created by the Saint is a second-class world. There are many impurities in the five grains, meat, and eggs. To put it bluntly, the material objects in the Divine Territory are all formed from chaos. Just eating them is beneficial. You see, abstaining from eating is something that the Gods can do with their eyes closed. But over the years, I don't see anyone who is willing to abstain from eating. You will understand once you make a trip to the Hanyue Building … "said the Evil Old Man.
The path of Godhood was long. If there was too much boredom in the endless years, these millions of True Gods would not be able to survive. Their path of cultivation was always for fame, profit, lust, and desire. There was always a legitimate reason. Just like what the Evil Old Man said, there were not many True Gods in the Divine Territory who abstained from eating because they did not have to consider the problem of "physical purity".
After some persuasion, Luo Zheng became curious. Since the God Martial Coins in his hands were given to him by the Evil Old Man, he came to the Hanyue Building today.
Once he entered the Hanyue Building, a lady dressed in green walked out gracefully. This lady did not have any cultivation and was only a God Citizen. She sized up Luo Zheng and saw that Luo Zheng was wearing a plain long gown. She had already underestimated Luo Zheng's status, but her expression did not change. She said in a graceful voice, "Young Master, are you invited by a friend?"
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I am alone."
"This way, please." The lady nodded. She led the way and led Luo Zheng to a seat on the left side of the first floor.
The Hanyue Building had a total of six floors. The interior was spacious, but the location of the first floor was not good.
Apart from tasting the delicacies, the most important reason to enter the Hanyue Building was to observe the battles between the martial artists who had proven themselves to be Gods. The building had a spacious patio for this purpose. There was no enchantment in the patio, so the first floor was the easiest to be affected by the battles between the martial artists.
For some reason, Luo Zheng sat alone while the Evil Old Man hurriedly introduced the dishes to Luo Zheng …
"The 'Snow Lotus Arch' here is very famous. The chill it contains can seep into the dantian and nourish the inner world …"
"There is also the 'Lovers Crossing Fire Terrace'. It is also a major specialty of the Hanyue Building …"
"…"
Before Luo Zheng could sit down, the Evil Old Man had already listed out a large number of dishes in his mind.
"Aren't these dishes too expensive?" Luo Zheng asked hesitantly.
"And the Moon Wine … expensive? Of course it's expensive. The price of the Hanyue Building is sky-high. One meal can eat an entire house. Back then, I … sigh … "The Evil Old Man suddenly became depressed.
The Big Mouth Monster who was imprisoned with him in the dungeon was even better at eating than the Evil Old Man. When they were first imprisoned in the dungeon, the Big Mouth Monster, Wu Yu, would recite all kinds of delicacies in the Divine Domain every day. Even the Evil Old Man had never heard of many of them. Naturally, he was extremely greedy. There was a time when the Evil Old Man could not stand it anymore and scolded the Big Mouth Monster. However, the Big Mouth Monster did not stop talking. Later, even Shi Qiaoqiao, who was on the second floor of the dungeon, could not stand it any longer.
After much difficulty, he finally escaped from the dungeon. Now, he was only a remnant soul and did not have the fortune to enjoy such delicacies.
When the receptionist of the Hanyue Building saw Luo Zheng sitting there in a daze without ordering, she frowned slightly. A trace of disdain appeared in her eyes. These poor people had entered Six Ren Divine City to enter the Heaven Containing Palace. They had no money to spend in the Hanyue Building, so they wanted to occupy a seat and watch the battles between the Proof of Divinity cultivators. It was truly annoying.
"Young Master, if you don't want to order, you can sit over there," the receptionist said grumpily. At the same time, she pointed with her jade-like hand.
The direction she was pointing at was the other side of the courtyard. A row of chairs had been neatly arranged there, and many people were already seated on them. Looking at the attire of these cultivators, one could tell that most of them were of humble origins and could not afford to spend money in the Hanyue Building. However, they were able to sit on a chair for free and inquire about the strength of this year's batch of Proof of Divinity cultivators.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Only now did he sense the disdain in the receptionist's eyes. However, he did not get angry. Instead, he simply smiled and listed the dishes that the Evil Old Man had ordered.
As he listed out the dishes, the receptionist's astonishment became more and more intense.
It turned out that Luo Zheng had ordered the best delicacies in the Hanyue Building. Although they were not the most expensive dishes, they were definitely not to be missed.
Some Proof of Divinity cultivators had the support of their clans. After entering the Hanyue Building, they wantonly splurged. However, they did not understand the essence of the Hanyue Building at all. This Luo Zheng was extremely experienced, which naturally made the receptionist feel strange.
However, as Luo Zheng listed out the names of these delicacies, the receptionist's expression changed slightly.
He had ordered more than ten delicacies in one go. The bill would probably cost more than two hundred Shenwu Coins. Looking at this person's attire, she was afraid that Luo Zheng would not be able to pay the bill.
However, the receptionist was not afraid. The Hanyue Building was one of the Han Clan's businesses. No one could renege on a debt here. If he really could not fork out that many Shenwu Coins, the one who would suffer a loss would not be her …
Just as the receptionist left, Luo Zheng suddenly felt an aura rushing towards him. He saw someone jump down from the third floor of the restaurant and stand firmly in the middle of the courtyard. Then, he cupped his fists and said, "I am Qian Hou from the Qian Clan of Mirror City!" Immediately after, he raised a Shenwu Coin and lightly flicked it into the air.
"Swish …"
That Shenwu Coin floated into the air. It was as if there was a mysterious force holding it in the air, causing it to remain motionless.

Chapter 1765
On the fourth floor of the Hanyue Inn, a well-built Proof of Divinity expert snorted and flicked a Shenwu Coin from his hand.
The black Shenwu Coin rolled down from the second floor. At the same time, the Proof of Divinity expert shouted, "Sun Hao accepts the challenge!"
As soon as he said that, the Shenwu Coin that was falling down stopped in mid-air. It seemed to have been caught by some mysterious force.
At the same time, Sun Hao jumped down from the second floor and lightly tapped the Shenwu Coin with the tip of his foot. He actually used the Shenwu Coin as a flight of stairs and landed in the courtyard of the Hanyue Inn!
"Huff … Huff …"
The Shenwu Coin that was still in mid-air circled around for a moment before flying up into the sky. It flew past the buildings of Six Ren Divine City and finally landed in front of a stone slab. In front of the stone slab, there was a small mountain of Shenwu Coins. There were probably tens of thousands of Shenwu Coins.
"These Shenwu Coins … should have been taken away by the power of the Thousand Commandments Stone Slab, right?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind.
"Of course. After paying two Shenwu Coins, both sides can fight. However, they are still not allowed to attack others," Old Man Extreme Evil said calmly.
The delicacies that Luo Zheng had ordered had yet to be served. Now, there was a good show to watch.
Although Luo Zheng was confident that he would have no problem entering the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, Luo Zheng still needed to understand the geniuses of the Divine Dominion.
"The Sun and Qian Families are at it again …"
"They come here almost every year. Aren't these two families tired of it?"
"What do you know? The outcome of their battle is very important to these two families!"
The whispers reached Luo Zheng's ears. Luo Zheng revealed a hint of curiosity. "It sounds like the Sun and Qian Families have some grudges?"
"Hehe, the grudges between these two families have been going on for a long time. I already knew about it when I was still in the Vast Sky Region," Old Man Extreme Evil said with a smile.
As it turned out, the Vast Sky Region was controlled by three major families. Each family occupied two divine cities.
To these third-rate clans in the God Domain, the divine cities were extremely important. Most of the Shenwu Coins were obtained from these divine cities. The Sun, Qian, and Yang Clans of the Vast Sky Region each had two divine cities. However, there was still a central divine city in the Vast Sky Region called Tianmu God City.
Tianmu City had the Hundred Commandments Stone Slab. Whether it was in size or size, it was much larger than the other six divine cities. so it could earn more Shenwu Coins.
Unfortunately, Han Jia had not given them the rights to Skyveil City back then. Hence, the Sun, Qian, and Yang Clans had been entangled in the issue of Skyveil City. Sometimes, there would be conflicts, and sometimes, they would even fight.
.. and Qian Family, the Sun and Qian and this caused the conflict of the Sun and Qian's and Qian.....
In the end, the two families alerted the Han Family. They stepped in to quell the conflict, but they also set a rule. Every year before the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's entrance exam, the Sun and Qian Families would each send three God Refinement Realm cultivators to compete to determine the ownership of Tianmu City.
This way, the Sun and Qian Families naturally understood the importance of nurturing the younger generation. They poured a lot of resources into their disciples. In the eyes of these two families, entering the Heaven-Containing Prefecture was secondary. Whether or not they could obtain control of Tianmu City was the most important thing.
That was why Luo Zheng saw such a scene today …
"In other words, these two people should represent the most powerful Proof Realm cultivators of the Qian and Sun Families?" Luo Zheng asked.
"One of them, hehe," Old Man Extreme Evil replied.
As they spoke, the two people in the courtyard began to fight.
Sun Hao took a step forward and went straight for Qian Hou. In his hand was a long spear shining with golden light. The shape of the spear was a little strange. The spear shaft was curved like a snake's body, and the spearhead was embedded with a triangular golden snake head!
"Coiling Dragon Spear!"
Although everyone in the Hanyue Inn was only sparring, paying Shenwu Coins was still considered a duel. It was common for people to die if they didn't hold back.
The long spear stabbed out like a coiling poisonous snake. It actually changed directions in the air. Sun Hao's spear looked like it was going for Qian Hou's head, but with a slight twist, it actually went for Qian Hou's chest!
"Good! Good spear skills! "
"The Sun Family's Coiling Dragon Spear is known for its strangeness. It's extremely difficult to guard against …"
"Looking at Sun Hao's momentum, I'm afraid Tianmu City will still be under the control of the Sun Family!"
Hearing these comments, Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes …
When he first came to the Divine Dominion, he had seen the strength of the Divine Dominion cultivators in Meng Village.
Not to mention Little Wen and Little Wu of Meng Village, as villagers of a remote village, it was understandable that they didn't cultivate superior martial arts techniques.
However, in Luo Zheng's eyes, the cultivators of Sun Moon Stockade were extremely lame. Although they were all at the Ultimate Divine Realm, their cultivation was nothing. In terms of the exquisiteness of their martial arts techniques, they were probably not as good as the Divine Ocean Realm cultivators in the Dayan Universe.
Now that Luo Zheng had arrived in Liuren Divine City, the people he was facing were the elites of the various third-tier clans. However, when Sun Hao made his move, Luo Zheng was still rather disappointed.
Although the Coiling Dragon Spear was exquisite, in Luo Zheng's eyes, it was too flashy. How could it be considered "good" if it could be easily broken through with strength and speed?
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that the development of martial arts techniques in the various universes had actually progressed further than in the Divine Dominion. This was because the people of the Divine Dominion could cross the Third Transformation of the Divine Transformation in a short period of time and become a Proof of Divinity cultivator.
After becoming a Proof of Divinity cultivator, they would immediately step into the Divine Dao.
The time for cultivation below the True Divinity stage was very short. Naturally, no one was willing to put in the effort to study these martial arts techniques. After all, after becoming a True Divinity, they would have the higher level of "Divine Codex" to cultivate.
Sun Hao's tricky spear thrust towards Qian Hou. Qian Hou was already prepared. He suddenly flipped his body and used an extremely ingenious movement technique to dodge Sun Hao's spear thrust. At the same time, Qian Hou took advantage of the momentum to roll forward, wanting to get closer to Sun Hao.
Although it was true that the longer a weapon was, the more powerful it would be. However, the longer a weapon was, the more afraid it would be if someone got close to it. Once someone got close to it, it would be difficult to display its power.
How could Sun Hao allow Qian Hou to do this?
Sun Hao gripped the snake-shaped golden spear in his hand. With a light shake, the snake-shaped golden spear's head wound around and continuously stabbed towards Qian Hou below.
"Boom, boom, boom!"
Three consecutive spears nailed to the floor.
The material in the Divine Domain was far tougher than the material in the universe. If they were in the universe, Sun Hao's three spear thrusts would be enough to destroy three enormous mountains. However, when they were nailed to the solid floor, they were only able to pierce out three tiny holes!
Luo Zheng also noticed that the floor actually had the ability to heal. The moment those tiny holes appeared, the floor would constantly squirm, and the tiny holes would quickly disappear.
Others might be used to such details, but Luo Zheng found it to be extremely novel. In the Great Evolution Universe, there was no "floor" that could withstand the attacks of a Sector Lord. Even if there was such a material, no one would waste it and use it to create a floor …
Sun Hao's three spears didn't manage to pierce Qian Hou, and Qian Hou had already smoothly closed in on Sun Hao. A bright blue light flashed in his hand. It was an icy blue sharp short sword!
The fatal short sword turned around and drew out an azure sharp light. Following the sound of cloth tearing, Qian Hou's short sword stopped in front of Sun Hao's neck …
Luo Zheng's brows raised slightly, "This move is not bad."
Sun Hao breathed heavily as he stared deadly at Qian Hou.
If the icy blue short sword moved another inch, Sun Hao would fall here today.
"Did you let me win?" Qian Hou asked indifferently.
Sun Hao glared hatefully. In front of so many people, it wasn't good for him to act too rashly. He then clasped his fists towards the people from Hanyue Inn and left the courtyard.
Qian Hou's simple and honest face smiled towards the crowd, "Sorry for my incompetence." With that, he prepared to leave.
However, before Qian Hou could leave the courtyard, a shenwu coin was thrown down from above. It bounced a few times on the ground, and an indifferent voice sounded, "Qian Hou, I challenge you. How about it?"
As the voice sounded, the shenwu coin on the ground suddenly jumped up and floated in mid air. When the voice issued the challenge, the Thousand Commandments Stone Slab's power came into effect, causing the shenwu coin to float in mid air.
"I, Qian Hou, still have matters to attend to. I'll pass this time."
Qian Hou only wanted to challenge Sun Hao. He didn't accept anyone else's challenge. With that, he turned and left the courtyard.
Luo Zheng looked indifferently at the shenwu coin floating in mid air.
He didn't intend to fight. Even if he won, he would lose a shenwu coin. Such a losing deal was meaningless to him.
However, the following scene caused Luo Zheng's eyes to light up.

Chapter 1766
These Shenwu Coins were neither gold nor wood. They made thumping sounds as they hit the floor.
They fell from the fourth floor and scattered all over the floor. There were about three to four hundred of them.
When the Proof of God martial artists in the Hanyue Inn saw this, their breathing quickened. This was especially so for those on the first and second floors. There were even a few True Gods who were tempted.
Four hundred Shenwu Coins was enough to buy two houses in Six Ren Divine City. To many Lower True Gods, this was a huge sum of money.
Although the martial artists in the Hanyue Inn were envious, no one dared to enter the hall to pick up the coins. Anyone who dared to throw out Shenwu Coins like this must have a powerful background.
"Hu …"
On the fourth floor, a figure shot down and stood silently on the floor.
In the Divine Dominion, those below the Upper True God level were unable to fly. The person on the fourth floor used an ingenious technique to disperse the force of the impact. He was like a nimble cat.
That person was wearing a purple robe and a purple gold crown. He had fair skin and red lips. He looked like a young man of about sixteen or seventeen years old and was as handsome as a young girl.
"Is no one going to challenge me? If you win, all of these will belong to you, "the young man said nonchalantly.
This young man had thrown out a Shenwu Coin to challenge Qian Hou, but Qian Hou had rejected him. Hence, he had thrown out a pile of Shenwu Coins to bet on the battle.
The Shenwu Coins on the floor were tempting. Many Proof of God martial artists' mouths twitched as they shook their heads. No one dared to challenge him!
"The young master of the Hulan Family …"
"That violent young master? Challenging him is courting death! "
"This young man is extremely strong and doesn't hold back at all. Breaking an arm or a leg is a small matter. He might even lose his life!"
Although Shenwu Coins were extremely tempting, one's life was obviously more important.
The Hulan Family was one of the top three families in the thirty-six regions under the Han Family's rule. It was also one of the few second-tier families in the thirty-six regions. It had a close relationship with the Han Family and many of its members were assisting the Han Family on the floating island.
This young master's real name was Hulan Zhuo. He was extremely talented and strong. He had just turned fifteen this year and was already a Proof of God martial artist! Huolan Zhuo had an appearance that was even more exquisite than a woman's, but his attacks were extremely cruel and ruthless.
He had already entered the Six Ren Divine City a month ago and fought with others. He had already crippled three Proof Divinity Martial Artists and even killed another Proof Divinity Martial Artist!
The deceased Testimonial Martial Artist was also an elite from another third-tier family. When he found out about this, he naturally went to the Hulan Family to reason with them. In the end, the Hulan Family only paid compensation.
With his misdeeds in front of him, the Shenwu coins on the ground didn't look as good anymore.
For a moment, the restless atmosphere in Hanyue Tower also quieted down …
After a short while, a burly man walked out from the right corner of the first floor. This burly man was eight feet tall and was as sturdy as an iron tower. The most eye-catching part was his right hand, which was covered in a layer of brown scales. It seemed to be the scales of some kind of fierce beast.
"I'll challenge you," the burly man said with a grin.
The Proof Divinity Martial Artists from second-tier and third-tier families were usually on the third floor. The elite disciples of these families might not care about a few hundred Shenwu coins.
However, it was different for the grassroots Proof Divinity Martial Artists on the first and second floor. If they won these Shenwu coins, it was very likely that their destinies would change. It was worth the risk. In addition, some grassroots Proof Divinity Martial Artists had never heard of the person "Huolan Zhuo," so they were naturally less fearful.
When Huolan Zhuo saw that someone had finally come to challenge him, the corners of his mouth curled up slightly and revealed a charming smile.
"He's so good-looking. This child from the Hulan Family is even prettier than a woman," a woman sighed softly.
As the two Shenwu coins shot into the air and flew out of Hanyue Tower, the burly man leaned forward without saying anything and charged at Huolan Zhuo like a ferocious beast.
While he was running, the burly man suddenly stretched out his right hand. The brown scales on his right hand started to flicker with a faint luster. There were indistinct colors flowing within them …
"It's a poisonous hand!"
"This person cultivates the Poison Dao. Pay attention to his right hand!"
"Those who dare to challenge Huolan Zhuo must have some skills after all …"
The burly man rushed forward and suddenly grabbed at Huolan Zhuo with his right hand. A fishy wind blew from his palm. Each of his fingers was as thick as an arm. How was this a hand? It was clearly the giant claw of a ferocious creature!
Huolan Zhuo stood still in the field. The moment he saw the giant claw coming at him, Huolan Zhuo suddenly stretched out his finger and pointed at the center of the giant claw.
"Dong."
Huolan Zhuo's finger pointed at the center of the giant claw. Just his index finger was enough to block the giant claw's momentum. At the same time, the crowd heard a buzzing sound as if some kind of subtle metal was vibrating.
Immediately after, circles of thin black ripples appeared around the burly man's arm. These ripples were very small and could only be detected by those with keen eyesight.
The moment these ripples appeared, they began to tighten rapidly!
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
Immediately, the crowd heard a series of shattering sounds.
The burly man's right hand was cut off neatly. It broke into seven or eight pieces and fell to the ground in pieces …
"Ah!"
At this moment, the burly man let out a loud roar. He covered his severed right arm and retreated seven or eight steps.
Normally, a fight would end at this point. Out of courtesy and respect, they would give the other party some time to catch his breath. After all, this burly man did not have a life-and-death feud with Huolan Zhuo.
However, Huolan Zhuo did not stop at all. While the burly man was retreating, he was still very close to him. His finger was about to hit the burly man's head. If it hit, the burly man's head would be cut into pieces …
At the thought of this miserable outcome, the burly man finally understood why no one had dared to challenge Huolan Zhuo earlier.
His face turned ghastly pale. In an emergency, he was quick-witted. Without any hesitation, he rolled sideways on the ground. With a sudden kick of his legs, he flew forward horizontally and crashed out of the courtyard.
There were still people eating outside the courtyard. When the burly man crashed into the courtyard, he naturally smashed several tables. However, he still managed to save his life.
Huolan Zhuo looked regretful that he had failed to take the burly man's life. He raised an index finger and gently swayed it. Faint black ripples surrounded his index finger. It seemed that some kind of sharp weapon was attached to his finger.
"What a ruthless fellow …"
Luo Zheng was already sitting upright in his seat. His eyes drifted indifferently in that direction.
This fellow did not have any scruples when making a move. He just wanted to kill. Under such circumstances, it was too much.
The Shenwu Coins in the courtyard were obviously tempting to Luo Zheng. However, he still did not intend to make a move …
At this moment, the girl from Hanyue Restaurant led a few servants to Luo Zheng with plates of exquisite dishes. The servants smoothly placed the dishes on Luo Zheng's table.
Originally, everyone's attention was on the young master of the Huolan Family. Coincidentally, Huolan Zhuo's seat was not far from Luo Zheng's. Moreover, on the first floor of Hanyue Restaurant, many Proving God martial artists would only spend a few Shenwu Coins to order one or two side dishes in order to get a table. They would not be as extravagant as Luo Zheng and order a table full of delicacies.
Even on the third and fourth floor, there were not many people like Luo Zheng.
Therefore, Luo Zheng immediately attracted the attention of everyone in Hanyue Restaurant, including Huolan Zhuo.
Everyone sized up Luo Zheng. Looking at Luo Zheng's clothes and face, they felt that he was not familiar with them. Moreover, he looked like a grassroots martial artist. How could he afford to spend such a table full of delicacies?
"Young Master, all the dishes you ordered have been served. Please enjoy your meal." The girl bowed to Luo Zheng and left with the servants. She had the same thought as everyone else. She wanted to see how this fellow would settle the bill later! If he could not take out so many Shenwu Coins, the True God Realm fighters in Hanyue Restaurant would not be easy to deal with …
Luo Zheng did not mind these gazes. He nonchalantly took a pair of chopsticks and poured himself a cup of wine. Then, he gently tapped the wine cup with his finger. A wisp of the Law of Fire dissipated.
"Hua …"
The wine in the cup was ignited, and a pale green flame rose.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1767
Blue flames soared and condensed into the shape of a new moon. It was very exquisite.
This was the famous wine of Liuren Divine City, the Moon Containing Wine. Just a small pot cost 20 Shenwu Coins.
Luo Zheng felt that the price was ridiculously high, but the Evil Old Man strongly recommended it and told Luo Zheng, "The most important thing is this pot of wine!"
When the crowd saw Luo Zheng ignite the wine, they were also curious. Most of them focused their attention on Luo Zheng. Even Hulan Zhuo's round eyes were fixed on Luo Zheng, wondering what Luo Zheng was up to.
Then, Luo Zheng's right hand reached for the first dish.
On the dish, snow lotus petals were stacked on top of each other, forming a small arch bridge. It was the famous dish of Hanyue Restaurant, the Snow Lotus Arch.
The snow lotus was harvested from the Saint Lotus Mountain in the Snow Region. It was a natural cold object and was still emitting dense cold air.
In the God Domain, even ordinary God Dwellers could swallow such a cold object. If it were the creatures in the universe below the Life and Death Realm, they wouldn't even be able to come within three feet of the table, let alone swallow it. It was no different from courting death.
When Luo Zheng's chopsticks picked up a snow lotus petal, a layer of frost appeared on the chopsticks.
Then, Luo Zheng placed the snow lotus petal on top of the wine cup and roasted it. The crescent-like flame curled around the snow lotus petal.
When an object of extreme cold came into contact with fire, it would have an extreme reaction. The snow lotus petal melted under the fire and emitted a strange stench.
"It stinks!"
"What is this brat burning?!"
"It stinks unbearably! Who is going to stop this brat … "
The stench permeated the air and rushed into the noses of others. Immediately, many people covered their noses and frowned, looking at Luo Zheng with disgust.
Luo Zheng turned a deaf ear to those voices. He was focused on listening to the Evil Old Man's instructions.
"The Moon Containing Wine is brewed from the 'Lunar Sea Jade Pillar'. The Sea Marrow smells very fragrant, but after burning, the stench is unbearable. After fusing with the Snow Lotus, the stench will be even worse …" Then, mix the Earth Crystal Tendons in the Crossing Fire Mandarin Duck Terrace, and then refine it with True Fire. Crush the Charming Eye Branch into powder and sprinkle it on … "
Luo Zheng's reaction was very quick. He did whatever the Evil Old Man said.
Amidst this foul stench, the onlookers were dumbfounded. How was this kid eating? He was clearly refining a golden pill!
Luo Zheng's hands didn't stop moving. He had the same thought as the others. This seemed to be alchemy. It was just that one alchemy furnace was missing.
He also felt rather depressed in his heart. Why did it take so much effort to eat!
Following the Evil Old Man's instructions, Luo Zheng reached out and pushed gently, once again congealing the True Fire.
After a short while, a crisp "ding" sound rang out, and a golden core really did fall out from the burning ingredients … And the moment this golden core appeared, a strange fragrance once again filled the air, and it quickly spread throughout the entire Boundless Moon Inn!
"He's really concocting pills …"
"It smells so good!"
"I've never smelled this kind of pill fragrance before … It doesn't smell like medicine at all. Instead, it smells like vegetables …"
Luo Zheng stared at this golden core with a surprised expression. He then said to the Evil Old Man, "So you want me to refine this golden core …"
"What a joke! Is it so easy to refine a golden core? This is just a food pill. It can only satisfy one's appetite, "the Evil Old Man said with a proud smile.
The fragrance of this food pill continued to spread, and it became stronger and stronger. The smell seemed to seep into one's soul, causing many cultivators present to gulp. They couldn't help but have a strong appetite.
Luo Zheng was no exception. He couldn't help but grab the food pill and put it into his mouth.
The extremely complex taste spread rapidly on the tip of Luo Zheng's tongue, causing Luo Zheng's soul to tremble. He couldn't describe the taste at all. He could only lower his head and taste it carefully. The moment he tasted it, he would remember the taste for the rest of his life. It would be unforgettable.
He finally understood why the Evil Old Man kept telling him to come to the Hanyue Inn after he entered Six Ren Divine City.
It turned out that the True Gods in the divine territory had endless lifespans. They had a lot of time to study all kinds of things. They could study the Divine Way to the extreme, and they could also study food to the extreme. This gave Luo Zheng a new understanding of the divine territory's cultural heritage.
On the top floor of the Hanyue Inn, Han Wenshan and Han Jiaming looked down at Luo Zheng with strange expressions.
"This person's bone age is less than 100 years old, but he's experienced and knowledgeable. He can even refine the Great Vegetarian Pill!" Han Wenshan said in surprise.
"I don't know which senior or expert he got the secret recipe from. It's interesting. Some strange people appeared in my Han Family's examination this year," Han Jiaming said with a smile.
The two brothers had represented the Han Family in managing the Hanyue Inn for three Divine Eras. They had heard of the Great Vegetarian Pill before, but they had never seen it with their own eyes. Now that they saw it in the hands of a cultivator who had proven himself to be a god, they were naturally confused.
"Should we bring this fellow up here for questioning?" Han Wenshan asked.
"No, this person will definitely take part in the Han Family's examination. We'll know whether he's a dragon or a phoenix by then." Han Jiaming shook his head.
Although the Great Vegetarian Pill was miraculous, it could only satisfy one's desire for good food. In some people's eyes, it was still not the right way to go …
…
…
Everyone in the Hanyue Inn focused their attention on Luo Zheng. They had all sorts of guesses about Luo Zheng's identity.
Meanwhile, Hulan Zhuo stood alone in the middle of the courtyard …
Hulan Zhuo had stood out in such a high-profile manner to establish his authority.
The Heaven-containing Prefecture's examination was not easy. This was because the Heaven-containing Prefecture did not only test one's strength, but also many other aspects. There were many variables, and it was not certain that one could pass just because they were strong.
After Hulan Zhuo had intimidated these cultivators, everyone was afraid of him during the examination. Naturally, they did not dare to act recklessly.
However, he had just won a round, and everyone had suddenly shifted their focus to the delicacies of the Hanyue Inn. Wasn't this somewhat strange?
Feeling depressed, Hulan Zhuo suddenly pointed at Luo Zheng and shouted, "Hey! You! "
Luo Zheng was still savoring the aftertaste of the Great Vegetarian Pill. When he saw Hulan Zhuo pointing at him and shouting at him, he frowned and asked, "What's the matter?"
"How about I challenge you?" Hulan Zhuo raised the corner of his mouth and said, "If you win, these are all yours!" As he spoke, he pointed at the Shenwu Coins at his feet.
Luo Zheng followed Hulan Zhuo's gaze and looked at him. He smiled faintly and then shook his head.
"What do you mean? You don't dare? " Hulan Zhuo continued to provoke him. He was a little unhappy. If Luo Zheng did not agree to the challenge, there was nothing he could do to Luo Zheng. Even though he was a member of the Hulan Family, he could not casually make a move in Liuren Divine City. The consequences of violating the rules of the Thousand Commandments Slate were severe.
Luo Zheng replied, "It's not that I don't dare. It's just that it's not enough."
"You mean the Shenwu Coins are not enough?" Hulan Zhuo's eyes lit up slightly.
Luo Zheng nodded and did not say anything.
Unexpectedly, Hulan Zhuo waved his hand and scattered another handful of Shenwu Coins on the ground. There were another 200 to 300 Shenwu Coins in this handful.
"The Hulan Family is really rich! Hulan Zhuo is just a Proving God expert, yet he has so many Shenwu Coins with him! "
"This world is so unfair. Many True Gods can't earn so much even if they work hard for a few Divine Epochs …"
"I think that brat is just making excuses. No matter how many Shenwu Coins Hulan Zhuo has, would he dare to accept the challenge?"
In the eyes of the crowd, Luo Zheng was no match for Hulan Zhuo.
However, Luo Zheng did not want to lose face when Hulan Zhuo challenged him. Therefore, he said that Hulan Zhuo did not have enough Shenwu Coins.
Now that Hulan Zhuo had scattered more Shenwu Coins, there were as many as 600 to 700 Shenwu Coins in the courtyard!
Everyone's gaze gathered on Luo Zheng again, waiting for his reaction.
However, Luo Zheng still shook his head and said, "Not enough."
Huolan Zhuo was not annoyed at all. He patted his waist, and with a flash of golden light, a dazzling golden bracelet appeared in his hand. As he shook the golden bracelet continuously, Shenwu coins fell from it again and again.
Another 300 Shenwu Coins!
In just a short while, there were as many as 1,000 Shenwu Coins in the courtyard.
Everyone's gaze was once again drawn back to the Shenwu Coins!
1,000 Shenwu Coins …
Many Proving God experts were tempted.
Many intermediate True Gods were probably only worth this much.
Some powerful family elites also had the urge to give it a try. However, Hulan Zhuo was challenging Luo Zheng, not them.
"It's still not enough." Luo Zheng continued to shake his head. At the same time, he poured himself a cup of wine.
This time, Hulan Zhuo was finally annoyed. He stared at Luo Zheng and asked coldly, "How much is enough?"
Upon hearing this, Luo Zheng finally smiled. This was what he had been waiting for.
He knew that there were not only fights in the Hanyue Building but also gambling fights.
However, he also understood that he only had one chance to fight. Once he won, no idiot would be foolish enough to give him Shenwu Coins for free.
Seizing this opportunity to earn as many Shenwu Coins as possible was Luo Zheng's real goal.
Previously, Luo Zheng was too lazy to challenge Hulan Zhuo because he felt that he did not have enough Shenwu Coins. 200 Shenwu Coins were not enough. One thousand Shenwu Coins were still not enough. He needed to wait for a suitable opportunity.
He did not expect this brat from the Hulan Family to be so hot-tempered that this opportunity presented itself in advance.
"This much." Luo Zheng extended five fingers and said nonchalantly.
"5,000 Shenwu Coins?" Hulan Zhuo frowned.
Luo Zheng's lips curled up slightly. "Yes, this is the amount!"
"5,000 …"
"That brat is really demanding an exorbitant price!"
"This is not an exorbitant price. He purposely said such a large number to put Hulan Zhuo in a difficult position. I'm afraid that Hulan Zhuo will have no choice but to give up challenging Luo Zheng."
"It's a pity that he has underestimated the financial resources of the Hulan Family. Although 5,000 Shenwu Coins is a huge sum, it is not a big problem for the Hulan Family …"
As expected, Hulan Zhuo pondered for a while and smiled coldly at Luo Zheng. "5,000 Shenwu Coins it is!"

Chapter 1768
As soon as Hulan Zhuo finished speaking, a person stuck his head out from the fourth floor of the Hanyue Building.
This person's head was shiny and had a strange pattern on it. His eyes were as cold as ice. He was the "Uncle Lian" that Hulan Zhuo had mentioned.
"Little Master." Uncle Lian cupped his hands at Hulan Zhuo across the guardrail.
"Money," Hulan Zhuo said faintly.
"Little Master …" Uncle Lian hesitated. At the same time, he stared at Luo Zheng faintly.
Uncle Lian was a Peak Lower True God. His abilities were much stronger than ordinary True Gods.
It was not that he did not believe in Hulan Zhuo's abilities, but it was unwise to take out five thousand Shenwu Coins at once. After all, five thousand Shenwu Coins was a large sum of money to many families.
Moreover, he had been secretly observing Luo Zheng. He could faintly sense that Luo Zheng was not an ordinary Proving God warrior. Although Luo Zheng was extremely reserved, he could still smell the indistinct soul aura of Luo Zheng. The aura was like invisible sharp swords floating around him. It gave Uncle Lian a vague but powerful feeling.
"What are you trying to say?" Hulan Zhuo looked at Uncle Lian and asked.
Uncle Lian frowned …
Although he was a Peak Lower True God, he was only a guard of the Hulan Family.
He had been guarding the Little Master for many years. He knew the Little Master's temper better than anyone else. In the current situation, the Little Master was certain that the warrior did not dare to accept the challenge. That was why he had demanded an exorbitant price. Moreover, the Little Master was very concerned about his reputation. Since he had already taken out a thousand Shenwu Coins, he could not give up halfway under the watchful eyes of so many people!
If he made the Little Master unhappy, he would have a hard time.
In the end, Uncle Lian could only clench his teeth. With a light pat, a small golden silk pouch appeared in his hand. He tossed the golden silk pouch down from the sky.
Hulan Zhuo caught the golden silk pouch and turned to smile at Luo Zheng. With a light twist of his finger, he opened the pouch.
"Pa pa pa pa …"
These Shenwu Coins were not made of metal. Instead, they seemed to be made of some kind of wood. They made a crisp sound when they were sprinkled on the ground.
When Hulan Zhuo scattered the Shenwu coins in the brocade pouch on the ground, there were a total of 5,000 Shenwu coins under his feet. The black coins piled up like a small hill.
When the surrounding Proof of Divinity martial artists saw the Shenwu Coins, they were all dazzled.
"Five thousand Shenwu coins …"
"For many third-tier clans, this is a huge sum of money!"
"He's confident in his own strength. So what? Let's see how that brat gets off the stage today! "
Seeing Hulan Zhuo's sincerity, the onlookers naturally focused their attention on Luo Zheng. Just now, Luo Zheng had asked for 5,000 Shenwu coins. Now that Hulan Zhuo had taken out the money, they wondered what excuse Luo Zheng would come up with.
Of course, they didn't know that before Luo Zheng opened his mouth, he had already thought about all the possible consequences.
First, he asked the Evil Old Man about Hulan Zhuo's clan. The Hulan Family was a second-tier clan in the 36 regions. To ordinary True Gods, they were a huge force. Moreover, they had an excellent relationship with the Han Family.
However, even the Hulan Family couldn't do as they pleased in Six Ren Divine City. Even if he offended this clan, Luo Zheng would still be safe in Six Ren Divine City.
Moreover, the Han Family had always been strict with the clans under their control. After Luo Zheng entered the Han Tian Prefecture, the Hulan Family wouldn't be able to do anything to Luo Zheng.
Moreover, although 5,000 Shenwu Coins wasn't a small sum, to the Hulan Family, which controlled two central divine cities and six ordinary divine cities, it was just a little painful.
After seeing the risks involved, Luo Zheng offered the price of 5,000 Shenwu Coins.
If he offered a price that was too high, Hulan Zhuo might not be able to take it out. After thinking about it, 5,000 Shenwu Coins was the most suitable price.
"Here are 5,000 Shenwu Coins!" Hulan Zhuo stared intently at Luo Zheng and sneered. "I believe that you won't dare to trick me. Moreover, you should know that the consequences of doing so are very serious!"
Hulan Zhuo actually wanted to hear what other excuses Luo Zheng could come up with …
Everyone had the same thought. They wanted to see if Luo Zheng dared to fight …
Luo Zheng slowly walked away from the table. Just as he started to walk away, the lady who served Luo Zheng in Hanyue Restaurant rushed over and stopped him. "Wait a minute!"
"What?" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback.
"Pay the bill first," she said stubbornly. She was afraid that if Luo Zheng died at the hands of Hulan Zhuo, no one would pay the bill for Hanyue Restaurant.
Looking at the stubborn look in the lady's eyes, Luo Zheng smiled helplessly and said to Hulan Zhuo, "Sorry, please wait a minute." With that, he took out some Shenwu Coins and paid for the meal.
After the lady left, a Shenwu Coin appeared in Luo Zheng's hand. He smiled at Hulan Zhuo and said, "Since you've lost, you can't go back on your word."
"You're so long-winded. Who do you think I am? Why would I go back on my word?" Hulan Zhuo said coldly.
Luo Zheng smiled and gently tossed the Shenwu Coin. Then, he said, "I accept your challenge!"
"Buzz …"
As soon as he finished speaking, the Shenwu Coin stopped in the air. Then, it flew up into the sky together with the Shenwu Coin that Hulan Zhuo had tossed out earlier …
In this way, the restriction of the Thousand Commandments Slate in Liuren Divine City was temporarily lifted.
The moment the Shenwu Coin flew away …
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows abruptly and a golden light flashed in his eyes!
A wisp of extremely powerful Soul Suppression shot toward Hulan Zhuo!
At the same time, Luo Zheng dashed toward Hulan Zhuo like an arrow released from a bow!
"Oh no!" Seeing this, Uncle Lian, who was on the fourth floor, suddenly changed his expression. Although Luo Zheng had only released a very small wisp of Soul Suppression, Uncle Lian could still sense the terror of this Soul Suppression!
Hulan Zhuo had never expected Luo Zheng to be so ferocious. Luo Zheng had charged at him with a thunderous momentum. His reaction was not slow, but when he was about to take action, he felt a sharp pain in his head. His soul had been penetrated by a small invisible arrow.
The pain was unbearable. However, before Hulan Zhuo could cry out, Luo Zheng had already crashed into him.
This collision was like a mountain in the divine territory smashing onto Huolan Zhuo's body. Huolan Zhuo opened his mouth, wanting to cry out in pain. However, due to Luo Zheng's collision, he only let out a muffled groan and flew backward like a kite with a broken string.
"Crash … Kacha …"
Hulan Zhuo flew backward, smashing many tables and chairs along the way. Even some Testimonies of the Divinity Realm warriors were unlucky enough to be injured.
For a moment, the entire first floor of the Hanyue Building was in chaos …
Hulan Zhuo lay motionless on the ground. No one knew whether he was dead or alive. The Testimonies of the Divinity Realm warriors who were implicated screamed miserably and looked at Luo Zheng in horror.
The Hanyue Building was dead silent.
They were already surprised when Luo Zheng agreed to Hulan Zhuo's challenge so readily.
However, most of them still thought that Luo Zheng was just trying to be brave and that the final result would be miserable. According to the character of the Hulan Family's young master, Luo Zheng was very likely to die.
They had never expected that the fight between the two would end in such a way!
It was only one move …
The girl in the Hanyue Building covered her mouth with her small hands and stared blankly at the young man not far away …
Although she was only a Divine People, she had been working in the Hanyue Building for many years and had seen many experts fight with each other. However, it was rare for someone to defeat an opponent with one move like Luo Zheng.
It would only be possible if there was a huge gap in strength and cultivation between two warriors. However, Luo Zheng was only a Testimonies of the Divinity Realm warrior while Hulan Zhuo was the young master of the Hulan Family!
Luo Zheng smiled and took out the Boundless Sword from his waist. He pulled out the scabbard and started to collect the Shenwu Coins on the ground.
After entering the Divine Domain, Luo Zheng's Sumeru Ring was scrapped. However, the Boundless Sword and the scabbard of this sword were both made from materials from the Divine Domain. The scabbard was originally a Sumeru Space. Therefore, this scabbard became Luo Zheng's temporary Sumeru Ring. Some important items such as the Soul-Searching Flag were also temporarily stored in it.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to take away the Shenwu Coins, "Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!" A few more people jumped down from the fourth floor of the Hanyue Building. The one leading them was the Hulan Family's "Uncle Lian".
Uncle Lian stared at Luo Zheng indifferently and said, "Do you dare to take these Shenwu Coins? “
Luo Zheng's bent body straightened up again. He smiled and asked, "Why wouldn't I dare?"
"Are you not afraid of our Hulan Family?" Uncle Lian saw Luo Zheng's strength and had some misgivings about Luo Zheng's identity.
Uncle Lian's words were testing the waters. He wanted to probe Luo Zheng's background and family background. At the same time, he was threatening Luo Zheng.
How could Luo Zheng not understand?

Chapter 1769
Luo Zheng's rhetorical question stunned Uncle Lian.
Uncle Lian stared at Luo Zheng and asked coldly, "Which family are you from?"
Luo Zheng smiled and replied, "Why should I tell you?"
Uncle Lian was a swift and decisive person. Luo Zheng had answered him with three "Why?"
He understood that threats were useless against this lad. This lad's background was not simple.
As a guard of the Hulan Clan, Uncle Lian was a decisive person. Since the Young Master had lost 5,000 Shenwu Coins, he would not continue to argue with him in front of so many people. After all, the elders of other clans were watching from the fifth and sixth floors of the Hanyue Tower.
Uncle Lian walked to the other side without looking back. The two servants of the Hulan Family had already helped Hulan Zhuo up.
Uncle Lian stretched out his hand and touched Hulan Zhuo's forehead gently. A blue ray of light shone from his glabella. Uncle Lian was relieved.
After being hit by Luo Zheng, many of Hulan Zhuo's bones were broken. However, injuries to the physical body were the easiest to heal. It would only take two or three days for him to fully recover. It would not affect Hulan Zhuo's participation in the examination of the Heavenly Prefecture. However, Uncle Lian was worried about his soul. Even Uncle Lian did not dare to underestimate the soul pressure that Luo Zheng had released in that instant.
Fortunately, Hulan Zhuo's soul was only pierced with a small hole and did not suffer any serious injuries. It seemed that the lad had shown mercy. However, Uncle Lian was still amazed. He could not imagine how a Proof of Divinity martial artist could cultivate his soul to such a level!
After collecting the Shenwu Coins on the ground, Luo Zheng cupped his hands at Uncle Lian and the others. Then, he walked out of the Hanyue Building as if there was no one around.
After Luo Zheng had left, whispers broke out in the Hanyue Building.
"Who on earth is that lad?!"
"He knows how to use the dishes in the Hanyue Building to refine food pills. He should be from Liuren Divine City, right?"
"That's not the point. The question is how can he be so powerful?! He defeated Hulan Zhuo with only one move? I think he must have shown mercy. Otherwise, it would have been hard for Huolan Zhuo to survive … "
What these people did not know was that Luo Zheng's current strength had far surpassed the Proof of Divinity martial artist realm.
Not to mention that Hu Lanzhuo was just an elite disciple of a second-tier family.
Even among the Proof of Divinity cultivators nurtured by the Floating Island's wealthy families, it would be difficult for anyone to match up to Luo Zheng …
It was only because Luo Zheng's inner world was special that he had yet to condense a godhead. Once his godhead was successfully condensed, Luo Zheng's strength would once again take a qualitative leap. At that time, ordinary lower level True Gods would be far from his match!
On the fifth floor, in a unique private room …
An old man stretched out his hands and waved them slowly. There was a stream of True Fire slowly rotating between his hands. The True Fire seemed to be wrapped around something and was constantly burning. There was a faint but unique fragrance coming from it.
"Dingdang!"
At this moment, a pale yellow pill fell from the true fire and slowly rolled on the golden plate, emitting a buzzing sound.
After the pill appeared, a strange and unique fragrance quickly lingered in the room.
Then, the old man gently pushed the plate to the other side of the table. "Miss, please enjoy."
On the other side of the table, a little girl dressed in green smiled sweetly at the old man. "It smells so good. Grandpa Hu, thank you for your hard work!"
After saying that, the little girl could not wait to put the pill into her cherry mouth. An intoxicated expression immediately appeared on her chubby face …
The food pill that the old man had refined was the same as the one that Luo Zheng had refined. It was also the Grand Vegetarian Food Pill.
There were very few people who knew the refining method of the Grand Vegetarian Food Pill. Most of them were old True Gods, and this' Grandpa Hu 'was one of them. After the little girl finished eating, Grandpa Hu said in a hoarse voice, "He is so young, yet he knows the refining method of the Grand Vegetarian Food Pill. His soul and strength are exceptionally strong. I don't know what kind of Divine Path he has cultivated, but he defeated Hu Lanzhuo with one move. This seems a little too strong …"
"He didn't defeat him." The little girl in green shook her head and said seriously, "He killed him with one move."
Luo Zheng indeed had the ability to kill Hu Lanzhuo with one move. However, he had shown mercy at the last moment. Anyone with a discerning eye could tell at a glance.
"Yes, he was able to kill him with one move. This person's background is definitely not simple. Miss, we need to investigate his background," Grandpa Hu said faintly.
The little girl in green smiled slightly. "It's necessary to investigate. I think this guy must be very interesting in the past!"
"Miss, you are not like that kind of person. Don't be love-struck." Grandpa Hu's beard curled up.
The little girl in green frowned and rebuked, "Grandpa Hu, what are you talking about? Who's love-struck?"
Grandpa Hu was not afraid. He only laughed and said, "Don't I know your character?"
The little girl in green just stared at him and did not speak anymore.
Other than this private room, some of the private rooms on the upper floor of the Hanyue Building were discussing Luo Zheng's identity and background.
However, Luo Zheng had transmigrated from Mirror City and did not report his background. No matter how much they discussed, it was difficult for them to investigate Luo Zheng's true identity. Thinking that Luo Zheng would eventually participate in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination, they believed that they would find out on that day.
…
…
After Luo Zheng left the Hanyue Building, he leisurely walked through the streets of Liuren Divine City.
Although he had spent a lot of money on this trip to the Hanyue Building, Luo Zheng did not expect to earn 5,000 Shenwu Coins in one go.
He could only blame that young master of the Hu Lanzhuo Clan for being too impulsive and allowing him to take advantage of him …
Unfortunately, this was a one-time deal. It would be difficult for him to encounter such a good deal in the future.
There were six to seven days left before the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination. During this time, Luo Zheng wanted to collect some materials in Liuren Divine City.
Old Man Extreme Evil had already imparted the disguise technique to Luo Zheng.
His disguise technique was very unique. It wasn't just a simple human skin mask or a strange face.
This disguise technique was actually a cultivation technique. Moreover, it was a body tempering technique. However, this body tempering technique was not used to temper one's strength or body. Instead, it was used to temper the muscles on one's face. Under this constant tempering, every muscle on Luo Zheng's face would move and achieve the goal of changing one's appearance.
This way, others would not feel that Luo Zheng was out of place when they saw him. Some people would even be able to recognize Luo Zheng based on their memories from a few days ago. They would only feel that Luo Zheng was a little unfamiliar.
However, when the muscles were tempered to a certain extent, Luo Zheng's face would constantly change. This way, even if Luo Zheng went to the Han Clan's floating island, the two Han Clan sisters and the Han Clan members who saw Luo Zheng in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement would not be able to recognize him.
Other than practicing the disguise technique, Luo Zheng also needed to expand the world in his body and revise his own martial techniques.
After cultivating to the World Lord realm in the universe, many of the martial arts techniques that one had cultivated in the past could no longer be used. But it was different in the Divine Domain. Because the characteristics of matter in the Divine Domain had changed, there was no way to fly in the air. When martial artists fought in close combat, the more basic martial arts techniques would be of great use.
However, Luo Zheng still had to be careful when using the Chaotic Energy. He did not even dare to use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao casually. The Emotion Severing Divine Dao was listed as a forbidden dao in the Divine Dominion. If he used it and violated the taboo, he would be in big trouble.
Luo Zheng would not use these trump cards at the current stage. However, he was still full of confidence.
Just like Old Man Extreme Evil had analyzed, the Hulan Clan was one of the three great clans under the Han Clan. Their strength and foundation were quite impressive. Hulan Zhuo, who had been nurtured by them, was one of the best.
However, Hulan Zhuo was unable to retaliate in front of Luo Zheng. In that case, the disciples of the other clans were even less of a threat.
Although there might be a few extremely strong competitors, there were at least ten spots in the Divine Dominion. No matter how weak Luo Zheng was, it was impossible for him to fall out of the top ten. Entering the Divine Dominion was something that he was determined to achieve.
Before the Divine Dominion's recruitment, many clans would order people to constantly gather information in Six Ren Divine City. They would have a clear understanding of their competitors. Therefore, every clan had a large list in their hands. The list contained detailed information about their potential competitors.
There was a group of people who specialized in collecting information and selling it to the various clans and sects. It was their business.
This included the martial artists' characteristics, strength, specialties … Even the Divine Dao and cultivation techniques that they cultivated were recorded in the list.
After this battle, Luo Zheng was also gloriously on the list. He was ranked fourth.
However, Luo Zheng's information was completely different from the others.
In Luo Zheng's information, his name was unknown, his age was unknown, and his place of birth was unknown.
The only thing that was noted was Luo Zheng's strength. The battle between him and Hulan Zhuo was briefly described. He defeated Hulan Zhuo in one move with the momentum of a thunderbolt!
