Chapter 1770
For the past few days, no matter where Luo Zheng went, there would always be people following him sneakily.
Although these people hid very well, they couldn't escape Luo Zheng's detection. In any case, Six Ren Divine City was absolutely safe, so Luo Zheng didn't bother to argue with them.
Luo Zheng still lacked some necessities, such as a spatial ring to store items.
Although Luo Zheng temporarily used the scabbard as a substitute, it wasn't very convenient. Now that Luo Zheng had earned five thousand Shenwu Coins, he could naturally go on a shopping spree.
Under Old Man Evil's guidance, Luo Zheng traversed more than half of Six Ren Divine City and stood in front of a small alley …
"Alley of Gold Coins? You're talking about this place? "
Standing in front of the alley, Luo Zheng had a strange expression on his face.
This small alley only allowed one person to pass through at a time. From the outside, it looked extremely simple and crude …
"Hehe, of course it's here. Six Ren Divine City's Alley of Gold Coins is very famous. As long as you have Shenwu Coins, you can buy any strange item there," Old Man Evil said with a smile.
"But why is everyone squeezed into this small alley?" Luo Zheng was puzzled.
"Move aside!"
At that moment, a thunderous voice suddenly sounded behind Luo Zheng. The voice was very rough, like stones rubbing against each other.
Luo Zheng nimbly dodged to the side. When he turned around, he saw a group of six or seven people walking toward Alley of Gold Coins.
The one who spoke was a fatty in the lead. His body was as wide as his height, like a round ball.
"Middle True God!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
After coming out of the Great Evolution Universe, Luo Zheng had seen many True Gods. However, most of them were lower True Gods. As for middle True Gods, there were only Old Man Evil, Big Mouth Monster, Yin Yuanzi, and a few others.
Luo Zheng had asked Old Man Evil why there were so few middle True Gods.
Naturally, Old Man Evil answered all of Luo Zheng's questions. He told Luo Zheng the reason behind it.
The Divine Dominion was an extremely stable world, completely different from the environment in the universe.
There were very few accidents in this world, and similarly, there were very few "geniuses" who could change the world. Most of the gods did not have any long-term ambitions. Even if they could become true gods, it would be very difficult for them to become a mainstream powerhouse, and it would be impossible for them to become a Paragon, let alone become a Saint.
In other words, the passageway for the bottom-level martial artists to go up was completely blocked.
"The martial artists in the Great Evolution Universe are like water. If the strong don't work hard, they will fall from a high place. Even if they don't fall, they will die along with the exhaustion of their lifespan, thus giving up their position. Heavenly Venerables are no exception. If the weak work hard, there will be a day when they can climb to a higher place!"
"But the True Gods of the Divine Realm are like mountains. They won't move, their lifespans are limitless, and the strong will always be strong. When the top seats are full, the martial artists below will lose the possibility of moving up. No matter how hard they try, it will be useless. Those Gods have lost all hope."
"This is the so-called stratification!"
This was what the Evil Old Man had told Luo Zheng.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng sighed slightly. He did not expect the Evil Old Man to be so insightful and come to such a conclusion about the Divine Realm.
This also explained why there were so few middle-level True Gods.
It was also no wonder that Yin Yuanzi was willing to hide in Meng Village for seven Divine Eras, waiting for the Evil Old Man's return, for a rat.
Because the Stone Forest Rat contained a glimmer of hope, neither Yin Yuanzi nor the Evil Old Man had any intention of becoming a Saint. They did not even hope to become a Paragon. They only wanted to go one step further, break through the middle-level True God, and become an upper-level True God.
Just for this glimmer of hope, two True Gods were willing to risk their lives. It was obvious how difficult it was to make a breakthrough in the Divine Realm.
The rotund fatty glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng and squeezed his way to the entrance of the Alley of Gold Coins. His body was wider than the alley, but he squeezed his way in with all his might …
After squeezing his way in, the fatty disappeared into thin air.
After the few people behind the fatty stepped into the alley, they also disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
"So there's another world in the Alley of Gold Coins." Luo Zheng finally understood and stepped into the Alley of Gold Coins as well …
The moment Luo Zheng stepped into the narrow alley, a wave of water rippled in front of his eyes. It was as if space had been distorted, and the world in front of him suddenly opened up as if he had come to another world. What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a wide street.
On both sides of the street were neatly arranged stalls.
Numerous True Gods moved slowly on the street. It looked like a market in the mortal world.
"So many True Gods?" Luo Zheng said in a daze.
In Six Ren Divine City, Luo Zheng did not encounter many True Gods. Most of them were martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods. It did not seem to fit the name "Divine City". He did not expect that there would be so many True Gods gathered in the Alley of Gold Coins …
As soon as he entered, a guard of the Divine City went straight to Luo Zheng and asked calmly, "Do you have something to sell?"
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I'm here to buy something."
The God City Guard expressed his understanding and moved to the side of the road.
"Why are the guards of Six Ren Divine City so enthusiastic?" Luo Zheng asked the Evil Old Man curiously.
Old Man Extreme Evil chuckled, "Of course. There are actually several entrances to the Alley of Gold. Most True Gods who return to Six Ren Divine City will place their gains here to sell. The shops outside the Divine City are for warriors and Gods below the True God level. If a True God wants to set up a stall in the Alley of Gold, they will have to pay a certain fee to Six Ren Divine City. This is actually a tax, so the guards are naturally enthusiastic."
"Even True Gods have to pay taxes …" Luo Zheng's eyes widened in disbelief. He recalled a book he had read in the Eastern Region. There was a sentence in the book that lamented that death and taxes were inescapable. These True Gods escaped death and obtained eternal life. However, they still could not escape the tax …
Luo Zheng sighed as he strolled along the straight streets of the Alley of Gold Coins.
Most of the people on the street were Lower True Gods. There were also a few Middle True Gods mixed in. There were not many martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods. Even if there were, they would be accompanied by True Gods. Clearly, they were the descendants of some big family clans.
As he walked down the street, the shops on both sides of the street were very rich in goods. There were many different kinds of goods. Luo Zheng did not know the names of many of them.
Most of the people setting up the stalls were True Gods. Some True Gods were very enthusiastic and greeted people when they saw them. Some True Gods were very quiet. They just casually placed the goods they were selling and sat at the side without saying a word.
"Celestial Spirit Root that was mined three Divine Epochs ago. Only 20 Shenwu Coins!"
Luo Zheng was suddenly attracted by a shout. Then, he saw a shiny piece of wood placed in front of a stall on the left. This piece of wood was crossed up and down like a slingshot. Its surface faintly flowed with a jade-green light. It was very eye-catching.
"Little Brother, are you interested?"
This stall owner was also a Lower True God. He did not seem to mind Luo Zheng's identity as a martial artist who had proven himself to be gods. He was still very enthusiastic about Luo Zheng.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Haven't you heard of the Celestial Spirit Root? It can increase one's talent. You can swallow it directly. It can also be refined into a Celestial Spirit Pill. After swallowing it, your mind will be clear and your comprehension ability will increase greatly, "the True God said with a smile.
Before Luo Zheng could say anything, the Evil Old Man said in his mind, "It's fake. A real Celestial Spirit Root can't be bought with 200 Shenwu Coins, let alone 20 Shenwu Coins."
Hence, Luo Zheng did not say anything and turned to leave.
"Little Brother! Little Brother, don't go … "the True God called out a few times. His face was full of helplessness. He could only sigh in his heart. It was getting harder and harder to fool young people these days …
As Luo Zheng continued walking, his eyes kept wandering. Suddenly, a bright light flashed and he quickly walked over.

Chapter 1771
Luo Zheng did not expect to see golden Sanskrit in the Alley of Gold Coins.
Moreover, it was a genuine Sanskrit. After all, the structure and meaning of this Sanskrit could not be faked …
This stall owner was also a lower True God. He was also an old man in plain clothes. His eyes were half-closed as he leaned against a chair. He was indifferent to everything around him.
"This Sanskrit can be sold directly?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind.
"What's so strange about that? Sanskrit has long become a commodity in the divine territory. Isn't it normal to trade it? "Old Man Extreme Evil asked.
"But …"
In Luo Zheng's opinion, every golden Sanskrit contained rich meaning. If one could decipher it, they would often benefit greatly.
The old man's stall was filled with brocade boxes. There were about a hundred of them. Didn't that mean there were hundreds of Sanskrit here?
"Hey, look at this Sanskrit. It was the first Sanskrit discovered by Feather Sage. Many people have long understood the meaning within and it has been widely spread in the divine territory. What's so great about that?" Old Man Extreme Evil said indifferently. "The Sanskrit in the other brocade boxes must have only been seen by ordinary people. Naturally, it's nothing special."
Ever since the first golden Sanskrit was discovered, this Sanskrit had a profound impact on the divine territory. Back then, every True God and even every God's citizen had frantically searched for this Sanskrit in the divine territory. Every time a Sanskrit was discovered, they would frantically copy it.
Therefore, in the early days, a batch of Sanskrit was indeed widely spread.
There were about ninety Sanskrit in this batch. Based on these Sanskrit, many talented and intelligent people had interpreted and translated them.
Therefore, these Sanskrit were not rare. Since this old man dared to copy these Sanskrit and sell them here, he must be relying on his new interpretation and annotation of these Sanskrit …
"If it's a Sanskrit that has never been seen before and can be deciphered at the same time, then the value of this Sanskrit would be terrifying. This kind of Sanskrit would often alarm the rich and powerful families of the floating islands. Why would it be placed here for sale?" Old Man Extreme Evil added.
"What do you mean by extremely valuable?" Luo Zheng's heart was slightly moved.
He suddenly recalled what the book immortal had said when Luo Zheng had deciphered the Sanskrit in the library back in the Immortal Abode. He had said that even though Luo Zheng hadn't realized the enormous value of the Sanskrit translations, he would naturally understand once he reached the divine territory …
"Hehe, the various Sages have their own methods of unearthing Sanskrit, but there aren't many Sanskrit that have been deciphered. Only about twenty percent of them have been deciphered," the Evil Old Man continued. "However, I've been imprisoned for seven Divine Eras, so I'm sure some of the Sanskrit have been deciphered. I don't know the specifics, but I've only heard rumors back then. Furthermore, those wealthy families have been secretive about deciphering the Sanskrit and have never made it public. There was once a divine citizen who deciphered three extremely rare Sanskrit, which caused six wealthy families to fight over it. The price they offered was so high that even some upper-level True Gods would tremble."
"I see …" Luo Zheng nodded silently.
Back then, when Luo Nian had displayed such talent, he hadn't taken it seriously. Now, it seemed that talent was indeed extremely important.
When the old man saw that Luo Zheng had been standing in front of the stall for a long time without moving, he finally opened his eyes and glanced at Luo Zheng coolly. "Little fellow, do you want to buy two Sanskrit manuscripts to study? This old man's painstaking efforts and annotations are recorded in them. They're worth a look. Only ten Shenwu coins … "
"No, thank you." Luo Zheng cracked a smile and walked to the next stall. At this stage, these Sanskrit manuscripts weren't of much use to Luo Zheng. Besides, not every Sanskrit manuscript was valuable. For example, the Sanskrit manuscript of the 'Imperial Weapon Refining Method' had never been circulated in the divine territory. No matter how much money one spent, it would be difficult to buy it.
The old man glared at Luo Zheng, then closed his eyes again.
Looking down the stalls one by one, Luo Zheng did indeed see all kinds of strange things.
For example, the stall in front of him had godhoods for sale. Those sparkling godhoods were like sparkling and translucent gems, emitting a soft light. The blue godhoods contained an intense power of ice, the red godhoods had a strange flame pulsing in them, and the black godhoods had some kind of unique law emanating from them.
To the divine citizens, godhoods were an opportunity to attain the Divine Dao.
If some wealthy divine citizens had no hope of attaining the Divine Dao, they would choose to buy a godhood and place it in their inner world. However, this method of attaining the Divine Dao was feasible for a short period of time. They could also obtain eternal life through this method, but unless it was absolutely necessary, the majority of the divine citizens wouldn't choose this path.
This was because the godhoods of other True Gods were rejected by the inner world. They weren't godhoods born in the inner world, so they couldn't truly fuse with each other. Therefore, the godhood couldn't be upgraded and their cultivation couldn't be increased. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't choose this method, even though the godhoods weren't that expensive.
After wandering around, Luo Zheng finally found the sumeru ring he needed at a small stall. The stall owner saw Luo Zheng's identity and quoted him ten Shenwu coins. The Evil Old Man told Luo Zheng that the price was too high. After some haggling, he only spent five Shenwu coins. It wasn't expensive to use it as a temporary storage space.
After taking the sumeru ring, Luo Zheng suddenly felt his dantian tremble slightly. A strange thought rose in Luo Zheng's mind.
There seemed to be a slight change in the inner world in his dantian, as if it was reminding Luo Zheng that it was hungry …
The inner world had its own consciousness. Luo Zheng had confirmed this when he was in the Great Yan Universe. The consciousness of the inner world in the Great Yan Universe wasn't the consciousness of his father, Luo Xiao. Instead, it was a wisp of spirituality born after the birth and death of millions of creatures.
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped into the Divine Sea Realm, he didn't feel that the inner world had its own consciousness. This was the first time he felt it.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He couldn't understand why there was such a consciousness in his dantian.
However, that consciousness reminded Luo Zheng to turn his head. Then, his eyes were firmly glued to another stall. The stall owner was a young girl who looked about 16 or 17 years old. She was wearing an orange dress and had a pleasant appearance. However, one couldn't judge age by appearance in the Divine Domain. Luo Zheng didn't deliberately check her bone age.
What he cared about was the items on the stall.
There were a bunch of strange objects on the stall. Luo Zheng couldn't name them, nor did he recognize them.
However, what attracted Luo Zheng's attention was a brown object on the edge of the stall. That object was like a piece of mud, unremarkable and covered in dust. It had been on display for a long time, but no one cared about it.
The little girl smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng. "What do you want to buy?"
"What do you like?" The Evil Old Man asked. He looked at the mess of things on the stall, but there was nothing valuable. At least, nothing that Luo Zheng liked. Moreover, he felt Luo Zheng's soul tremble slightly, as if it was attracted by something important.
Luo Zheng didn't answer. He still stared blankly at the brown "piece of mud" …
At the same time, the consciousness transmitted from his dantian tried to resist Luo Zheng's own consciousness. His feet involuntarily moved toward the "piece of mud". The consciousness in his inner world actually took control of Luo Zheng's physical body and controlled Luo Zheng's actions!

Chapter 1772
Although Luo Zheng had suppressed the consciousness of the Inner World, the waves of consciousness continued to surge.
However, the surging consciousness kept reminding Luo Zheng that this item was extremely important.
As the creator of this world, Luo Zheng's consciousness belonged to him and could not go against him …
However, when Luo Zheng communicated with the Evil Old Man, the latter had told him that the consciousness of the Inner World was not absolutely safe. If careless, this consciousness could really take away a warrior's physical body!
There were some True Gods who neglected to trim their Inner Worlds. Ambitious "secondary living beings" were born in that Inner World. Through controlling the Godhead, they destroyed the True God's soul.
However, only the secondary living beings in the Saints' Inner Worlds could comprehend the Divine Dao and escape from the Inner World. Although those secondary living beings could take away the physical body of the "God of Creation" through the Godhead, they were still confined to the Inner World and could not set foot in the Divine Realm. Therefore, these True Gods' shells became walking corpses.
Previously, when Luo Zheng's dantian was sealed, people like the Evil Deities were born. If Luo Zheng did not interfere, Evil Deities could have done this. However, after Luo Zheng's suppression, Evil Deities had quietened down …
Now that the Inner World was so interested in that "piece of mud", Luo Zheng naturally would not ignore it.
"Let me take a look," Luo Zheng replied to the little girl. At the same time, he asked in his mind, "Do you recognize that piece of mud?"
"Mud?" Old Man Evil stared blankly for a moment before noticing the brown mud. He paused for a moment before muttering, "This mud looks like Brown Cloud Mud, but it also looks like Dust Mud … No, no, this …"
The Evil Old Man was considered an extremely knowledgeable person in Luo Zheng's mind. He had a deep understanding of the objects in the Divine Realm. After dawdling for a while, he finally gave an answer that made Luo Zheng faint. "This is just an ordinary piece of mud!"
Luo Zheng curled his lips. There must be a reason why this piece of mud could cause the consciousness of his Inner World to become so agitated. He wouldn't believe that this was ordinary mud even if he was beaten to death. Now, he could only ask the stall owner.
As a result, Luo Zheng casually flipped left and right in this small stall. Unintentionally, his gaze focused on that piece of mud. He opened his mouth and asked, "May I ask why this piece of mud is being sold here?"
The little girl blinked her eyes and covered her mouth as she smiled. "This piece of mud is a treasure!"
"Treasure?" Luo Zheng was puzzled. Since it was a treasure, why did he throw it aside so casually? Luo Zheng naturally didn't believe him.
Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't believe her, the little girl continued to say in a crisp voice, "It's true. Twenty years ago, when this piece of mud was dug out, there was a towering tree growing on it. That tree was more than a thousand feet tall and almost pierced through the sky!" The little girl said while gesturing with her hands.
Of course, it was impossible for the tree to pierce through the sky. The sky in the Divine Realm was unimaginably high. Besides, a tree that was more than a thousand feet tall was not very tall. In Takeshi Xuan Forest, there were plenty of trees that were thousands of feet tall.
Seeing Luo Zheng's indifferent expression, the little girl pursed her lips and said, "That hillside was planted with a Golden Apricot Tree. Ordinary Golden Apricot Trees can only grow up to twenty to thirty feet tall. But the Golden Apricot Tree on this hillside is very tall. Someone said that there must be some kind of spiritual object at the bottom of the hillside, so our Tie family sent people to dig it out. Unexpectedly, after digging for half a day, this piece of mud jumped out from it … At that time, this piece of mud even turned into a human form and wanted to escape. Its speed was very fast.
Tie family was a small family located in Liuren Domain. It was certainly much stronger than Meng village, but it was still not enough to reach the threshold of a third-class family.
"Human form, escape? Then this piece of mud did indeed have a spiritual consciousness. "Luo Zheng nodded.
"Yes, Second Uncle was very happy at that time." The little girl smiled and said, "But after Second Uncle bound this piece of mud, it seemed to have lost its spiritual consciousness and became like this …"
It turned out that after the Tie family obtained this piece of mud, they also concluded that it was an extraordinary treasure.
There were many things in the Divine Realm that had spiritual consciousness. In the endless years, there were always some strange things that were born. Time could give spiritual consciousness to many things, but it was extremely rare for a piece of mud to have spiritual consciousness.
After the Tie family obtained this piece of mud, they secretly studied it for a long time. However, they could not find anything special about this piece of mud. Just like what the Evil Old Man said, this was just a piece of ordinary mud.
Naturally, the people of the Tie family were unhappy. After studying it for more than ten years, they finally gave up. Although they firmly believed that this thing was not balanced, when they brought it to Liuren Divine City and asked some True Gods with good eyesight to study it, the first thing those True Gods said when they saw this piece of mud was ridicule. "Is your Tie family so poor that they went crazy? Using mud dug out from the ground to fool us?"
In a fit of anger, the person in charge of the Tie family simply threw this piece of mud into Gold Coin Alley.
The Tie family rented a stall in Gold Coin Alley all year round. Now, it was run by the daughter of the Tie family's patriarch, this little girl called Tie Lin. In the past two years, many True Gods had noticed this piece of mud. After all, it was very unusual to find a piece of ordinary mud for sale in Gold Coin Alley.
Every time someone asked about it, Tie Lin would tell them the story. Just based on this story alone, there really were some people who were interested in this piece of mud. After all, there were always some people who were willing to spend some money to buy a chance. What if this piece of mud really was a treasure?
However, after hearing the price this little girl Tie Lin offered, those interested True Gods were so angry that they blew their beards and glared at her. They cursed this little girl for being crazy. It turned out that Tie Lin wanted five hundred Shenwu coins!
Not to mention that these True Gods were just interested, even if this piece of mud was a necessity, the majority of True Gods would not be able to afford it. In their hearts, it was only worth one or two Shenwu coins …
Actually, even the people of the Tie family did not take this piece of mud seriously. They just threw it here for Tie Lin to see what she could do with it. As long as she could sell it, they did not care about the price. How much could an ordinary piece of mud be worth?
This stall in Gold Coin Alley was very important to the Tie family. The reason Tie Lin could take on such a heavy responsibility at such a young age was because this little girl was very clever and very good at business. She just had to sell this piece of mud for a high price. Thus, this piece of mud had been thrown here for several years and no one was interested in it.
Today, when Luo Zheng asked about it, Tie Lin once again vividly described the story. At the same time, she looked at Luo Zheng with a smile and asked, "Do you want to buy this piece of mud?"
Don't be fooled by this little girl's delicate appearance, her eyes were surprisingly vicious. When she first saw Luo Zheng loitering around, she was rather unnatural. When her gaze fell on this piece of mud, she seemed to see something special about it. After that, she saw Luo Zheng looking left and right in front of her stall. Tie Lin secretly laughed in her heart. This guy was clearly very interested in this piece of mud, but he just had to pretend like this!
She had a faint premonition that she could use this piece of mud to rip off Luo Zheng. Unfortunately, this person was only a Proof of Divinity expert. Although his attire was decent, it was somewhat shabby. She wondered if he could take out that many Shenwu coins!
"How much do you want to sell this piece of mud for?" Luo Zheng asked casually. Of course, he did not know that his every move had been seen through by this little girl.
The little girl smiled and stretched out five fingers.
"Five Shenwu coins?" Luo Zheng relaxed. Whether or not this piece of mud was useful, Luo Zheng was bound to buy it. If this little girl really asked for an exorbitant price, it would be bad.
The little girl shook her head.
"Fifty?" Luo Zheng asked again. His heart was in his throat. Fifty Shenwu coins was not a small amount. If she was not born into a big family, she would not be able to take out so much money.
Who knew that the little girl would still shake her head. At the same time, she smiled sweetly, revealing her silver teeth. "Five hundred Shenwu coins!"
"Which family is this little girl from? Is she crazy?"
Before Luo Zheng could say anything, the Evil Old Man in his mind cursed first …
In the Evil Old Man's eyes, this piece of mud was worthless. However, Luo Zheng had some secrets that the Evil Old Man did not know. Perhaps he could see the value of this piece of mud, but he did not expect that this little girl would dare to ask for five hundred Shenwu coins. This was too outrageous.
Hearing this price, Luo Zheng's brows knitted together. He read a trace of craftiness on the little girl's fair face.

Chapter 1773
Luo Zheng felt a headache coming on.
It seemed like he wasn't good at being pretentious. Instead, this little girl saw through him.
"Actually, this price isn't targeted at you alone." Tie Lin spoke again, "In the past few years, I've treated everyone who asked for the price of this mud equally and offered 500 shenwu coins. However … I feel that it's more appropriate to offer you 1000 shenwu coins!"
Tie Lin was speaking the truth. She knew that this mud was definitely not an ordinary item. However, she couldn't find anything special about it and no one in Six Ren Divine City could recognize it either.
Now that Luo Zheng finally came and seemed to see the uniqueness of this mud, how could she let it go?
Tie Lin was speaking the truth. Even if she offered 1000 shenwu coins, Luo Zheng would have no choice but to buy it.
He naturally knew what was going on in his inner world. To be able to cause such a reaction from the consciousness of his inner world, this thing must be very useful to him. However, he didn't know what it was.
However, he was rather unwilling to spend 500 shenwu coins. He shook his head and said, "It's too expensive. I'm just a testimonial martial artist. How can I have such a large sum of money?"
Five hundred Shenwu coins was a sum that even some of the poorer middle True Gods would not be able to take out. Most of the Proof of Divinity martial artists were extremely poor. They would run around for a long time just for a single Shenwu coin, so how could they possibly take out such a large sum?
Tie Lin's eyes revealed a look of regret. She naturally thought of this. How could testimonial martial artists have so much money? However, testimonial martial artists who dared to walk into Gold Money Alley more or less had some wealth. She gave this price to test Luo Zheng's bottom line.
"Then how much do you want?" Tie Lin asked.
"I only have 30 shenwu coins …" Luo Zheng replied.
"Only 30?" Tie Lin stared at Luo Zheng and smiled, "That's too little. I'm not selling at this price."
"Sigh, then there's no other way." Luo Zheng made a regretful expression and then turned around to leave.
Tie Lin maintained that smile from beginning to end. She didn't say a word as she stared at Luo Zheng's gradually disappearing figure. She was still silently counting in her heart. One step, two steps, three steps … ten steps, eleven steps …
After Luo Zheng had walked some distance away, he suddenly turned around and shouted, "Double it, 60 shenwu coins!"
However, Tie Lin still shook her head calmly.
If the elders of the Tie Clan saw this scene, they would probably scold Tie Lin to death.
It would already be a miracle if that piece of mud could be sold for 10 Shenwu Coins. Now that someone was offering 60 Shenwu Coins, Tie Lin wasn't willing to sell …
"Luo Zheng, why must you buy this piece of mud?" The Evil Old Man was very confused.
Although he was temporarily residing in Luo Zheng's mind, he didn't know much about Luo Zheng. Previously, the Evil Old Man had been puzzled. With Luo Zheng's talent and foundation, it wouldn't be difficult for him to attain godhood. Why hadn't he condensed a godhood yet?
He wanted to enter Luo Zheng's inner world to see if he could help, but Luo Zheng refused. Luo Zheng didn't even allow the Evil Old Man to spy on him.
The Evil Old Man knew that Luo Zheng still didn't trust him. After all, he didn't have a close relationship with Luo Zheng. But what kind of shocking secret did Luo Zheng have that he had to hide it so well?
"One hundred, I'll take it." Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and offered another price.
Tie Lin was tempted as well. If she could really sell this piece of mud for over a hundred Shenwu Coins, she would definitely be rewarded by the clan. Therefore, she started to hesitate in her heart. However, she didn't show it on her face. If Luo Zheng turned back, she would just chase after him again …
Right at this moment, two people rushed over from afar. They were Tie Lin's second uncle and third uncle. Both of them were lower True Gods.
As a small clan, the Tie Family sold everything they had through this small stall in Liuren Divine City. This Alley of Gold Coins was actually a large market in Liuren Divine City. It relied on Tie Lin's management to maintain the entire Tie Family's operations.
"I can't sell it for 100 Shenwu Coins … Second Uncle, Third Uncle, you're here?" Tie Lin greeted her two uncles while observing Luo Zheng's expression.
"What can be sold for such a price?" Tie Lin's second uncle was startled. In his impression, the Tie Family hadn't obtained any valuable treasures during this time, so he was shocked when he heard this.
"It's that piece of mud you dug up, Second Uncle." Tie Lin smiled.
"You're still not selling it for 100 Shenwu Coins?!" Second Uncle's eyes almost popped out of their sockets.
Third Uncle was also startled. He didn't pay too much attention to Luo Zheng because he was only a Proof of Divinity cultivator. However, since this person was willing to pay such a high price, he naturally had to carefully size him up. His eyes flashed as if he was thinking of someone.
"Come, come, come. Friend, sigh, young man, I was the one who dug up this mud back then. I'll sell it for 100 Shenwu Coins …" Second Uncle impatiently picked up the piece of mud.
However, just as he was about to hand it over, Tie Lin angrily said, "Second Uncle!"
At the same time, Third Uncle stretched out his hand to stop Second Uncle and pulled them to the side. Third Uncle said something while Tie Lin and Second Uncle glanced at Luo Zheng from time to time.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's face was filled with a bitter smile. He had already expected that person to recognize him.
The matter of Luo Zheng being in Hanyue Building had already spread throughout Six Ren Divine City.
However, Luo Zheng didn't leave his name. Although the various gods in Six Ren Divine City knew of his existence, there weren't many who could truly recognize him. However, this little girl's uncle seemed to have recognized him!
As expected …
When the little girl came back, she said to Luo Zheng with a smile, "This thing is 500 Shenwu Coins. Not one less."
Now that Tie Lin knew that Luo Zheng was the person who defeated Hulan Zhuo in one move in Hanyue Building and won 5,000 Shenwu Coins from the Hulan Family, her tone was naturally firm! Tie Lin was a rather principled person. Otherwise, she wouldn't have asked for 500 Shenwu Coins. She would have asked for at least 2,000 Shenwu Coins.
Luo Zheng took a deep look at this little girl. He knew that it was useless to talk nonsense. If he had known earlier, he would have been more decisive.
Before he could transfer the Shenwu Coins he obtained from Hulan Zhuo into his Sumeru Ring, Luo Zheng could only shake the scabbard slightly. The Shenwu Coins within fell out one by one …
500 Shenwu Coins wasn't a small transaction even in Gold Money Alley. Instantly, it attracted the attention of the passersby on both sides.
Some of the True Gods were surprised to see that Luo Zheng, who was merely a Proof of God expert, could take out such a large sum of money. Naturally, some of them recognized Luo Zheng's identity.
"Isn't this the brat from Hanyue Building?"
"He won 5,000 Shenwu Coins in one move. That's a huge windfall!"
"This brat's luck is not bad. Haha …"
"Luck? If you didn't rely on your True God cultivation, could you have defeated the young master of the Hulan Family? "
Amidst the discussions, Luo Zheng took the piece of mud from Tie Lin's hand. The moment he took it, the consciousness of the Inner World surged out continuously. It was like a hungry wolf that saw a sheep. It couldn't wait any longer.
"It can't be. So much Shenwu Coins to buy a piece of mud?"
"The Tie Family has treated that piece of mud as a treasure for a few years now. That young lady from the Tie Family knows how to do business, but that piece of mud was priced at 500 Shenwu Coins!"
"500 Shenwu Coins? If I can sell it, I'll eat that piece of mud! "
"She has already sold it …"
Everyone was dumbfounded. They couldn't believe that someone would buy that thing at such a high price. Moreover, it was that brat who had recently become famous and defeated Hulan Zhuo.
After counting the Shenwu Coins, Luo Zheng stuffed the piece of mud into his Sumeru Ring. He then slipped into the crowd and left quietly.
Although he had been ripped off by that young lady, Luo Zheng still felt that it was worth it. He believed that this seemingly ordinary item hid a great secret …
Along the way, the Evil Old Man didn't ask anything. Since Luo Zheng wasn't willing to tell him, this item must be related to his secret. The Evil Old Man also knew that he was relying on Luo Zheng now. He wouldn't ask about things that he shouldn't know.
After returning to his temporary residence in Six Ren Divine City, Luo Zheng sat down cross-legged. He placed the piece of mud in front of him and carefully observed it.

Chapter 1774
On the surface, this piece of mud looked like an ordinary object that could be found everywhere outside Liuren Divine City.
Luo Zheng immersed his divine sense into it but did not find anything out of the ordinary.
If this was really an ordinary piece of mud, it would be fine even if he placed it in his inner world.
Thinking up to this point, Luo Zheng said to Old Man Extreme Evil in his mind, "I'm going to meditate."
The Extreme Evil Old Man rolled his eyes and shrunk his soul into a ball. His body flickered with purple light and the light condensed to form the word "seal". He had sealed his own soul.
Old Man Extreme Evil's seal could only be undone from the outside. He would only release it after Luo Zheng finished meditating.
Luo Zheng had previously told Old Man Extreme Evil that he would not allow anyone to spy on him while he meditated.
Back in Meng Village, Luo Zheng had neglected this point because he was in a hurry to suppress the Evil Deities. However, Old Man Extreme Evil's soul was on the verge of collapse at that time, so he did not attempt to spy on Luo Zheng's inner world. Although it was not a big deal if Old Man Extreme Evil knew some of his secrets, it was better to be cautious.
Luo Zheng held the piece of mud with both hands and placed it flat on his dantian. As his dantian revolved, a faint light escaped from his lower abdomen and formed a temporary passage between his inner world and his Divine Domain.
The consciousness of the inner world was still restless, constantly urging Luo Zheng.
Looking at the brown piece of mud, Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment. However, that hesitation disappeared in an instant. He separated his hands, and the piece of "mud" disappeared from Luo Zheng's hands.
Inner world …
Dust particles rapidly condensed in the air and condensed into Luo Zheng's inner incarnation in a ten-thousandth of a breath's time. He then looked up and saw a black dot falling from the sky.
Luo Zheng personally entered his inner world as a precaution.
As he flew, he quickly approached the lump of mud and maintained the same speed as the lump of mud. He paid close attention to the movement of the lump of mud.
However, this piece of mud continued to tumble and fall, but there was still nothing strange about it …
As it fell faster and faster, this piece of mud began to disintegrate in the air, turning into small pieces.
"Plop, plop …"
Not long after, the mud finally fell into the sea of chaos.
The color of the mud was exactly the same as the brown color of the Sea of Chaos. It disappeared after falling into it, but Luo Zheng could still feel the existence of this thing.
Standing on the surface of the Sea of Chaos, Luo Zheng remained motionless, quietly observing the changes within.
Swish!
Someone was flying over from the horizon. It was none other than the Evil Deities.
After the Evil Deities had disbanded his Sacred Land, he had become a hermit. He had erased almost all traces of his existence in this world. Generations of living beings had multiplied, and after the decline, the number of people who knew about the existence of the "Evil Deities" was very small.
"Master!"
Evil Deities bowed respectfully to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. "You may rise."
"What is the purpose of your visit, Master?" Evil Deities asked respectfully.
When the Evil Deities complied with Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng also imparted some very valuable experience to him.
Many years ago, the Evil Deities had occupied all the continents in this world, except for the huge World Tree and the Ice Tree at the other end of the world. The World Tree aside, even the Evil Deities could not set foot in the "Desolate Ice Field" where the Ice Tree was located. The Ice Flame it contained could freeze all things.
Later, after Luo Zheng taught him the cultivation method of the "Ice Soul Devil Flame", he had always stayed in the Desolate Ice Field to cultivate. Although he had not fully comprehended the "Ice Soul Devil Flame", the mysterious power of law within it had broadened his horizons!
"I found something that might … be of great benefit to this world," Luo Zheng said as he stared at the turbulent brown sea.
Luo Zheng could not be sure of the effect of this piece of mud, but from the information transmitted from the consciousness of the inner world, his judgment should not be wrong.
"Great benefit?" Evil Deities' eyes lit up slightly.
Before the Evil Deities fought with Luo Zheng, he understood that this world had restrictions on him. After he cultivated to the Extreme Divine Realm, he seemed to have hit the ceiling of his cultivation. It was not his bottleneck, but this world's bottleneck. This world did not allow him to become stronger.
But according to the Evil Deities' own speculations, there should be two more realms above the Extreme Divine Realm.
After Luo Zheng spared his life, his doubts were finally resolved. After the Extreme Divine Realm, there was indeed the Divine Transformation Realm and the Realm Lord Realm. Moreover, the Realm Lord Realm was not the end of one's cultivation!
Only when Luo Zheng's own cultivation became stronger and his inner world became larger would Evil Deities have the qualifications to advance. So when he heard Luo Zheng's words, Evil Deities was naturally happy.
"Gudong …"
At this moment, a bubble emerged from the sea.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and Evil Deities stared fixedly at those bubbles …
"Gudong, gudong, gudong, gudong …"
One after another, huge bubbles emerged from the depths of the Primal Chaos Sea, creating a huge commotion.
"Hualala …"
A huge fish was frightened and rushed out of the sea. It somersaulted in the air and swam towards the depths of the Primal Chaos Sea.
Tens of thousands of small fish also fled in all directions. Their biological instincts told them that a great disaster was about to befall them.
Luo Zheng could already feel that when this piece of mud sank into the Divine Sea, it dissolved into fine sand and fused into the Primal Chaos Sea. These bubbles were also the result of the Primal Chaos Sea fusing with the piece of mud.
"Gudong, gudong …"
As the number of bubbles increased, the sound became louder and louder.
With Luo Zheng as the center, the sea within a radius of 100,000 feet was filled with these huge bubbles. It was as if the entire sea was boiling!
"What is this?"
Evil Deities was confused. He had always been conceited. Even though he was subservient to Luo Zheng, he still believed that he was smarter than Luo Zheng. It was just that his knowledge and experience limited his talent.
Luo Zheng also shook his head. "I'm not sure either. It's just that this world is telling me that it needs it …"
"This world … it's speaking?" As a native of Luo Zheng's inner world, Evil Deities also vaguely knew that this world seemed to have given birth to its own consciousness. It was just that he wasn't completely sure.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He didn't answer Evil Deities' question. He continued to pay attention to the changes in front of him. He had already discovered some subtle changes.
After those bubbles appeared, extremely fine droplets of water accompanied them in the air and slowly rose up. These droplets of water were extremely fine. It was impossible for the naked eye to miss them. However, Luo Zheng could sense them with his "omniscient" ability.
Tiny droplets of water were released by the bubbles. They constantly rose up and gathered together. A misty scene began to appear in the sky.
"What is that?"
Evil Deities asked in surprise when he saw the misty sky.
Luo Zheng smiled. "You can call them 'clouds'."
The inner world constructed by Luo Zheng was actually not complete. In other words, the essence of his inner world was still somewhat different from that of the Domain of the Gods. The biggest difference was that everything in the Domain of the Gods was transformed from the Aura of Primal Chaos.
For example, a tree in the Domain of the Gods was indeed a creature of the same level because it was transformed from the Aura of Primal Chaos. However, after it dissipated, it would not return to the Aura of Primal Chaos.
Moreover, the Aura of Primal Chaos contained an irresistible poison to the creatures in the Domain of the Gods. Only the Saints and those sacred trees could resist the corrosion of the Aura of Primal Chaos …
However, the creatures in Luo Zheng's inner world were directly born from the Sea of Primal Chaos. This Sea of Primal Chaos was nothing more than the product of Luo Zheng's "liquefaction of Primal Chaos". However, these creatures often came into contact with the Sea of Primal Chaos without being corroded.
Therefore, the Sea of Primal Chaos did not transform into "seawater" in the ordinary sense. The liquefied Aura of Primal Chaos would not evaporate. Naturally, there were no clouds in this world. Evil Deities naturally did not know what clouds were.
"So those are clouds …" Evil Deities murmured as he stared at the gradually thickening clouds in the sky.

Chapter 1775
Condensing a Godhead on one's own was a very complicated process.
Moreover, each martial artist's comprehension was different, and the situation was completely different.
Luo Zheng's talent was not inferior to Hua Tianming's, but Hua Tianming's ability to condense a Godhead in advance was still related to the composition of his Inner World.
Hua Tianming's Inner World was completely condensed from Vital Essence. After the Sea of Vital Essence was formed, it turned into ordinary seawater. Every day, the seawater would evaporate into clouds and form clouds in the sky, forming a complete cycle.
In fact, this cycle was a process of "purification."
The ten Daoists all received the Godhead Plate given by Jiang Yu 'er, and this Godhead Plate was fixed in their Inner Worlds to this day.
The constantly purified Vital Essence turned into pure white clouds. These clouds contained the comprehension of the Divine Dao. They were constantly filtered by the Godhead Plate, and a Godhead was slowly condensed in the Godhead Plate …
However, Luo Zheng's Inner World did not have this process.
Without this process, Luo Zheng found it very difficult to condense a Godhead. He tried to activate the energy of the Chaotic Qi, combined it with his comprehension of the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao, and poured it into the Godhead Plate. However, after countless experiments, there was not much of an effect.
When his father followed him into his Inner World, he also discovered this problem. However, Luo Xiao did not provide a perfect solution. After all, the Primal Chaos Secret Technique was not complete. Even if Gu Bei was present, he would not be able to do anything.
At that time, Luo Xiao suggested that Luo Zheng use the Chaotic Qi to transform into pure clouds. Luo Zheng also tried it, but it ended in failure …
Therefore, during the period of time before he left the Great Yan Universe, Luo Zheng himself was also very perplexed. He was unable to figure out a method to prove the Divine Dao, and the path taken by others was also not suitable for Luo Zheng.
However, after cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Technique to this level, Luo Zheng also understood that there was no turning back. Regardless of whether this path could be taken or not, it was impossible for him to turn back and cultivate his Quintessential Essence.
After exploring until now, Luo Zheng faintly had a kind of judgment. Under his cultivation, he seemed to be missing an extremely important link.
Even if Gu Bei was a "Saint", he was still a creature of the same level, and a member of the Divine Realm. Although the "Primal Chaos Secret Technique" was a cultivation method written in a whimsical manner, Gu Bei could not escape the limitations of being a creature of the same level.
For example, the Evil Deities in Luo Zheng's Inner World. Some of the things he did and some of the things he predicted, even Luo Zheng was quite amazed. If this person was born in the Divine realm, he would probably be an extremely powerful person. However, because this person was born in his Inner's Inner world in his Inner Inner world,'s's was's's knowledge............
Therefore, the incompleteness of the Primal Chaos Secret Technique was not Gu Bei's problem. Because he was a creature of the Same Level, his understanding of the Primal Chaos Secret Technique was not complete. Therefore, imparting the Primal Chaos Secret Technique to Luo Zheng was originally a risk.
After the grayish-brown clouds formed little by little, they were reflected in Luo Zheng's pupils and condensed into his hope. Because he saw that these clouds gave birth to traces of spirituality and floated towards the east. That was the location of the God Lattice Disk!
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed, and golden light appeared. His understanding of the Severing Emotions Divine Path was constantly revolving in his heart.
At the same time, in that mass of brown clouds, faint sword shadows were faintly flowing. These sword shadows rapidly moved, emitting a sharp aura and even the sound of metal clashing.
"Sword Cloud Condensation!"
Now, Luo Zheng condensed all his understanding of the Severing Emotions Divine Path into this mass of brown clouds. This mass of brown clouds turned into a mass of sword clouds!
"This aura!"
Evil Deities looked at the "clouds" in the sky, and their eyes were full of shock.
Although Evil Deities had lost to Luo Zheng before, the power that Luo Zheng used was the power to control this Inner World. Evil Deities could not resist this power, nor could they block it. This was the privilege of the creator.
However, Evil Deities had never seen Luo Zheng's true strength on the path of martial arts.
Now that Evil Deities saw Luo Zheng's understanding of the Severing Emotions Divine Path, they were greatly moved.
The mass of sword clouds floated rapidly in the sky, and finally reached the side of the God Lattice Disk. The mass of sword clouds kept converging towards the God Lattice Disk …
This process lasted for a long time. Finally, the mass of sword clouds became dimmer and dimmer. It was completely absorbed by the God Lattice Disk!
Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he arrived above the God Lattice Disk. Looking at it, he saw a hair-like object in the center of the God Lattice Disk. It was also shining with brown light, but it looked like some kind of crystal.
After observing it for a while, Luo Zheng reached out to touch the thin crystal.
The moment Luo Zheng touched the crystal, it suddenly released an extremely sharp energy.
"Pu!"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng's arm was cut off!
Luo Zheng instinctively flashed away, and he managed to avoid this energy. He gently waved his severed arm, and a brand new arm was formed. Although he was injured by the hair-like crystal, Luo Zheng still smiled.
This hair-like crystal should be the necessary material to form the Godhead. Or it could be said that it was an extremely small Godhead, and it was formed from all of Luo Zheng's understanding of the Severing Emotions Divine Path.
As for the formation of this hair-thin crystal, it was all thanks to that piece of mud!
Although it was still very far from the complete Godhead, Luo Zheng had finally found the right direction, and this was the most important thing!
After a while, Evil Deities caught up with him. He looked at Luo Zheng with a different gaze. Even though he was completely suppressed by Luo Zheng, this extremely arrogant person's unyielding spirit did not dissipate. He thought that he lost to Luo Zheng only because of his own limitations, and not because Luo Zheng was stronger than him.
Now, Evil Deities understood that Luo Zheng's true understanding of martial arts was as vast as the sea, and it was almost unfathomable. He was completely convinced.
"Master, can we comprehend the sword essence in the sword cloud?" Evil Deities asked.
Luo Zheng glanced at Evil Deities indifferently, and said, "Now is not the time."
He had only stepped into the threshold of the Severing Emotions Divine Path, and had not seen the Dao tablet of the Severing Emotions Divine Path. His understanding was limited, and it was impossible for him to create a Dao tablet and leave it in his Inner World, right?
Evil Deities showed a trace of disappointment when he comprehended the Dao tablet. He no longer had any thoughts of confronting Luo Zheng. Instead, he was immersed in martial arts. In his eyes, all martial arts were like difficult problems, waiting for him to solve them.
However, there were simply too few difficult problems in this world. Right now, the only thing that he had yet to fully comprehend was probably the "Ice Soul Devil Flame" in the Savage Icy Plains. However, he also understood that it wouldn't be long before he would be able to completely comprehend the Ice Soul Devil Flame …
The Severing Emotions Divine Path in the sword cloud was like a peerless beauty that attracted him, but he was not qualified to cultivate it. Naturally, he was a bit depressed.
"If there is a chance one day, I will let you leave this world and see the real Dao tablet. You will comprehend it yourself," Luo Zheng added. As a creature in his Inner World, Evil Deities was completely under Luo Zheng's control. It would be a waste if Luo Zheng did not guide such a talented person.
However, the reason why Luo Zheng made such a promise was because of the crystal that had condensed on the Godspark Plate. It made him unusually happy.
Evil Deities was overjoyed when he heard Luo Zheng's words.
Leave this world and see the Dao tablet!
Evil Deities did not understand what a Dao tablet was, and he did not need to ask too much. What was important was that he had a chance to leave this world that was like a cage for him!
"Lord, I will help you take care of this world," Evil Deities said seriously.
Luo Zheng nodded faintly. Evil Deities was indeed very clever. He was now acting like the gardener of this world, helping Luo Zheng carefully repair the Inner World so that many forces would no longer fight. The number of creatures in this world would also steadily increase.
Not long after, Luo Zheng's Inner Incarnation dissipated again …
Then Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes. A smile hung on the corner of his mouth, but that smile did not last for long.
Now it seemed that the magical mud was indeed helpful for him to condense the Godspark. The problem was that he was lucky enough to get one in Gold Money Alley. If he really wanted to condense the Godspark, he was afraid that he would need a considerable amount of "mud". But where could he find it?

Chapter 1776

Even the Han Family's branch family was far more powerful than ordinary second or third-rate families. Based on past experience, the top five most popular candidates in the Heavenly Palace would usually be taken by them.

Most of the Proof of Divinity cultivators participating in the Heavenly Palace's examination were unusually excited and excited.

Most Divinity cultivators who wanted to cultivate to the Realm of Immortal Lords didn't have much difficulty relying on their willpower. The true difficulty lay in proving divinity. Using one's own ability to condense a godhood was practically a rarity. If one could join the Heavenly Palace, their chances of success would greatly increase with the help of the Heavenly Palace. As long as they successfully condensed a godhood and gained eternal life, they would have limitless possibilities.
Thus, a considerable number of Proof of Divinity cultivators participated in the Heavenly Palace's examination almost every year. Although there were very few who passed, they still hadn't given up hope.

The group of people who made this list also relied on the Han Family for a living. While he was busy, his gaze kept wandering among the crowd. As a True God who imparted doctrine, although his strength was ordinary, it was very easy for him to find someone among more than 100,000 people.

"Is it this person?"
"The one who defeated Hulan Zhuo with one move?"

Chapter 1777

Chapter 1778

The number of people entering the Heaven-containing Palace was recorded by these white jade tokens. These white jade tokens all had Han Jia's special symbol, so they could not be faked.

This white jade token was covered in a layer of faint green light. It shot into the air, but it seemed to have encountered an invisible net. That net formed a strong resistance, and the momentum of the white jade token rapidly decreased. In the end, it was embedded in the air, unable to go up or down.

Luo Zheng leaned against the wall and stared at the little white dot in the air. Although he hadn't been in Six Ren Divine City for long, he had at least done some research and knew the rules of the Heaven-containing Palace's examination.
Han Jia's examination wasn't equal. After all, there were a hundred thousand people taking the examination together. There had to be an order, and the first martial artist naturally had the advantage. So every year, before the examination began, they would toss out white jade tokens to determine the order of each martial artist who proved divinity.
In the sky above the Heaven-containing Palace, there was an extremely strong binding force. The higher one went, the stronger the binding force.
Last year, the highest toss height in the Heaven-containing Palace's examination was only five hundred and fifty feet. This year, Han Xuan had surpassed last year's best result.
But because this year's Heaven-containing Palace's examination was much better than last year's, whether it was the number of people or the quality, Han Xuan's result was not unexpected.
After Han Xuan made the first move, there were martial artists who started to throw White Jade Tokens into the air. These White Jade Tokens recorded their names and origins. When all of the White Jade Tokens were bound by the power in the air, the order and the details of the martial artists who proved their divinity would be recorded in the records.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
In the process of tossing the white jade tokens, these martial artists who proved divinity used various methods to imbue the tokens with the power they comprehended. This allowed the white jade tokens to fly higher and fight for a higher ranking for themselves.

Some of the other White Jade Tokens had different kinds of energy attached to them. A considerable portion of the energy came from various Divine Daos, and there were also some that relied purely on brute force.
These martial artists who could be called martial artists who proved divinity had more or less stepped into the Divine Daos. It was just that they didn't have a deep understanding of the Divine Daos, so they couldn't condense the Godhood …

A very small number of martial artists who proved divinity could go beyond five hundred feet. These people were the best of the best!

Hiss …
A sharp hiss cut through the sky, attracting everyone's attention. The white jade token was wrapped in a black flame. Like a black meteor, it rushed into the sky at an extremely fast speed!
This white jade token was thrown by Han Zhen!

The higher the white jade token climbed, the greater the resistance, and the slower the speed.
Moreover, the black flame on the white jade token was still slowly burning. It slowly climbed up with difficulty and finally stopped at a height of seven hundred and ninety feet!
Seeing this scene, Han Zhen's face was full of surprise. He didn't expect that he could surpass Han Xuan by so much.
"Han Zhen actually threw it to seven hundred and ninety feet …."

Chapter 1779
Luo Zheng had planned to throw the White Jade Token up casually. He had no intention of fighting for first place.

At the same time, most of the Proof of Divinity cultivators focused their attention on him.
These Proof of Divinity cultivators also wanted to see if Luo Zheng was really strong or if the rumors were just exaggerations.
"The rest of you, please quickly toss your White Jade Medallion up so that our Heaven-containing Mansion can compile the list," said Han Meng. Although his tone was very calm, and he did not mention anyone, anyone could tell that he was targeting Luo Zheng.
After Han Meng finished speaking, the rest of the Proof of Divinity cultivators threw their White Jade Tokens up as well. Soon, only Luo Zheng was left.
"That kid must be scared," one of the Proof of Divinity cultivators said with his head lowered.
"Most likely, he's afraid of embarrassing himself …"
"Since he's in the Heaven-Containing Mansion, he should behave himself. Why go against Han Jia?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng felt a little helpless.
When did he go against Han Jia?
It was this group of people who were looking for trouble …
"You're the only one left. Don't pretend to be mysterious. Whether you're a mule or a horse, drag him out for a walk!" Han Zhen said again. He looked at Luo Zheng's half-smile and felt very unhappy.
"Phew …"
Luo Zheng sighed softly.
Then, he took the White Jade Token in his hand and gently turned it over twice.
At this moment, everyone, including Han Zhen, Han Xuan, Mansion Master Han Meng, and Hulan Zhuo, fixed their eyes on the White Jade Token.
Before the examination of Han Jia, they did not know Luo Zheng's surname and background. Even under Han Zhen's interrogation, Luo Zheng did not tell them. Out of curiosity, they paid close attention. Now, they could clearly see the words engraved on the White Jade Token.
Meng Village, Luo Tianxing!
"So his surname is Luo …"
"Meng Village? Which corner of the village is that? "
"Small villages like this can be found everywhere in the thirty-six domains. I'm afraid there are hundreds of thousands of them. Who knows which one it is …"
Since Luo Zheng came from Meng Village, he decided to disguise himself as a God who came from that village. After all, Luo Zheng had stepped into the realm of Divinity, and no one would be able to tell that he was once a 'secondary living being'.
After taking a few steps forward, he looked up at the sky and smiled. "Are you looking forward to it that much?"
Han Zhen's expression froze, and he said, "What the hell am I looking forward to? I just want to see you make a fool of yourself!"
"Is that so …"
Luo Zheng gently held the white jade token with a faint smile on his face. Then, he lifted the white jade token to his back. At the same time, the nine stars in his inner world slowly rotated. His entire body suddenly rose up, and blue veins bulged out from his temples all the way to his heels!
Thirty-five percent of his strength!
Although his cultivation hadn't improved during this period of time, his body and inner world had improved to a certain extent under continuous cultivation.
With his own body, Luo Zheng could now easily withstand thirty-five percent of the nine stars' energy source!
The energy source crazily rotated in Luo Zheng's body, driving every joint in Luo Zheng's body. This energy moved along with Luo Zheng's body, and faintly, there was a slight feeling of distortion!
"Slap!"
When the White Jade Token was thrown out, a crisp sound was suddenly heard.
The sound was as crisp as a slap in the face. At the same time, the white jade token turned into powder not far from Luo Zheng's head.
These powder splashed onto some of the Proving Divinity Warriors around. Together with the crisp sound, they really thought that they were slapped by Luo Zheng. They all stared at Luo Zheng blankly, not knowing what had just happened.
Han Zhen saw that the white jade token was broken, but he didn't see clearly what method Luo Zheng had used. He only thought that Luo Zheng had deliberately crushed it secretly. While everyone was silent, he sneered and said, "Humph, not only did you not dare to attack, but you also deliberately damaged the white jade token. You're smart!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng just opened his hand and waved it at Han Zhen. He said innocently, "I forgot that the white jade token is just a piece of jade … It's too fragile."
Han Zhen retorted, "You're good at finding excuses. This …"
"Shut up!"
Han Meng finally stopped Han Zhen. At this moment, he looked at Luo Zheng with a serious face.
Not only Han Meng, but the other True Gods and the old man surnamed Wu were all staring at Luo Zheng with a surprised look!
Before this, they had some doubts. Could this ordinary-looking guy really defeat Hulan Zhuo, who had been cultivated by the Hulan Family with a single move?
But now, they didn't have any doubts.
Just now, when Luo Zheng suddenly threw it, it caused a slight distortion in the space!
In the Dayan Universe, Luo Zheng could easily tear the space apart. However, the stability of the space in the God Territory was beyond his imagination. Even if he exploded with 35% of his power and concentrated it all on his right hand, he could only cause a slight distortion in the space …
However, it was this slight distortion that caused all the Real Gods present to be dumbfounded.
Apart from Han Meng, who was a middle True God, the others were all lower True Gods. However, these True Gods were very experienced. Of course, they knew that there were only two kinds of people who could affect the space in the God Territory.
One kind was people who had broken through the realm of upper True God and cultivated to the Great Perfection. The other kind was saints. Luo Zheng had used his power to distort the space. This was simply unimaginable. The people present had never heard of such a thing.
As for the shattering of the White Jade Token, they saw it clearly. It wasn't Luo Zheng who had crushed it. It was because the impact was too great that it had shattered on its own.
The air in the God Territory was much thicker than the air in the Dayan Universe. Therefore, living beings who stepped into the God Territory would feel an invisible pressure on their bodies. However, the living beings in the God Territory were already used to it. It was similar to the strong gravitational force in the Mountain Crumbling Clan in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Because the air was too thick, when the White Jade Token reached an extremely high speed in an instant, the air formed an air wall in front of the White Jade Token! The White Jade Token itself wasn't very strong. Before it could break through the air wall, it exploded into pieces!
The Real Gods were trying to figure out just how much strength this young man had.
As for Han Meng, he already knew that this Luo Zheng from Meng Mountain Village wasn't simple. He was already very cautious. However, Han Zhen was still talking nonsense and losing face for the Han Family. Han Meng was naturally angry, so he scolded him.
Han Zhen finally seized this opportunity to disgust Luo Zheng. Even though Han Meng scolded him, he still said, "Third Uncle, he broke it on purpose …"
"I told you to shut up!" Han Meng's voice also became cold.
In the end, Han Meng was their senior. Even if Han Zhen's performance today was not bad, he couldn't lose face like this …
"Han Zhen, don't say anymore. Listen to Third Uncle," Han Xuan said.
Han Xuan also didn't see clearly what had happened. However, Han Xuan knew how to read people's faces. Right now, it wasn't just his Third Uncle's attitude. The other True Gods' gazes were all filled with shock. Han Xuan naturally understood that Luo Zheng's actions were no small matter.
Han Meng slowly walked in front of Luo Zheng. His attitude had already changed. The cold and indifferent smile from before was completely gone. Now, he had an amiable expression. He then said, "Luo Tianxing, right? You came from Meng Mountain Village, but our Han Family controls thirty-six domains. There are a total of thirteen Meng Mountain Villages. Which domain are you from? "
Luo Zheng replied, "I came from Meng Mountain Village in the Vast Sky Domain."
"Vast Sky Domain …" Han Meng memorized this name. Naturally, he would send people to Vast Sky Domain to investigate this matter. He then smiled at Luo Zheng and said, "Although it's a pity that the White Jade Token was broken, it's not your fault. How about I give you something else to throw again? Otherwise, you don't have a ranking, so I can't arrange a sequence for you … "

Chapter 1780
Han Meng also understood that the power that Luo Zheng had just erupted with was too terrifying. It had already surpassed the limits of a Proof of Divinity martial artist. Just throwing it out was enough to shatter the White Jade Token. This was enough to prove his ability …
Proof Gods of this level didn't need to fight for an order. He could even make an exception and directly recruit Luo Zheng into the Heaven-Containing Palace.
However, Han Meng needed an excuse to observe Luo Zheng's strength!
The Heaven-Containing Palace was facing a lot of pressure. In the past thousand years, the Han Clan's Saint Emperor had only accepted one disciple. Moreover, that disciple didn't come from the Heaven-Containing Palace, but from the Han Clan's floating island.
Although the Heaven-Containing Palace had nurtured many elites in the past thousand years, after these geniuses became True Gods, their performance was very mediocre. Even those who were recommended by the Heaven-Containing Palace to the floating island didn't have any outstanding areas.
The Han Clan's main branch on the floating island had already announced that if the Heaven-Containing Palace didn't produce a single disciple within the next thousand years, they would give the Heaven-Containing Palace's recommendation rights to the thirty-six regions. At that time, all the 'Proof Palace' in the thirty-six regions would be able to recommend disciples to the floating island!
To become a disciple, one had to be chosen by the Han Clan's Saint Emperor. How difficult was that?
Han Meng wished he could become the Saint Emperor's disciple, but unfortunately, he didn't have the qualifications …
The reason the Han Clan's branch was able to grow to its current state in Liuren Divine City was because of the Han Clan. Because the Han Clan had given the Heaven-Containing Palace the recommendation rights, the Proof Gods of the thirty-six regions had no choice but to gather in Liuren Divine City to participate in the Heaven-Containing Palace's examination.
Although Liuren Divine City was the Han Clan's ancestral land, the main branch had already moved to the floating island. The members of the Han Clan's branch were only distant relatives of the Han Clan. Some of them weren't even related to the Han Clan by blood. Being able to give the Han Clan these benefits was already very considerate.
If the main branch on the floating island gave the recommendation rights to the other Proof Palaces, then the status of the Han Clan's branch would be greatly weakened!
However, finding a disciple who could meet the requirements of a Saint was too difficult. Ever since Han Meng took up the position, he hadn't seen such a genius …
Thinking about the heavy burden of the Han Clan's branch on his shoulders, Han Meng's heart felt incomparably heavy.
This Luo Zheng had just displayed his strength, proving that he was a Divine Martial Expert and was able to shake space. The flames of hope in Han Meng's heart had already been ignited!
If Luo Zheng really had the slightest chance of catching the eye of the Han family's Saint Emperor, then his position in the Heaven-Containing Mansion would be preserved. That was why Han Meng had mercilessly reprimanded Han Zhen for targeting Luo Tianxing.

. When Han Meng gave the halberds to Luo Zheng, the flames of jealousy in Han Zhen's eyes nearly spat out.

He hadn't displayed his sword dao in front of Han Meng. He also put the Great Thousand Heavy Sword back into its scabbard and threw it into his spatial ring.
Han Meng smiled faintly. "I see that the web between your thumb and index finger is a little flat. There's also some wear and tear on your index finger. This is the result of years of sword cultivation." As the Mansion Master of the Heaven Containing Mansion, he was a peak middle True Divinity cultivator. He definitely had this level of observation.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. When he held the pair of short halberds in his hand, he felt as cold as iron. The short halberds were twice as heavy as the White Jade Token, but because they were heavy, they were easier to throw. There wasn't much of a difference between the two halberds.
Then, Han Meng waved his hand and smiled. "You may begin."
Luo Zheng grabbed one of the short halberds and took several steps back. He raised his head high once more. Within his inner world, the nine stars constantly revolved. The source of strength that escaped from the surface of the stars began to flow through his dantian and into Luo Zheng's right hand. There was still only 35% of the source of strength.
Luo Zheng took a deep breath and threw the short halberd in his hands.
"Wu –"
There wasn't any energy attached to the short halberd. It only relied on the purest of strength.
But this short halberd shot upwards at an unprecedented speed. It tore through the air, creating an ear-piercing howl. Because its speed was too fast, the surrounding air constantly condensed, forming a pale white trajectory …
In the blink of an eye, the short halberd had reached a height of 500 feet!
"It's so fast!"
"I wonder if it will break the record!"
"The Mansion Master seems to think highly of this boy. That's why he gave him this short halberd …"
The Proof of Divinity martial artists present widened their eyes as they watched the black short halberd rise in a straight line.
After the black short halberd reached a height of 500 feet, it encountered the invisible net like the other hundred thousand White Jade Tokens. Its speed began to decrease rapidly.
But the short halberd's initial speed was too fast. Even with the decrease in speed, it was still shockingly fast.
"I wonder if it can break Han Zhen's …" A Proof of Divinity martial artist wanted to ask if it could break Han Zhen's record. But before he could finish speaking, the short halberd had already broken through the barrier of 800 feet. It went straight for 900 feet …
"The highest record in the history of the Heaven Containing Palace is only 820 feet. It's going to break through 900 feet!"
"It's already broken through 900 feet. I think it's going to break through 1000 feet."
A Han Family disciple heard this and laughed. "Break through 1000 feet? That's too ignorant! The restrictive force above the Heaven Containing Palace is written according to the rules of the Thousand Commandments Slate. The highest is only 900 feet! No matter how powerful this Luo Tianxing is, can he break the rules of the Thousand Commandments Slate? Even an upper True Divinity would find it hard to break free from this restrictive force! "
What this Han Family disciple said was the truth. The Han Family branch controlled the Thousand Commandments Slate. The rules on it were written by them. Naturally, it was biased towards the Heaven Containing Palace. As for the other 'Proof of Divinity Palaces' in Six Ren Divine City, they didn't receive such treatment.
But after the short halberd broke through 900 feet, it continued to climb upwards without any suspense. It rushed all the way to 900 feet!

Chapter 1781
When the short halberd hit the invisible area, the sky above the Heaven-containing Palace started to turn into an illusory color. It was as though different colors of ink were mixed together.
Just as the Han Family disciple had said, the restrictive force above the Heaven-containing Palace was actually a boundary created by borrowing the power of the Thousand Commandments Slate.
The boundary floated above the Heaven-containing Palace. Normally, it couldn't be seen. Only when an enemy attacked would the boundary be activated!
However, the True Gods who were qualified to attack in Six Ren Divine City were at least upper True Gods. True Gods like the Extreme Evil Old Man were powerless against the Thousand Commandments Slate.
The boundary above the Heaven-containing Palace hadn't been activated for two God Eras …
In the entire Heaven Containing Mansion, from the Dao Instructing True Gods to the Proof Divinity martial artists, all of them stared blankly at the sky. Some of the True Gods who had lived for too long had naturally seen this enchantment being triggered before, but it was the first time for the young Proof Divinity martial artists present to see such a spectacle!

Han Meng looked at the flowing colors and seemed to have thought of something important. His expression changed.
At the same time, he turned his head and shouted, "Old Chen! Quick! Use the Veto Order! I'll compensate you! "
The Dao Imparting True Gods in the Heaven-containing Palace were all gathered in another corner, including Old Man Wu. These Dao Imparting True Gods were also paying attention to Luo Zheng's every move …
