Just as he said this, Han Meng's voice sounded.
The Old Chen that Han Meng mentioned was the oldest and most respected Dao Imparting True God in the Heaven-containing Palace.
When Old Chen heard Han Meng's words, his expression changed abruptly. He instantly recalled an extremely important matter that had been overlooked by everyone!
Under the influence of the Thousand Commandments Slate, no one was allowed to fight in Six Ren Divine City. Even the slightest bit of energy fluctuation could be punished by the Thousand Commandments Slate.
However, the Heaven-containing Palace's examination rules were a lot looser. Thus, Proof Gods could extract the True Yuan in their internal worlds and throw the White Jade Token. They wouldn't be punished by the slate.
However, when Luo Zheng threw the Divine City, Luo Zheng had directly hit the Palace's boundary, it had Divine City's Divine City. Although the short halberd didn't strike the barrier with much force, the Thousand Commandments Slate would recognize it as an 'invasion' and launch a counterattack of the highest level.
With the might of the Thousand Commandments Slate, if it were to counterattack with all its might, even Han Meng would be turned into ashes. Although Luo Tianxing's strength and talent were excellent, it was impossible for him to withstand such an attack. He would probably be turned into ashes in an instant!
.... There had never been a Proof God that could throw the White Jade Token to a height of 99 zhang!
Let alone a Proof of Divinity martial artist, even a middle True God would find it difficult to do so. This was because after reaching a height of 900 feet, the restrictive force would double for every 10 feet. As the multiplier increased, the restrictive force in that space would become extremely terrifying when one reached a height of 900 feet.
But Luo Zheng had done it so inconceivably. Han Meng was naturally shocked. In Han Meng's eyes, Luo Zheng was no different from the geniuses of the Floating Island. He was also the strongest Proof God he had seen since he was promoted to the Heaven-containing Palace.
If Luo Zheng really died under the Thousand Commandments Slate's punishment, Han Meng would regret it so much that his intestines would turn green.
"Zi zi …"
In the center of Six Ren Divine City, there was a green stone slab. This green stone slab was filled with small golden words. There were over a thousand of them. They were personally written by the Han Clan's Saint Emperor. They were the rules of the Han Clan's ancestral land!
Whenever someone triggered the Thousand Commandments Slate's rules, the small golden words would flash with light.
At that moment, one of the small words in the middle of the stone slab began to flash …
At the same time, a bolt of lightning appeared in the air above the stone slab. This bolt of lightning circled around in the air, then condensed into a small ball of lightning!
As the stone slab flashed with golden light, the ball of lightning grew rapidly. From the size of a fist, it grew to the size of a house!
"Pa pa pa …"
The outer layer of the ball of lightning was purplish blue, while the inner layer was deep black. It contained the power of the lightning punishment.
After it finished condensing, it headed straight for the Heavenly Palace, leaving behind a series of frightening sounds of thunder in the air.
Seeing Han Meng's expression, Luo Zheng had a bad feeling. The Extreme Evil Old Man also reminded him in his mind, "Luo Zheng, you better be careful. You seem to have triggered the Thousand Commandments Slate."
In the Mirror City, Luo Zheng had seen the power of the Ten Commandments Slate. The Thousand Commandments Slate in Six Ren Divine City was much stronger than the Ten Commandments Slate. He didn't expect the Heaven-containing Palace to be so careless. They clearly knew how dangerous it was to trigger the Ten Commandments Slate, so why would they do such a boring ranking test?

The ear-piercing sound of thunder came from afar, and it came straight towards the Heaven-containing Palace.
Many quick-witted Proof Gods seemed to have understood something, and they scattered in all directions, avoiding Luo Zheng. They were afraid that they would be implicated by the punishment of the Thousand Commandments Slate. After the ball of lightning entered the Heaven-containing Palace's barrier, it smashed towards Luo Zheng's head!
The power contained in this lightning ball was enough to seriously injure or even kill an upper True God. The moment it entered the Heaven-containing Palace, the powerful lightning power formed a strange electric field that sucked in the 100,000 Proving Divinity martial artists in the Heaven-containing Palace.
There was only one thought in everyone's mind. That brat Luo Tianxing was going to suffer.
Most people didn't have any conflicts with Luo Zheng. After thinking it through, they felt sorry for Luo Zheng. It was very sad.

From the moment Luo Zheng triggered the Thousand Commandments Slate to the moment it activated the punishment, only one or two breaths of time had passed.

Hearing Old Chen's words, Han Meng's heart sank. This time, he was really done for.
Just as the ball of lightning was about to hit Luo Zheng, at the critical moment, a young girl in a corner of the Heaven-containing Palace appeared.
In the corner, a little girl in green blinked her eyes. She tilted her head slightly and swung her lotus-like arm. A token appeared in her hand. The token was the same as the White Jade Token. It was thick at the top and thin at the bottom. However, the color and material were different. In the center of the token, there was a large word "Exempt" engraved on it.
This was the "Veto Order" of Six Ren Divine City!
These tokens were also made by the main branch of the Han Family. This Veto Order could negate most of the rules on the Thousand Commandments Slate.
However, the main branch of the Han Family also knew that if the Veto Order was allowed to spread, the Thousand Commandments Slate would become nothing more than a decoration. This concerned the safety of Six Ren Divine City, so the main branch of the Han Family rarely made Veto Orders. Right now, the price of a Veto Order in Six Ren Divine City had already risen to over twenty thousand Shenwu coins!
Even if one could afford twenty thousand Shenwu coins, a Veto Order was still difficult to obtain. Right now, even the Han Family branch only had three Veto Orders. Moreover, these three Veto Orders weren't in Han Meng's hands.
Old Chen had made great contributions to the Heaven-containing Palace in his early years. He had also helped the Sage Emperor find two disciples. Thus, the main branch of the Han Family personally gave him a Veto Order. Unfortunately, Old Chen didn't have it with him. Distant water couldn't quench a present thirst …
"Crackle …"
The lightning ball was only three to four feet away from Luo Zheng. Han Meng and the True Gods in the Heaven-containing Palace all subconsciously closed their eyes.
Luo Zheng was already prepared to use the Concentric Garment to protect himself. Then, he would do his best to defend against the lightning ball. Even though he felt that the chances were slim, he also felt a faint sense of irony. He actually encountered such an inexplicable situation!
Right at this moment, the Veto Order in the hands of the green clothed girl in the corner suddenly emitted a red light. It drilled out of the Heaven-containing Palace at an incredible speed, crossed over half of Six Ren Divine City, and hit the Thousand Commandments Slate. At the same time, the lightning ball strangely stopped above Luo Zheng's head. Chapter 1782
Luo Zheng stood in place. His body was tense, and he was still in a state of shock. His chest was heaving up and down, and his back was drenched in cold sweat.
If the lightning ball had descended another foot, he would have used all of his divine abilities to defend himself.
However, Luo Zheng knew that the power of the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet was enough to kill an upper True God. With his current cultivation, any resistance would be futile …
Because of this small accident, he had almost been killed. Luo Zheng really didn't expect this. If he really died in Six Ren Divine City, it would be too unjust!
Before the lightning ball disappeared, Luo Zheng saw a red light shoot out from a corner of the courtyard.
Although Luo Zheng didn't know what that red light was, the disappearance of the lightning ball definitely had something to do with it!
He turned his head and saw a girl dressed in green standing at the corner of the wall. That girl had a simple ponytail, revealing her pure white neck. Her baby fat little face was full of spirit and beauty. Her hair was very long. Even though it was tied into a ponytail, the braid still hung down to her heels.
At this moment, it wasn't just Luo Zheng who was paying attention to her. Everyone's eyes were focused on her.
The eyes of a few young Proof of Divinity cultivators lit up when they saw this girl. Their hearts immediately thumped. At the same time, they were somewhat puzzled. A girl like this would attract countless gazes no matter where she appeared, but they hadn't seen her just now!
The attention of the Han Jia people, including all of the Dao Imparting True Gods, naturally wasn't on this girl. Their eyes were firmly fixed on her hand.
The girl's jade-like hands seemed to have been smeared with mutton fat. They were white and delicate, without a single wrinkle. She held a token in her hand. On the token was a large word, "Exempt." This was Han Jia's veto token.
Palace Lord Han Meng calmed himself down and let out a sigh of relief.
Just now, he thought that Luo Zheng was really going to die. He didn't expect this girl to make a move at that moment. It was truly out of his expectations.
Han Meng didn't recognize this girl, but how could someone with a Veto Token be an ordinary person? He already had some guesses in his heart, and a respectful look appeared on his face. He cupped his hands towards the little girl in green, "Miss, thank you for saving me just now."
The little girl blinked her bright eyes and waved her hand. "No need to thank me. Besides, I saved him. He should be the one thanking me."
"Yes, yes," Han Meng agreed. "This … Luo Tianxing, this girl used the Veto Order to save your life …" Han Meng guessed that this girl was most likely from the Han Family's main branch. As for why she wanted to return to the Han Family's ancestral grounds, and why she wanted to participate in the examination, he had no idea.
However, none of the people who had returned from the floating island could afford to be negligent.
Hearing Han Meng's words, Luo Zheng also cupped his hands towards the green-clothed girl and thanked her, "Many thanks to this young lady for saving my life."
The green-clothed girl glanced at Luo Zheng and waved the already activated Veto Token in her hand. She smiled slightly: "I heard that this token is very valuable. How can it be resolved with just a thank you? I saved your life. How are you going to repay me? "
She had returned to the ancestral grounds this time to pay respects to her ancestors.
A rich and powerful family like the Han Clan, after countless years, had already evolved into a huge power. The internal structure of the Han Clan was also very complicated. Other than the great ancestral worship that was held once every ten thousand years, many members of the main family would return to the ancestral grounds alone.
Moreover, this was the first time she had left the floating island. Everything in the Liuren Domain was very fresh to her. During this period of time, she had also toured the entire Liuren Divine City.
However, the scene that left the deepest impression on her was the scene of Hu Lan Zhuo challenging Luo Zheng in the Hanyue Building!
Along the way, Second Grandpa Hu had told her not to have any expectations for the Proof of Divinity cultivators in the ancestral grounds. Second Grandpa Hu had said that although the Divine Dominion was vast, in terms of strength, there were only two definitions: those outside the floating island and those inside the floating island.
Both were Proof of Divinity cultivators, but the ones inside the floating island could easily kill those outside the floating island. It was the same for lower True Gods and intermediate True Gods …
However, what had happened in the Hanyue Building was that the little bit of strength that Luo Zheng had displayed was enough to refute Second Grandpa Hu's point of view. Based on the little bit of strength that Luo Zheng had displayed, it was enough to instantly kill 90% of the Proof of Divinity cultivators on the floating island.
She and Second Grandpa Hu both knew that based on Luo Zheng's skillful control, his true strength was probably even higher. Wouldn't such a person be comparable to the true geniuses of the main family?
Because of this, she had developed quite a bit of interest in Luo Zheng. However, Second Grandpa Hu didn't allow her to interfere in this matter. He would naturally send people to investigate. In the end, she couldn't dissuade Second Grandpa Hu and could only give up.
After she went to the Great Spirit Tomb in the ancestral land of the Han Family to worship the ancestors, she still had plenty of time. Thinking that the big examination of the Heaven Containing Palace would be very lively, she begged Second Grandpa Hu to give her a spot and quietly entered the Heaven Containing Palace. Before entering the Heaven Containing Palace, Second Grandpa Hu even used a "Face Concealing Technique."
After this Face Concealing Technique was used, it could conceal a person's entire aura. Standing in a crowd, one wouldn't be conspicuous at all. It would be very difficult for bystanders to notice.
Therefore, when she entered the Heaven Containing Palace, no one noticed that there was such a little girl in the corner.
After staying in the Heaven Containing Palace for a while, she saw Luo Zheng slowly walk in. However, she didn't expect that Luo Zheng's throw of the black short halberd would directly trigger the Thousand Commandments Slate!
The little girl obviously knew the power of the Thousand Commandments Slate. When the slate's punishment came crashing down, Luo Zheng would most likely die without a doubt. In her heart, she already had the thought of using the Veto Decree. However, when she heard that Palace Master Han Meng had asked "Old Chen" to use the Veto Decree, she immediately dismissed that thought.
She absolutely didn't expect that "Old Chen" would say that he didn't have the Veto Decree with him at the crucial moment.
Therefore, the little girl fiercely glared at Old Chen. In her heart, she cursed, "Why didn't you say you didn't have it earlier?" Only then did she activate the Veto Decree and negate the Thousand Commandments Slate's punishment.
"How to repay …" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. He suddenly had a strange feeling.
Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't know how to respond, Han Meng hurriedly said, "Our Han Family branch still has a few Veto Decrees. After the exam, I'll have someone return them to you. What do you think?"
After hearing this, the little girl said, "The Veto Decree is easy to say. It's not like it's some rare object. But I saved his life. According to the rules, his life is mine, right? So he's one of my people, right? "
When these words came out of the mouth of this naïve little girl, everyone's face was filled with astonishment.
Luo Zheng was also completely speechless …
Saving a life was admittedly a great favor, but that didn't mean that the life saved belonged to a certain person …
This was the first time this little girl had left the floating island. She originally didn't know much about worldly affairs. Because Grandpa Hu had followed her and taken care of her, she hadn't caused any trouble. Now that she had entered the Heaven Containing Palace, it wasn't suitable for Grandpa Hu to follow her. Therefore, she began to talk nonsense.

Chapter 1783
Whether it was Luo Zheng or Manor Head Han Meng, neither of them took her words to heart. They only thought that she was spouting nonsense. p
However, Han Meng didn't dare offend this little girl. He laughed and changed the topic, "Which branch of the main family do you come from?" p
There were three main branches on the floating island of the Han Family. They were the spirit supplementing branch, the qing branch, and the di yuan branch. These three branches were the backbone of the Han Family. They controlled the floating island together. p
When the little girl heard Han Meng's question, she looked confused, "What branch? I don't know either … "
"You don't know?" Han Meng's face froze. The other true gods were also surprised. p
Even they, as members of the Han Family branch, knew the three branches like the back of their hands. If this little girl really did come from the Han Family floating island, how could she not know? Could it be that they guessed wrong? p
Han Meng thought for a moment, then changed his question, "Little girl, if you don't know which branch of the main family you come from, do you know who your father is?" p
"You want to know who my father is?" The little girl in green suddenly realized, "You should have asked earlier. My father is …"
"Hu …"
She didn't expect that halfway through her sentence, a gust of wind would blow in from outside the Heavenly Palace.
In an instant, an old man appeared in the Heavenly Palace. p
"Grandpa Hu," seeing the old man, the little girl in green smiled, "Didn't you say you were waiting for me outside?" p
Grandpa Hu shook his head at the little girl in green, then turned to Han Meng and said indifferently, "You're the Heavenly Palace's master? As a member of the Han Family branch, do you know that there are some questions you can't ask? " p
Being questioned by this old man, Han Meng's eyes flashed. He was unconvinced. Although he knew this little girl was from the main family, how could he not ask clearly? Just as he was about to open his mouth to explain, he saw the old man gently lift up his clothes, revealing a small green sword at his waist. This small sword was carved out of jade. The surface of the sword was densely hollowed out. The craftsmanship was very complicated. p
When Han Meng saw the small green sword, his expression suddenly changed. His face was filled with awe, and he immediately knelt on one knee and cupped his hands. "Han Meng of the Han Family's thirteen branches greets you!" p
Not only Han Meng, but Han Xuan, Han Zhen, and the others also recognized the small green sword. Like Han Meng, they knelt on one knee and paid their respects to the old man. p
"Han Zhen, one of the nine branches of the Han Family, pays his respects!" p
"Han Xuan, one of the three branches of the Han Family, pays his respects …"
"…"
Every disciple with the surname Han kneeled on the ground. p
The people in Han Jia, including the true gods and the testimonies, didn't know what was going on. p
Luo Zheng faintly glanced at the small green sword. He thought that it was most likely related to the small green sword. It didn't have much to do with him. p
Luo Zheng's guess was right. p
The floating island of the Han Family's main branch was like a huge kingdom. Any disciple that came out of the floating island had a much higher status than the disciples of the Han Family's branches in Liuren Divine City. However, the difference in status between the disciples of the main branch was also very large. p
The floating island was like a kingdom, and the Holy Emperor of the Han Family was naturally the king. p
However, the Holy Emperor's whereabouts were hidden. He only showed his face once every tens of thousands of years. Although some of the Han Family's disciples had the blood of the Holy Emperor, they might not have the chance to see the Holy Emperor in their entire lives …
However, there were some people in the Han Family who had special status. They had the right to meet the Holy Emperor directly. p
These people didn't have high status in the Han Family. They didn't even have any jobs, but no one dared to ignore them. And these people had a small green sword on them. p
This small green sword meant that the old man, or perhaps the girl in green, was one of the Holy Emperor's people. It could reach the heavens! p
Therefore, the people of the Han Family didn't pay their respects to the old man. They paid their respects to the small green sword on his waist. p
Grandpa Hu didn't pay much attention to them. He turned to the young miss and said, "Young miss, you said that if your identity was exposed, you would have to go home with grandpa …"
"No," the girl in green didn't wait for Grandpa Hu to finish before she rejected him. p
Grandpa Hu shook his head. "Young miss, you have to keep your promise." Although Grandpa Hu was a servant, his tone was very firm. There was no room for negotiation. p
The girl in green probably knew that she couldn't change his mind. Her beautiful eyes stared fiercely at Luo Zheng. She pouted. "Then bring this brat back with you! If it wasn't for saving him, I wouldn't have been exposed! " p
When she said this, the eyes of the Han Family's disciples flashed. They sighed inwardly at Luo Tianxing's good luck. This was Han Family's floating island. It was their life's goal. If they could really go to the floating island with this unknown young miss, then they would reach the heavens in a single step! p
When Han Meng and the other true gods heard this, they became anxious. p
A genius like Luo Tianxing had appeared before the examination had even begun, so Han Meng had high hopes for Luo Zheng. If Luo Tianxing really did become a disciple of the Sage Emperor in the future, Han Meng would benefit greatly, and the status of the Han family branch would be preserved for the time being. p
If he really was taken away by this girl, then it would be very bad for the Han Family! p
Before Grandpa Hu could speak, Han Meng gathered his courage and said, "This person came to the Han Family. He should be accepted by the Han Family. How can we just take him away like that?" p
Second Grandpa Hu looked at Han Meng and revealed a meaningful look. At the same time, he said, "We have no interest in him, but this person does indeed have extraordinary talent. I also hope to see him on the floating island one day." p
"Grandpa Hu." The little girl in green frowned. p
However, Hu Second Grandpa didn't allow any explanation. He reached out and a horsetail whisk appeared in his hand. He gently waved the horsetail whisk and a cloud of dust appeared. Accompanied by gusts of wind, Hu Second Grandpa and the green-clothed girl disappeared in front of everyone's eyes. p
After the two left, Han Meng and the others let out a sigh of relief. The stiff muscles on their faces relaxed. When they looked at Luo Zheng again, Han Meng was a bit embarrassed …
In the eyes of many, his Heaven-containing Province was extremely proud. Even though Han Meng had long since taken a liking to Luo Zheng and was determined to bring him into the Heaven-containing Province, he still had to participate in the entire examination process. However, when he had fought with the green-clothed girl just now, his true intentions had been revealed without a doubt. It seemed that he had cut off Luo Zheng's path to the floating island. He was afraid that Luo Zheng would blame him. p
"This … Luo Tianxing, if you enter the Han Family with your talent, you will be able to enter the Han Family's floating island in a few years," Han Meng explained. p
Seeing Han Meng, a middle true god, explain things to him so politely, Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. The rules of the god region and the Great Yan Universe might be completely different, but in terms of the ways of the world, they were almost exactly the same. p
"Mansion Lord, I, Luo Tianxing, am indeed going to the floating island. However, I have already planned my own path. Entering the Han Family is a necessary step. There are some shortcuts in this world, but I may not be willing to take them," Luo Zheng said with a smile. p
Hearing this, Han Meng's eyes lit up. He couldn't help but praise, "Good ambition! With this kind of mentality, there is nothing in this world that is difficult for you! " p
Han Meng's words came from the bottom of his heart, but others might not think so. p
That's a chance to enter the floating island ah ….
If the Mansion Lord didn't stop him, Luo Zheng would really be like a fish leaping over the dragon gate. p
Entering the floating island was an unattainable dream for the hundred thousand Proving Divinity experts present. And Luo Zheng was only one step away from this dream! p
"I think this Luo Zheng is swallowing his teeth and blood!" p
"You say it so easily. After losing this chance, you want to enter the floating island again? In your dreams … "
"The Han Family has so many geniuses every year. They are far stronger than the average Proving Divinity expert, but they still don't have the qualifications. Back then, the Han Family branch, Han Xiaotian, was rejected by the floating island. Do you think you can enter the floating island just because you want to? Don't take shortcuts? What a beautiful way to put it … "
These people stood in different positions, and their thoughts were ultimately different. p

Chapter 1784
Now, everyone had already thrown their White Jade Tokens into the sky above the Heaven-Containing Mansion, and there was an order. p
The old man surnamed Chen walked out with a roll of yellow paper in his hand. p
He opened the roll of yellow paper, pointed it at the sky above the Heaven-Containing Palace, and gently blew …
The White Jade Tokens hanging above the Heaven-Containing Palace began to flash with a green light. Every time a White Jade Token flashed, a name and location appeared on the roll of yellow paper. p
The first shining White Jade Token was Han Zhen's. Therefore, Han Zhen's name was also high up, at the top of the yellow paper. As for the others, their names appeared one by one on the yellow paper, according to the different height, in order of precedence. p
Not long after, this roll of yellow paper had already recorded the names and origins of the 100,000 Divine Martial Artists present. p
Since Luo Zheng didn't throw the White Jade Token, but the black short halberd, his name wouldn't appear on the yellow paper. p
Of course, the old man surnamed Chen would not neglect Luo Zheng. He took out an ink brush and lightly shook it, causing a little bit of black ink to spill out from the tip of the ink brush. With a few strokes, he wrote the words "Luo Tianxing, Vast Sky Domain, Meng Village" on the yellow paper. p
Luo Zheng's name was at the top, above Han Zhen's. p
Although Han Zhen wasn't happy, Luo Zheng had easily thrown the black short halberd to a height of ninety-nine zhang, so he could only accept it. p
Just as Han Xuan had said, as the children of the Han family branch, they were born with a huge advantage. No matter if it was bloodline or talent, they were far stronger than ordinary warriors. Once they proved the Divine Way and became true gods, they would be far stronger than ordinary true gods. p
But the Han Clan also needed to strengthen its position. A closed off rich family would be eliminated sooner or later. p
In the past, some rich families on the floating islands were completely closed off. They even stipulated that their children couldn't marry outsiders. Many important positions didn't allow outsiders to participate in. The consequences of these closed off methods wouldn't appear for one or two god eras, but after ten god eras, the ranking of these rich families on the floating islands dropped again and again. There was even a precedent of a floating island falling into the sea of time. p
The wealthy clans of the floating island were quite open-minded. Even if it was an outsider, as long as they could be used by the clan, they would treat them equally in many matters. p
After Old Man Chen finished organizing the list, he took the yellow paper and left. p
Mansion Master Han Meng took down the black short halberd and returned it to Luo Zheng. He then informed everyone in the Heavenly Mansion, "Everyone, please wait for another hour. After an hour, please move to my Heavenly Mansion's inner courtyard's Divine Inquiry Palace!" After saying that, he left. He needed to prepare a lot of things for the Heavenly Court's examination, so he didn't have much free time. p
Seeing that Luo Zheng had nothing to do, the old man surnamed Wu finally stepped forward and smiled at Luo Zheng. "There's something that this old man has to say. I want to know if the Black Gold Taboo Breaking Technique that you taught me was really created by you or taught by someone else?" p
In the eyes of this group of True Gods, Luo Zheng's talent was comparable to the elites of the wealthy clans on the floating islands. Although Grandpa Hu didn't take Luo Zheng away, what he said before he left was very significant. He should have concluded that Luo Zheng was qualified to enter the floating island. p
In the eyes of the old man surnamed Wu, this kind of genius probably had the ability to comprehend and create such an ingenious technique …
The Black Gold Taboo Breaking Technique was originally taught to Luo Zheng by the Evil Old Man. Luo Zheng naturally didn't want to take the credit for it. He just shook his head and said, "It was indeed taught by that senior …"
"I wonder what kind of person that senior is to be able to teach such a talented disciple. He's probably at the peak of the Perfected Realm …" The old man surnamed Wu sighed. p
"Perfected Realm …"
Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback. At the same time, the Evil Old Man laughed proudly in Luo Zheng's mind. p
"Hahaha, I'm lucky to be seen as a Perfected Realm expert. It's worth teaching him the Black Gold Taboo Breaking Technique," the Evil Old Man laughed. p
Only when one reached the peak of the True God Realm would one be considered a Perfected Realm expert. Only after reaching the Perfected Realm would one be qualified to become a Saint. p
"However, this old man is probably trying to get some information out of you to find out more about that expert senior you made up. Hehe," Old Man Evil reminded at the same time. p
Luo Zheng could also tell that the old man surnamed Wu seemed to be very interested in the "senior" he mentioned. p
There were indeed many "seniors" who taught Luo Zheng. Luo Xiao, Gu Bei, Jiu Sheng, and even the Craftsman Sage were all his seniors. However, these people's identities were too sensitive. How could Luo Zheng reveal anything? p
Sure enough, the old man surnamed Wu asked a few more questions, all of which were about the characteristics of that senior. Luo Zheng didn't immediately refuse, and just randomly made up some physical characteristics and personality. Seeing that the old man surnamed Wu seemed to be in deep thought, he was probably trying to figure out who this nonexistent senior was. "Senior, an hour passed in the blink of an eye. p
The old man surnamed Chen returned and announced to everyone, "Everyone, please follow me to the backyard of the Heaven-Containing Mansion!" p
After speaking, the old man surnamed Chen turned and left. p
Then, under the arrangements of these True Gods, the Proof of Divinity cultivators moved to the backyard of the Heaven-Containing Mansion one after another. p
The interior of the Heaven-Containing Mansion was very spacious and everything was planned in an orderly manner. There were quite a number of 100,000 Proof of Divinity cultivators, but they still entered in an orderly manner. p
Luo Zheng followed the crowd and walked forward. Along the way, there were many Proof of Divinity cultivators who came up to Luo Zheng to express their goodwill. These Proof of Divinity cultivators were the descendants of many aristocratic families. After realizing the value of Luo Zheng, they naturally understood the importance of befriending him. If in the future Luo Zheng could enter the floating island and return, his influence would be far greater than before. p
Luo Zheng only gave a simple reply. In any case, he did not expect to stay in Six Ren Divine City for long. p
"Luo, Luo Tianxing …"
At this time, a Proof of Divinity cultivator suddenly approached him and whispered. p
Luo Zheng turned to look. It was a young man in his early twenties. He had a very stocky figure and a somewhat uneasy expression. p
"You are …" Luo Zheng did not recognize this person. p
"My surname is Tie, my name is Tie Yan," the young man said with a smile. p
"Tie Family?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. p
Tie Yan hurriedly nodded and said, "My sister didn't intend to scam you that day when she sold you a piece of mud. After the examination, I can come out and ask her to return some Shenwu coins to you …"
Tie Yan's talent and strength were not bad. He was at least the top of the Tie Family's third generation. However, he was not ranked in the top 50. p
He had heard about how Tie Lin had sold a piece of mud for a sky-high price before the examination. He naturally paid attention to the ranking since he was going to participate as well. He felt extremely uneasy after hearing about the events that had happened in the main courtyard of the Heaven's Containing Mansion. p
Tie Lin's mouth was indeed eloquent. However, selling a piece of mud for 500 Shenwu coins and scamming Luo Tianxing was definitely not a good thing. p
However, five hundred Shenwu coins was still a huge sum to the Tie Family. Even if Tie Yan wanted to return it all, he didn't know if the elders would agree. Therefore, he said that he would only return a portion of it. He didn't have the authority to decide how much he would return. He wanted to show his goodwill. p
However, Luo Zheng did not care about a mere 500 Shenwu coins …
The Evil Old Man said that Shenwu coins had great uses. However, Luo Zheng did not care about the use of Shenwu coins. Since he had spent so much Shenwu coins to condense a crystal, he knew that there was a way to condense godhood. From this point alone, 500 Shenwu coins was indeed worth it! p
"There's no need to return it," Luo Zheng said faintly. p
Seeing Luo Zheng reject the matter, Tie Yan panicked. "This must be …"
"There's really no need. However, I have another request," Luo Zheng said. p
He had originally planned to wait until after the examination to find the Tie Family girl. However, it seemed that he could avoid it now. That girl's brother seemed much more honest. This was an opportunity. p
"You have a request?" Tie Yan was startled. p
Luo Zheng nodded and said seriously, "I wonder where your Tie Family dug up that piece of mud. After the examination, do you have time to take me to see it?" p
"That piece of mud?" p
The surprise in Tie Yan's eyes deepened. Even though his second uncle kept saying that the mud was good stuff, he did not believe it. In his eyes, it was just a piece of ordinary mud. p
From Luo Tianxing's words, it seemed that he still wanted to find the mud. What was the use of it? p

Chapter 1785
In the Domain of the Gods, the study of all kinds of materials was very thorough. p
For example, the cheapest Black Winter Grass. Take the lower half of it and boil it into glue. When refining pills, it can form an excellent "Pill Foundation". It can fuse most of the ingredients in the pill. So, although this material can be seen everywhere, some seventh and even eighth level golden pills need it. p
The most expensive materials are sky-high prices, such as the "Winged Dragon Horn". Through this horn, ancient illusions can be stimulated. Through time, one can achieve the highest level of enlightenment …
All kinds of tricky materials and the use of substances have been studied over countless God Epochs. Other than the depths of the forbidden areas, there are not many secrets in the Domain of the Gods. p
But the mud dug up by the Tie family, other than the initial trace of spirituality, there is nothing special about it. After several tests, the results were the same. In the end, Tie Yan's uncles gave up. p
Now that Luo Tianxing asked again, Tie Yan was even more puzzled. If that was the case, then the mud was indeed very useful to him. p
No matter what the mud is used for, it seems that only Luo Zheng needs it. So, Tie Yan agreed. He is not as scheming as his sister Tie Lin. If Tie Lin is here, she will inevitably extort a large amount of Shenwu coins from Luo Zheng. p
As the two talked, they followed the flow of people into the backyard of the Heavenly Han Mansion. p
The backyard is also a wide field, but in the center of the field, there is a building half buried in the ground. The building has a unique shape, it looks like a wide well, but there is a black entrance in front of it. From the entrance, there is a wisp of green smoke slowly floating out. p
Luo Zheng's nose twitched slightly. He knows that there is someone burning incense in the building. p
When all the Proof of Divinity warriors have gathered in the backyard, a figure came out. It is the Mansion Master Han Meng who left before. He smiled at the crowd and said, "I believe that many of you have participated in my Heavenly Han Mansion's examination for the first time. You have a good understanding of our Heavenly Han Mansion's Temple of Inquiry, but I still want to talk about the rules." p
Among the Proof Divinity martial artists who participated in the examination of the Heaven Containing Mansion, a considerable portion of them had participated more than ten times, or even dozens of times in the examination. Some of the Proof Divinity martial artists had been defeated time and time again, but they kept on fighting. They had become familiar faces in the Heaven Containing Mansion. p
However, there would always be a portion of them participating in the examination for the first time. If they were not careful in the Temple of Inquiry, it would be very dangerous. p
Luo Zheng had only been in Liuren Divine City for a short period of time. Although he had done some homework, he didn't have a thorough understanding of the city. p
"This Divine Inquiry Palace was built by our Han Clan's Saint Emperor. It has been twenty-three divine eras, and the entire palace has sunk one zhang into the ground …"
According to Han Meng, the Divine Temple had been built a long time ago. Back then, the Han Clan hadn't reached Saint Ascension yet. However, back then, the Han Clan's Saint Emperor had already reached the peak of Saint Ascension. The Han Clan was one of the top second-tier clans. p
There were over 300 dao steles in the Ask God Palace, which included one-tenth of all the dao steles in the Divine Realm. p
Of course, all of the Dao tablets were just imitations. p
Creating a fake dao tablet was not an easy task. Even if a true god got too close to a dao tablet, they would still be injured by the dao tablet. Although the dao contained in the fake dao tablet was not as strong as the real dao tablet, it could still test the talent of a martial artist who had proven divinity. At the very least, it could tell which dao path this martial artist was most suited to walk. p
At that time, with Han Jia's strength, it was indeed a difficult task to organize a Divine Inquiry Hall like this. Every Dao Stele had been created by Han Jia's upper true gods bit by bit. They also contained their understanding of the divine dao. p
Now, the Han Clan didn't need the Divine Inquiry Palace anymore. The Divine Inquiry Palace was left to the Han Clan's branch. In the end, the Divine Inquiry Palace was controlled by the Divine Inquiry Palace. p
"These dao tablets were built too long ago. The dao contained in them has changed slightly. If you can't withstand the pressure, don't force yourself. In the past, there were always some cultivators who clenched their teeth and forced themselves to endure. In the best case, their cultivation would be greatly damaged, and in the worst case, they would die on the spot. If something happens, the Divine Inquiry Palace won't be responsible!" p
This has happened many times before. p
The biggest dream of countless Proof of Divinity martial artists was to enter the Heaven-Containing Palace. They all carried the ardent hopes of their elders. Some extreme Proof of Divinity martial artists were unable to comprehend the Dao Connotation within, and were unable to obtain the recognition of the Dao Stele. However, they still gritted their teeth and persevered. In the end, their souls were scattered by the Dao Connotation within. p
Some second and third rate clan leaders were depressed and went to the Divine Inquiry Palace. Although these clans didn't dare offend the Han Clan, they still caused a huge commotion. The Divine Inquiry Palace lost face. p
Now, before entering the Divine Inquiry Palace, Han Meng, as the Palace Master, had to remind them not to force themselves. If they really died or were injured, the Palace wouldn't be responsible. p
After Han Meng finished speaking, he turned to look at Luo Zheng. "According to the rules of the Heaven-containing Mansion, the first place can enter first. Luo Tian Xing, please." Han Meng gestured with his hand. p
Luo Zheng nodded and walked towards the Divine Inquiry Palace. p
After Luo Zheng entered the Divine Inquiry Palace, the surroundings darkened. The smell of incense became stronger and stronger. p
"This is soul-waking incense," the Extreme Evil Old Man said coldly. p
"What's the use of it?" Luo Zheng asked. p
The Extreme Evil Old Man chuckled. "It's a generous gift from the Heaven-containing Prefecture. This Soul-Awakening Incense isn't a cheap item. It can make one's soul more sensitive. Naturally, one's comprehension of the Dao would be more sensitive … I don't remember the Heaven-containing Prefecture being so generous in the past. p
The Extreme Evil Old Man didn't participate in the Divine Inquiry Palace's examination. When he was qualified to roam the Vast Sky Domain, he was already a lower true god. A true god like the Extreme Evil Old Man was considered unorthodox among the wealthy clans of the floating island. Therefore, some of the wealthy clans' disciples were left to grow up freely in the divine territory. p
The Extreme Evil Old Man's judgment was very accurate. Everyone in the Han Family branch was very anxious. After all, the Divine Inquiry Palace hadn't produced a truly outstanding genius for a long time. The 16 elites who were recommended to the Han Family floating island in the last round were all defeated. The few leaders of the Han Family branch were also ridiculed by the main family …
This soul-waking incense could increase a warrior's comprehension for a short period of time. It was also a supplementary method. The Han Family branch was anxious to find a batch of elites who could enter the main family's eyes during this "New Year's". p
"It has such an effect …" Luo Zheng took a deep breath. The thick incense burrowed into Luo Zheng's nose. However, Luo Zheng didn't feel any change. He only felt a trace of coolness in his mind. However, that coolness disappeared as soon as it entered his soul. p
"Heh, this soul-waking incense has a miraculous effect on the dark green Battle Soul. It has some effect on the silver Battle Soul. Its effect on the gold Battle Soul is negligible. With your soul realm … it's naturally useless." The Extreme Evil Old Man shook his head and smiled. p
Luo Zheng had forgotten about this point. p
The soul cultivation of the warriors outside was very weak. This soul-waking incense had the miraculous effect of increasing their comprehension. It was naturally the best for them. p
Luo Zheng was the first to enter the Temple of Inquiry. The soul-waking incense was also the strongest. p
As the hundred thousand people gradually entered the Temple of Inquiry, the soul-waking incense would be slowly absorbed and diluted. Therefore, the higher the ranking, the more soul-waking incense they could absorb. However, it was a bit of a waste for Luo Zheng …
He hadn't even taken a few steps when a series of hurried footsteps came from behind him. It was the second ranked Han Zhen. p
After entering the Temple of Inquiry, Han Zhen greedily took in deep breaths. It was as if he wanted to suck in all the soul-waking incense floating around into his stomach. He suddenly noticed that Luo Zheng turned around and looked at him indifferently. His eyes suddenly emitted a trace of ferocity! But now that he had absorbed the Soul Awakening Incense, his mind was incomparably clear. He instantly thought of the warning Han Xuan had given him, and an ominous glint flashed in his eyes. p
However, he still didn't like Luo Zheng. He nodded at Luo Zheng indifferently and even reminded him in a low voice, "Those who are ranked at the top have the right to choose the best seats. Don't blame me for not reminding you!" After that, he walked past Luo Zheng and went deep into the temple. p

Chapter 1786
Luo Zheng had only taken a few steps when urgent footsteps came from behind him. It was the third ranked Han Xuan. p
Han Xuan looked at Luo Zheng's unhurried manner, his face full of confusion. How did Han Zhen run in front of this guy? p
When passing by Luo Zheng, Han Xuan and Luo Zheng looked at each other indifferently. Han Xuan gave him a polite smile and quickly left. p
Han Xuan's personality was much deeper than Han Zhen's. He saw many things more clearly. He also didn't have a good impression of Luo Zheng, but he wouldn't take the initiative to provoke Luo Zheng. At least on the surface, he still had to be polite. However, entering the Ask God Temple depended on one's own ability. He didn't need to be like Han Zhen and foolishly remind Luo Zheng of anything …
After entering the Temple of Inquiry, there was only a narrow slope that led downwards at a small angle. Luo Zheng's speed was neither fast nor slow, so the Proof of Divinity martial artists who were ranked at the top overtook Luo Zheng. p
These Prove Divinity martial artists were all elites of large clans. When they passed by Luo Zheng, their actions were the same as Han Xuan's. They didn't dare offend Luo Zheng, and they didn't give Luo Zheng any pointers. In their hearts, they even mocked Luo Zheng. His slow speed was wasting the advantage of being first place …
"Heaven Containing Palace inherited the Han Family's heritage. It really can't be underestimated. Unfortunately, the Han Family's branch didn't manage well. These years, they didn't have much success." p
While Luo Zheng was looking at the Ask God Temple, Old Man Extreme Evil also sighed. p
Although this Ask God Temple was ancient, the carvings left behind on the walls were still of uncanny craftsmanship. They were majestic, and all kinds of ancient legends were drawn on them. There was a sacred feather bird forming a rainbow bridge, and there was also a creation axe …
"If the Han Family became a saint, they would naturally move all the elites of the Han Family to the floating island. What would be left behind would only be the branch of the Han Family. What could this branch of the Han Family do?" Luo Zheng asked curiously. p
"Hehe, you can tell just by looking at my family's attitude. Back in the day, I traveled to the ancestral lands of the other major families in God's Domain, but their circumstances were much better than Han Jia's …"
Some of the Ancestral Lands of the Great Clans were still taken care of by the main family. There were even examples of people becoming Saints again. Looking back at the Han Clan, the Proving Divinity cultivators who participated in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination were looked down upon by the main family of the Han Clan. Even the Evil Old Man found it difficult to enter their eyes. p
"Look at the attitude of the Heaven-Containing Mansion's Mansion Master towards you. Hehe, it's as if he's picked up a life-saving straw …" Old Man Extreme Evil continued, "It seems that it won't be long before he hurriedly recommends you to the floating island, even before you attain the divine way." p
Although the Evil Old Man didn't say much just now, he was hidden in Luo Zheng's mind and could clearly see what was going on outside. p
As the two chatted, they followed the long slope all the way to the bottom. It was still pitch black in the distance, immersed in the darkness. Only a few faint lights floated around, barely enough to guide the way. In the middle of the path, there was a row of green bricks that formed a horizontal line. p
When Luo Zheng crossed the row of green bricks, he felt a very complicated aura coming from all directions towards him! p
"Aura … or Taoist Connotation." The corner of Luo Zheng's eye twitched. p
Aura was just a description for the power of faith in the True God's Inner World. Condensing the aura into one's own understanding was called Taoist Connotation. p
For example, Luo Zheng used that piece of mud to create a large "sword cloud". The aura contained within the sword cloud could also become Taoist Connotation. p
In God's Domain, the purest Taoist Connotation was naturally the tablets that recorded all kinds of divine ways. The tablets in the Temple of Inquiry were just replicas made by Han Jia. p
When he heard Han Meng's words, Luo Zheng didn't think much of it. After all, the tablets in the Temple of Inquiry were just replicas. After the Taoist Connotation was comprehended on its own, it was slightly different from the original tablets. The Taoist Connotation hidden within was naturally far inferior. p
But after Luo Zheng crossed the line, under the onslaught of the aura, the last trace of contempt in his heart disappeared without a trace. p
It wasn't a coincidence that Han Jia could produce a Saint. This was the accumulation of a rich and powerful family. With this kind of accumulation, there should be a second or even a third Saint. Unfortunately, Han Jia's branch didn't produce such a person. In the past thousand years, they couldn't even produce a single disciple. This was why the Evil Old Man felt that Han Jia's branch had declined. p
The Taoist Connotation exerted on Luo Zheng was very complicated. It was a kind of aura formed by many Taoist Connotations stacked together …
As Luo Zheng continued to move forward, this complicated aura grew stronger and stronger. However, it didn't pose much of a hindrance to Luo Zheng. He still maintained a steady pace. p
At this time, more people overtook Luo Zheng. Seeing how slow he was walking, they all thought that this guy's soul was weak and couldn't withstand the aura. Some of the Proof of Divinity cultivators showed off in front of Luo Zheng and sped up. p
Luo Zheng naturally knew what these people were thinking. He still had a faint smile on his face. The Heaven-containing Province's examination was like a hundred boats competing against each other. Everyone was doing their best to occupy a good position, hoping to gain the favor of the Heaven-containing Province. p
But Luo Zheng was no longer on the same level as them. The things they were fighting for were meaningless to him. p
After about half an hour, the aura had grown ten times stronger than before. A huge temple appeared in front of Luo Zheng. This was the real Heaven-seeking Temple! p
Huge steles of Dao stood on the ground. Words flowed across the steles. Each stele glowed with a different color, and the words were different as well. Some of the words looked like coiling snakes, while others looked like they had been carved with a knife. Clearly, these steles had not been written by the same person. p
Below these dao tablets, there was a narrow staircase. It was like a small hill, and the dao tablets stood at the top. p
Although Luo Zheng had seen all kinds of strange things in his life, seeing this scene, his eyes were still quite shocked! p
"Just the imitation tablets are enough to capture people's souls. I don't know what the real tablets are like." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Since he entered the G.o.d Domain, he hadn't been able to personally observe the real tablets. p
At this point, Luo Zheng was still walking slowly. p
The Proof of Divinity cultivators who had surpassed Luo Zheng before also slowed down. Several of them were dripping with sweat, and white fog was coming out of their heads. p
These Proof of Divinity cultivators were already considered outstanding existences in the Heaven-containing Mansion. After all, there were only a hundred people who had surpassed Luo Zheng. These Proof of Divinity cultivators were naturally ranked in the top one hundred …
Although there was still some distance between them and the tablets, to resist the Dao essence on the tablets was still a burden for them. p
Only Luo Zheng was walking leisurely. When he overtook these Proof of Divinity cultivators, there was still a faint smile on his face. The Proof of Divinity cultivators who had previously ridiculed Luo Zheng in their hearts now understood that it wasn't that this guy couldn't walk fast, but that he simply couldn't be bothered to compete with them …
According to Mansion Master Han Meng, there were a total of 327 imitation tablets in the Temple of Inquiry. p
These tablets were not built here for them to completely absorb the Dao essence within. Many Proof of Divinity cultivators who had observed them for a hundred years might not be able to comprehend the Dao essence within. Any Proof of Divinity cultivator only needed to obtain the recognition of these imitation tablets to be accepted by the Heaven-containing Mansion! p
This requirement seemed simple, but there weren't many people who could truly accomplish it. Every year, twenty people would appear, which was already considered a lot. This year was the year of the Heaven-containing Mansion's examination, so there might be more people, but it was estimated that there wouldn't be more than thirty people. p
"This … is the tablet of the Five Elements Divine Dao," Luo Zheng stood below the first tablet and looked up. p
From this tablet, Luo Zheng could feel a thick Dao essence of the Five Elements. p
The Five Elements Divine Dao was one of the most widely cultivated Divine Daos in the Realm of the Gods. Many martial artists had already climbed up the stairs. The higher they climbed, the stronger the Dao Essence pressure they faced. However, there were more than a dozen Proof of Divinity martial artists on the stairs. In order to seize a better position, they silently and slowly climbed up. p
"Get lost!" p
One of the Proof of Divinity cultivators pushed another person down. These stairs were very narrow and could only accommodate one person. When they had climbed to the limit, they naturally had to fight for the same position. Because of this, a dispute arose. p

Chapter 1787
The Dao tablets in the entire Ask God Temple were arranged according to the same distance. p
Therefore, the closer one got to the Dao tablets, the greater the influence of the Dao, but it also allowed one to communicate with the tablets more clearly. p
But if one got too close, they would be injured by the Qi. All the Proof of Divinity martial artists would keep a suitable distance, which was the limit of their tolerance …
Most of the Proof of Divinity martial artists had similar soul strength, but the steps on the Dao tablets were narrow and the space was limited, so those who entered first could often choose a good position, and those who came later could only be further away from the Dao tablets. p

The Proof of Divinity martial artist called Han Zhen was in front of the sixth Dao tablet on the right. p
The words on the Dao tablet emitted a faint blue light, exuding a biting chill. p

The stele of Dao emitted layers of purple mist, which transformed into all kinds of unique shapes. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng became a little interested in this Dao tablet. Therefore, he climbed up the stairs of this Dao tablet. p
The moment he stepped on this flight of stairs, the Dao essence emitted from the other Dao tablets was rejected by the Dao essence of this purple Dao tablet. The Dao essence of this Dao tablet was overwhelming and surrounded Luo Zheng. p
After Luo Zheng climbed more than 20 steps, he saw a woman in white in front of him. p
Luo Zheng had gone before this woman. She should be an elite disciple of some third-tier family. She was ranked in the 40s because she surpassed Luo Zheng in the 40s. p

"Isn't the Purple Qi Divine Dao something that only women can cultivate?" p

Everyone's eyes were full of puzzlement …
People who had the Extreme Purple Yin Body exuded a different aura from ordinary experts. For example, the woman in white was the eldest daughter of the Xue family of the Liuren Domain. Although the Xue family was only a third-tier family, Xue Mei was ranked forty-first on the list by virtue of her own body. p
But when they saw Luo Zheng before, they believed that Luo Zheng could not have the Extreme Purple Yin Body …
Luo Zheng did not know that his strange behavior had attracted so much attention. After all, each Dao tablet was some distance away from each other. He did not know what these people were talking about. p
After the white light on the Snow Dao tablet disappeared, Luo Zheng no longer paid attention to it, but continued to go up! p
Xue Mei had previously dissuaded Luo Zheng, but this person's ability was far stronger than hers. Now that Luo Zheng insisted on going up, she naturally could not stop him. p
As Luo Zheng continued to climb, he got closer and closer to the Tablet of Dao. The purple gas that lingered around the Tablet of Dao was like muddy water that was being stirred up. It began to constantly surge! p
"Hu hu hu …"
Under the tumbling of the Purple Qi, dozens of sharp sword shadows formed an ingenious sword array and shot toward Luo Zheng. p
"Be careful …" Xue Mei whispered. She knew that Luo Zheng was very capable, but she also understood that caution was the key to success. How many geniuses had fallen because of conceit? p
When the sword array came down, Luo Zheng did not have the slightest intention of dodging. p

Chapter 1788
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped into the Divine City, he had been very cautious. p
Since the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao was listed as a forbidden Dao, Luo Zheng would not use it easily, let alone when there were many people around. p
In the Hanyue Building, Luo Zheng had used his divine thoughts to launch a soul attack. He had also deliberately erased the sharpness in his soul. It could only be regarded as a strengthened version of the "Soul-Petrifying Thorn" …
Facing the sword formation formed by Taoist Connotation, Luo Zheng did not dodge. He let the sword shadows enter his mind. p
When these sword shadows gathered in his mind and formed a sword formation, the small golden sword on the surface of Luo Zheng's soul instantly shone brightly. The golden light reverberated in his mind, and all the sword shadows were shattered by the golden light! p
The eyes were the windows to the soul. When the golden light flashed, Luo Zheng closed his eyes and covered the golden light from his soul in his mind. p
When he opened his eyes again, everything had returned to normal …
This scene made Xue Mei, who was not far away, dumbfounded. Seeing that Luo Zheng did not dodge, she thought that Luo Zheng had some treasure to protect his soul. According to Luo Zheng's talent, this was very likely. p
She did not expect that Luo Zheng did not have such a treasure at all. Instead, he let the "sword formation" formed by Taoist Connotation penetrate his mind. p
Just when she was worried about whether Luo Zheng would become a fool, Luo Zheng had calmed down and stepped onto a new level as if there was no one else around. p
"No wonder the old man on the floating island thinks so highly of him." Xue Mei sighed secretly. Thinking of her own fate, she could not help but sigh. p

There were purple nine-story pagodas, thousands of giant swords, double-headed snakes, and all kinds of things that were transformed by the purple mist. However, no matter what shape they took, they were all transformed by the pressure of the Taoist Connotation, so their power was not far from the truth. p
When these things entered his mind, Luo Zheng couldn't help but close his eyes and exterminate them with his soul pressure. p
It was just that there were too many of them. Wave after wave, they poured into his mind. Luo Zheng simply closed his eyes and used his spiritual awareness to explore the surrounding environment. He continued to climb the stairs and quickened his pace. p
There were hundreds of steps below each stele. Most of the Proof of Divinity martial artists could only stop at the 50th to 60th step. For example, Xue Mei stopped at the 52nd step. If she took another step forward, the pressure she faced would multiply, and she could no longer bear it. p
But Luo Zheng had already reached the 90th stair with his speed...
"Does that guy want to climb to the top?" p
"In theory, the closer one is to the Dao tablet, the easier it is to communicate with it. But the pressure is also greater. How can one comprehend it?" p
"You're mistaken! Let me remind you again, Luo Tianxing is a man, he can't cultivate the Violet Cloud Divine Dao! " p
"..."
For a time, many Proof of Divinity martial artists couldn't concentrate on comprehending the Dao tablet. Instead, they wasted their attention on Luo Zheng. They all wanted to know what this guy wanted to do. p
Luo Zheng rushed to the high platform on the top under everyone's gaze. p
The moment he stood on the high platform, the Violet Cloud on the Dao tablet became more and more restless, like a tide flocking. Before it could take shape, it had already poured into Luo Zheng's mind. p
Luo Zheng's mind was like a black hole. The Violet Cloud kept pouring in, but it didn't come back! p
After a while. p
Probably because the Taoist Connotation on the Dao tablet had been consumed too much, the Taoist Connotation finally calmed down and no longer rushed to Luo Zheng. Instead, it continued to flow slowly around the surface of the Dao tablet, without the slightest threat. p
Luo Zheng just opened his eyes and quietly looked at the Dao tablet in front of him...
"The Great Violet Cloud Art, turning the Violet Cloud into an auspicious holy realm, forming a world of its own. There are three ways in which all things move. It is the beginning of the rule of fate. Don't push the Saint Fate, don't welcome my birth..."
The purple characters recorded on the Dao tablet were very beautiful. The person who made this Dao tablet should be a woman. p
After careful observation, Luo Zheng vaguely understood the magical power of the Great Violet Cloud Art. p
The Great Violet Cloud Art was actually another manifestation of Zhenqi, but it inherited some of the abilities of the Chaotic Qi, such as creating a world. p
The "Extreme Violet Realm" in the Great Yan Universe was an isolated world. The rules in that world were completely different from the rules in other big worlds. At that time, it was created by a True God who cultivated the "Great Violet Cloud Art". p
The world constructed by the Great Violet Cloud Art was different from the inner world. When one cultivated it to a deep level, one could create a world in the Divine Domain alone. Although this world couldn't be compared with the universe, it was still stronger than the ordinary inner world. Therefore, a True God who cultivated the Great Violet Cloud Art to the "Great Perfection" could also be called a quasi-sage. p
The control of the Great Violet Cloud Art was the most mysterious. A True God who cultivated this Shinto Dao was best at sword formations. Therefore, the cultivation method that Xi Youqin received in the Extreme Violet Realm was the sword formation method. p
"Although Luo Yan's Extreme Violet Yin Body was bestowed by Father, I don't know if she cultivated the Great Violet Cloud Art or if she turned to other Shinto Daos. I don't know if Father has other arrangements for this … "

"Boom!" p

Chapter 1789
In Luo Zheng's eyes, two fiery red halos were still constantly rotating.
In this process, those disordered Dao accumulations became orderly in his eyes, and a feeling of enlightenment sank into the depths of his soul.
At this moment, Luo Zheng seemed to be able to see the true essence of the Purple Clouds Divine Dao!

Luo Zheng made this judgment in an instant.
There were 3,000 Divine Daos, and each Divine Dao was a completely different path. The Great Purple Qi Art could evolve into thousands of God-slaying Sword Formations, and it could transform into several extremely powerful True Spirits. After the light of the Infinite Ruler dissipated, the two golden rings in Luo Zheng's eyes also disappeared.

The several Dao Imparting True Gods and Han Meng looked at each other in dismay. From their expressions, they couldn't make up their minds.
"What do we do? Do we announce that Luo Zheng has passed? "Old Man Chen asked.
Han Meng's face was filled with a strange expression. "He has indeed passed. But this Purple to the Divine Dao …. Logically speaking, he shouldn't be able to cultivate it!"
"That's not the worst part." Another Dao Imparting True God reminded him.
Han Meng sighed. "I also thought of that."
Although there were many martial artists in the Realm of the Gods who possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body, there weren't many who truly had the talent to cultivate it. Moreover, most of them were women.
Aunt Hanjiu was the secondary sage of the Han Family. Her status and authority were second only to the Han Family's Saint Emperor. She could also recruit disciples from the floating island, but the most basic requirement was to be able to cultivate the Great Purple Qi Art.
According to the rules of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, once a martial artist was recognized by the Dao stele of the Purple to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, they wouldn't enter the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. Instead, they would be gifted to Aunt Hanjiu.
This way, the Heaven-Containing Prefecture had nothing to do with it.
Previously, Han Meng had been afraid that the little girl in green from the main family would take Luo Tianxing away. Now, another layer of worry had appeared.
"If I'd known, I wouldn't have let this kid participate in the exam." Han Meng shook his head and sighed heavily.
The Heaven-Containing Prefecture's rules were strict, but there were always people who could make exceptions. If he'd known that Luo Zheng had been recognized by the Dao stele of the Purple to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, he would've found a reason to make an exception and directly accept Luo Tianxing!
However, they were all curious as to which Divine Dao Luo Zheng was most suited to cultivate. Moreover, during the exam, they would be able to observe Luo Zheng's talent more clearly. They hadn't expected such a strange thing to happen.
"We'll keep this matter under wraps." Old Man Chen's eyes flashed with determination.
The other Dao Imparting True Gods also looked hesitant. Aunt Hanjiu was a lofty existence to everyone present. The consequences of deceiving her would be difficult to bear.
Hearing Old Man Chen's words, Han Meng nodded fiercely. "Let's not announce it for now … Hm? What else does Luo Tianxing want? "
Inside the Temple of Inquiry …
When the crowd saw that Luo Zheng had been recognized by the Dao stele, on one hand, they were very envious. On the other, they felt that it was strange.
What made everyone feel even more strange was that Luo Zheng had clearly been recognized by the Divine Dao. Why hadn't the Master announced it yet?
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't foolishly wait for Han Meng to announce that he had been accepted. Compared to entering the Heaven-Containing Mansion, Luo Zheng was more anxious to verify the jade ruler in his mind.
Since this jade ruler could incorporate the Purple Clouds Divine Dao, it must be able to incorporate other Divine Daos!
Although these Dao steles weren't the real ones, they were only replicas made by some True Gods. However, the Dao essence in them was genuine. If he could incorporate another Divine Dao, it would be of more help to Luo Zheng.
He continued to jump down the stairs, and when he passed by Xue Mei, he smiled faintly.
From the beginning, Xue Mei had planned to comprehend the Dao stele honestly, hoping to be recognized by the Dao stele. Now that Luo Zheng had taken the lead, she felt very disappointed. Looking at Luo Zheng's back, she could only sigh softly. She sighed in her heart. Everyone was different after all.
After Luo Zheng jumped down from the top, he quickly chose another Dao stele.
Raging flames were erupting from the Stele of Dao. Streams of flames were swirling around the flamboyant words. Before Luo Zheng climbed up the stairs, he could already feel an extremely hot aura coming from it.
"Fledgling Flame Divine Dao …"
This Fledgling Flame Divine Dao was the origin of all Strange Flames in the world. It was also the purest flame.
By relying on the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao, one could comprehend all kinds of wonderful Strange Flames. There were also many people who cultivated the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao.
On the stairs of this Dao stele, there were already three Divine Realm martial artists. One of them was the second on the list, Han Xuan.
When Han Xuan saw Luo Zheng climb up the stairs, his eyes flashed slightly. This guy seemed to want to be recognized by the second Dao stele.
As long as one was recognized by a Dao stele, they would have the qualifications to enter the Heaven-Containing Mansion. In the Heaven-Containing Mansion's examination, there were indeed some extremely talented geniuses who had done so. However, the number of such geniuses was extremely small. In history, there had only been four such cases.
Now, Luo Zheng seemed to be planning to do so …
What Han Xuan didn't know was that Luo Zheng's goal wasn't just the two Dao steles. If he was given enough time, he was afraid that he would absorb all three hundred Dao steles in the Temple of Inquiry into the Immeasurable Ruler!
Seeing Luo Zheng climb up the stairs, Han Xuan and the others made way for Luo Zheng to climb up.
The Divine Realm martial artists present simply stopped comprehending. They wanted to see what Luo Tianxing wanted to do.
"Huff huff …"
Just like the Purple Cloud Divine Dao, when Luo Zheng climbed to the top of the Dao stele, the turbulent Dao Accumulations from the Dao stele turned into fiery birds. They flapped their wings and pounced on Luo Zheng.
These Dao Accumulations turned into flames that could burn the soul. If one's soul was weak, they would be burned clean in an instant.

Chapter 1790
The endless stream of firebirds rushed into Luo Zheng's mind, ending up in the same way as the Dao Connotation of the Great Purple Qi Art.
When Luo Zheng closed his eyes, a sharp golden light flashed from the surface of his soul. Those fiery birds created by the Taoist flames were all killed in a blink of an eye.
While killing the firebirds that drilled into his mind, Luo Zheng also quietly observed the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind.
With his current means, he was still unable to control this ruler. Just now, the Immeasurable Ruler had activated its special ability on its own.
Now that Luo Zheng was paying attention, he discovered the reason behind it.
After the firebirds were crushed by Luo Zheng's soul pressure, they turned into scattered Dao Connotation power, which was quietly absorbed by the Immeasurable Ruler!
When the Immeasurable Ruler absorbed the remaining Dao Connotation power to a certain extent, a golden light suddenly burst out from one end of the Immeasurable Ruler …
"Buzz …"
The golden light moved from one end of the Immeasurable Ruler to the other in a flash, and finally stopped at a scale at the top of the Immeasurable Ruler!
"This scale should be the position reserved for the Fledgling Flame Supreme Dao by the Immeasurable Ruler." Luo Zheng understood. Indeed, as Luo Zheng had guessed, these 3,000 scales corresponded to 3,000 Divine Daos, and the position of each Divine Dao had been set long ago!
When the light stopped at that scale, another blazing light burst out and shrouded Luo Zheng's mind.
When Luo Zheng opened his eyes again, a pair of golden light circles were embedded in his pupils, just like the Fiery Golden Eyes!
"Hualala …"
With Luo Zheng's eyesight, he stared at the Dao tablet of the Fledgling Flame Supreme Dao in front of him. The pure flames on the Dao tablet were like waves that constantly surged, and at the same time, the Dao Connotation became chaotic!
When others saw this Dao Connotation, it was incomparably chaotic, but in Luo Zheng's eyes, he directly saw through everything that was contained within the Dao Connotation.
"The Fledgling Flame Supreme Dao … is the source of fire. All Fire Laws are derived from the Fledgling Flame. This Divine Dao is different from the Great Purple Qi Art. There are a total of four levels. The first level is to control fire, the second level is to create fire, the third level is to create fire, and the fourth level is to create fire …"
In the eyes of others, the Dao Connotation was incomparably mysterious, and it would be difficult to completely understand it even after ten thousand years. However, in Luo Zheng's eyes, these mysteries were incomparably simple!
Although Luo Zheng's perception far surpassed that of ordinary geniuses, it was impossible for him to reach such a level. That sudden epiphany allowed Luo Zheng to see through the true meaning of the Dao tablet in a short period of time. All of this was because of the Infinite Ruler …
Not long after, the light on the Infinity Ruler dimmed, and the circle of light inlaid in Luo Zheng's pupils also began to dim. Finally, it returned to normal, and a bright yellow flame symbol condensed on one end of the Infinity Ruler.
"Immeasurable Ruler of the Immeasurably Of God 'Divine divine of the Zheng dao dao Ims Of the Immeasurable Of the Of the Divine Ruler Ruler's's.
Was Of course of the Of Great Divine Of Divine Dao? What would happen if all the Of Great Divine Dao Light were integrated into the Immeasurable Ruler?
However, it was not easy to gather all the Of Great Divine Dao Light. Luo Zheng had yet to see a real Dao Stele with his own eyes. Although there were more than three hundred Dao Steles in front of him, the Dao Connotation within them was not perfect.
"Since it can record imperfect Dao Connotations, theoretically speaking, it can also record the Dao Connotations of other True Gods?"
At this moment, Luo Zheng made this conjecture in his heart. However, this conjecture could not be verified. If he had the chance to spar with a True God in the future, he could take the risk and give it a try.
When the Dao Connotation of the Fledgling Flame Dao Stele began to calm down, a fiery red pillar of light shot into the sky. At the same time, a ball of fire escaped from the Dao Stele and enveloped Luo Zheng. This fire was not scorching. Instead, it emitted a trace of warmth.
"He was recognized by the second Dao Stele …"
"Ai, even in my dreams, I want to be recognized by a Dao Stele. I've tried so hard to comprehend it, but I can't even begin to comprehend it. Why is it as simple as drinking water for this kid?"
"He shouldn't have come to the Heaven Containing Palace. He should have gone straight to the floating island. Why does he have to compare himself with us …"
The Proof Divinity experts in the Temple of Inquiry were full of envy. There was an indescribable feeling in their hearts. These Proof Divinity experts came from second-tier and third-tier clans. They were also the proud sons of heaven in their clans. Every movement of theirs had an innate pride. However, Luo Zheng's actions had destroyed their self-confidence!
However, Han Meng and the others outside the Temple of Inquiry were now relieved.
If they announced Luo Zheng was recognized by the Great Purple Qi Art, they would have offended Aunt Han Jiu. In the future, when the news reached Aunt Han Jiu's ears, if the Quasi-sage was really angry, no one in the Han Clan would be able to withstand it.
Now that Luo Zheng was recognized by the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao, things became much easier to handle. As for what happened before, everyone acted as if they did not see it.
When Luo Zheng turned around and left the Dao Stele, Han Meng's voice once again lingered in the Temple of Inquiry. "Meng Mountain Village, Luo Tianxing has been recognized by the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao …"
Now, Luo Zheng was the second Proof Divinity expert to pass the Han Clan's examination.
Just now, after Han Zhen passed the examination, he left the Temple of Inquiry without looking back. The Temple of Inquiry was filled with the pressure of Dao. It put a lot of pressure on one's soul. No one wanted to stay here for too long.
However, Luo Zheng had no intention of leaving. Everyone silently watched as Luo Zheng jumped down the stairs of the Dao Stele. Then, they silently looked at the Dao Stele next to him.
Even if there were some geniuses who could comprehend several Divine Daos at the same time and be recognized by several Dao Steles, it was only limited to a few specific Divine Daos. However, Luo Zheng did not choose. He just followed the order …
"No way, this guy didn't choose at all! One by one? "Han Xuan also opened his mouth wide and stared at Luo Zheng dumbly.
"It's impossible for there to be such a heaven-defying guy. Maybe he just happened to comprehend the Great Purple Qi Art and the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao. As for the third Divine Dao, he might not be able to comprehend it …" A Proof Divinity expert next to him tried his best to deny it. In fact, he also did not believe in such a coincidence. However, Luo Zheng's actions were too monstrous. It was beyond his knowledge, so he did not want to admit it.
"If he can be recognized by the third Dao Stele of Black Tortoise Divine Dao, I will be convinced!"
"… …"
Not long after, the third Dao Stele, which was the Dao Stele of Black Tortoise Divine Dao, flashed with a grayish white light …
Luo Zheng was recognized by the Dao Stele of Black Tortoise Divine Dao.
Seeing this, many Proof Divinity experts couldn't help but gulp. Some were shocked, while others rejoiced that they had seen this unbelievable scene.
Outside the Temple of Inquiry, Han Meng and the other imparting True Gods looked at each other. They could see great shock in the depths of each other's eyes. The older True Gods had lived for eight or nine Divine Epochs, while the younger ones were born in this Divine Epoch, but they had never heard of such a wondrous event.
"I heard that Han Cangyan of the main family became a Proof Divinity expert in the past. After returning from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he comprehended four Divine Daos in a single year. He's already an outstanding existence on the floating island. Luo Tianxing has already been recognized by three Divine Daos. He …" Han Meng's expression was grave, but his heart was filled with ecstasy.
He had to prove his worth to the main family once he entered the Heaven-Containing Palace. If he really did recommend someone as monstrous as Luo Tianxing, the main family would no longer give face to the branch family every time.
"He has no intention of stopping. He's already climbed to the fourth Dao Stele," the old man surnamed Chen said.
"Should we stop him? This is too exaggerated," another Imparting True God said.
Han Meng's face showed a trace of hesitation. In the end, he shook his head. Although he didn't know how Luo Tianxing had done it, if he didn't want to come out, Han Meng had no intention of stopping him.
And so, in front of everyone, Luo Zheng comprehended the fifth, sixth, and seventh Dao Stele …
In the end, this scene made everyone feel numb.
