Many Proof Divinity experts could only sigh as they explored their own Divine Daos, hoping to gain their recognition by comprehending the Dao Steles.
Soon, the third Proof Divinity expert appeared. It was Han Xuan of the Han Family, but there was no joy on his face. After all, a monstrous genius had come to the Shrine of Inquiry. Gaining the recognition of a Divine Dao was as simple as eating and drinking. He had worked so hard to gain the recognition of the Fledgling Flame Divine Dao. It was nothing. He just sighed, his face full of sadness, and turned to leave the Shrine of Inquiry.
Although Han Xuan was very experienced in dealing with people, he was still shocked.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1791
The Heaven-containing Ministry's examination in the God-Inquiring Palace usually lasted for seven days.
For the Proof of Divinity martial artists, the Heaven-containing Ministry's examination was not only an examination, but also a chance to cultivate.
Some Proof of Divinity martial artists usually listened to the teachings of True Gods outside the Heaven-containing Ministry. Now that they had entered the God-Inquiring Palace, they could comprehend the Dao on the tablets.
Although these tablets were forged by later generations, it was still a rare opportunity for these Proof of Divinity martial artists.
In seven days, more and more Proof of Divinity martial artists were recognized by the tablets. For these Proof of Divinity martial artists, they had stepped into the most important stage of their lives. Their future would be completely changed.
Because the chances of Proof of Divinity in the Heaven-containing Ministry were quite high, becoming a True God in the future was equivalent to obtaining eternal life and endless possibilities.
As for Luo Zheng …
In these few days, the Proof of Divinity martial artists were completely numb.
He tirelessly walked through the tablets and absorbed the Dao on them. He was recognized by the tablets again and again.
Other than the Great Purple Qi Art, there were some Divine Paths that required special conditions and physiques to cultivate. However, Luo Zheng directly bypassed these conditions and was recognized by the tablets as usual.
This could no longer be described as a miracle. In the eyes of others, Luo Zheng seemed to be using some kind of method to cheat. However, this method was unheard of and was already frightening.
The Heaven-containing Ministry did not stop Luo Zheng from doing this. Although Luo Zheng disturbed the Dao on the tablets in a short period of time, it did not cause much damage to the tablets. Now that Luo Zheng's performance was outstanding, the Heaven-containing Ministry would be more confident in recommending him to the main family.
It took Luo Zheng about an hour to absorb the Dao essence of a Dao stele into the Immeasurable Ruler. He didn't rest for seven days and seven nights, and he didn't stop to absorb the Dao essence of 260 Dao steles.
On the last day, Luo Zheng's face was full of exhaustion.
Every time he absorbed a Stele of Doctrine, he had to resist the attack of the Doctrine Connotation. Even though Luo Zheng's soul strength was comparable to a Real God's, his soul power was constantly depleting. Without any rest, his soul power was gradually depleting. Naturally, he was very tired.
When Han Meng announced the end of the examination, Luo Zheng helplessly looked at the remaining Dao tablets. The Dao tablets standing in the distance were still emitting light, but due to the rules, he could only follow the crowd and leave the Palace of Inquiry.
Among the one hundred thousand martial artists who had verified their divinity, a total of sixty-seven people had obtained the recognition of the Dao stele.
Compared to last year's less than 20 people, this year's number had tripled. Even if it was the new year of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's recruitment exam, this number was already quite good.
Han Meng and the other imparting true gods were quite satisfied with this number. The one who gave them the most confidence was still Luo Zheng.
Now, Han Meng had the urge to contact his family and send Luo Zheng to the floating island!
Every sixteen years, the family would send people to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture to recruit a batch of people to the floating island to cultivate. It had been fifteen years since the last recruitment.
Next year, the family would come again to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture to select people. Not only would they select a portion of the true gods, but they would also pay attention to a few outstanding martial artists who had verified their divinity. Therefore, the year before the family's selection was the year of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination. These martial artists also hoped that they would be lucky enough to be selected by the family and enter the floating island. They would then join the core power of the divine realm and soar to the heavens.
However, the Han family branch also had special recommendation rights. If they met an extremely outstanding person, they didn't have to wait sixteen years. They only needed to send someone to the family to apply. However, the prerequisite was that this person had to be exceptional in some way.
The martial artists who passed the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination were arranged to stay in a courtyard in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. The others were dismissed and could only wait until next year to participate in the examination again.
The construction of this courtyard was very elegant. A few small bridges were built on top of an exquisite pond. In the pond, there was a school of small fish with golden scales swimming happily. Pink lotus flowers decorated the pond, and they would never wither.
The sixty-seven martial artists in the courtyard were the lucky ones who passed the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination this time.
Some of them, such as Han Zhen and Han Xuan, had been waiting here for five or six days because they had been recognized by the Dao Tablet on the first day.
Luo Zheng sat upright on a large rock on the side of a rockery, his eyes tightly closed as he meditated and recuperated.
Now, his soul had completely sunk into a deep sleep. This was the Soul Returning Technique that the Evil Old Man had taught Luo Zheng. Some of the Evil Old Man's cultivation techniques weren't flashy. They didn't even have a glorious origin. However, for a true god like the Evil Old Man, they were all extremely useful techniques.
For example, the Soul Returning Technique was to reserve a bit of soul power to maintain a minimum level of vigilance while the soul sank into a deep sleep. This way, one could quickly recover soul power in a short period of time.
Luo Zheng was really too tired these days. He needed a good rest to replenish the soul power that he had consumed.
The other martial artists gathered in twos and threes. Most of them looked very excited. It seemed that they were very surprised that they had passed the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination.
Xue Mei had only been recognized by the Dao Tablet at the last moment of the seventh day. She had also passed the examination of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. As for Hu Lan Zhuo, who had a conflict with Luo Zheng, he had passed the examination on the fifth day.
After waiting in the courtyard for a day, the Heaven-containing Prefecture gave them their own residences as well as some very basic items. From now on, they were considered members of the Heaven-containing Prefecture and could cultivate in the Heaven-containing Prefecture. Every day, they could also listen to the teachings of the True Gods.
Luo Zheng, who had settled down in his residence, continued to explore and absorb the Divine Dao's Taoist Connotation.
Luo Zheng sat upright on the ground. With a light clap of his hands, a wisp of Purple Qi floated out.
This Purple Qi split into several faintly discernible paths. These paths could be randomly distributed into a unique arrangement. According to the arrangement of these Purple Qi, some mysterious formations could be created.
"The Great Purple Qi Art itself is also a manifestation of the rules. Using the rules within it, one can create a sword formation. No wonder Xi Youqin could master the God-slaying Sword Formation," Luo Zheng said calmly. After comprehending the Taoist Connotation, Luo Zheng could use the Great Purple Qi Art at will.
Then Luo Zheng clapped his hands, and the Purple Qi transformed into a Winged Dragon in the shape of Zi Qing. This Winged Dragon crouched on his finger, and with a light flick of his finger, the Winged Dragon flew out. After circling around the room, it turned back into a ball of Purple Qi and returned to Luo Zheng's hand!
"This ruler of yours is simply unreasonable. I've been in the God Realm for so many years, but I've never heard of such a divine object. Even a first-grade grandmist artifact doesn't have this kind of ability. Absorbing three thousand Divine Dao, what the hell is this thing …"
In the past few days, the Extreme Evil Old Man had witnessed the entire process of the Immeasurable Ruler absorbing the Divine Dao. Every time he activated the Immeasurable Ruler, Luo Zheng had to draw the Taoist Connotation into his mind. This Taoist Connotation also posed a threat to the Extreme Evil Old Man's remnant soul. In the end, the Extreme Evil Old Man had no choice but to hide in a corner and quietly watch this scene.
Just like that, the Extreme Evil Old Man watched as the Immeasurable Ruler absorbed the Divine Dao, turning into tiny symbols on the jade ruler …
After living in the God Realm for so long, the Extreme Evil Old Man could be considered half an old monster. He had long heard of some of the powerful treasures in the God Realm. Those treasures were in the hands of the various Saints. For example, the Regicide Sky Blade of the Han Family's Saint Emperor was an extremely rare grandmist artifact. However, it was impossible for it to have such an exaggerated ability. If Luo Zheng were to observe the Divine Dao, wouldn't he be able to grasp all three thousand Divine Dao in the God Realm?
As soon as the Extreme Evil Old Man's voice fell, the Purple Qi in Luo Zheng's hand dissipated, and in its place were wisps of greyish-white Taoist Connotation.
These wisps of greyish-white Taoist Connotation quickly condensed into a small round shield. A large number of hexagonal patterns appeared on the round shield, resembling a tortoise shell. This was the Black Tortoise Divine Dao's protective divine ability.
As Luo Zheng continued to experiment, a voice suddenly sounded from outside, "Luo Tianxing, the Manor Head invites you in."

Chapter 1792
Han Meng was sitting in the main pavilion of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. Beside him were two other people, both of whom were peak middle true gods.
They weren't True Gods of the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, but leaders of the Han Family's branches.
The Han Family's branches had many branches and leaves. Although they weren't as powerful as the other clans on the floating islands, they were still a first-tier clan. Whether it was the strength of their true gods or the number of their members, they were far superior to other second-tier and third-tier clans.
There were currently twenty-seven branches in the Han Family, led by twenty-seven branch leaders. These two were the leader of the ninth branch, Han Chen, and the leader of the twelfth branch, Han Yu. Han Zhen, who had just entered the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, was Han Chen's son.
"The news from Mirror City has already been confirmed. This Luo Tianxing's background is indeed suspicious," Han Chen said with a frown.
Ever since Luo Zheng had made a name for himself in Hanyue Tower, many factions had tried to investigate Luo Zheng's background. However, Luo Zheng had provided too little information. Even after many investigations, Luo Zheng's background was still a blank sheet of paper. There were no clues at all, so he could only be replaced by "mysterious youth" on the list.
However, during the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination, Luo Zheng had introduced himself, saying that he was from Meng Village in the Vast Sky Domain.
At that time, Han Jia had sent people to investigate Luo Zheng's background.
Han Jia's efficiency wasn't low. In just a few days, they'd found the Divine Citizens of Meng Village.
At first, the answers given by the people of Mengshan Village were exactly the same as what Luo Zheng had said. He was indeed from Mengshan Village, but after a thorough investigation, they quickly uncovered some of the truth, including the conflict between Mengshan Village and Sunmoon Village, and even the return of the Evil Old Man.
At the same time, they also found out that Luo Zheng wasn't from Meng Village. He'd appeared in Meng Village less than half a year ago, and the Divine Citizens of Meng Village also didn't know about Luo Zheng's background.
Thus, Luo Zheng's identity became very suspicious. After all, Luo Zheng was deliberately hiding his identity.
"I think so too!" Han Yu also nodded. "This kid deliberately hid his identity and infiltrated our Han Family. I'm afraid he's up to no good."
The factions in Divine Dominion all developed according to the family model. They paid a lot of attention to the background of the people they nurtured. Although Han Jia wanted to recruit more geniuses, they would also nurture outsiders, but the background of these people had to be thoroughly investigated.
Except for Luo Zheng, of the sixty-seven people who were accepted into the Heaven-containing Mansion, the backgrounds of the others were quite clear. Their ancestors could be traced back to several generations.
Luo Zheng had such a terrifying talent, but his background was like a blank piece of paper, which was indeed very dazzling.
"How could there be so many conspiracies?" Han Meng smiled. "Perhaps Luo Tianxing is a prodigy from one of the Floating Island's wealthy families?"
Hanmeng also didn't believe that Luo Zheng had been born from an ordinary god. It was simply impossible for such a character to be born from a small and remote mountain village. The Realm of the Gods had developed for so many years, and all the resources had been monopolized. Talent was also constantly passed down through the bloodline. For a poor family to produce a noble son had already become a legend.
"Are there still any Floating Island's wealthy families willing to be unruly? Even if he really is unruly, would the main family be willing to accept him? "Han Yu asked again.
Han Meng looked straight at Han Yu and smiled faintly. "Since the two of you have such thoughts, how do you propose we deal with Luo Tianxing? Expel him from the Heaven-containing Mansion? As long as the two of you and 'Han Daozhi' agree, I will not hesitate in the slightest! "
At this point, Han Yu and Han Chen didn't utter a word again …
"To put it bluntly, the two of you just want me, Han Meng, to take on this responsibility. You want to recommend Luo Tianxing to the main family, but you're also afraid of his background. If something happens in the future, you don't want to be implicated." Han Meng's eyes flashed. "Since that's the case, I, Han Meng, will take on this responsibility!"
Han Meng's words had indeed exposed Han Yu and Han Chen's worries.
If the Han Family still couldn't find a suitable candidate, the main family would most likely make a move. When that time came, the ones to suffer would be the twenty-seven branches of the Han Family.
Through the Heaven-containing Mansion's examination, they had indeed seen Luo Zheng's talent. This kind of person could no longer be measured by the standards of ordinary geniuses. Even among the wealthy families of the floating islands, there had never been such a heaven-defying cultivator who had proven divinity!
In their eyes, proving divinity cultivators were like small sprouts that had just sprouted out of the ground. Luo Zheng was a little too strong. Once he condensed a godhood in the future, wouldn't it be natural for him to be chosen as a disciple of the Sage Emperor?
"Brother Han Meng, you're treating us as outsiders. Our Han Family branches have always advanced and retreated together. How could we let brother Han Meng take on the responsibility alone?"
"Yes, we'll ask Luo Tianxing in detail later. Perhaps things aren't as bad as we think?"
The two of them said somewhat awkwardly.
At this time, a voice came from outside the main pavilion. "Luo Tianxing has been brought here!"
Han Meng nodded. "Let him in."
Before long, Luo Zheng walked in.
Han Meng smiled at Luo Zheng and said politely, "Tianxing, please sit here."
Luo Zheng nodded and sat on a chair at the side. At the same time, he asked, "May I know why the Mansion Master has summoned me?"
In fact, Luo Zheng knew that since he had told them about his background, they would definitely investigate.
Although he had told them before leaving Meng Village, the Divine Citizens of Meng Village had never even entered the Divine City. He was afraid that he would not be able to hide from the people of the Heaven-containing Mansion.
Today, Han Meng had personally invited him over. He was afraid that they were going to investigate his background.
However, Luo Zheng had already made up his mind. He did not panic at all. Instead, he asked Han Meng.
When Han Meng was asked by Luo Zheng, an unnatural expression appeared on his face. That expression disappeared in a flash, and he laughed. "To tell you the truth, the disciples of the Heaven-containing Mansion are treated like family members. However, due to the rules passed down by our ancestors, there are some backgrounds that must be investigated."
Luo Zheng understood. Just as he had guessed, the Heaven-containing Mansion had really gone to Meng Village, and they had already found out that something was wrong …
"From what we have learned from Meng Village, it seems to be different from what you have said." Han Meng said again.
Han Yu and Han Chen also looked at Luo Zheng, as if they wanted to find some clues from his face.
"I am not from Meng Village." Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and openly admitted it. The three True Gods were not used to Luo Zheng's honest answer. They were slightly stunned.
Han Meng continued to ask, "Then where are you from?"
It was not important where Luo Zheng came from. What was important was that they needed a real and trustworthy background.
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I can't tell you."
Of course, he would not admit that he came from the Great Yan Realm and that he was the son of Luo Xiao in order to enter the Heaven-containing Mansion. Furthermore, Luo Zheng could not fabricate an identity. With the Han family's ability, they would definitely verify his identity. It would be very easy for them to see through him, so he could only answer honestly.
Hearing this, Han Yu said coldly, "The Heaven-containing Mansion does not accept people of unknown origins."
Han Chen agreed. "That is indeed the case. I hope you can cherish this opportunity."
There was a hint of threat in their words. They were telling Luo Zheng that if he did not explain his origins, he would most likely be expelled from the Heaven-containing Mansion.
"If that is the case, then it is truly regrettable. I am afraid I will have to choose to leave the Heaven-containing Mansion." Luo Zheng's face was filled with regret. Joining the Heaven-containing Mansion was only a shortcut for Luo Zheng to reach the floating island. If this shortcut did not work, then Luo Zheng would choose another path.
But revealing his identity was too important. He could not reveal his identity for the sake of a mere Heaven-containing Mansion!
As soon as he said this, Han Chen and Han Yu choked. They wanted to threaten Luo Zheng with the Heaven-containing Mansion's qualifications, but they did not expect him to care at all.
Han Meng glanced at Han Chen and Han Yu. This Luo Tianxing was not an idiot. If he had such heaven-defying talent, which Divine Mansion would not want him? Would they care about the qualifications of the Heaven-containing Mansion?
Just as Han Meng was pondering what reason to use to convince Luo Zheng, a figure floated down from the sky above the Heaven-containing Province and slowly descended into the main pavilion of the Heaven-containing Province.
"This person can actually fly in the divine realm …"

Chapter 1793
In the divine realm, only upper true gods had the right to fly.
Luo Zheng didn't understand the rules set by the sages. It wasn't that godhood experts, lower true gods, and middle true gods couldn't fly. It was just that their abilities were restricted.
However, Old Man Evil's explanation cleared up Luo Zheng's doubts.
Those who entered the sea of time first wanted to solidify their position, so they would use all sorts of methods to prevent other families from rising.
Thus, in the middle ages, several families came together and set this rule on the Governing Slate, limiting upper true gods to the ability to fly. Only peak true gods could use the law of space …
This way, the latecomers lost many opportunities. After all, there were many forbidden areas in the divine realm that couldn't be explored.
Thus, there were very few latecomers who ascended to sainthood!
The person in the sky descended, landing firmly in the main pavilion.
After he landed, a faint aura of dignity spread out. This person didn't release any soul pressure, but he already had a natural aura. This aura didn't come from him, but from his inner world.
The inner world of an upper True God had already been completed. The inner world had become a huge whole, which was why it gave off such a majestic feeling. It was just like how Shi Xiaoqiao, who was on the second floor of the dungeon, gave Luo Zheng an indescribable feeling of mystery.
This person looked very young, but there was a faint trace of lethargy on his face. He must have lived for a long time. His bone age was probably unimaginably old!
Han Meng, Han Chen, and Han Yu all stood up and bowed to that person. Luo Zheng naturally stood up as well. Although it looked like the Heaven-containing Mansion wanted to expel him, at the very least, Luo Zheng would not forget the basic etiquette.
This person was the Han Daozhi mentioned by Han Meng. He was also the patriarch of one of the Han family's twenty-seven branches.
A few days ago, Han Daozhi had heard about Luo Tianxing. Later, he found out that this person's background was unknown. Today, he would be the one to make the decision.
After replying to everyone present, Han Daozhi kept sizing up Luo Zheng. When he saw Luo Zheng's clothes, he said, "What kind of treasure are these clothes you're wearing?"
When Han Daozhi asked this, Han Meng and the others also paid attention to Luo Zheng's clothes. Luo Zheng's dark-colored clothes looked very ordinary. Normal true gods wouldn't be able to see anything out of the ordinary.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Before he could answer Han Daozhi's question, Han Daozhi suddenly stretched out his hand. Sharp red spikes appeared between his fingers, and he grabbed at Luo Zheng's chest!
Even though Han Daozhi hadn't used his full strength, he was still an upper true god who had cultivated the divine way to a higher level. The speed of this sudden attack was shockingly fast. Not only was Luo Zheng unable to react in time, but even Han Meng and the others were completely caught off guard.
"Creak …"
This grab was like a black bear digging out its heart. Even if a true god was grabbed, their heart would be dug out instantly.
However, when Han Daozhi's sharp claws touched Luo Zheng's clothes, it only made a sharp friction sound. It didn't even leave a mark on Luo Zheng's clothes.
As the patriarch of one of the Han family's branches, Han Daozhi's strength was unquestionable. Just now, Han Daozhi's casual grab contained power that normal true gods couldn't resist. Many upper true gods started to condense nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier in order to break through to the consummate level. For example, Han Daozhi condensed six stars that contained the source of power!
When they saw this scene, the eyes of Han Meng, Han Chen, and Han Yu shone brightly!
They didn't think that Luo Zheng had such a protective treasure on him …
Luo Zheng took Han Daozhi's grab and retreated a few steps, his brows furrowed.
In Six Ren Divine City, martial artists were restricted by the rules of the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet. Martial artists couldn't casually attack each other. If two people challenged each other, they would have to pay one Shenwu Coin. However, this upper true god attacked as soon as they met, as if he wasn't restricted by the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet at all.
Luo Zheng was a bit angry, but he quickly controlled his emotions. Some people were born free from the rules. Luo Zheng wasn't naive enough to think that everyone in the Han family was kind. To the weak, there wasn't much to reason with. He entered Six Ren Divine City alone and displayed a certain amount of talent and strength. It was a risk in itself.
At this moment, he had to endure.
If he really angered these people, he might not even be able to leave Six Ren Divine City!
"What's the meaning of this?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
"Nothing. I just wanted to test you. Brat, your reaction isn't bad!" Han Daozhi chuckled. He didn't care whether Luo Zheng agreed or not. He turned into a blur and charged at Luo Zheng.
Han Daozhi's speed far surpassed Luo Zheng's reaction. Before Luo Zheng could react, a pair of sharp claws had already reached for Luo Zheng's chest.
On one hand, Han Daozhi wanted to test Luo Zheng's reaction and strength. On the other hand, he seemed to be trying to determine what material Luo Zheng's concentric garment was made of!
However, his actions were too rude. After all, Luo Zheng was still restricted by the Thousand Commandments Stone Tablet. He didn't know if he could fight back. Moreover, Han Daozhi was an upper true god. Toying with Luo Zheng was as easy as an eagle catching a chick.
Luo Zheng instinctively leaned back, his body twisting backward like a carp. At the same time, he took a sword step forward in an unimaginable way!
Han Daozhi's claws brushed past Luo Zheng's chest, but in the end, they didn't touch Luo Zheng at all. Luo Zheng really did dodge the attack …
"Hm?"
Han Daozhi didn't manage to grab Luo Zheng. He took a few steps forward and pinched his right hand, an awkward expression on his face.
Even though Han Daozhi was toying with Luo Zheng, it wasn't something a mere proof god expert could dodge. Forget Luo Zheng, even Han Meng, Han Chen, and the others wouldn't be able to dodge it easily!
"Senior, I've already passed the Heaven Containing Palace's examination. If you still want to test me …" Luo Zheng's tone had a hint of anger.
"Nice movement technique! Interesting! " Han Daozhi didn't listen to Luo Zheng at all. He turned around and pounced again, his speed increasing by 30%.
This time, Luo Zheng used his movement technique again, and even used Eight Zigzag Flying Mist. His entire body fluttered like a willow leaf. However, Han Daozhi's speed was far beyond what Luo Zheng could handle. Eight Zigzag Flying Mist was most afraid of absolute speed. Han Daozhi flashed behind Luo Zheng, one hand grabbing Luo Zheng's collar like lightning, and threw him far away!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's body spun in the air for a while, then smashed into the other side of the main pavilion.
Fortunately, Han Daozhi had gone easy on him. Luo Zheng twisted his body in midair, glided lightly on the ground for a while, then flipped over and stood back up!
However, as soon as he stood up, he felt a vast force coming from behind him. It pushed him a few steps forward and he almost fell to the ground again. He was in a very sorry state!
Han Meng, Han Chen, and Han Yu looked at each other, their hearts in turmoil.
They'd asked Han Daozhi to decide whether Luo Zheng should stay or leave. After all, he was the head of this branch. They never would've thought that Han Daozhi wouldn't ask about Luo Zheng's background, nor would he discuss Luo Zheng's future. Instead, he'd make a fool out of Luo Tianxing!
What was the point of an upper true god toying with a Proof of Divinity cultivator? Or rather, did he have any sense of shame?
Besides, Luo Zheng had already acted very tough. Wouldn't Han Daozhi's actions completely offend Luo Tianxing?
Han Meng's heart sank. After a moment's hesitation, he reached out and shouted, "Head of the family …"
Han Daozhi ignored Han Meng. He laughed with relish, "You still haven't fallen? Heh heh! I don't believe it! "With that, he turned into a gust of wind again and floated toward Luo Zheng!
This time, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a fierce light. He respected this person as an upper true god, but this person had gone too far. Luo Zheng didn't dodge. Instead, he clawed at Han Daozhi with one hand.
Han Daozhi's expression was one of disdain. A Proof of Divinity cultivator's meager strength was as weak as a baby's to him. He reached out with his left hand, wanting to push Luo Zheng's right hand away!
But as soon as he did, Luo Zheng's hand suddenly changed. It was like a pair of iron pliers, clamping down on Han Daozhi.
With Han Daozhi's reaction speed, it was actually very easy for him to avoid Luo Zheng.
However, he didn't do that. He waited until Luo Zheng had clamped down on his arm, then used brute force to break free from Luo Zheng's grasp.
But when he tried to break free, his expression suddenly froze!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1794
Luo Zheng didn't want to offend this guy.
But to be toyed with like an ant … even a Buddha would get angry!
As Luo Zheng attacked, the Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars in his body spun madly, and the surging power formed a torrent that instantly gathered in his hands!
Aside from being able to bear thirty-five percent of the power source himself, he also released the remaining seventy percent through the "Transcendence Mystical Power". This seventy percent power formed an invisible hand that grabbed the middle of his opponent's arm.
At this moment, it was as if Luo Zheng had three hands that grabbed Han Daozhi at the same time.
Han Daozhi was an upper level True God, and had already condensed six stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Six Stars, so he also had his own power source.
However, the six stars hidden in his body were much smaller than Luo Zheng's.
The Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars in Luo Zheng's body came from Saint Jiu's power source, and at that time, he had absorbed a third of Saint Jiu's power source. Though Luo Zheng's efficiency in using this power source was far inferior to Saint Jiu's, and far inferior to Han Daozhi's, the pure outpouring of power was still very terrifying!
Moreover, Han Daozhi completely didn't expect Luo Zheng to be able to explode with such terrifying power. He felt as if Luo Zheng's hands were a pair of iron pincers, and there was even an invisible hand grabbing him at the same time. Moreover, the power of that invisible hand was even stronger, and for a moment, his arms were creaking.
This power only lasted for an instant. Luo Zheng flipped over, twisted his hand, and suddenly threw Han Daozhi behind him!
Under the tremendous power, Han Daozhi couldn't resist at all. Countless question marks were written on his lethargic face as he saw his body fly away from Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed, involuntarily flying, and then heavily smashing into the wall of the main pavilion.
That wall seemed to be made of flour, instantly turning into dust, completely unable to stop Han Daozhi's momentum.
"Hong long, hong long, hong long, hong long …"
After smashing through seven or eight walls, relying on Han Daozhi's own power, he finally stopped his momentum and smashed into the ground …
Han Daozhi was covered in dust. As he flew through the air, he smashed through a few pots of fresh flowers. Several fresh and juicy flowers were stuck in his head. He looked both wretched and strange.
Lying on the ground, Han Daozhi was still in a daze. It took him seven or eight breaths to react. When he got up, he saw a room full of people behind him. Old Man Wu was teaching the students of the Heaven-Containing Palace.
When he saw Han Daozhi barge in, his mouth gaped open. Old Man Wu rarely saw Han Daozhi, but he knew that he was the head of a branch of the Han Family. He stammered, "M-Clan Head, are you alright?"
Many of the students were from the Han Family branch, and they also looked at their leader with strange expressions. Han Daozhi was an upper true god, and in Six Ren Divine City, who could make him look so miserable?
Everyone looked as if they had seen a ghost, or rather, it was easier to accept seeing a ghost than seeing this scene!
Han Daozhi shook his head and smiled helplessly. "It's nothing. I was just sparring with a little fellow. But there was a little accident …"
"Little, little fellow?" Old Man Wu's eyes twitched. He suddenly had a premonition that this little fellow couldn't be Luo Tianxing, right? But then again, that didn't seem likely. Luo Tianxing was a mere proof god. No matter how heaven-defying he was, there was no way he could've thrown Han Daozhi aside.
Han Daozhi was very embarrassed. He had lost a lot of face this time, so he got up and crawled back into the hole.
Old Man Wu wanted to confirm his guess. He took a few steps forward and stuck his head out to look.
A series of holes had been created in the walls of the Heaven-containing Palace. The holes were arranged in a straight line, so the old man surnamed Wu's gaze could directly fall on the main pavilion. With one look, his pupils suddenly widened!
Luo Tianxing stood in the middle of the hole in the main pavilion, looking over with a cold gaze ….
"It's, it's really that kid?"
Old Man Wu's face paled, and he couldn't say a word.
At this time, Old Man Wu was not the only one who was speechless. In the main pavilion, Han Meng, Han Chen, and Han Yu were also staring with their eyes wide open, their expressions stupefied.
In truth, when Han Daozhi had made his move, the three of them hadn't approved of it.
The Heaven-Containing Palace's goal was to send a qualified genius to the main family and resolve the crisis of trust the main family had in them. And from Luo Tianxing's performance, they could see that he had the qualifications. Han Daozhi's "probing" was completely meaningless.
But as the patriarch of a branch of the Han Family, Han Daozhi's personality wasn't stable. His bone age was already over thirty god eras, and he could be considered an "old man" of the Han Family. Although he'd lived for a long time, he wasn't a stable person.
Many True Gods, after their skeletal age exceeded a certain limit, would allow their appearance to age. Therefore, the Realm of the Gods was filled with 'old grandpa' level True Gods. Because they had lived for too long, their bodies would naturally exude a lethargic aura. They might be able to change their appearance to that of a youth in their teens, but it was completely incompatible with the lethargic aura they exuded!
But there were some women with long lifespans who could use secret techniques to get rid of their lethargy. No matter how long they'd lived, they were still beautiful and charming.
Han Daozhi hadn't gotten rid of his lethargy, but he still had an extremely youthful appearance. From this point alone, one could see that his personality wasn't reliable!
One's personality wasn't absolutely related to one's strength. Han Daozhi's talent wasn't bad, and he'd almost been taken in as a disciple by the Sage Emperor. Unfortunately, he'd been too careless and lost an extremely important opportunity in his life. In the end, he'd taken the initiative to leave Han Family's floating island and return to the Han Family branch, becoming its patriarch.
In a fit of anger, Luo Zheng had thrown Han Daozhi out. Even now, his face was shrouded in a layer of fury.
The Malevolent Old Man was also quite shocked inside Luo Zheng's mind. However, he'd seen Luo Zheng reveal many secrets and knew Luo Zheng's background, so it was easier for him to accept it than others. But now that Luo Zheng had actually attacked Han Daozhi, he couldn't help but say, "Too impulsive, too impulsive. Your strength is shocking, but in a real fight, you might not be a match for a lower true god, and an intermediate true god can easily kill you. Han Daozhi is considered one of the best among upper true gods. Offending him will make you miserable!"
The Malevolent Old Man had been in the Vast Sky Domain for a long time, so he'd naturally heard of Han Daozhi. In the Han Family ancestral land, aside from a few old fellows who'd retired and returned to their ancestral land, Han Daozhi was probably the strongest. Now that Luo Zheng had been caught off guard, he'd made a fool of himself.
"If I keep tolerating him, what will he do?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind. "He's only doing this to force me to my limits or to investigate my background. I can die, but there are some things that I definitely won't reveal."
His father had indeed placed his greatest hope on him, but he wasn't the only one. After all, there was still Xuanyuan Chenfeng and the other Daozis in the Great Evolution Universe.
Hearing this, the Malevolent Old Man fell silent. He could feel the determination emanating from Luo Zheng, and he also understood how heavy of a target Luo Zheng was shouldering. His background was indeed extraordinary. He was both the son of a Sage and the disciple of another Sage. However, under his unique circumstances, he still had to pay a much greater price than ordinary people.
Luo Zheng's gaze was as calm as a still lake as he watched Han Daozhi pass through.
Han Daozhi passed through the hole and lightly shook his body. An invisible force spread out from his body, and all the dust, stones, and leaves disappeared. His entire body was clean, and his expression was unreadable.
Han Meng and the other two didn't say a word. They didn't dare to say anything now. Although Han Daozhi's temperament was strange, he was still the leader of a branch of the Han Family. They didn't know how he planned to deal with Luo Zheng. This guy's personality had always been unpredictable.

Chapter 1795
With Han Daozhi's words, the tension in the main pavilion eased.
Luo Zheng let out a small sigh of relief, but his tone didn't soften. "If you still want to test me, I'll withdraw from Heaven-Containing Prefecture. Everyone wants to go to the floating island, but this isn't my only chance!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Han Meng sighed inwardly.
Heaven-Containing Prefecture had plenty of extremely talented Proving Gods, and quite a few of them were madmen, but no one could guarantee that they'd be able to enter the floating island! He could see the absolute confidence on Luo Zheng's face, and he also believed that Luo Zheng had the strength to do so.
Even if he didn't rely on Heaven-Containing Prefecture's help, it wouldn't be too difficult for him to enter the floating island.
"Kid, you don't seem to be happy?" Han Daozhi chuckled at Luo Zheng. "You almost broke my old bones. I'm not going to settle this score with you …"
Luo Zheng said seriously, "Prefecture Lord said before that Heaven-Containing Prefecture doesn't accept people of unknown origins!"
Han Daozhi blinked and stared at Han Meng.
In truth, they'd called Han Daozhi over to discuss this matter, but Han Daozhi had attacked as soon as he'd appeared. He was interested in Luo Zheng's true strength, but he'd also tested Luo Zheng's inexplicable strength!
"Who said you're someone of unknown origins? Isn't your name Luo Tianxing? He was born in Meng Village outside of Mirror City in Long Sky Domain. He was born in Meng Village, outside of Mirror City in Long Sky Domain. He has been eating strange things since he was a child, which gave him extraordinary talents and great strength. He was taught by an expert … "Heart of the Dao made up a long story, and even made up some stories about Luo Tianxing's childhood.
Han Meng's face lit up. It looked like Han Daozhi was on his side. Luo Tianxing's background was checked by the Han Family branch. The main family had been on the floating island for a long time, and there were many things they couldn't investigate so thoroughly. Usually, whatever the branch said, the other side would believe. As long as Han Daozhi was willing to help Luo Tianxing forge an identity, there wouldn't be much of a problem.
"What if, I mean what if …" A trace of vigilance appeared on Han Chen's face. "It's not that I don't trust Luo Tianxing, but what if Luo Tianxing really has some sort of conspiracy? What if it's exposed …"
Han Daozhi glanced lazily at Han Chen, displeasure written all over his face. "You're in no position to shoulder this responsibility. Who knows, maybe the main family will be pissed at me and remove me from the position of family head. Then you'll have a chance to climb up, right?"
"Patriarch …" Han Chen's face was filled with embarrassment.
Han Daozhi couldn't be bothered with Han Chen and Han Yu. His expression changed quickly. Just a moment ago, he was unhappy, but now he was full of excitement. He turned to Luo Zheng and smiled. "Are you satisfied now? Since I'm such an interesting person, why don't you teach me the secret of cultivating strength? Hehehe … "
Only Han Daozhi knew how terrifying Luo Zheng's fall was. Others had only broken a few walls, but didn't know that Han Daozhi had used almost all of his strength to stabilize himself. Otherwise, it wouldn't have been a matter of breaking a few walls, but his body would've fallen apart!
It wasn't realistic for a Proof of Divinity cultivator to have such strength. It was beyond the realm of common sense. The Proof of Divinity cultivators raised by the great clans of Floating Island were also very strong, but Han Daozhi had encountered them before.
Han Cangyan, who was at the peak of his power, had a fortuitous encounter in the forbidden grounds of god refinement and cultivated great strength as well. However, when he was a realm lord, he was still far from the current Luo Tianxing.
"No comment." Luo Zheng knew this person was joking, but he still replied stiffly.
"I can give you the Han Family's secret scriptures …" Han Daozhi smiled.
Han Meng coughed dryly. "Since patriarch agrees to keep Luo Tianxing, do you want to wait until the Han Family sends someone over next year, or recommend him now?"
"Next year?" Han Daozhi smirked. "If we wait until next year, it'll be too late!"
"What do you mean?" Han Meng was slightly taken aback. He didn't quite understand what Han Daozhi was saying.
"The six branches already sent people to the Sea of Time a day ago!" Han Daozhi said coldly.
"Six branches!" Han Meng frowned. The thing he was most worried about had happened.
The twenty-seven branches of the Han Clan weren't a monolithic block. Some of the experts on the floating island came from the branches. These branches had a closer relationship with the experts from the main branch. The secondary sage of the main branch, Aunt Han Jiu, was from one of the six branches!
When Luo Zheng was acknowledged by the Violet Cloud Divine Dao, many people had seen it. Han Meng had been worried about this since then.
Aunt Hanjiu had an agreement with the Han family branch that anyone who could cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Dao would be accepted as a disciple by her. Previously, the Heaven-containing Prefecture had always followed this agreement and sent all the women who had the Extreme Violet Yin Body and were recognized by the Purple Qi Divine Dao to Aunt Hanjiu.
However, Luo Zheng didn't have the Extreme Violet Yin Body. Although he was acknowledged by the Violet Cloud Divine Dao, there had to be other reasons.
Aunt Hanjiu was the Han Family's secondary sage, second only to the Han Family's Sage Emperor. It was said that she had always been rude and unreasonable in the Han Family. Even the Sage Emperor had to give her some leeway. If she knew about this and forced Luo Zheng, the Heavenly Palace wouldn't be able to withstand it!
However, Han Meng hadn't expected the six branches to act so quickly. It had only been a few days, but they'd already sent someone to the Sea of Time!
"What should we do?" Han Meng's face revealed an anxious expression.
Han Daozhi chuckled. "I'm even faster. The second day after the exam, I sent someone over. I'm sure they'll be faster than the six branches. When the time comes, we'll send Luo Tianxing to Floating Island. If Aunt Hanjiu wants someone, she can ask the main branch!"
"This … the Patriarch is wise." Han Meng looked surprised. He sighed inwardly. The Patriarch's way of doing things was hard to fathom, but he was always one step ahead of them. His reactions were much faster than theirs.
Luo Zheng wasn't interested in their discussion. It didn't matter who helped him enter Floating Island. Since the Han Family's branches were willing to cooperate with him, it couldn't be better. If the Han Family didn't suspect him, then the other families on Floating Island had even less reason to suspect him.
After bidding farewell to the main pavilion, Luo Zheng didn't return to his residence. Instead, he went to Gold Coin Alley.
Han Daozhi had already informed Floating Island Han Jia. Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to stay in Liuren Divine City for much longer. He had to hurry and investigate the origin of the 'mud'. He didn't expect Heavenly Prefecture to help him condense a godhood. The 'mud' from the Tieh Family was the key to condensing a godhood!
In Gold Coin Alley, Tieh Lin was bored to death watching over her family's stall.
She didn't participate in the Heavenly Prefecture's exam this time. It wasn't that the Tieh Family couldn't take out a spot. With Tieh Lin's' management skills', not to mention one or two spots, even ten spots wouldn't be excessive.
It was just that Tieh Lin's cultivation talent was average. With her own strength, condensing a godhood would take a long time. In the future, she'd probably have to implant a masterless godhood.
When Luo Zheng appeared in front of Tieh Lin, her eyes released a strange light. Those familiar with her knew that she'd only reveal this look when she saw shenwu coins.

Chapter 1796
When the girl saw Luo Zheng, she smiled faintly and asked, "What do you want to buy this time? I dug up a few more pieces of mud! "
"What?" Luo Zheng was slightly startled. His gaze fell on the stall in front of her. Sure enough, there were a few pieces of mud on the stall …
However, this time, Luo Zheng's inner world did not react like last time. This meant that these pieces of mud were genuine mud! This girl from the Tie Family was really daring. She actually dug up a few pieces of mud and dared to sell them!
It turned out that after Tie Clan sold that piece of mud to Luo Zheng, there were different opinions within the clan. At that time, Tie Lin's second uncle had already determined that that piece of mud was not an ordinary item. Later on, Tie Clan lost their patience after multiple appraisals. They could only throw that piece of mud to Tie Lin casually. After all, nobody knew the purpose and origin of that piece of mud.
Luo Zheng naturally did not know the inside story of the Tie Family. He also did not expect that his "buying horse bones with a thousand gold coins" would have a series of effects.
The Tie Family never expected that someone would actually spend 500 Shenwu coins to buy a piece of mud that day!
In the eyes of the Tie Family, Luo Tianxing was definitely not a fool. He was willing to pay such a high price and Luo Zheng's shocking performance in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. The Tie Family was certain of the mud's value even though they did not know the use of the mud!
A portion of the Tie Family was very regretful. They felt that Tie Lin had made a loss in this transaction. Two days ago, someone actually jumped out to criticize Tie Lin.
Tie Lin herself did not care about these voices of the Tie Family. Those pedantic elders in the family were getting worse and worse. Even if the mud was really an extraordinary treasure, since the transaction was done, the money and goods were no longer relevant.
In the little girl's world view, doing business was to pursue the greatest profit. Therefore, she was not soft-hearted when she ripped off people.
However, she was very particular about integrity. Even if she lost a lot of treasures, she would not regret it.
During this period of time, everyone in the Tie Clan was busy. On one hand, it was for Tie Yan's participation in the Heaven Concealment examination. On the other hand, it was for that piece of mud. The Tie Clan had turned over the hill where the mud was found. They hoped to dig up a second piece of mud. Unfortunately, they had not found anything so far.
On the other hand, Tie Lin casually dug out some mud outside Liuren Divine City and set a price of five Shenwu coins per piece. There were some people who came to try their luck and bought it. After all, Luo Zheng had spent five hundred Shenwu coins, and they could buy the exact same piece of mud for only five Shenwu coins. They felt like they had profited. Perhaps there was something special about this piece of mud?
Tie Lin didn't think that she was deceiving people. What was the use of the mud? It was up to the individual to figure it out. Maybe the piece that Luo Zheng bought was also ordinary mud?
A random piece of mud from the ground could be sold for five Shenwu coins. Such a thing was unheard of in the Alley of Gold Coins!
In those few days, Tie Lin really sold more than twenty pieces of mud and earned more than a hundred Shenwu coins for the Tie Clan!
The True Gods next door were all envious!
Seeing that the little girl's mud was selling so well, they all started to imitate her. Suddenly, there were many people selling mud in the Alley of Gold Coins …
Some True Gods who didn't know the inside story came to the Alley of Gold Coins and were stunned by this scene. They even suspected that the people in the Alley of Gold Coins had lost their minds.
As the number of people selling mud increased, the competition became more and more intense. Therefore, some people started to make up stories and boast about how magical their piece of mud was. They even said that it was a miracle pill.
Those True Gods weren't fools. After being tricked by Tie Lin at the beginning, it was hard for anyone to fall for it. Tie Lin also despised those who imitated her. Now that the mud couldn't be sold, she was thinking of new ideas. She didn't expect Luo Tianxing to appear in front of her again.
"I'm not buying anything." Luo Zheng shook his head and replied.
Tie Lin smiled faintly, as if she saw through Luo Zheng's thoughts. "I know. You want to find out where the mud was dug up, right?"
"Not really." Luo Zheng laughed.
Luo Zheng was indeed here for this matter. However, since he knew that this little girl was so cunning, why would he stretch his neck and let her take advantage of him again?
"Oh?" Tie Lin's face showed a hint of surprise. "Not for this matter? Then what is it for? "
Tie Lin had already made up her mind. The mountain where the mud was dug up originally belonged to the Tie Clan. Although it was a mess now, in her opinion, it was still very attractive to Luo Zheng. She planned to sell the entire mountain to Luo Zheng directly.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng's reply made her unable to say what she originally wanted to say.
"I'm looking for your brother, Tie Yan. He agreed to let me visit your Tie Clan," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Before entering the Temple of Inquiry, Luo Zheng had a short contact with Tie Yan. Tie Yan's personality was simple and honest, much easier to deal with than Tie Lin. However, Tie Yan didn't pass the Heaven-Containing House's examination. After the examination, Luo Zheng hadn't seen him again. Now, he could only look for her brother through Tie Lin.
"My brother …" Tie Lin's thin eyebrows slightly knitted. In the end, she was still too young. After her idea fell through, her face revealed a depressed expression.
She knew her brother's personality the best. He was honest, honest, and extremely stubborn. Once he agreed to something, it was very difficult to change his mind. Even if Tie Lin's mouth was full of flowers, she wouldn't be able to persuade Tie Yan at all. If her brother really agreed to this matter, she naturally didn't expect to extort Luo Zheng again.
What a pity. She silently said in her heart. However, after thinking about it, this Luo Tianxing had been the talk of the town recently. It was said that he would definitely be able to enter the floating island.
Tie Clan definitely didn't have the qualifications to rope in such a person. However, if they could form a good relationship with Luo Tianxing, it would be greatly beneficial to Tie Clan if he really made a name for himself in the future.
Doing business wasn't always measured by "shenwu coins". There were some favors that couldn't be exchanged with shenwu coins. After thinking through this point, Tie Lin no longer hesitated. She told Tie Yan where he lived in Liuren Divine City. At the same time, she urged Luo Zheng to comfort her brother.
Tie Yan was ranked fifty-sixth on the list. This time, sixty-seven people passed the Heaven-Containing House's examination. Tie Yan was naturally depressed that he was eliminated. According to the ranking, he should have passed the examination. Unfortunately, obtaining the recognition of the dao stele didn't depend on the ranking. It was based on one's affinity with the divine dao. There were a few Proof of Divinity cultivators ranked in the thirties who didn't pass the examination.
Luo Zheng immediately agreed to Tie Lin's request.
Tie Clan still had some properties in Liuren Divine City. Following Tie Lin's guidance, he quickly found Tie Yan.
Tie Yan's mood was indeed somewhat low these days. However, his temperament didn't affect him too much. Instead, he was even more determined. If he didn't pass the examination this year, he could just try again next year. If he didn't pass the examination next year, he would try again the year after. For Proof of Divinity cultivators, a few decades was nothing.
Three days later, under Tie Yan's lead, Luo Zheng left Liuren Divine City and headed towards Tie Clan.
As the Han Clan's ancestral land, Liuren Domain was much larger than ordinary small domains.
Although Tie Clan was said to be just outside Liuren Divine City, it was still very far away. Luo Zheng and Tie Yan only rushed back half a month later.
In the eyes of the Tie Clan, Luo Zheng, this genius who had suddenly appeared out of nowhere, was already an honored guest. However, after a round of warm hospitality, the elders of the Tie Clan tried to fish for information from Luo Zheng. They wanted to know what the use of that piece of mud was.
How could Luo Zheng explain it to them? He could only brush them off perfunctorily. Tie Clan's elders saw that Luo Zheng wasn't willing to answer, so they stopped asking.
After a round of hospitality, Tie Yan brought Luo Zheng out of Tie Clan's residence and headed towards the mountain where Tie Clan had dug out the mysterious mud.
When Luo Zheng arrived at the mountain peak, he was speechless when he saw the huge hole full of potholes. It wouldn't be an exaggeration to say that Tie Clan had dug three feet into the ground for that mud.
However, it seemed like Tie Clan didn't gain anything.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1797
Around the mountaintop, there were some members of the Tie Clan who were either sitting or standing. All of them were quietly sizing up Luo Tianxing.
The fact that a piece of mud was sold for 500 Shenwu coins had shocked every member of the clan. The elders of the Tie Clan had also said that whoever could dig up that kind of mud would be rewarded handsomely by the clan!
Because of these rewards, everyone in the Tie Clan had moved out. Even the women and children of the Tie Clan had come to try their luck.
However, no matter how hard they dug, there was only ordinary mud at the bottom of the mountain. They didn't come across any strange mud that could run, jump, or possess spirituality …
Luo Zheng stood at the edge of the huge pit and looked down. There was actually a huge pit that was 3000 feet deep at the bottom of the mountain!
It was impossible for the God Folk to do this. To be able to dig up such a huge pit in such a short time, it must have been done by the Tie Clan's True Gods. Moreover, there must have been a middle-level True God involved.
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. These True Gods were really different from what he had imagined.
Back when Luo Zheng was in the Great Yan Universe, the Illusion God was a lofty existence. They didn't eat the food of mortals and looked down on all living beings below them. They were omnipotent and nothing could restrict them.
After arriving at the Divine Domain, Luo Zheng realized that these True Gods were more 'human' than ordinary people to a certain extent.
For example, the martial artists in the Great Yan Universe would usually choose to abstain from eating in order to preserve the purity of their bodies. This would save them a lot of trouble. If they didn't abstain from eating, they would have to consume the five grains every day and purify their bodies every once in a while.
However, the True Gods in the Divine Domain rarely chose to abstain from eating. They saw all kinds of delicacies as a great pleasure.
There were many True Gods in the Tie Clan, but because of the benefits of the 500 Shenwu coins, they dug up such a huge pit crazily. Every one of them was influenced, dominated, and driven crazy by the Shenwu coins …
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng faintly felt that something was wrong. He asked in his mind again, "Old Man Extreme Evil, what is the use of the Shenwu coins?"
Just from the value of the Shenwu coins, the value of the Shenwu coins was terrifying. Martial artists who proved themselves to be gods worked hard for many years and could only save up one Shenwu coin. However, Luo Zheng was curious about the source of the Shenwu coins' value. What was the basis of this dark and swarthy piece of round plate?
"Hehehe …" Old Man Extreme Evil laughed. "I will tell you when you enter the floating island................. of course........... Shenwu coins..
It was no secret that Shenwu Coins could be found all over the Celestial Domain. Because of this, Luo Zheng did not dare to ask. If he did not even have basic knowledge, others might suspect his origins again.
The Evil Old Man kept Luo Zheng in suspense, so Luo Zheng didn't force him.
.......... and a........................
Luo Zheng couldn't distinguish the mud, but his inner world could. His inner world was like a starving puppy. Once it got close to the special "mud", it would impatiently transmit a strong consciousness!
After Luo Zheng slid down, Tie Yan followed behind him. Tie Yan wasn't greedy. Since he had brought Luo Zheng to the Tie Clan, he would definitely help him to the end. He only followed Luo Zheng for safety reasons.
Not long after, Luo Zheng slid to the bottom of the pit.
The pit was three hundred feet deep. Before that, the soil was soft and moist. After three hundred feet, the soil disappeared and was replaced by black gangue. These gangue were relatively sturdy. It wouldn't be easy for Luo Zheng to break it.
Standing at the bottom of the pit, Luo Zheng looked around. His inner world's consciousness was still immersed. It didn't respond at all.
Although Luo Zheng was mentally prepared, he couldn't help but feel a little disappointed.
That special mud was indeed very difficult to find. It was already very lucky for him to find a piece. The chances of finding another one here were very slim.
"The Tie Clan has been trying their best to find it. If they could find a piece, Tie Yan would definitely give it to Brother Tianxing. Unfortunately, they didn't." Tie Yan shook his head.
If Tie Lin heard her brother's words, she would probably explode in anger. A piece of mud could be exchanged for 500 Shenwu coins from Luo Tianxing! The Tie Clan wasn't rich. 500 Shenwu coins was a huge sum. How could they give it to him?
If someone else said this, they would definitely think that they were just showing off. However, Tie Yan was simple and honest. He always meant what he said. The Tie Clan really didn't find that kind of mud. If they did, he would definitely do it!
"It's okay. I'm already very lucky to be able to find a clue," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
As he spoke, he wandered around the bottom of the pit. At the same time, he constantly paid attention to the movements in his inner world. Once his inner world reacted, the result was self-evident!
After circling around the pit a few times, Luo Zheng asked, "Tie Lin said that her Second Uncle dug it out from under a big tree. Do you know the original location of the big tree?"
This place had been dug beyond recognition. All the trees had been removed. Luo Zheng naturally couldn't see where it was.
"The Tie Clan dug this place with the big tree as the center. Logically speaking, it should be in the center of the pit," Tie Yan answered truthfully.
Luo Zheng nodded and walked towards the center of the pit. After wandering around for a while, Luo Zheng couldn't help but let out a sigh. If he couldn't find that kind of mud here, he didn't know when he would encounter it again.
If he couldn't find that mud, perhaps he really couldn't condense a godhood. Then, the Primal Chaos Secret Technique would be considered to have reached the end. He couldn't even enter the True God Realm. How could he realize his greater ambitions and plans?
"Did you find anything?" Tie Yan asked with concern.
Luo Zheng revealed a bitter smile and shook his head. "Let's go. Let's climb up and take a look …"
Since he didn't find anything in the pit, Luo Zheng could only walk around. However, he knew that the chances of finding the outside were even lower.
Tie Yan was also slightly disappointed. He had invited Luo Zheng to the Tie Clan with the approval of the Tie Clan.
A portion of the Tie Clan might be greedy for shenwu coins and strongly opposed outsiders entering this place. However, Tie Yan believed that it was better to be on good terms with Luo Tianxing than to earn shenwu coins. A clan's development couldn't be without shenwu coins, but shenwu coins couldn't decide everything.
Thus, Luo Zheng climbed up from the other side. Just as Luo Zheng was climbing along the small path, he suddenly felt a slight tremor in his body!
Because of that slight tremor, Luo Zheng's entire body froze on the spot. A strange light flashed in his eyes.
"Seems like it's here …" Luo Zheng thought to himself.
Although the movement in his body was rather weak, Luo Zheng still caught it.
Perhaps it was because the reaction to the special "mud" was very weak, and the consciousness in his body wasn't very certain. However, it still sent a trace of a signal to Luo Zheng.
"Brother Tianxing?" Tie Yan, who was behind him, saw Luo Zheng suddenly stop and asked curiously.
Luo Zheng turned around and smiled at Tie Yan. "Can I dig here?"
"It's fine." Tie Yan waved his hand. He sincerely hoped that Luo Zheng would be able to find what he wanted.
Luo Zheng reached out and placed his hand on the scabbard. Suddenly, the Da Qian Heavy Sword appeared in his hand. Then, with a gentle twist, the Da Qian Heavy Sword suddenly spun and pointed at the side of the pit!
"Pop!"
The Da Qian Heavy Sword touched the soft soil. The power within was like an invisible ripple that began to spin along with the soil!
After Luo Zheng withdrew the Da Qian Heavy Sword, a circular passage about six to seven zhang long appeared in front of him. This passage seemed to have been carved out with a sharp knife. It was neat and tidy, enough to show Luo Zheng's foundation in using power.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and rushed into the passage in the blink of an eye.
The moment he entered the passage, the consciousness in his body gradually became clearer. Luo Zheng's heart also began to beat faster. There was indeed more than one piece of that special mud!

Chapter 1798
Tie Yan didn't follow Luo Zheng into the cave.
Seeing Luo Zheng's actions, the people of the Tie Family above the big pit all showed signs of interest. It seemed that Luo Zheng had indeed discovered something!
It had to be said that the people of the Tie Family felt somewhat aggrieved now.
Luo Tianxing was digging in their Tie Clan's territory, and the Tie Clan could only watch helplessly. Anyone would feel unhappy.
However, they didn't know what that thing was!
They had directly shaved off a layer of a mountain and dug a big pit, but they still couldn't find it. Luo Tianxing hadn't been here for long, and he had already discovered some clues …
"If Luo Tianxing really found the mud, would we really let him take it away?"
"I really don't want to let this kid take advantage of us!"
"What else can we do? Let's not talk about anything else. Even if we really found the mud, what's the use of it? Let Tie Lin sell it? Don't you know that there are people selling mud everywhere in the Money Alley? "
Some of the people of the Tie Family also realized the crux of the problem.
Even if they really found the spiritual mud, they wouldn't be able to sell it at a high price in the Money Alley. Others wouldn't believe the Tie Family. Who could be sure that the mud of the Tie Family was the mud that Luo Zheng bought? Even if it was really the same as Luo Zheng's mud, it was useless to buy it. No one knew the purpose of this mud.
Moreover, there were even rumors that Luo Tianxing and the Tie Family had teamed up to play a double act and sold the ordinary mud at a sky-high price just to deceive some people who didn't know the truth …
The elders of the Tie Family weren't fools. They listened to Tie Yan's suggestion and let Tie Yan lead Luo Zheng to the Tie Family after careful consideration. Even if the mud that Luo Zheng dug was indeed something extremely rare, Luo Zheng's insight was like a torch, and the Tie Family could only do him a favor.
The tunnel that Luo Zheng dug at the beginning was parallel. He constantly changed his position in this long and narrow cave, testing the movements of his Inner World.
After he dug along the cave for a distance of more than a hundred feet, his Inner World returned to normal.
"He should have walked past it." Luo Zheng quickly made a judgment.
Then, he turned around and walked through the passage, paying attention to the changes in his inner world. When that consciousness came from his inner world again, he immediately stopped. "It should be here …"
Luo Zheng didn't have much confidence in his judgment. He was completely relying on his Inner World to sense the "mud". He could only estimate a vague location.
From this position, Luo Zheng began to dig with the Great Thousand Heavy Sword.
Therefore, Luo Zheng opened up a vertical tunnel leading downwards …
When Luo Zheng came back from the bottom of the pit, he climbed up to a height of four hundred feet. Now, he was going down again. After digging for about four hundred feet, the Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword touched the solid gangue. "Dang!" A crisp sound was heard. He had reached the gangue layer!
The upper layer of the soil was soft, but that was relative to the Divine Realm. In fact, the weight of the soil was not light. A bucket of soil was equivalent to the weight of a mountain in the Dayan Universe.
As for the solid gangue below, it was even more terrifying. Even Luo Zheng would have a hard time digging through it. Therefore, the Tie Family had stopped at the gangue layer.
After reaching this place, Luo Zheng found that the consciousness of his Inner World was getting stronger and stronger. This consciousness wasn't the consciousness of a living creature. The Inner World couldn't clearly express what was below. However, it was exactly the same as when Luo Zheng encountered the mud in the Alley of Gold Coins.
Now, Luo Zheng certainly wouldn't give up. No matter how solid the gangue was, he would continue to dig.
"Boom, boom!"
Luo Zheng held the Great Thousand Heavy Sword and suddenly stabbed down!
When he was digging the soil, Luo Zheng was very relaxed. He didn't use too much strength and only used a certain technique to carve out a smooth hole. Right now, Luo Zheng was using 30% of his strength!
This huge force passed through the Great Thousand Heavy Sword and pierced through the gangue. Under one strike, the solid gangue was instantly covered in cracks. Under another strike, the cracked gangue turned into fingernail-sized gravel and splattered in all directions.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
Luo Zheng continued to bombard the gangue underground. Even though he was more than three hundred feet underground, the sound was still transmitted to the surface. The violent vibrations were also fed back to the surface through the soil. The surrounding Tie Clan people felt the ground trembling continuously, as if there was a small earthquake here!
"That fellow is digging through the gangue layer …"
"Does he really have that much strength? The gangue layer is so solid. Even a mid-level True God would have a hard time digging through it! "
"Otherwise, why would the Heaven-Containing Manor think so highly of him? After all, he is a genius qualified to enter the floating island. "
The Tie Family members felt the vibrations beneath their feet and sighed emotionally.
Naturally, they didn't know that Luo Zheng had thrown the upper level True God Han Dao Capital out and even smashed a few of the Heaven-Containing Manor's walls. This kind of news couldn't be spread out.
"Boom, boom …"
The loud rumbling continued to reverberate, and Luo Zheng's forehead was already covered with a layer of fine sweat.
He had already dug through the gangue layer for about seven to eight feet. The consciousness of his inner world was also getting stronger. It seemed like he was getting closer and closer!
"It shouldn't be far." Luo Zheng wiped the sweat off his forehead and raised the Great Thousand Heavy Sword again. "Break for me!"
"Peng …"
This time, when Luo Zheng's long sword stabbed forward, he felt empty in his hand. He had actually pierced through something!
It was pitch black all around. The outside light couldn't shine in at all. Luo Zheng reached out and patted lightly. A wisp of flame burned along the Great Thousand Heavy Sword. Under the illumination of the flame, a fist-sized hole appeared beneath his feet. It was a small hole made by the Great Thousand Heavy Sword.
"It's empty below the gangue layer." Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of relief. The Great Thousand Heavy Sword in his hand suddenly smashed the last layer of gangue into pieces. As he fell, the Great Thousand Heavy Sword suddenly pierced through the gangue layer. His entire body hung in mid-air by the sword hilt.
Luo Zheng then freed a hand and lightly waved it. A ball of flame burned fiercely in his hand.
This flame was extremely pure. The light it emitted was also very pure. It was a 'fledgling flame'. Comprehending those divine daos might not increase his strength by much, but it had many wonderful uses!
Under the illumination of the flame, Luo Zheng could clearly see the situation below. This was a cave with a width of thousands of feet. In the center of the cave, there was a brown stone with a length of 200 feet …
Just as Luo Zheng was sizing up the stone, he suddenly felt a wave of power coming from behind him!
He had been vigilant. The moment the wave appeared, he had already pulled out the Great Thousand Heavy Sword from the wall. His entire body suddenly fell down. While falling, Luo Zheng's body suddenly rolled in the air. Only then did he see clearly that there was a black thing lying on the top of the cave!
That black thing was like a person made of mud. Just now, that thing had stretched out its spike-like hand to sneak attack Luo Zheng from behind. After this attack failed, the spike-like hand quickly changed into a gecko's claw. It clung to the top of the cave and began to climb up rapidly.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
That thing made a series of strange sounds as it climbed. Its speed was extremely fast. Soon, it disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight!
"This thing … is that mud that I swallowed before?"
When Luo Zheng saw that thing, his Inner World transmitted a turbulent consciousness. It was almost the same feeling as that day in the Gold Money Alley. Now, the consciousness of his Inner World was constantly reminding Luo Zheng that he must swallow that thing. He must swallow it …
Of course, Luo Zheng wouldn't let go of this opportunity. The moment he landed, his feet were filled with a powerful force. He suddenly kicked off the ground, bounced up, and rushed directly to the place where the black thing had disappeared.
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng once again inserted his sword into the gangue and hung himself on the top of the cave. His eyes followed the rock wall at the top, and his eyebrows slightly wrinkled.

Chapter 1799
After a careful examination, he couldn't find any clues.
The consciousness transmitted from his inner world was still very strong, but after entering the cave, he could no longer use it to determine his position.
He could only pull out the Da Qian Heavy Sword from the gangue and place it firmly on the ground.
This cave was in absolute silence. It seemed that no living creature had entered this cave for countless years.
If someone who was well-versed in geography and feng shui were to see such a huge cave in the gangue, they would definitely be dumbfounded.
The entire divine domain's topography was very neat and orderly.
The outermost layer of the divine domain was ordinary soil. Below the soil was the gangue layer, and below the gangue layer was the flint layer. The flint layer was a hundred times harder than the gangue layer, and below the flint layer was the tendon layer.
The tendon layer was an extremely flexible stone. Even an upper true god would have a hard time tearing it apart. Below the tendon layer was a type of green magma known as the 'Black Fire Torrent'. This' Black Fire Torrent 'was even more dangerous. Even saints would find it hard to survive if they were sucked in!
For so many years, countless true gods had explored the various forbidden areas of the divine domain. Saints, on the other hand, had explored the primal chaos outside the divine domain. As for what the core of the divine domain was, there was still no conclusion.
Over a hundred divine epochs ago, three saints had ventured deep into the Black Fire Torrent. In the end, one of them died and the other two were injured. They had no choice but to retreat.
Therefore, many true gods believed that their understanding of the divine domain was far from as thorough as the various floating island clans claimed. Some people asserted that there were countless secrets hidden beneath the vast divine domain.
The gangue layer was considered the second layer of the divine domain's topography. Most of the caves in the divine domain were in the soft layer of soil. Under the pressure of countless divine epochs, it was very difficult for such a huge cave to appear.
Luo Zheng raised the Da Qian Heavy Sword and moved slowly in the cave. At the tip of the Da Qian Heavy Sword, a flame silently released a clean light. Luo Zheng couldn't find the black thing and could only explore the cave first.
The most eye-catching thing in the cave was the round stone in the middle.
When Luo Zheng walked to the side of the boulder and took a closer look, a strange look appeared in his eyes. Both he and the Evil Old Man in his mind exclaimed at the same time, "What the hell is this?"
A man and a woman were lying on this round rock with a diameter of twenty zhang!
However, the state of these two people was very strange. Their hands and feet seemed to be embedded in the stone. They looked like people who grew in the stone.
If that was all, Luo Zheng and the Evil Old Man would not have been so surprised. The two people on the stone gave off a very strange feeling, especially their faces. They were pieced together from several identical faces, forming a clever three-dimensional effect. It looked strange, but also very natural, as if they were born to look like this.
This was especially true for that woman. She had a total of four faces pieced together. Each face, according to human standards, was extremely beautiful. However, each of the four faces had a different expression. There was joy, anger, sadness, and joy …
In addition to their faces, their hands and feet were also the same. As for their torsos, they were deeply embedded in the stone and could not be seen at all.
"Is there such a race in the divine territory?" Luo Zheng asked.
During his time in God's Domain, Luo Zheng had also absorbed all sorts of knowledge from the game.
There were undoubtedly many races in the Divine Territory, but the difference between these races and "humans" was not that great. This was especially so after the God's People had cultivated to become True Gods. They would hide the characteristics of their own races.
"Definitely not! This is not a living creature … It's probably a stone sculpture that someone threw here, "the Evil Old Man replied with certainty.
The Evil Old Man was an experienced and knowledgeable person. He did not dare to say that he understood the entire divine territory. However, this strange race in the stone completely exceeded his understanding of the word "living creature." That was why the Evil Old Man was so certain.
Luo Zheng scratched his head. He could not figure out the situation in front of him.
Since the special mud appeared here, it should be related to the people in the stone. However, he had no way to find out what the connection and secret was.
"Let's find that thing first …" Luo Zheng looked to the side.
It was unknown how long the two people embedded in the stone had been through. Their bodies seemed to have turned into the same kind of brown stone. However, when Luo Zheng looked at each other earlier, he had a feeling that the woman was staring at him. It gave him a very unnatural feeling!
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a fluctuation in the space behind him.
"Whoosh …"
"It's here!"
Luo Zheng was delighted.
His purpose in entering this place was to find that mysterious mud!
Tie Lin had once told Luo Zheng that when her second uncle caught the mud, it was a black thing that could walk and jump. It must be the thing that had attacked him previously. He was having a headache over how to find that thing. Now, it had come out of its own accord!
Without any hesitation, he turned around and swung his sword!
The young fire on the Da Qian Heavy Sword burned fiercely. When the sword was swung out, it bloomed with a strong flame!
"Dong!"
When Luo Zheng's sword slashed out, it actually hit something extremely sturdy. His arm immediately went numb. The Great Chiliocosm Sword itself wasn't light. Even though the materials in the divine realm were generally many times sturdier than the Great Expansion Universe, Luo Zheng was still able to cut through the layers of gangue with the Great Expansion Universe unimpeded.
He had never expected that there was something that could block his attack.
"Whoosh!"
The moment the Da Qian Heavy Sword was bounced back, Luo Zheng slid back like a ghost. He held the Da Qian Heavy Sword horizontally in front of his chest and looked at it vigilantly. He saw a protruding stone not far away. It was a gangue!
"What's going on …"
Luo Zheng's expression became more and more surprised.
Before exploring that round stone, Luo Zheng had already taken in the entire cave. He did not see that protruding gangue. In other words, that gangue had quietly grown out behind Luo Zheng!
