Moreover, ordinary gangue would not be able to block his attack. That gangue was much stronger than ordinary gangue!
"Luo Zheng, this cave has changed. The entrance seems to have disappeared …" Old Man Evil reminded.
Luo Zheng frowned and looked up at the top of the cave!
It was really strange. The hole he had made just now had actually disappeared!
Luo Zheng's face was full of vigilance. He had experienced many dangerous places over the years. However, although those places were extremely dangerous to Luo Zheng, they were all existences that he could understand!
Now, the strangeness of the cave he was in had completely exceeded his scope of understanding …
"Whoosh …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt something move beneath his feet. His eyes flashed sharply. He exerted strength in his heel and his entire body suddenly rolled.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Long, thin, and sharp gangue constantly pierced out from the ground. These gangue were like rapidly growing stalagmites, wanting to directly pierce through Luo Zheng! The strength of these special gangue was not weaker than Luo Zheng's Da Qian Heavy Sword. Not to mention a Proof of Divinity expert, even an ordinary True God would be severely injured if they were stabbed!
Luo Zheng's rolling speed was extremely fast. Those gangue that protruded from the ground were always a little bit stronger than him!
In a short while, the entire cave was densely packed with sharp gangue. It was like a monster's nest full of spikes.
During this process, Luo Zheng didn't stop at all. As long as he paused for a moment, stalagmites would immediately shoot out from under him. In the end, Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and could only jump up and onto the round rock!
Just as he was jumping in the air, from the top and bottom of the cave, slender and sharp gangue extended dozens of feet. They were like long snakes spiraling upwards, wanting to pierce through Luo Zheng!
"Dong dong!"
Luo Zheng used the Da Qian Heavy Sword to slash at those rapidly growing stalagmites in the air. With the help of the momentum, he jumped onto that round stone!
"Rumble …"
He did not expect that just as he stood on this round stone, the entire cave would tremble.
Luo Zheng's judgment was not wrong. This round stone did not have any spikes. However, at the top of the cave, hundreds of stalagmites spread out at the same time, forming a cage, completely enveloping Luo Zheng and that round stone!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1800
The Tie Family members were still waiting outside …
Tie Yan stood at the edge of the pit and looked at the tunnel Luo Zheng dug out with a hesitant expression.
Half an hour ago, there were violent vibrations coming from the ground. Luo Zheng was probably trying to break through the layer of gangue. The violent vibrations lasted for a while before stopping. According to Tie Yan's estimation, Luo Zheng should have given up.
Even Tie Yan's elders would find it difficult to break through the layer of gangue. Not to mention, there was nothing in the layer of gangue. Luo Zheng couldn't find what he was looking for.
After waiting for a long time without any movement, Tie Yan couldn't help but want to go in and take a look.
At this time, Tie Yan's second uncle and third uncle also slid down the edge of the pit.
Back then, it was Second Uncle who had discovered that piece of mud, and Second Uncle and Third Uncle had dug this big hole together.
"That Luo Tianxing still hasn't come out?"
The elders of the Tie Family were also paying attention to this place. However, it wasn't convenient for them to come out. Since Luo Tianxing was in contact with Tie Yan, this matter was left to the younger generation to deal with. Now that there was no movement for a long time, they were worried.
After all, Luo Tianxing was a popular figure in the Heaven Containing Mansion. If something strange happened and he died in their Tie Family, it wouldn't be good.
Tie Yan shook his head, "There's still no movement."
Second uncle and third uncle looked at each other. Second uncle said, "Let's go in and take a look. Don't let anything happen."
"Hehe, what could happen …" Third uncle said with a lazy smile.
Tie Family had been stationed here for a long time. This place wasn't far from Liuren Divine City. It was almost impossible for a powerful monster to appear here. Although Tie Yan's third uncle said that, he still followed second uncle into the tunnel Luo Zheng dug out. Tie Yan also followed behind.
After walking for a short while, they heard Second Uncle's warning. "Be careful, there's a hole under your feet." After saying that, Second Uncle stepped over it. The three of them continued to walk forward, and before long, they reached a dead end.
"Haha, that guy should have gone down from that hole. Let's go down and have a look." Tie Yan's second uncle was very sensitive and could guess Luo Zheng's thoughts. Perhaps Luo Zheng felt something was wrong halfway down, so he turned back and continued to go down. "I suspect that he might have sensed something. Tie Yan, you don't have to go down. Your third uncle and I will go down together."
However, when the two Real Gods slowly descended to the bottom of the cave, their faces revealed a strange expression.
Second Uncle stomped his feet at the bottom. "Good lad, he actually managed to break through so much of the gangue layer. But … where did he go?"
Tie Yan's third uncle was also puzzled. "That's impossible. Along the way, we didn't see any other tunnels. He could only be down there. It's impossible for him to go anywhere else!"
Second Uncle squatted down and pondered for a while. His expression became more and more confused. "There's a small hole here. It should have been caused by a blunt weapon. It should be Luo Tianxing's weapon … In other words, the bottom should be empty. He used his weapon to hang here …"
After saying that, Second Uncle was also confused by his own deduction. He felt that his deduction was correct, but the gangue under his feet clearly didn't match the traces left by Luo Tianxing!
Third Uncle's brain wasn't as good as Second Uncle's. After hearing this, he became even more confused. "You're saying that there's a big hole in the gangue layer? And that big hole ran away? "
"That's what the traces here tell me, but that shouldn't be the case …" Second Uncle smiled bitterly.
"Then let's dig down and take a look." Third Uncle's heart skipped a beat. Perhaps there really was a big secret in the territory of the Tie Clan?
Luo Tianxing came to the Tie Clan and was lucky enough to find the mud. The Tie Clan could generously let Luo Tianxing take it away as an investment to befriend a future genius. However, if there were other amazing treasures, the Tie Clan would definitely want a share of it. Perhaps with these treasures, the Tie Clan could become a second-tier clan in one fell swoop!
Faced with this unknown temptation, Second Uncle and Third Uncle continued to dig along the tunnel dug down by Luo Zheng.
However, this layer of gangue was too hard. Even if the two True Gods were digging, it was still very difficult. Using various methods, the two of them once again dug down a tunnel that was five hundred feet deep. But no matter how they dug down, it was still a thick layer of gangue … In the end, the two of them had no choice but to give up and climb back down this vertical tunnel.
In the cave …
Luo Zheng only knew that the hole he dug had disappeared. Now, the cave he was in was in a completely sealed state. This cave was like a bubble in the water, producing a miraculous displacement!
The gangue extending from the top of his head formed a fence-like cage, completely sealing Luo Zheng in this round stone. The gangue was also changing. The originally black area had now turned into a translucent light green.
Not only did the gangue that "grew out" change in color, but the color of the entire cave walls also changed!
"What the hell is going on …" Luo Zheng smiled bitterly, but he didn't panic.
Although these special gangue were very hard, Luo Zheng could break them easily with his full strength. However, this scene was too strange, causing Luo Zheng to feel uneasy. Humans were always afraid of the unknown. Who knew what would jump out of this cave?
"I don't know either. This old man has been to the Three Great Forbidden Lands and even sneaked into the Soul Wilderness. However, I have never encountered such a thing …" The Evil Old Man also had a helpless expression. Luo Zheng only wanted to find a piece of mud. How could he encounter such a bizarre thing?
Just as Luo Zheng was feeling depressed, he suddenly saw the black thing appear again not far away … That thing turned into the shape of a starfish and slowly moved along the walls of the cave. As it moved, it left behind colorful mucus on the walls of the cave. Then, the walls of the cave began to change.
Under the illumination of the fire, huge shadows began to appear on the translucent green walls around the cave. These shadows all had huge heads and long and narrow arms. They were like huge monsters!
"There's something inside the walls." Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
"What are those shadows? Why are they constantly growing?" The Evil Old Man was also shocked.
Luo Zheng smiled bitterly. "They're not growing. Those things are walking inside the walls …"
There was indeed a huge creature approaching from behind the translucent walls. It seemed that it would not be long before these things came out of the walls.
"I think you should hurry up and think of a way to escape …" The Evil Old Man advised. He knew that Luo Zheng had held back a lot of his strength. However, the strange scene before them was too strange and dangerous. He advised Luo Zheng to give up on that piece of mud. This was obviously a trap set by that black thing.
Generally speaking, the most dangerous places in the Divine Territory were the various Forbidden Lands. Some Forbidden Lands were difficult for even Saints to enter.
However, outside the Forbidden Lands was the world of the True Gods! Ordinary monsters could not survive outside the Forbidden Lands!
However, the ferocious creatures that could survive outside the Forbidden Lands, that is, in places like the Liuren Domain, either had terrifying strength that even the Sages did not dare to touch, or had a very strong concealment ability that was not known to the world at all.
Regardless of whether it was the former or the latter, Luo Zheng could not fight against them now.
In the Evil Old Man's opinion, the monster in this cave should belong to the latter. It relied on its powerful concealment abilities to hide here. Over the countless years, it had probably quietly devoured many True Gods …
"I definitely have to escape." Luo Zheng looked at the swaying shadows behind the walls of the cave. His gaze was fixed on the wriggling black "mud" on the walls. The shadows behind the walls should have been summoned by this thing!
The consciousness of the Inner World was still reacting strongly. That "mud" was too important to him. Leaving this place as soon as possible was definitely a wise choice. However, in order to condense Godhood as soon as possible, he had a reason to fight!
